# cowork-sync Plan 4 (regions and claims) — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build 04-regions and the claim half of 02-protocol on the Plan 2 engine and the Plan 3 holds: one pinned web-tree-sitter 0.27.x WASM runtime with five grammars and cowork's own query files running in a `worker_threads` pool (`dist/worker.js` becomes real), symbol-level region tables with the six path classes, `extent-convention/1` and `label-convention/1`, the frozen line mapper FoldMapper/1 (`hunks`, `mapRanges` in `fold` and `local` mode), typed `claim` and `release` ops and their fold (PRO-17 validity with `foldOverlap`, the claim effects of `join`, `leave`, `seat_end` and `save`, grants and reservations as fold data, compaction), the claim view, CONFIRM and PREDICT (04 §3.11) with Algorithm CLAIM, claim emission at publish (Algorithm EMIT, re-anchors, tombstones, `claimPrecheck`, late-bound claim ops in the outbox), the one touch predicate and its containment form, the B1 publish gate bound to Plan 3's `PublishGatePort` (K3 on seat change-sets, K6 on human change-sets), the post-merge placement check on every apply and publish (K4 by placement), the D8 step 4 claim tie-break (`claim-breach`) and holder-side breach detection (K5 input), the relative region tripwires and the interface/caller/callee/import checks bound to Plan 3's `T0RegionsPort` (K7 items 1–2) and `SymbolPort`, the grammar legs of the D9 insertion-concat, the edit check `editCheck` (glossary §27.1, REG-36) bound to Plan 3's `EditCheckPort`, label verification (REG-34), the region message codes, the real `regionSpec`, and the rev 2.5 shares of QUEUE Q6: F14 `line` in failed edit-check entries, the F10 revert-preview predictions (`WOULD_HOLD` from `gate`, `WOULD_BREAK` from the edit check), claims and daemon-verified labels for the F04 `sinceReport` fixtures, and the F05 prerequisites (claim view, `mapRanges` local mode, verified labels). Exit criterion (roadmap row 4): INV-FENCE and the O-REG-* oracles (O-REG-MAP, O-REG-FOLD, O-REG-TRIPWIRE, O-REG-NOLABEL, O-REG-COORD) pass on 1,000 seeds of the `claims` profile on every OS, with Plan 2's six and Plan 3's three exit oracles still green on every profile; the edit-check unit corpus (T-REG-28, TC-REG-09, TC-REG-10, 05 U10) passes and kills M46 per check.

**Architecture:** Plan 2's source directories and import order stay, with two new directories inserted (ruling R4-02) into the one cross-plan order of the 2026-10-08 reconciliation (X-110): `src/ports` → `src/text` → `src/i18n` → `src/git` → `src/sec` → `src/data` → **`src/regions`** → `src/board` → `src/since` → `src/holds` → **`src/claims`** → `src/engine` → `src/claude` → `src/coord` → `src/lifecycle` → `src/cli`; `src/daemon` and `src/entry` on top (`src/text` and `src/i18n` are Plan 8a's leaves, `src/since` is F04-a's, the directories after `src/engine` are Plans 5–7's; Plan 4 creates no module in any of them, and `src/product` stays pure, L-PRODUCT-PURE). `src/regions` is the region module of 04 §3.1: pure functions (line arrays, FoldMapper/1, path classes, touch, containment, tripwires, the edit-check checks, `foldOverlap`), the parse side (`src/regions/worker/**`, the only importer of `web-tree-sitter`), the pool port that runs parse, map and check jobs in workers, and a main-thread `RegionService` that reads blobs through `GitRepo` (read-only commands), runs the caller/partial-type `git grep`s and caches tables; it reads nothing from the board, the holds or the claims, so REG-13's "no input from the hold registry or own claims" holds by import direction. `src/board` gains the typed `claim`/`release` ops and their fold, which stays pure and synchronous: the fold's tree lookups and `foldOverlap` answers are prefetched per board commit (`claimFoldNeeds` in `src/board/fold.ts`, resolved by `src/claims/foldInputs.ts` in the worker pool) and passed to `foldCommit` as an optional fourth argument (R4-26). `src/claims` holds everything that knows claims and Plan 3's records: the claim view and the local claim store (TENTATIVE drafts, pinned post-images, local order), CONFIRM/PREDICT/Algorithm CLAIM, EMIT and `claimPrecheck`, `gate`, `placementCheck`, `breach`, the D8 step-4 tie-break, label verification, the F10 prediction entry and the four Plan 3 port bindings (`plan4Ports`). `src/engine` gains step modules that call them at the 01-engine pipeline points (`claimsStep.ts`, `emitStep.ts`, `mergeChecks.ts`, `labelStep.ts`, `regionCodes.ts`) plus call sites in Plan 2/3's `publish.ts`, `apply.ts`, `core.ts`, `loop.ts`, `status.ts`, `start.ts`, `restart.ts` and `recover.ts`. Plan 3's `src/holds` stays claim-free (HLD-06): no `src/holds` module imports `src/claims`; `mask.ts` imports only the pure `src/regions/mapper.ts` for its held ranges (R4-17). The SIM harness gains a `claims` profile with scripted seats (no hooks before Plan 5) and the INV-FENCE and O-REG-* oracles, each recomputed by test-side code.

**Tech Stack:** Plan 3's — Node ≥ 20 (`engines.node` `>=20.6` for contributors), TypeScript 5.x, esbuild, `node:test` via `tsx`, `fast-check`, git ≥ 2.43, GitHub Actions on windows-latest, macos-latest and ubuntu-latest — plus one named exception to "No runtime dependencies" (R4-09): `web-tree-sitter` at an exact 0.27.x version and the five grammar packages `tree-sitter-python`, `tree-sitter-javascript`, `tree-sitter-typescript` (typescript and tsx), `tree-sitter-c-sharp` at exact versions, all as devDependencies; esbuild bundles `web-tree-sitter`'s JavaScript into `dist/worker.js` only, and the runtime's and the grammars' `.wasm` files ship as data in `dist/grammars/`. `node:worker_threads` is a built-in, imported only by `src/regions/pool.ts` and `src/entry/worker.ts`.

**Plan form (as Plans 2 and 3).** Ruling: this plan is design-complete, not code-complete — every task names its files, the exact interfaces it consumes and produces (§Interfaces), the spec rule ids it implements and every test by title with what it asserts; implementers write the code test-first from that contract — 18 tasks written out as code would double the plan's volume — cost if wrong: an implementer misreads an interface, caught by the per-task review and by the next task's compile. Executed with superpowers:subagent-driven-development (a fresh implementer and a reviewer per task).

## Global Constraints

The constraints below are Plan 3's, which carry Plan 2's, carried verbatim (they still bind), followed by Plan 4's. Three carried lines are amended, each by name, in "Regions and claims (Plan 4)": Plan 1's "**No runtime dependencies**" line (one named exception, R4-09), Plan 1's "`dist/` stays the glossary §6 list" line (`dist/worker.js` and `dist/grammars/` become real) and Plan 3's "**CI per PR**" line (replaced by "Tests and SIM (Plan 4)").

**Carried from Plan 1 (still binding):**
- Node floor **20** for everything under `src/` and `dist/` (no APIs newer than Node 20); dev tooling Node ≥ 20.6.
- Git floor **2.43.0** (glossary §18); `meetsFloor` rejects anything below it; `daemon.js run` refuses below it with `START_TOOL_VERSION`.
- **No runtime dependencies**; nothing under `src/` imports a package that is not a Node built-in.
- `dist/` stays the glossary §6 list; Plan 2 turns `dist/daemon.js` from a stub into the foreground daemon, the other five entry points stay stubs.
- Every git process is started by `createGitRunner(...).run(...)` (lint L-GIT-SPAWN); engine code calls git only through `GitRepo.run` (Task 2), which calls the runner.
- No `Date.now()`, `new Date()`, `performance.now()` outside `src/ports/clock.ts`; no `Math.random`, `crypto.randomBytes`, `randomUUID` outside `src/ports/random.ts` (L-CLOCK, L-RANDOM). `process.hrtime` is read only in `src/ports/clock.ts` (`machineMonoMs`).
- No `child_process` outside `src/git/runner.ts` in `src/` (L-PORTS). Plan 2 adds: no `fs`/`node:fs`/`fs/promises`/`node:fs/promises` import in `src/` outside `src/ports/fs.ts` (L-FS-PORT, Task 2).
- Every read and write of user and cowork files goes through the `Fs` port on `Buffer`s.
- Forced git environment (glossary §18): `LC_ALL=C`, `LANGUAGE=`, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, `GCM_INTERACTIVE=never`, `SSH_ASKPASS_REQUIRE=never`, `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0`, stdin ignored unless a Buffer is piped.
- Watchdogs (glossary §18): fetch 45 s, push 90 s, read/hash commands 120 s; `update-ref`, `update-index`, `commit-tree`, `read-tree` are never killed.
- `enc(b)`: percent-encoding outside `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, ≤ 150 bytes, else refuse.
- Push rejections are classified by outcome after a fresh fetch (glossary §26.2 item 2; `classifyPush`); message text never decides.
- Files written by tools and fixtures: LF, UTF-8 without BOM, except fixtures that test BOM/CRLF.
- Commit messages end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

**Git use (Plan 2):**
- **`--no-replace-objects` on every git call** (Plan 1 ledger carry): `buildGitInvocation` puts `--no-replace-objects` as `argv[0]` for all four classes, before `--git-dir`/`--work-tree` and the `-c` list, so `merge-tree`, `cat-file`, `read-tree`, `rev-list` and `write-tree` never see `refs/replace/*` (`GIT_NO_REPLACE_OBJECTS` stays never-listed). Plan 1's argv snapshots in `test/unit/flags.test.ts` change accordingly (Task 2).
- `GIT_INDEX_FILE`, `--git-dir` and `--work-tree` are absolute; a relative value throws before spawning (ENG-08, 01-engine test 2).
- Every engine git call runs with cwd = the main-worktree root (filters name their scripts repo-relative, glossary §25 F12); a command that may run a filter never gets `--work-tree=<tmp/stage>` (ENG-08).
- **No git process reads a user's working file** (glossary §26.1 W1, ENG-27): content is read only by the staged read (01-engine §2) into `<gitdir>/cowork/tmp/stage/<p>`; listing commands (`ls-files -o --exclude-standard`, `check-attr`) may name the real work tree.
- Blobs come only from route (a) (private-index `add` of stage copies, unfiltered paths only), route (b) (`hash-object [-w] --path=<p>` of a stage copy or stdin, cwd = repo root) and route (c) (`hash-object [-w] --stdin --no-filters` for canon and concat output and for metadata blobs) (ENG-08, DAT-01).
- **Detection ticks write no object** (glossary §26.1 W6, ENG-04): objects are written only at ready-pair captures, publishes, applies and hold records. `update-index --refresh` never carries `-q`, runs only on a private or scratch index whose work tree is `tmp/stage`, and its result is read only from the `diff-files -z` that follows it on the same index (ENG-24, ENG-58).
- Code trees (A, `S_raw`, `S_disk`, `M_disk`, ready-pair `R`/`A_cap`, P, OUT, C) are produced only by ENG-10's four operations; metadata trees by `mktree` (ruling R-15).
- Never in daemon code: `git merge-base`, `-X …`, `--union`, `read-tree -u`/`-m`, `checkout`, `checkout-index`, `switch`, `restore`, `reset --hard`, `stash`, `apply`, `add -A`/`add .`, `push --atomic`, `--force`, a `+` push refspec, `git gc` (L-GIT-FORBIDDEN, L-MERGE-BASE, L-MERGE-OPTS, L-GIT-ATOMIC, L-REFS). The only non-fast-forward push is the human-typed recover, and it uses `--force-with-lease` (INV-FF).
- Every push refspec is built by `pushSpec()` (ENG-09, L-PUSH-ARGV); the only remote deletion is `deleteProbeRef()` (INV-OID); every push attempt goes through `governedPush()` and is counted (PRO-41).
- Every local ref update uses `--create-reflog` under `core.logAllRefUpdates=always` (refupdate class; fetch passes `-c core.logAllRefUpdates=always`) (ENG-02). The engine never writes `FETCH_HEAD`, `ORIG_HEAD`, `refs/remotes/*`, `refs/heads/*` or `.git/index` (INV-ENG-USER).
- Outcomes come from exit codes and `-z`/`--porcelain` output. The only reader of git's text is `readHttpThrottle` (Plan 2 Task 12), and its result can only start the throttle fallback (ENG-46, ENG-58, L-CLASSIFY-TEXT).
- Every git call carries `-c filter.<d>.required=true` for every discovered driver and `filter.lfs` (glossary §18, DAT-15), `-c attr.tree=<EPOCH_BASE>` on attribute-reading commands (DAT-13), `-c core.fsmonitor=false` and, on the private index, `-c core.untrackedCache=false` (ruling R-38).
- Every git call also carries `-c core.safecrlf=false` (controller ruling P2-SAFECRLF): a user's `core.safecrlf=true` makes `hash-object -w`/`add` die (rc 128) on CRLF↔LF conversion, which would read as a filter failure.
- Every git call also forces `-c i18n.commitEncoding=UTF-8` and `-c i18n.logOutputEncoding=UTF-8` (controller ruling P2-UTF8: a user's commitEncoding would put an `encoding` header and non-UTF-8 bytes into shared commits) and the environment `GIT_GRAFT_FILE=<hooksPath>/.no-grafts` (a path that never exists; `GIT_GRAFT_FILE` joins the never-list so it cannot be passed through) — controller ruling P2-GRAFTS: `.git/info/grafts` is still honoured under `--no-replace-objects` and would give one peer different first-parent chains.
- Every git call also forces `-c core.splitIndex=false` (ruling P2-SPLITINDEX: a user's split index would make scratch/private index calls write shared index files).

**Time:**
- Loop period **2.5 s** active (work pending or a capture due), **10 s** idle; one iteration at a time; a fetch starts ≥ 2.5 s after the previous fetch started, or right after it ends if it took longer; a fresh-fetch request during a fetch waits for that fetch (ENG-13, glossary §25 F10).
- Every timer is a Δ from a local `Anchor` (`anchorNow`, `elapsedMono`, `ownStaleness`, `suspendGapMs`); no wall-clock value from another machine is ever compared (INV-CLOCK).
- **Monotonic time across sleep is not trusted alone** (Plan 1 ledger carry): every iteration computes, against the previous iteration's anchor, both `suspendGapMs` and the plain Δmono; Δwall − Δmono > 5 s (`SUSPEND_GAP_THRESHOLD_MS`) **or** Δmono > 3 × the loop period (a monotonic clock that kept counting through a sleep, or a stalled event loop) both mean "suspended": abandon in-flight network calls and fetch before any apply or publish decision (01-engine §3.5 step 0a).
- The snapshot quiet period compares this PC's wall clock with this PC's file mtime only: a path whose mtime is < **300 ms** old is not read; a path whose mtime is in the future needs its stat unchanged for ≥ 300 ms Δmono.
- `meta.json` carries no wall-clock or monotonic timestamp (ENG-05); every timer restarts at daemon start.

**Snapshot and data:** deletion confirmation: missing in two reads ≥ **1 s** apart (Δmono); shrink guard: a > **50 %** shrink or a shrink to 0 bytes needs **2 stable snapshots** plus attribution (ruling R-25); staged read: `lstat`, one whole-file read, `lstat`, one re-read when `(size, mtime_ns)` changed, else UNSTABLE; an open or read error is UNREADABLE (01-engine §2). New-file cap **1,048,576** bytes; blobs ≥ **52,428,800** bytes (50 MiB) are SAVE_ONLY; binary = a NUL in the first **8,000** bytes; a new path ≤ **200** UTF-16 code units, each component ≤ **255** UTF-8 bytes; concat marker size `max(32, longest run + 1)` (glossary §20).

**Writer and apply:** ≤ 1 apply per **10 s** per worktree; free space ≥ **2 × bytes to write + 256 MB** (else `DEFERRED(disk_low)`); ≤ **3** re-merge retries per path, then the group defers `mismatch`; `.old` kept ≥ **2 s**; a gate query waits ≤ **250 ms** for an in-flight group; the writer is two-phase (rename target → `.old`, link `.new` → target) with the copy fallback only on a volume without hard links (glossary §26.1 W4, §26.2 item 7).

**Publish and governor (glossary §27.2, 02-protocol §3.1 and §3.8):** `B = protocol.json.budget` — 12 by default, seeded once at genesis from `host.pushBudgetPerMin` when it is an integer in 6–24, else 12 (ruling R-19); `G_s = ⌈60,000 / budget⌉ / 1,000` s and `S_s = ⌈18,000 / budget⌉ / 1,000` s (12 → 5 s, 1.5 s); local constants normal `(B_local, G_local, S_local) = (24, 2.5 s, 0.75 s)`, fallback `(6, 10 s, 3 s)`; `B_eff = min(B_local, budget)`, `G_base = max(G_local, G_s, 60 s / B_eff)`, `S_eff = max(S_local, S_s)`; throttle level k doubles `G_eff` up to **60 s**; per-peer cap `B_eff` attempts per 60 s Δmono; sliding team guard: no attempt while ≥ `B_eff` observed landings in the last 60 s Δmono; aging after 2 pre-emptions; fallback for **30 min** Δmono after a throttle signal (HTTP 429 or any 5xx on a push or fetch, a push taking > **10 s** Δmono or killed, a transient-rejection streak of **2**) or after folding a `HOST_THROTTLED` event; at most one `HOST_THROTTLED` event per **15 min** per peer; `NONRETRYABLE` at the **3rd** consecutive transient rejection of the same publish with the board unchanged; offline after > **30 s** without a successful fetch; network backoff **30 s doubling to 5 min**. Push only on change: no periodic, heartbeat or renewal push.

**Board:** caps **5,000** folded records, **5 MB** board tree, **1,000** ops and **64 KiB** per `ops.jsonl`, **16 KiB** per line; checkpoint at first-parent heights divisible by **256**; a joiner replays ≤ **255** commits; compaction drops terminal records with `terminal_height ≤ h − 512`; `K_evict` = **200** heights; `proto = 1`.

**State, incidents, convergence:** the state ref changes only by one CAS `update-ref` per transition; a CAS loss is a second writer (security log + exit, ENG-03); ≤ **3** displaced refs per branch; lost-state rebuild reads ≤ **500** first-parent commits; INV-CONVERGE: within **K = 20** loops after quiescence, then zero pushes (the SIM oracle runs **40** more loops).

**Status and lease:** `status.json` is written by temp file + rename on every iteration and transition, retried 3 × 50 ms on EPERM/EACCES then skipped; a heartbeat older than 3 loop periods means `down`; without a lease the loop stops syncing after **2 min** and drains and exits after **10 min** with `IDLE_NO_SESSION` (CCI-50, ENG-56).

**Tests and SIM:**
- Integration tests run real git against `file://` bare remotes (Plan 1 `createBareRemote`, `clonePeer`); git is never mocked; temp roots come from `makeTempRoot` (spaces and non-ASCII).
- SIM determinism (TST-03): the same seed tuple gives an identical op-log, trace and remote ref oids; every id comes from the peer's `seededRandom`; every commit carries `wallMsForDates = clock.wallMs()` (the engine passes it on every commit; the runner honours it only in test builds — Plan 7's L-DIST must keep that branch out of release bundles); generated edits set the file mtime to the peer's virtual wall clock (`utimes`) so the quiet-period rule compares one clock.
- Generated edits to "the same file, different lines" are separated by ≥ 2 unchanged lines: git's merge treats edits on adjacent lines as one conflict (measured on git 2.55 while planning: lines 1 and 2 of a 3-line file conflict; one unchanged line between them merges).
- Message codes are the registry names verbatim (`^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,47}$`); no rendered text reaches the board.
- Crashpoints: Plan 2 places exactly 14 names — the 13 of 01-engine ENG-59 plus `registry.cas` (HLD-01, two call sites); all are already in `test/crashpoints.json` (L-CRASHPOINTS).
- Yield points (Scheduler port; SIM interleaving): `publish.beforePush` (after the push intent is journaled, before `governedPush`), `apply.beforePhase2` (after P1 of a write group, before P2), `start.beforeGenesisPush` (after L0 and B0 are built, before their push). No other yield point is part of the contract.

**Holds (Plan 3):**
- **Plan 2 gate.** Plan 3 executes only on top of a merged Plan 2 (Task 14's fix round and Tasks 15–20 included, ruling R3-01). Every Plan 2 name this plan cites is used exactly as Plan 2's contract and ledger state it; a task that finds a cited name missing or different stops and reports.
- **One holding store** (HLD-01, HLD-12): every reason this PC's disk or outgoing tree differs from TIP plus this peer's ready, unheld publication groups is a record in `refs/cowork-local/<enc(b)>/holds`, except path-class masks (`QUARANTINED`, `SAVE_ONLY`, `UNMANAGED`), HLD-32 transient exclusions younger than 60 s, and an unreadable or unstable never-staged new file whose `h0` and `h1` are both ABSENT (nothing to hold; listed in `not_converged`, R3-56). No other ref, file, board record type or the `.old` ring holds bytes (lint L-HOLD-STORES). The board's `held/` tree is a mirror, never a store of record.
- **Record before displacement:** a write that removes local bytes from the disk (PARK, markers park, S6, the stop park, the collision take, a K4 displacement) runs only after the registry commit holding those bytes has passed its CAS; a record is never half-parked (HLD-16); a crash between the two is settled at restart by observation (DISK_EQ), never by guessing.
- **Every Plan 3 disk write goes through `Writer.writeLocal`/`writeLocalGroup`** with an origin (ENG-37) and is recorded as attributed (`ObservedWrites.record`; the park family and `--discard` also set the private-index entry and commit a snap-only state transition, R3-36, R3-60), so no detector counts it as an unobserved delta, in this run or after a restart; none runs while `view.intent.kind === 'push'` or `Writer.strandedPaths()` is non-empty (the operator reports `POSTPONED` and retries at a later loop, ruling R3-23); while any FREEZE reason is set only calls with `humanEvidence = true` write (`writeLocal` returns `FROZEN` otherwise).
- **Hold arithmetic** (HLD-13, 05 §3.4 preamble, DAT-40): every operator is Plan 2's `mergeFile` (`git merge-file -p --object-id` with MERGE_FLAGS through `runGit`); the base of REBASE and MASK is ENG's `A[p]`, never `tree(LA_C)[p]`; `--diff3` with the labels `cowork:disk`, `cowork:base`, `cowork:held` appears only in RESTORE; `--ours`, `--union` and `--theirs` never appear; no `merge-file` runs on a binary or filtered path (whole-path rules instead, ruling R3-18); every disk-versus-blob test is `DISK_EQ` (ENG-11).
- **`-U0` hunks** are computed and applied only in `src/holds/revert.ts`: `revertDetector` reads them, `taintOnlyH1` is the single applier and its output is only ever a record's `h1` (HLD-18, L-HOLD-STORES).
- **Objects (W6):** record content enters only through the staged-copy read with route (b) `-w`, or route (c) for `merge-file` outputs (stored form, ruling R3-19); MASK and REBASE run only in iterations that materialize or publish; a detection tick runs neither and writes no object; the revert detector runs only on a newly materialized `S_disk`, the mass-change breaker on every tick's dirty set (paths only) (ENG-14).
- **Claim-free** (HLD-06): no module that implements the revert detector, MASK, REBASE, PARK, RESTORE or R1 reads claim state; the only claim-dependent input is `PublishGatePort`.
- **Clocks:** a record's age comes only from its `age_anchor` (`max(Δwall since anchor_wall_ms, mono_accum_ms + Δmono of this run)`, INV-CLOCK); the 60 s transient clocks, the 30 s MASS window, the observed-write set and the edit-checkpoint quiet periods live in memory and restart at daemon start; Plan 3 adds no field to `meta.json` (ENG-05, R-14).
- **Board:** a `hold` op is ≤ **16 KiB** per line; `held/<hold_id>/<stage>/<repo path>` entries exist only for LIVE paths of non-`local_only` records, never for an ABSENT side; a `local_only` record's op is a content-free stub (its paths, no oid, no `held/` subtree); no new `held/` blob other than one already in `tree(TIP)` contains a restore-marker row (DAT-46); `hold` ops go board-only under FREEZE and the regionSpec VERSION pause and wait (queued, never dropped) under every other pause (PRO-45, HLD-10).
- **Codes:** every new message code Plan 3 introduces is a constant in `src/holds/codes.ts` (`HOLD_CODES` also declares four codes reserved for Plans 5–7, R3-65); Plan 3 also raises Plan 2's `FROZEN` and `UNFROZEN` unchanged; Plan 3 writes codes to `status.json` (`message_code`, `message_params`) and the daemon log only (R-27 carried); `HOLD_NOT_DURABLE` (removed in rev 2) never appears.
- **Names:** the 03-data incoming form check is `formCheckIncoming`, the 05-holds revert detector is `revertDetector`; neither is called "D2" alone in code, test titles or task titles.
- **Crashpoints and yield points:** Plan 3 places no new crashpoint name and no new yield point; its registry commits pass `registry.cas` and its disk writes pass the writer's existing anchored steps.
- **Shared helpers, never copied** (Plan 2 Task 14 ratification; the Task 17 carry "Never copy them"): `src/engine` code uses `src/engine/treeDiff.ts` (`diffPaths`, `sameTreeEntry`, `isRegularEntry`), `src/engine/treeEdit.ts` (`editTreeIndexOnly`, `editsBetween`, `editIndexOnly`, `INDEX_ONLY`) and `core.ts`'s `hooksDir` for every tree diff, index-only tree edit and private-index edit (`Snapshotter.setEntry` is `editIndexOnly` on the private index with `INDEX_ONLY`); `src/holds` (which cannot import `src/engine`) uses `src/git/scratchIndex.ts`'s `resetScratchIndex` before every `read-tree` into a scratch index (P2-SCRATCHLOCK) and takes tree diffs as caller-supplied parameters; no module keeps a private copy of any of them.

**Tests and SIM (Plan 3):**
- **CI per PR** (replaces Plan 2's "CI per PR" line): 100 SIM seeds per leg — 60 `edits` + 20 `faults` + 20 `holds` — with Plan 2's per-Node seed offsets, so 200 seeds per OS per PR; every SIM run asserts Plan 2's six exit oracles plus INV-NOLOSS, INV-MASK, INV-GROUP and the 05 oracle rows of Task 14. The exit criterion run is `sim-exit.yml`: 1,000 `holds` seeds per OS (and its existing 1,000 `edits` seeds).
- **SIM test time (R3-55):** a multi-seed SIM integration test runs its first seed under `npm test` and all its seeds under `npm run test:sim` (which `sim-exit.yml` runs per OS); every test file has a 10-minute `node:test` timeout; exit shards are sized from the per-OS per-seed time Task 16 measures on the PR legs (R3-58).
- Oracles recompute from git objects, board trees and working files with test-side code (their own `merge-file` calls, scratch indexes, file reads); an oracle never calls `maskEntries`, `writeGroups`, `HoldRegistry`, `isDurable` or any other engine function whose mutant it must catch.
- Every generated edit of the `holds` profile carries one fresh marker `CWM_<run>_<n>_<peer>` (own line or line suffix, placed safely in BOM, CRLF and cp949 files) (TST-08).
- Determinism (TST-03) covers records: ULIDs from the peer's seeded Random, `anchor_wall_ms` from the virtual clock, registry and board commits dated by `wallMsForDates`; Plan 3's trace events carry no duration and no wall time.

**Regions and claims (Plan 4):**
- **Plan 3 gate (R4-01).** Plan 4 executes on top of a merged Plan 3: Tasks 1–4, 6 and 7 edit no file Plan 3 edits except `package.json` (devDependencies) and `tools/lint/rules.ts` (appended rule rows), and no Plan 2 file except `src/data/attrs.ts` (R4-20), so they may start once Plan 2 has merged and are rebased onto merged Plan 3 before Task 5 starts (`tools/lint/rules.ts`: Plan 3 Task 8 merges first, Plan 4 Task 1 rebases and appends after it); every other task waits for Plan 3 merged through its Task 18 — Plan 3's Tasks 11–18 edit the engine files (`core`, `loop`, `publish`, `status`, `start`, `restart`) that Plan 4's Tasks 5, 8 and 11–16 edit, so no Plan 4 task from Task 5 on runs beside them (verify pass, reconcile §7 V-15); Plan 3 Task 19 (F04-a-gated, `src/since/report.ts` only) does not gate Plan 4 — and it needs Plan 3 Task 10's contract (the four ports; its amended contract also declares `T0Verdict.findings?`, `EditCheckVerdict.entries?`, the `CoreOptions.ports` function form and the `last_check` store, X-05…X-08, X-15) and, where it says so, for Plan 3's round-2 Tasks 17 (F10 kernel, X-09) and 18 (F14 inputs, X-11). Every Plan 2 and Plan 3 name this plan cites is used exactly as their contracts and ledgers state it; a task that finds a cited name missing or different stops and reports.
- **Plan 4 ‖ Plan 5 merge order (verify pass V-11; the same paragraph stands in Plan 5's header).** Plans 4 and 5 may run side by side (roadmap "Execution order") and both edit about 22 shared files; the rules: (1) `src/engine/deps.ts` (`MutantId`) and `src/ports/trace.ts` (trace variants): Plan 4 Task 5 merges first, Plan 5 Task 1 rebases onto it (both only append union members); (2) `src/engine/{core,loop,publish,csStep}.ts`: each plan edits only its own named lines (Plan 4: Tasks 5, 8, 11–16 as listed in their Files; Plan 5: Task 8's wiring, Task 11's `Engine.freshFetch`, Tasks 9, 13, 16 as listed) and whichever task merges later rebases and re-runs its tests — no task rewrites the other plan's lines; (3) `src/daemon/run.ts`, `test/sim/engineAdapter.ts`, `test/helpers/{coreFixture,engineFixture}.ts`, `tools/real/two-clone.ts`: one wiring line each (X-111, X-33), the later merge rebases; (4) `test/sim/{oracles,generators}.ts`, `tools/sim/{run-seeds,run-shard}.ts`, `tools/lint/rules.ts`: append-only, the later merge rebases; (5) `tools/build/build.mjs` and `test/integration/build.test.ts`: X-107's order and explicit list, each plan appending its entries; (6) `.github/workflows/{ci,sim-exit}.yml` and `test/unit/ci-config.test.ts`: only X-113's table rows and each plan's own `sim-exit` shard. A conflict outside these rules stops the later task for a controller ruling.
- **Runtime dependency — amends Plan 1's "No runtime dependencies" line (R4-09):** the only package under `src/` that is not a Node built-in is `web-tree-sitter` (exact 0.27.x, a devDependency), and only `src/regions/worker/**` imports it (lint L-REGION-DEPS); esbuild bundles it into `dist/worker.js` only; `dist/daemon.js`, `cli.js` and the hook entries contain no `web-tree-sitter` code (a bundle test greps them). Grammar and runtime `.wasm` files are data, never imported.
- **`dist/` — amends Plan 1's "`dist/` stays the glossary §6 list" line:** Plan 4 turns `dist/worker.js` from a stub into the region worker and adds `dist/grammars/` (`tree-sitter.wasm` plus the five grammar files, glossary §6 `grammars/*.wasm`); the entry points other than `worker.js` (and Plan 8a's `viz.js`, R-V36) are unchanged by Plan 4, and its build assertions run over the entry list of `build.mjs`, never a fixed count (Table C C-20). Copying to `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA/runtime/<version>/`, `SHA256SUMS` and the doctor row are Plan 7's (glossary §6 per-user table).
- **Workers (REG-31, 04 §3.1):** parsing, mapping, the interface and edit checks, `foldOverlap` batches and label verification run only as `RegionWorkers` jobs; production binds `threadWorkers` (two threads: `fg` and `bg`, priority `fg > save > bg`), unit tests and SIM bind `inlineWorkers` (same thread, deterministic order). Workers import no `fs`, no `child_process` and no git: the main thread reads every `.wasm` through the `Fs` port and transfers the bytes (L-FS-PORT kept), and every git call (`grep`, `cat-file`, `ls-tree`, `check-attr`) is made by the main thread through `GitRepo.run`. `node:worker_threads` is imported only by `src/regions/pool.ts` and `src/entry/worker.ts`.
- **Region module boundary (REG-02, REG-35, INV-REG-READ):** `src/regions/**` imports only `src/ports`, `src/git`, `src/sec` and `src/data`; it calls only the read side of `GitRepo` (`run` with `grep`, `ls-tree`, `cat-file` or `check-attr`, `readBlob`, `readBlobs`, `blobSizes`, `readTreeMap`, `hasObject`, `resolve`), never `hashBytes`, `writeTreeMap`, `editTree`, `mktree`, `commitTree`, a ref update or an index write (lint L-REGION-WRITE, REG-02); it never opens a working file; working bytes reach it only as a `DiskSnapshotId` whose bytes are read from `SnapshotStore`, and a `DiskSnapshotId`'s oid is never passed to `cat-file` (INV-REG-READ, T-REG-27 (f)).
- **Coordinates (REG-01, INV-REG-COORD):** lines are split on byte `0x0A` only; tree-sitter results are read only through `startPosition.row`/`endPosition.row` inside `src/regions/worker/lineTable.ts` (lint L-OFFSETS: `startIndex`, `endIndex` or any byte/char offset outside that helper fails CI); text is decoded read-only with `TextDecoder('utf-8', {fatal: true, ignoreBOM: true})` and never encoded (lint L-UTF8-WRITE); no op, trace event, log record, IPC value or region API value carries a byte, character or UTF-16 offset.
- **One mapper, one touch, one claim shape (04 P3, REG-07, REG-13):** every hunk and mapping used by a claim, the fold, `touch`, `gate`, `placementCheck`, `breach`, the edit check and Plan 3's held ranges comes from `src/regions/mapper.ts` (FoldMapper/1, frozen for `proto = 1`); `git diff` output and Plan 2's `matchedRows` never feed them (lint L-ONE-MAPPER); every consumer of the touch test calls `touch` (or `contained` for containment); a claim is never found, matched, released or cleared by a label or name (REG-12, REG-14).
- **Fold (PRO-17, REG-23, INV-REG-FOLD):** `foldCommit` stays pure, total and synchronous; claim validity uses op fields, the prefetched `ClaimFoldInputs` and FoldMapper/1 in `fold` mode only — no parse, no regionSpec, no label, no clock, no time cap; a missing object is never guessed (R4-26).
- **Budgets (REG-31, 04 §3.18):** every time budget is a `Deadline` from `RegionBudgets` on the Clock port's monotonic time; under SIM's virtual clock no budget expires unless a test injects the overrun (`BudgetInjector`), so verdicts are seed-deterministic; `fold` mode never has a time cap; an overrun always degrades to a larger claim, a touch, `WHOLE_FILE` or `incomplete`, never to "no conflict"; an edit-check overrun never raises `REGION_BUDGET`.
- **Objects (glossary §26.1 W6):** Plan 4 writes no object outside the occasions Plans 2 and 3 already write them; a claim draft, a `predict`, a `confirm`, an edit check (beyond the candidate Plan 3 materializes, R3-41), a label verification and an F10 prediction write none (REG-35); the region greps never write an index entry.
- **LIVE only (REG-32, glossary §27 S8):** tables, predictions, drafts and claim ops exist only for paths of scope class `LIVE`.
- **Verdicts only (REG-02, INV-ONE-EVENT):** region and claim functions return verdicts; records are created only through Plan 3's constructors at the engine call sites (`pinCs` with guard `claim:<claim_id>` for K3/K6, `conflict()`'s `TAKE_TIP` plus guard `claim-breach` for D8 step 4, the K7 PIN of Plan 3's T0 slot); Plan 4 creates no `event` op, no `request` and no message text (Plans 5–7).
- **Codes:** every region code is a constant in `src/regions/codes.ts`, the one Plan 4 codes module (X-117: `REGION_CODES`, `CLAIM_REJECTED`, `START_REGION_RUNTIME` and the log-only `REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES` — `FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING`, `EDIT_CHECK_OVERRUN`, and the five named by the verify pass (V-17) `REGION_OBJECT_MISSING`, `EDIT_CHECK_INPUT_MISMATCH`, `REGION_WORKER_RESPAWN`, `REGION_RUNTIME_MISMATCH`, `CLAIM_BREACH_HIT`; Plan 7's `carried.ts` imports the module and registers each, the log-only ones with `surfaces: ['log']`); every code Plan 4 passes to `Log.write` is such a registry code (X-30: no lowercase diagnostic record names); Plan 4 raises codes into `status.json` (`message_code`, `message_params`) and the daemon log only (R-27 carried), with params made of ids, paths, numbers and closed enums (SEC-38); `REGION_BUDGET` at most once per path per 30 min Δmono, `CLAIM_REJECTED` once per `(claim_id, reason)`.
- **Edit-check results (owner decision D1, R4-08):** a failed entry carries only `check?`, `path`, `reason` (closed enum) and `line?`; the full sorted list is returned to the caller in the verdict's `entries`, from which Plan 3's `settleCandidate` writes the in-memory `last_check` slot (X-05, X-06, X-08); Plan 4 writes it to no record, board op, log line, `status.json` field, carrier, mirror, notification or trace event (the `edit_check` trace carries check numbers only).
- **Claim ids:** RFC 4648 base32, lowercase, unpadded, 16 characters from 10 bytes of the `Random` port (`^[a-z2-7]{16}$`, R4-24); never derived from a name, label, ordinal, hash of code or clock.
- **Claim-free holds (HLD-06):** no `src/holds` module imports `src/claims`; the only `src/regions` imports in `src/holds` are `src/holds/mask.ts` → `src/regions/mapper.ts` (R4-17) and the type-only `EditCheckEntry`/`EditCheckReason` import of `src/holds/ports.ts` from `src/regions/editCheck.ts` (erased at build); a structural test in Task 5 pins both.
- **Crashpoints and yield points:** Plan 4 places no new crashpoint name and no new yield point; claim ops ride the push intent's `op_ids` through Plan 2's existing `publish.*` anchors.
- **Deferred by 04 §1.3 and glossary §22 (not built; checked by each task's reviewer, never asserted by a test; requirement map R1-025…R1-030):** no sub-block claims (a claim covers the whole unit at any length, REG-15); no rename aliases (a rename is a removal plus an addition, which the caller check catches, REG-27); no fork detector and no possible-fork note; no notebook cell-level claims (`.ipynb` is a whole-file path); no Claude-facing or human text (codes and params only; the `en`/`ko` templates are Plan 6's, the six-language texts Plan 7's); no type-aware (LSP) caller resolution, statement-level claims below the blank-line block, YAML key-path checks, C# `using`/namespace import resolution, compiler-backed C# partial-type index or human-held claims (humans take no claims in v1, COO-09).

**Tests and SIM (Plan 4):**
- **CI per PR** (replaces Plan 3's "CI per PR" line by the one cross-plan rule of the 2026-10-08 reconciliation, X-113, asserted by `test/unit/ci-config.test.ts`): 100 SIM seeds per leg; every merged non-`edits` profile gets its final count — `faults` 15, `holds` 15, `claims` 15 (Plan 4's), and when merged `seats` 15, `coord` 15, `lifecycle` 10 — and `edits` takes the remainder (55 while Plans 3 and 4 are the only merged plans with a profile), in any merge order; with Plan 2's per-Node seed offsets, so 200 seeds per OS per PR; every SIM run asserts Plan 2's six exit oracles, Plan 3's three exit oracles and its 05 oracle rows, and INV-FENCE, O-REG-MAP, O-REG-FOLD, O-REG-TRIPWIRE, O-REG-NOLABEL, O-REG-COORD and the INV-REG-READ/INV-REG-NOWRITE checks of Task 17. The exit criterion run is `sim-exit.yml`: 1,000 `claims` seeds per OS (and its existing `edits` and `holds` seeds; each plan adds its own shards, X-113).
- Region and claim oracles recompute with test-side code: they may load FoldMapper/1 (`src/regions/mapper.ts`, which is the spec's frozen algorithm, 11 §3.4 INV-FENCE "the bundled mapper") and the test-side fixture extents, but never call `touch`, `contained`, `gate`, `foldOverlap`, `placementCheck`, `breach`, `regionTripwire`, `editCheck` or the fold's claim branch, whose mutants they must catch; the per-line seat attribution comes from the generator's op log.
- Unit and integration tests load the real grammars through `testRegionRuntime()` (the pinned packages in `node_modules`, the same bytes `npm run build` copies to `dist/grammars/`); a test never mocks a parse.
- Region fixtures live in `test/fixtures/regions/**` with a provenance README (R4-21); the fast-check properties run ≥ 1,000 cases per property per PR; the 100,000-case nightly runs are Plan 7's (R4-64).
- Every Plan 2 and Plan 3 suite stays green under `plan4Ports()` and the real `regionSpec` (Task 16); a task that must change a Plan 2 or Plan 3 test's expected value stops for a controller ruling (R4-58).

## Review Focus

The five conditions most likely to break in real use that no single task's tests would otherwise exercise (each has a test in the owning task):

1. **A clean `merge-tree` result that silently moves a teammate's line into the user's function (the same-tail case).** Two peers append to adjacent functions with identical last lines (`return round(total, 2)`); git merges with exit 0 and puts the line into the wrong function. Expected: with a claim on the function the B1 gate holds the requester's change-set before P is composed; without one the identity sub-check routes the path to the conflict function (TIP on disk and in OUT, the local blob a CONFLICTED record); in every pushed blob the neighbour function is byte-identical to its owner's version; FoldMapper/1 places the insertion after line 5341, never git's 5339. Pinned in Task 15 (T-REG-06 premerge fixture, apply and publish sides) and Task 17 (the `claims` generator's `same_tail_append` op under INV-FENCE).
2. **Mixed line endings and encodings across peers.** A Windows peer with `core.autocrlf=true` and a BOM+CRLF Python file, a LF peer editing the same function, and a cp949 C# file over 300 lines. Expected: EOL/BOM-only differences give zero hunks, identical tables (432 units) and no claim, K3 or K6 from form alone; claim ranges map identically in stored and working form; the cp949 file is a raw-split block table and its claims are block claims; no message carries an offset. Pinned in Task 3 (T-REG-03 mapper half), Task 4 (tables on both forms, cp949), Task 11 (a seat edit of the cp949 file drafts its raw-split block), Task 12 (a gate run between the BOM+CRLF and LF peers) and Task 17 (odd peers `core.autocrlf=true` in the `claims` profile).
3. **Writes that never pass a hook.** A `sed -i`, a `python -c` rewrite, a PowerShell `Set-Content` and a human editor save on another PC into a held function. Expected: the remote live ref never contains the change; the change-set is held whole as one PIN with guard `claim:<id>` (K3 for a seat write, K6 for the human one); no overlapping claim op is pushed; the holder's own PC's human edit inside its own seat's claim publishes. Pinned in Task 12 (T-REG-12 human case, T-REG-17) and Task 17 (generator writes that skip `predict`, under INV-FENCE with M21 and M22).
4. **A held function renamed, moved or deleted while its holder keeps working.** The holder moves `compute_total` within a file and across files, a teammate's daemon still has the old location in its base, and a third peer renames a callee. Expected: the old location stays blocked by a tombstone until the holder's push; the new-location claim and the old-location tombstone land in the same op batch; no pushed blob ever contains two `compute_total` definitions; a caller of the renamed function is a K7 hold (removal plus addition), never a silent publish. Pinned in Task 14 (T-REG-05, TC-REG-06), Task 15 (identity sub-check after the move) and Task 17 (`rename_unit`, `move_unit` generator ops).
5. **Peers on different plugin builds or with a grammar that fails to load.** One peer upgraded (new `regionSpec`), one peer whose C# grammar `.wasm` is corrupt. Expected: each daemon treats the other's claims as whole-file and shows `REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH`; claim validity in the fold is identical on both (overlap ignores regionSpec); `teamRegionSpec` stays at the genesis value until every peer in the ranked set announces the new one; only the differing peer enters the regionSpec VERSION pause and publishes no claim op; the corrupt-grammar peer reports `REGION_GRAMMAR_UNAVAILABLE`, treats C# as grammarless (larger claims, never a false pass) and keeps syncing. Pinned in Task 1 (corrupt `.wasm`), Task 8 (fold regionSpec-blind), Task 6 (touch step 1) and Task 16 (T-REG-16, T-PRO-30 with two daemons).

## Scope rulings

Each ruling is `Ruling: <decision> — <why> — <cost if wrong>`; the ids in parentheses are the requirement-map questions and rows it settles. Task writers follow them; a task that finds a ruling impossible stops and reports instead of improvising. Plan 4 extends Plan 2's and Plan 3's files and records; it never replaces a Plan 2 or Plan 3 file, store or schema, and every Plan 2 ruling (R-01…R-39), Plan 2 ledger ruling and Plan 3 ruling (R3-01…R3-70) not amended here still binds.

**Cross-plan reconciliation (2026-10-08).** The rulings of `.worktrees/proposals/.superpowers/reconcile/RECONCILIATION.md` (X-01…X-119; precedence: spec rev 2.5 → landed code and ledger rulings → the producer's form) are applied in place; an R4 ruling they change says "as amended by the 2026-10-08 reconciliation" and names the X ruling, and the Cross-plan tables now carry the resolved owner of every row. Applied to this plan:
- **`last_check` and Plan 3's round 2:** X-05, X-06 (the store `ChangeSets.lastCheck` and every write are Plan 3's; R4-43 amended, R4-75 withdrawn, Task 5 creates no store, Task 10 returns `entries` only; C-1), X-07, X-08 (Plan 3 Task 10 declares `T0Verdict.findings?` and `EditCheckVerdict.entries?`; Task 5 narrows their element types; R4-38, R4-39, A-10), X-09 (the F10 kernel is Plan 3 Task 17, the step-6 caller Plan 7's verb; C-2), X-15 (`CoreOptions.ports`' function form `(deps: EngineDeps, peerIndex)` is Plan 3's; R4-77, A-17, flag 14).
- **D1 and the spec batch:** X-01 (no gate on `last_check` reads; R4-08, C-9), X-03, X-04 (spec edit 1 dropped; one batch order, applied by Task 18 after owner approval).
