Import direction (R4-02, X-110): each source directory imports only from those before it — `src/ports`, `src/text`, `src/i18n`, `src/git`, `src/sec`, `src/data`, `src/regions`, `src/board`, `src/since`, `src/holds`, `src/claims`, `src/engine`, `src/claude`, `src/coord`, `src/lifecycle`, `src/cli` (Plan 4 creates modules only in `src/regions`, `src/claims` and `src/engine` of these); `src/daemon` and `src/entry` may import everything. Inside `src/regions`, only `src/regions/worker/**` imports `web-tree-sitter`, and `src/regions/worker/**` imports nothing from `src/git`, `src/ports/fs.ts` or any I/O module; `src/regions/worker/inline.ts` is imported only by `test/**` and `tools/**`. No `src/holds` module imports `src/claims`; `src/holds/mask.ts` is the only `src/holds` module that imports `src/regions` at run time (the mapper), and `src/holds/ports.ts` imports only types from `src/regions/editCheck.ts` (`import type`, erased at build). `src/data/conflict.ts` imports nothing from `src/regions`: the engine binds the `ConflictCtx` hooks. Unit tests live in `test/unit/`, real-git tests in `test/integration/`, SIM tests in `test/sim/`, each named in its task.

## Interfaces (the contract every task implements exactly)

Plan 1, Plan 2 and Plan 3 names are used as their contracts state them (`GitRepo`, `RunOpts`, `GitCallOptions`, `Oid`, `OidOrAbsent`, `ABSENT`, `TreeMap`, `TreeEntry`, `FileMode`, `mergeTree`, `MergeTreeResult`, `hashRouteB`, `hashRouteC`, `rows`, `stripEnding`, `isBinaryBytes`, `conflictMarkerRows`, `AttrCache`, `AttrSnapshot`, `PathAttrs`, `Scope`, `ScopeClass`, `ConflictCtx`, `ConflictVerdict`, `Stage`, `conflict`, `regionSpecId`, `PLAN2_REGION_SPEC`, `OpType`, `Op`, `OpDraft`, `OpaqueOp`, `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES`, `parseOpLine`, `serializeOps`, `BoardDraft`, `BoardBuild`, `WriterId`, `CsTrailer`, `parseCsTrailers`, `decodeCsPaths`, `parseWriter`, `FoldState`, `PeerRec`, `foldCommit`, `FoldStepResult`, `FoldOutcome`, `Folder`, `OwnRejection`, `Outbox`, `PendingOp`, `PushState`, `allowedInState`, `Governor`, `ReadyPair`, `HoldRecord`, `NewRecord`, `pinCs`, `makeGuard`, `heldPins`, `PublicationGroup`, `CsSignal`, `captureStep`, `publishStep`, `applyStep`, `IterationCtx`, `EngineCore`, `Engine`, `EngineInspection`, `EngineDeps`, `CoreOptions`, `Trace`, `TraceEvent`, `Log`, `fileLog`, `Clock`, `Random`, `Fs`, `Json`, `PeerId`, `plan3Ports`, `grammarlessEditCheck`, `passT0Regions`, `openGate`, `noSymbols`, `t0Shrink`, `OpVerdict` (Plan 8a); added by the 2026-10-08 reconciliation, names the tasks already cite (Task 5's re-baseline checks them too): Plan 1's `testBuild`; Plan 2's `GitRepo.isAncestor`, `mergeFile`, `MERGE_FLAGS`, `lostStateRebuild`, `restartCore`, `prepareIteration`, `iterate`, `computeStatus`, `StatusInputs`, `NotConvergedCause`, `wallMsForDates`; Plan 3's `hold`, `commitMany`, `holdCode`, `isHoldCreation`, `mirrorSync`, `settleCandidate`, `oracleOffDisk`, `TeamWriter`, `PortsOverride` (Task 10's `CoreOptions.ports` function form, X-15), `ChangeSets.lastCheck` (read only by tests, X-05), `ExitOracleOptions` (Task 16, X-112)). Mutant switches reach `src/regions` and `src/claims` as plain flags (`ClaimHostView.flags`) that the engine sets from `EngineDeps.testMutants`, because those directories cannot import `src/engine`.

### Region types, lines and FoldMapper/1

```ts
// src/regions/types.ts (04 §2, §3.1; REG-01, REG-35) — Task 1
/** 04 §2: working bytes the daemon holds in memory; crosses IPC, a board op, a log line and status.json only as (path, oid); its bytes live only in SnapshotStore (INV-SECRET, REG-35). */
export interface DiskSnapshotId { readonly kind: 'disk'; readonly path: string; readonly oid: Oid; }
/** 04 §3.1 "stored blob oid | diskSnapshotId"; a DiskSnapshotId's oid is never read through cat-file (INV-REG-READ). */
export type BlobRef = Oid | DiskSnapshotId;
export function isDisk(r: BlobRef): r is DiskSnapshotId;
export function blobOid(r: BlobRef): Oid;
/** 1-based inclusive stored-form rows (REG-01). */
export type Range = readonly [lo: number, hi: number];
/** Sorted, merged, closed intervals (04 §3.4 step 3's normal form). */
export type Ranges = readonly Range[];
/** 04 §3.1: a pure insertion after base line k has aLo = k+1, aHi = k; a pure deletion has bHi = bLo−1. */
export interface Hunk { readonly aLo: number; readonly aHi: number; readonly bLo: number; readonly bHi: number; }
export type MapResult = Ranges | 'TOMBSTONE' | 'WHOLE_FILE';   // REG-07
export type MapMode = 'fold' | 'local';
export type Language = 'python' | 'javascript' | 'typescript' | 'tsx' | 'c_sharp';
export type Granularity = 'symbol' | 'block' | 'file';
export type RegionUnitKind = 'function' | 'class' | 'method' | 'const' | 'type' | 'ctor' | 'dtor' | 'property' | 'indexer' | 'event' | 'field' | 'operator' | 'block' | 'file';
/** label-convention/1 (04 §3.3; REG-14): display, label counts, the group-name rule, the moved-code check and the identity sub-check only — never identity. */
export interface Label { readonly kind: string; readonly qualname: string; readonly simple: string; readonly type: string; readonly disc: string; }
export type LabelKey = string;   // kind + '\0' + qualname + '\0' + disc (R4-19)
export function labelKey(l: Label): LabelKey;
/** 04 §3.10 "Signature slices" (REG-26). */
export interface ParamSlice { readonly name: string; readonly kind: 'posonly' | 'pos' | 'kwonly' | 'varargs' | 'varkw' | 'rest' | 'plain' | 'ref' | 'out' | 'in' | 'params'; readonly hasDefault: boolean; readonly destructured: readonly string[] | null; readonly type: string | null; }
export type SigSlice =
  | { readonly lang: 'python'; readonly unit: 'function'; readonly params: readonly ParamSlice[]; readonly decorators: readonly string[]; readonly returns: string | null }
  | { readonly lang: 'python'; readonly unit: 'class'; readonly bases: readonly string[]; readonly methods: readonly string[]; readonly assignments: readonly string[] }
  | { readonly lang: 'python'; readonly unit: 'module'; readonly all: readonly string[] | null; readonly upperNames: readonly string[] }
  | { readonly lang: 'javascript' | 'typescript' | 'tsx'; readonly params: readonly ParamSlice[] | null; readonly exported: boolean; readonly exportName: string | null; readonly members: readonly string[] }
  | { readonly lang: 'c_sharp'; readonly isPrivate: boolean; readonly modifiers: readonly string[]; readonly returns: string | null; readonly params: readonly ParamSlice[]; readonly memberType: string | null; readonly overloadKey: string };
/** 04 §3.1 Unit; leaf units of one table are pairwise line-disjoint (INV-REG-PARTITION).
 *  Unit model (Task 4): a class/type/record/interface/enum contributes one non-leaf unit (its whole extent, leaf: false, residue: false) and one residue leaf unit
 *  (residue: true, parentUnitId = the non-leaf unit); members, functions, consts and gap blocks are leaf units whose parentUnitId is the enclosing class unit or null;
 *  C# namespaces and JS IIFE wrappers contribute no unit. Leaf units are the claimable ones and the ones INV-REG-PARTITION and BIG_UNITS count; the last-good
 *  lookup's "top-level unit" is the outermost unit (leaf or not) covering the rows. A Python module's __all__ / UPPER_CASE slice (SigSlice unit 'module') is carried
 *  by the const unit whose qualname is __all__ when the file has one; every UPPER_CASE module name is a const unit of its own (R4-19), so its removal is a unit removal. */
export interface Unit { readonly unitId: number; readonly kind: RegionUnitKind; readonly label: Label; readonly ranges: Ranges; readonly parentUnitId: number | null; readonly leaf: boolean; readonly residue: boolean; readonly sigSlice: SigSlice | null; readonly granularity: Granularity; }
/** 04 §3.1 RegionTable; a class-6 block table has grammar null and block units only. */
export interface RegionTable {
  readonly grammar: Language | null; readonly regionSpec: string; readonly decodable: boolean; readonly lineCount: number;
  readonly units: readonly Unit[]; readonly imports: Range | null;   // <imports> (04 §3.13), never claimed
  readonly errorSpans: Ranges; readonly errorCount: number; readonly labelCounts: Readonly<Record<LabelKey, number>>;
}
export type NoTableReason = 'conflict_markers' | 'binary' | 'special' | 'grammarless_small' | 'budget';
export interface NoTable { readonly kind: 'NO_TABLE'; readonly reason: NoTableReason; readonly lineCount: number | null; }
export type TableOrNone = RegionTable | NoTable;
export type PathClass = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6;
/** 04 §3.6: the reason touch reports. Declared here (Task 1) because the RegionJob union and Task 5's claim types need it before Task 6; src/regions/touch.ts re-exports it. */
export type TouchReason = 'base-overlap' | 'insert-inside' | 'boundary-in-extent' | 'boundary-in-error' | 'boundary-no-table' | 'group-name' | 'degraded-whole-file' | 'none';
/** 04 §3.1 "tree oids or in-memory tree views": a stored tree plus an in-memory overlay (M' = TIP with M[p] on the checked paths; an F10 result). */
export interface TreeView {
  readonly tree: Oid | null; readonly overlay: ReadonlyMap<string, BlobRef | 'ABSENT'>;
  entry(path: string): Promise<{ readonly blob: BlobRef; readonly mode: FileMode } | 'ABSENT'>;
}

// src/regions/lines.ts (04 §3.2; REG-01; R4-18) — Task 3's first commit (with test/unit/lines.test.ts), merged before Task 4's table step; Task 4 edits nothing in it
/** Rows split on 0x0A only — a re-export of Plan 2's rows() (one helper, never copied): BOM in row 1, CR kept in its row, a non-empty unterminated last row. */
export function lineArray(blob: Buffer): readonly Buffer[];
/** TextDecoder('utf-8', {fatal: true, ignoreBOM: true}); null = not UTF-8 (decodable: false). Read-only: nothing is ever encoded (L-UTF8-WRITE). */
export function decodeStrict(blob: Buffer): string | null;
/** R4-18: empty or only 0x20/0x09 after one trailing \r (and, on row 1, a BOM) is removed; a byte test. */
export function isBlankRow(row: Buffer, rowNumber: number): boolean;
/** 04 §3.4 step 3's normal form: sorted, merged (adjacent intervals merge), zero-length intervals dropped. In lines.ts (Task 3's first commit) because Task 4's residue
 *  units and TableCache.lastGood need it in parallel with the rest of Task 3; mapper.ts re-exports both. */
export function normalizeRanges(rs: readonly (readonly [number, number])[]): Ranges;
export function rangesIntersect(a: Ranges, b: Ranges): boolean;   // closed intervals (04 §3.9 step 4)

// src/regions/mapper.ts (04 §3.4 FoldMapper/1; REG-07, INV-REG-MAP, INV-CLOCK; R4-17) — Task 3
export const FOLD_MAPPER = 'FoldMapper/1';
export const D_CAP = 5_000;            // step 5: D > 5,000 → WHOLE_FILE in every mode
export const LOCAL_MAP_CAP_MS = 200;   // step 5: local mode only
export const D_REANCHOR = 1_000;       // glossary §20
/** Step 1: equal after removing one trailing \r from each and, on line 1 only, a leading EF BB BF. */
export function linesEqual(a: Buffer, b: Buffer, lineOne: boolean): boolean;
/** Steps 1–2: common prefix/suffix trimming, then Myers O(ND) with the greedy forward snake and leftmost tie-break; deterministic on every peer. */
export function hunks(from: readonly Buffer[], to: readonly Buffer[]): Hunk[];
/** D (04 §2): the minimal Myers edit distance in lines; null once it exceeds cap. */
export function editDistance(from: readonly Buffer[], to: readonly Buffer[], cap: number): number | null;
/** Step 3 over a hunk list: images of unchanged lines ∪ b-sides of intersecting hunks ∪ pure insertions after k with lo ≤ k < hi (k == hi, k == lo−1 excluded); TOMBSTONE when every line is deleted and nothing is added. */
export function imageOf(hs: readonly Hunk[], ranges: Ranges): Ranges | 'TOMBSTONE';
/** Steps 3–5 by one direct mapping (step 4): D > D_CAP → WHOLE_FILE in both modes; 'local' with an expired deadline → WHOLE_FILE; 'fold' has no time cap. */
export function mapRangesLines(from: readonly Buffer[], to: readonly Buffer[], ranges: Ranges, mode: 'fold'): MapResult;
export function mapRangesLines(from: readonly Buffer[], to: readonly Buffer[], ranges: Ranges, mode: 'local', deadline: Deadline): MapResult;
export { normalizeRanges, rangesIntersect } from './lines';   // declared in lines.ts (Task 3's first commit); one helper, re-exported
```

### Runtime, budgets, workers, codes

```ts
// src/regions/runtime.ts (04 §3.3 "Runtime"; REG-03, REG-04; R4-09…R4-11, R4-16) — Task 1
export const LANGUAGES: readonly Language[];   // python, javascript, typescript, tsx, c_sharp
export const GRAMMAR_VERSIONS: Readonly<{ 'web-tree-sitter': string } & Record<Language, string>>;   // pinned (R4-10), checked against package-lock.json
export const GRAMMAR_FILES: Readonly<Record<Language, string>>;   // tree-sitter-python.wasm … tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm
export const RUNTIME_WASM = 'tree-sitter.wasm';
/** R4-16: the byte-exact lowercase extension of the last path component → its language, else null. */
export function languageOf(path: string): Language | null;
export interface RegionRuntime {
  readonly dir: string;   // the grammars directory it was loaded from
  readonly runtimeWasm: Buffer; readonly grammars: ReadonlyMap<Language, Buffer>;
  readonly querySources: Readonly<Record<Language, string>>;   // src/regions/queries/*
  readonly regionSpec: string;                                  // R4-11
}
export class RegionRuntimeMissingError extends Error { readonly file: string; }
/** Reads every .wasm through the Fs port (R4-12); a missing file throws RegionRuntimeMissingError (daemon start refuses). */
export function loadRegionRuntime(fs: Fs, dir: string): Promise<RegionRuntime>;
/** R4-11: regionSpecId([...sha256 hex of each .wasm, ...sha256 hex of each query source, 'extent-convention/1', 'label-convention/1', 'FoldMapper/1']).
 *  Reimplements Plan 2's regionSpecId formula (sha256 lowercase hex over the parts sorted by UTF-16 code units and joined by '\n', createHash().update(<string>))
 *  because src/regions may not import src/engine (R4-02); a Task 1 test pins the equality. Computed from the shipped bytes, never from load success. */
export function computeRegionSpec(wasm: readonly Buffer[], querySources: readonly string[]): string;

// src/regions/queries/index.ts (R4-11) — Task 1 (capture skeleton per language), Task 4 (complete)
export const QUERY_SOURCES: Readonly<Record<Language, string>>;
// The query files' capture names are fixed (Task 4): @unit.function, @unit.class, @unit.method, @unit.const, @unit.type, @unit.ctor, @unit.dtor, @unit.property,
// @unit.indexer, @unit.event, @unit.field, @unit.operator, @name, @decorator, @attribute, @export, @signature, @comment, @import, @iife, @branch;
// src/regions/worker/tables.ts consumes only these.

// src/regions/budgets.ts (04 §3.18, §2 chapter-local numbers; REG-31; R4-13, R4-15) — Task 1
export const BUDGETS: Readonly<{
  gateMs: 15; parseMs: 400; mapLocalMs: 200; predictWaitMs: 10; t0Ms: 500; t0StopFactor: 4; editCheckMs: 500; editCheckStopFactor: 1;
  callerGrepMs: 150; partialGrepMs: 100; cacheBytes: 314_572_800; liveTreesPerWorker: 8; regionBudgetDedupeMs: 1_800_000; workerGraceMs: 1_000;
}>;
export type BudgetKind = 'parse' | 'map' | 'predict_wait' | 't0' | 'edit_check' | 'caller_grep' | 'partial_grep' | 'gate';
export interface Deadline { readonly kind: BudgetKind; readonly budgetMs: number; expired(): boolean; remainingMs(): number; }
export interface RegionBudgets { deadline(kind: BudgetKind, factor?: number): Deadline; }   // factor: T0 stops at 4×, the edit check at 1×
/** Test builds only (Plan 7's L-DIST strips it): the next n deadlines of a kind start expired, or a job of that kind stalls ms (T-REG-29's 0.6 s, T-REG-15's 200 ms cap).
 *  expire(kind, n): the next n deadlines of that kind start expired. stallMs(kind, ms): the next deadline of that kind reports ms of extra elapsed time (remainingMs()
 *  lowered by ms; expired once ms ≥ budgetMs × factor); no thread sleeps, so inline and thread runs and SIM stay deterministic. */
export interface BudgetInjector { expire(kind: BudgetKind, times: number): void; stallMs(kind: BudgetKind, ms: number): void; }
/** Deadlines on the Clock port's monotonic time; under SIM's virtual clock with inline jobs nothing expires unless injected (TST-03). */
export function clockBudgets(clock: Clock, inject?: BudgetInjector): RegionBudgets;
export function testBudgetInjector(): BudgetInjector;

// src/regions/pool.ts (04 §3.1, §3.18; REG-31; R4-12…R4-15) — Task 1 declares the union and the dispatcher; each kind's handler file (src/regions/worker/jobs/<kind>.ts) is its task's
export type WorkerPriority = 'fg' | 'save' | 'bg';
export type RegionJob =
  | { kind: 'table'; lang: Language | null; cls: 4 | 6; ref: BlobRef; bytes: Buffer; regionSpec: string; prevRef: BlobRef | null }   // Task 4 (prevRef: a live tree for incremental re-parse, T_predict)
  | { kind: 'hunks'; from: readonly Buffer[]; to: readonly Buffer[] }                                                                 // Task 3
  | { kind: 'map'; from: readonly Buffer[]; to: readonly Buffer[]; ranges: Ranges; mode: MapMode }                                    // Task 3
  | { kind: 'fold_overlap'; pairs: ReadonlyArray<{ c1: OverlapClaim; c2: OverlapClaim }>; lines: ReadonlyMap<string, readonly Buffer[]> }   // Task 8
  | { kind: 'sites'; lang: Language; ref: BlobRef; bytes: Buffer; names: readonly string[] }                                         // Task 7 (calls, imports, identifiers with rows)
  | { kind: 'label_at'; lang: Language; ref: BlobRef; bytes: Buffer; line: number; regionSpec: string };                             // Task 13
/** Per kind: table → TableOrNone; hunks → Hunk[]; map → MapResult; fold_overlap → boolean[]; sites → SiteSet; label_at → Label | null. */
export type JobOutput<J extends RegionJob> = J extends { kind: 'table' } ? TableOrNone : J extends { kind: 'hunks' } ? Hunk[] : J extends { kind: 'map' } ? MapResult : J extends { kind: 'fold_overlap' } ? boolean[] : J extends { kind: 'sites' } ? SiteSet : Label | null;
export type JobResult<J extends RegionJob> = { kind: 'ok'; value: JobOutput<J> } | { kind: 'deadline' } | { kind: 'crashed' };
export interface RegionWorkers {
  run<J extends RegionJob>(job: J, priority: WorkerPriority, deadline: Deadline | null): Promise<JobResult<J>>;
  /** Per-language grammar load status (REGION_GRAMMAR_UNAVAILABLE; Plan 7's doctor row). */
  availability(): ReadonlyMap<Language, 'ok' | 'unavailable'>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}
/** Production: two worker_threads (fg, bg) running dist/worker.js with the .wasm bytes posted at spawn; one queue fg > save > bg; a worker silent BUDGETS.workerGraceMs after a deadline is terminated and respawned (04 §6 "worker crashes").
 *  The pool posts remainingMs (never an absolute time); the main thread races the reply against clock.sleep(deadline.remainingMs()) and resolves {kind: 'deadline'} on expiry.
 *  onRespawn (tests; the daemon logs it as REGION_WORKER_RESPAWN {name, cause}, Task 16, log-only, V-17) is called once per respawn. */
export function threadWorkers(runtime: RegionRuntime, o: { threads: 2; workerPath: string; clock: Clock; onRespawn?(name: 'fg' | 'bg', cause: 'crash' | 'stall'): void }): Promise<RegionWorkers>;   // threads is always 2 in v1 (fg, bg; R4-13)
/** R4-13's dispatch rule, unit-tested without threads: fg > save > bg, FIFO within a class; next('bg') never returns an fg job; an idle fg worker takes save/bg work. */
export class JobQueue {
  push(job: RegionJob, priority: WorkerPriority): number;
  next(worker: 'fg' | 'bg'): { id: number; job: RegionJob; priority: WorkerPriority } | null;
  readonly size: number;
}

// src/regions/worker/inline.ts (test builds and SIM only; never imported by src/daemon or src/engine) — Task 1
/** The same handlers on the calling thread, FIFO, deterministic; the caller's Deadline object is passed into the handler, so under a VirtualClock only an injected overrun expires.
 *  A job whose deadline is already expired at dispatch resolves {kind: 'deadline'} without running its handler (as threadWorkers abandons it); a handler that throws resolves
 *  {kind: 'crashed'}. o.handlers (tests only) replaces the named kinds' handlers, so Task 1's pool tests never depend on what a production handler returns. */
export function inlineWorkers(runtime: RegionRuntime, o?: { handlers?: Partial<JobHandlers> }): Promise<RegionWorkers>;

// src/regions/worker/protocol.ts (types only; imported by pool.ts, main.ts and inline.ts) — Task 1
/** Posted as workerData; bytes copied, so every spawn and respawn gets them. */
export interface WorkerInit { name: 'fg' | 'bg'; generation: number; runtimeWasm: Uint8Array; grammars: ReadonlyArray<readonly [Language, Uint8Array]>; }
export type ToWorker = { type: 'job'; id: number; job: RegionJob; remainingMs: number | null };
export type FromWorker =
  | { type: 'ready'; availability: ReadonlyArray<readonly [Language, 'ok' | 'unavailable']> }
  | { type: 'result'; id: number; result: JobResult<RegionJob> };
export interface WorkerPortLike { on(ev: 'message', cb: (m: unknown) => void): void; postMessage(m: FromWorker): void; }

// src/regions/worker/main.ts — Task 1 (only src/entry/worker.ts imports node:worker_threads on the worker side and calls it with parentPort and workerData)
/** The worker builds its own deadline from realClock and the posted remainingMs and checks it between files, between query captures and in the parser's progress callback.
 *  handlers (test worker entries only, e.g. test/helpers/workers/throwWorker.ts) replaces the named kinds' handlers; src/entry/worker.ts never passes it. */
export function runWorkerLoop(port: WorkerPortLike, init: WorkerInit, handlers?: Partial<JobHandlers>): Promise<void>;

// src/regions/worker/parser.ts — Task 1 (Task 4 adds incremental re-parse through lineTable.inputEdit)
export interface ParserHost {
  availability(): ReadonlyMap<Language, 'ok' | 'unavailable'>;
  /** ≤ BUDGETS.liveTreesPerWorker live trees, LRU by key; old.edit is built by lineTable.inputEdit. */
  parse(lang: Language, bytes: Buffer, key: string, deadline: { expired(): boolean } | null, old?: { key: string; edit: unknown }): Tree | 'deadline' | 'unavailable';
  liveKeys(): readonly string[];
}
/** Initialises the runtime from init.runtimeWasm (Emscripten wasmBinary), loads each grammar with Language.load(Uint8Array), compiles its query and parses a built-in
 *  one-line self-test snippet; any failure marks that language unavailable (a language whose query does not compile is grammarless). */
export function initParser(init: WorkerInit, queries?: Readonly<Record<Language, string>>): Promise<ParserHost>;

// src/regions/worker/jobs.ts — Task 1 (each src/regions/worker/jobs/<kind>.ts exports handle(job, ctx); Task 1's stubs throw Error('job <kind>: Task <n>'))
export interface JobCtx { parser: ParserHost; deadline: { expired(): boolean } | null; }
/** One handler per RegionJob kind (the default table imports src/regions/worker/jobs/<kind>.ts). */
export type JobHandlers = { [K in RegionJob['kind']]: (job: Extract<RegionJob, { kind: K }>, ctx: JobCtx) => Promise<JobOutput<Extract<RegionJob, { kind: K }>> | 'deadline'> };
/** A throw becomes {kind: 'crashed'}, after which a thread worker exits and the pool respawns it (a wasm trap may leave the module unusable). handlers overrides the default table (tests only). */
export function runJob<J extends RegionJob>(job: J, ctx: JobCtx, handlers?: Partial<JobHandlers>): Promise<JobOutput<J> | 'deadline'>;

// src/regions/codes.ts (04 §3.19; 02 §2.1; R4-54) — Task 1
export const REGION_CODES: readonly ['REGION_BLOCK_ONLY', 'REGION_CONFLICT_MARKERS', 'REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH', 'REGION_WHOLE_FILE', 'REGION_BUDGET', 'CLAIM_OVERLAP', 'REGION_GRAMMAR_UNAVAILABLE'];
export const CLAIM_REJECTED = 'CLAIM_REJECTED';
export type RegionCode = (typeof REGION_CODES)[number] | typeof CLAIM_REJECTED;
export interface RegionCodeParams {
  REGION_BLOCK_ONLY: { path: string; reason: 'no_grammar' | 'not_utf8' | 'grammar_unavailable' };
  REGION_CONFLICT_MARKERS: { path: string };
  REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH: { path: string; claim_id: string; peer: string };   // peer = display name, glossary §19 cap
  REGION_WHOLE_FILE: { path: string; claim_id: string };
  REGION_BUDGET: { path: string; budget: 'parse' | 'map' | 'grep' | 't0' | 'gate' };
  CLAIM_OVERLAP: { cs_id: string; claim_id: string; overlaps: string; path: string };
  REGION_GRAMMAR_UNAVAILABLE: { language: Language };
  CLAIM_REJECTED: { claim_id: string; reason: 'anchor' | 'base_blob' | 'ranges' | 'validator' | 'reservation' };
}
/** X-117: Plan 4's start refusal (Task 16 raises it; its text, registry row and LIF-36 handling are Plan 7's). */
export const START_REGION_RUNTIME = 'START_REGION_RUNTIME';   // params { file: string }
/** X-30: the log-only registry codes Plan 4 writes (surfaces: ['log'] in Plan 7's registry; never in status.json): FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING { oid } (Task 8, R4-76),
 *  EDIT_CHECK_OVERRUN { cs_id } (Task 10, 04 §3.18's perf count), and the five the verify pass named (V-17): REGION_OBJECT_MISSING { oid, path } (Task 4,
 *  RegionService.bytes → 'MISSING', REG-35), EDIT_CHECK_INPUT_MISMATCH { path } (Task 10, R4-41's M[p] contradiction), REGION_WORKER_RESPAWN { name, cause }
 *  (Task 16, threadWorkers' onRespawn), REGION_RUNTIME_MISMATCH { engine, runtime } (Task 16, R4-81's exit-2 line; the two regionSpec hashes) and
 *  CLAIM_BREACH_HIT { claim_id, holder, commit, path, base_move } (Task 15, logBreach's line per hit, R4-51). Params are ids, oids, paths, hashes and enums only
 *  (SEC-38). Plan 7's carried.ts imports this module; no other module declares these names (X-29 scan; CLAIM_BREACH_HIT ≠ Plan 6's CLAIM_BREACH). */
export const REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES: readonly ['FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING', 'EDIT_CHECK_OVERRUN', 'REGION_OBJECT_MISSING', 'EDIT_CHECK_INPUT_MISMATCH',
  'REGION_WORKER_RESPAWN', 'REGION_RUNTIME_MISMATCH', 'CLAIM_BREACH_HIT'];
```

### Path classes, snapshots, cache, the service

```ts
// src/regions/pathClass.ts (04 §3.3 "Path classes"; REG-05, REG-06, REG-32; R4-16, R4-20) — Task 4
export const GRAMMARLESS_WHOLE_FILE_MAX_LINES = 300;   // glossary §20
export interface PathFacts { readonly mode: FileMode; readonly attrs: PathAttrs; }   // attributes at EPOCH_BASE (DAT-13)
export interface PathClassVerdict { readonly cls: PathClass; readonly lang: Language | null; readonly decodable: boolean; readonly blockReason: 'no_grammar' | 'not_utf8' | 'grammar_unavailable' | null; }
/** First match wins: 1 conflict-marker rows (Plan 2 conflictMarkerRows), 2 binary (isBinaryBytes or -text/binary), 3 mode 120000/160000, .ipynb, any filter=, any working-tree-encoding, 4 a loaded grammar and decodable, 5 otherwise ≤ 300 lines, 6 otherwise; called only for LIVE paths. */
export function pathClassOf(path: string, blob: Buffer, facts: PathFacts, grammarOk: (l: Language) => boolean): PathClassVerdict;

// src/regions/snapshots.ts (04 §2 "diskSnapshotId"; REG-35, INV-REG-READ, INV-SECRET; R4-46) — Task 4
export class SnapshotGoneError extends Error {}
export class SnapshotStore {
  /** Registers working bytes under the oid the caller computed without -w (01-engine "Hook requests"); the same (path, oid) returns the same handle; an owner token keeps it alive. */
  register(path: string, oid: Oid, bytes: Buffer, owner: string): DiskSnapshotId;
  bytes(id: DiskSnapshotId): Buffer;   // throws SnapshotGoneError once released
  retain(id: DiskSnapshotId, owner: string): void;
  release(owner: string): void;        // drops every handle no owner retains
  readonly size: number;
}

// src/regions/worker/conventions.ts (04 §3.3; REG-16, R4-19) — Task 4
export const EXTENT_CONVENTION = 'extent-convention/1';
export const LABEL_CONVENTION = 'label-convention/1';

// src/regions/worker/lineTable.ts (REG-01; L-OFFSETS) — Task 4: the only reader of tree-sitter positions (and the only place a byte offset may appear, inside inputEdit)
/** 1-based stored-form rows of a node: [startPosition.row + 1, endRow(endPosition)]. */
export function rowsOf(node: { readonly startPosition: { row: number; column: number }; readonly endPosition: { row: number; column: number } }): Range;
/** 1-based end row: row + 1, minus one when column == 0 (endRow({row: 9, column: 0}) === 9, endRow({row: 9, column: 4}) === 10). */
export function endRow(pos: { row: number; column: number }): number;
/** The tree-sitter edit for an incremental re-parse from prevBytes to nextBytes (the ParserHost.parse old.edit value); never leaves the worker. */
export function inputEdit(prevBytes: Buffer, nextBytes: Buffer): unknown;

// src/regions/cache.ts (04 §3.3 "Cache", "Last-good table"; §3.18; R4-14) — Task 4
/** R4-14's per-unit estimate: an entry's size is its line-array bytes plus UNIT_COST_BYTES per unit. */
export const UNIT_COST_BYTES = 256;
export class TableCache {
  constructor(maxBytes: number);
  get(ref: BlobRef, path: string): TableOrNone | undefined;
  put(ref: BlobRef, path: string, t: TableOrNone, lines: readonly Buffer[]): void;
  lines(ref: BlobRef): readonly Buffer[] | undefined;
  /** REG-18: the newest cached table of path whose top-level unit covering `lines` has no ERROR/MISSING span; null when none. */
  lastGood(path: string, lines: Ranges): { readonly ref: BlobRef; readonly table: RegionTable } | null;
  pin(ref: DiskSnapshotId, owner: string): void;   // never evicted while pinned; counts toward maxBytes
  unpin(owner: string): void;
  readonly bytes: number;
}

// src/regions/service.ts (04 §3.1, main-thread side; REG-02, REG-05, REG-31, REG-35) — Task 4 (Task 5 adds hunks and mapRanges over Task 3's job handlers)
export interface RegionServiceDeps {
  repo: GitRepo; workers: RegionWorkers; budgets: RegionBudgets; cache: TableCache; snapshots: SnapshotStore;
  regionSpec: string; log: Log; clock: Clock;
  attrs(epochBase: Oid, paths: readonly string[]): Promise<AttrSnapshot>;   // Plan 2 AttrCache at EPOCH_BASE
  privateIndex: string;                                                      // GIT_INDEX_FILE of the partial-type grep (R4-45)
  onBudget(path: string, budget: 'parse' | 'map' | 'grep' | 't0' | 'gate'): void;   // → raiseRegionCode('REGION_BUDGET'), deduplicated there
}
export class RegionService {
  constructor(d: RegionServiceDeps);
  readonly deps: RegionServiceDeps;
  /** A stored blob by the cat-file --batch reader (only oids in the object store), a DiskSnapshotId from SnapshotStore; a missing object → 'MISSING' and one daemon-log line REGION_OBJECT_MISSING {oid, path} (log-only, V-17) (REG-35: callers degrade like NO_TABLE{budget}, mapping WHOLE_FILE). */
  bytes(ref: BlobRef): Promise<Buffer | 'MISSING'>;
  lines(ref: BlobRef): Promise<readonly Buffer[] | 'MISSING'>;
  /** REG-05/REG-06: path class, then the worker parse (class 4) or the block table (class 6); NO_TABLE{reason} for classes 1–3 and 5; an overrun, a crash or a missing object → NO_TABLE{budget}; cached by blobRef. */
  table(ref: BlobRef, path: string, o: { epochBase: Oid; mode?: FileMode; priority?: WorkerPriority; prevRef?: BlobRef }): Promise<TableOrNone>;
  /** FoldMapper/1 over two blobRefs, in a worker. */
  hunks(from: BlobRef | 'ABSENT', to: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'): Promise<Hunk[]>;
  /** 04 §3.1 mapRanges(fromBlob, toBlob, ranges, mode); local mode carries a BUDGETS.mapLocalMs deadline; a missing blob → WHOLE_FILE.
   *  path (Task 5) is used only to attribute a local-mode overrun's REGION_BUDGET {path, budget: 'map'} through onBudget; without it no code is raised. */
  mapRanges(from: BlobRef, to: BlobRef, ranges: Ranges, mode: MapMode, path?: string): Promise<MapResult>;
  view(tree: Oid | null, overlay?: ReadonlyMap<string, BlobRef | 'ABSENT'>): TreeView;
  availability(): ReadonlyMap<Language, 'ok' | 'unavailable'>;
}
```

### Touch, containment, group names, tripwires, the interface check, the edit check, fold overlap

```ts
// src/regions/touch.ts (04 §3.6; REG-13, REG-14, REG-04) — Task 6
export type { TouchReason } from './types';   // declared in src/regions/types.ts (Task 1)
/** The claim as touch sees it: granularity and regionSpec only; no registry, no own-claims input, labels only through step 5's precomputed hit (REG-13, REG-14). */
export interface TouchClaim { readonly granularity: Granularity; readonly regionSpec: string; }
export interface TouchInput {
  readonly inBase: Ranges | 'WHOLE_FILE';                   // the claim (ranges and tombstones) mapped into base[p] by REG-07 local mode
  readonly inPost: Ranges | 'TOMBSTONE' | 'WHOLE_FILE';      // its image in post[p] through hunks(base, post) (step 4's "leaf unit containing c's image")
  readonly post: TableOrNone;
  readonly groupNameHit: boolean;                           // step 5 (groupName.ts)
}
/** Steps 1–6 in order; every consumer calls it: predict, confirm, gate, placementCheck (b), breach, the D8 step-4 tie-break, the HLD dependency scan. */
export function touch(h: Hunk, c: TouchClaim, inp: TouchInput, ownRegionSpec: string): { touch: boolean; reason: TouchReason };

// src/regions/contained.ts (04 §3.6 "Containment form"; REG-33) — Task 6
export interface OwnClaimImage { readonly granularity: Granularity; readonly inBase: Ranges | 'WHOLE_FILE'; readonly inPost: Ranges | 'TOMBSTONE' | 'WHOLE_FILE'; }
/** Conditions (1)–(4): a WHOLE_FILE own claim adds nothing to U; a boundary insertion decided only by an ERROR span or NO_TABLE, a group-name hit and every uncertainty are not contained. */
export function contained(h: Hunk, own: readonly OwnClaimImage[], post: TableOrNone): boolean;

// src/regions/groupName.ts (04 §3.12; REG-17) — Task 6
export interface PartialTypeInfo { readonly simpleName: string; readonly files: readonly string[]; readonly failed: boolean; }
/** C# methods, constructors, operators and indexers and TypeScript function overload sets only: a unit the hunk adds whose (type qualname, simple) equals a member covered by the claim in this daemon's own table, or a member of a partial part of that type; never Python or JavaScript.
 *  hunk is used only for the 04 §3.12 failure fallback: when partial?.failed and lang === 'c_sharp', the result is true iff hunk.path ∈ partial.files or hunk.fileDeclaresPartial. */
export function groupNameHit(lang: Language, added: readonly Unit[], claimed: readonly Unit[], partial: PartialTypeInfo | null, hunk?: { path: string; fileDeclaresPartial: boolean }): boolean;

// src/regions/partial.ts (04 §3.12; REG-17; R4-44, R4-45) — Task 6
/** git grep -z -n -E 'partial[[:space:]]+(class|struct|record|interface)[[:space:]]+<Name>([^[:alnum:]_]|$)' --cached on the private index through GitRepo.run
 *  (GIT_INDEX_FILE = the private index; no RunOpts.config — core.quotepath=off is one of the runner's forced -c pairs, R4-44), deadline 100 ms; a failure or a timeout →
 *  {failed: true, files: []}: every hunk of a file whose post bytes declaresPartial touches, through groupNameHit's hunk.fileDeclaresPartial (R4-45).
 *  The spec's trailing \b is spelled ([^[:alnum:]_]|$) (a GNU extension some platforms' regex libraries lack; the matches are the same); <Name> is a C# identifier from
 *  this daemon's own table, matched against ^[\p{L}_][\p{L}\p{N}_]*$ before it enters the argv. */
export function partialTypeFiles(s: RegionService, simpleName: string): Promise<PartialTypeInfo>;
/** The grep's pattern over one file's post bytes: a file-level boolean that can only add a conflict and supplies no range (REG-03). */
export function declaresPartial(bytes: Buffer, simpleName: string): boolean;

// src/regions/tripwire.ts (04 §3.15; REG-24, INV-REG-TRIPWIRE; glossary §10 K7) — Task 7
export type TripwireViolation =
  | { readonly form: 'a'; readonly theirs: number; readonly out: number }   // errorCount(out) > errorCount(theirs) (0 for a new path)
  | { readonly form: 'b'; readonly key: LabelKey; readonly theirs: number; readonly ours: number; readonly out: number }   // count(out) > max(count(theirs), count(ours))
  | { readonly form: 'c' };                                                   // theirs decodes as UTF-8, out does not
/** The relative forms only (the absolute forms are forbidden); a null side = an absent path. */
export function regionTripwire(theirs: TableOrNone | null, ours: TableOrNone | null, out: TableOrNone, decodes: { theirs: boolean; out: boolean }): TripwireViolation[];

// src/regions/interface.ts (04 §3.10; REG-26, REG-27) — Task 7
export const CALLER_STOPLIST: ReadonlySet<string>;   // get set run main init __init__ update load save close
export const MAX_HITS_PER_NAME = 50;
export type ChangeClass = 'breaking' | 'compatible' | 'note';
export type BreakingKind = 'removed' | 'param_removed' | 'param_reordered' | 'param_renamed' | 'required_added' | 'varargs_removed' | 'overload_gone';
export interface Breaking { readonly path: string; readonly label: Label; readonly name: string; readonly kind: BreakingKind; readonly removal: boolean; }
/** Net per unit over the whole CS (base vs ours), never per edit; a rename is a removal plus an addition. */
export function classifyUnit(base: Unit | null, ours: Unit | null): { cls: ChangeClass; breaking: BreakingKind | null };
// CallArgs, Site and SiteSet are declared here as types only by Task 1 (the RegionJob union's 'sites' output needs them before Task 7); Task 7 adds the rest of the module.
// C# SigSlice.overloadKey = <type qualname> + '\0' + <simple>.
export interface CallArgs { readonly positional: number; readonly keywords: readonly string[]; readonly splat: boolean; }
export interface Site { readonly path: string; readonly line: number; readonly name: string; readonly kind: 'call' | 'import' | 'identifier'; readonly receiver: 'bare' | 'self' | 'module' | 'type' | 'other'; readonly module: string | null; readonly args: CallArgs | null; readonly inComment: boolean; readonly inString: boolean; }
export interface SiteSet { readonly sites: readonly Site[]; }
/** 04 §3.10 "Arity compatibility": Python, JS/TS, C# (some overload covers the count and the named arguments; types unchecked); a splat call is 'note'. */
export function arityCompatible(lang: Language, sigs: readonly SigSlice[], args: CallArgs): boolean | 'note';
/** 04 §3.10: a JSON key-path change is a note in v1. */
export function jsonKeyPaths(blob: Buffer): readonly string[] | null;
/** line = the 1-based row in out of the call or import (F14). */
export interface Finding { readonly path: string; readonly line: number; readonly name: string; readonly removal: boolean; }
export interface InterfaceResult {
  readonly breaking: readonly Breaking[];
  readonly incompatibleCallers: readonly Finding[];   // definite calls not written by this CS
  readonly orphanCalls: readonly Finding[];
  readonly unresolvedImports: readonly Finding[];
  readonly notes: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly path: string; readonly line: number | null; readonly kind: 'possible' | 'too_many_hits' | 'stoplist' | 'json_key' | 'decorator' | 'default_value' | 'splat' }>;
  readonly incomplete: boolean;
  /** R4-72: which deadline stopped an incomplete check — 'grep' = the caller search's own 4× stop (deadline('caller_grep', 4), 600 ms), 'caller' = the deadline the caller passed
   *  (T0's 2,000 ms, the edit check's 500 ms) or an aborted grep; null when complete. */
  readonly incompleteBudget: 'grep' | 'caller' | null;
  readonly symbolDelta: { readonly added: readonly Label[]; readonly signatureChanged: readonly Label[] };          // HLD-48/49 (05 §8 Q2 default)
  readonly referencedNames: { readonly identifiers: readonly string[]; readonly imports: readonly string[] };
}

// src/regions/interfaceService.ts (04 §3.10; §3.20 "Caller search without a stored merged tree"; REG-27; R4-44) — Task 7
export interface InterfaceInput {
  readonly base: TreeView; readonly ours: TreeView; readonly out: TreeView;
  readonly csPaths: readonly string[];               // the CS's (or the group's) LIVE paths
  readonly csRows: ReadonlyMap<string, Ranges>;      // rows of ours the CS wrote (from hunks(base, ours)): "a definite call not written by this CS"
  readonly epochBase: Oid;
}
/** Callers: git -c attr.tree=<EPOCH_BASE> grep -z -n -w -F -e <name> on out's stored tree with :(literal) paths and one :(exclude,literal) per overlay path, plus the same whole-word fixed-string predicate over the overlay's in-memory line arrays (the union equals a grep of out); callees of the CS's new calls; import names; a deadline → incomplete.
 *  The caller search (part (i), part (ii) and the hit filtering) runs under the earlier of `deadline` and one s.deps.budgets.deadline('caller_grep', 4) created when the search
 *  starts (R4-72; composed inside interfaceService.ts, no new export); incompleteBudget names the one that expired. */
export function interfaceCheck(s: RegionService, inp: InterfaceInput, deadline: Deadline): Promise<InterfaceResult>;
/** The byte-wise git grep -w -F predicate (word constituents [A-Za-z0-9_], every other byte a boundary, as under LC_ALL=C), 1-based rows; part (ii) of the split caller search. */
export function wholeWordRows(lines: readonly Buffer[], name: string): number[];
/** The raw union of part (i) (one git grep on out.tree with :(literal) paths and one :(exclude,literal) per overlay path) and part (ii) (wholeWordRows over each overlay
 *  path's line array), before the definition/comment/string filter, so the R2-E12 property compares hit sets exactly; a deadline or an aborted grep → 'incomplete'. */
export function callerHits(s: RegionService, out: TreeView, name: string, paths: readonly string[], epochBase: Oid, deadline: Deadline): Promise<ReadonlyArray<{ path: string; line: number }> | 'incomplete'>;

// src/regions/editCheck.ts (04 §3.20; REG-36, INV-REG-EDITCHECK; glossary §27.1; R4-08, R4-41, R4-42) — Task 5 creates it with the EditCheckReason and EditCheckEntry
// declarations (types only; src/holds/ports.ts imports them as types), Task 10 adds the rest
export type EditCheckReason =
  | 'new_error_node' | 'undecodable' | 'conflict_markers'       // check 1
  | 'incompatible_caller' | 'orphan_call'                      // check 2
  | 'unresolved_import' | 'removed_name_called'                // check 3
  | 'label_count' | 'shrink' | 'emptied' | 'deleted'           // check 4
  | 'no_snapshot' | 'merge_conflict' | 'no_merge';             // no check (F12)
/** `check` absent exactly for no_snapshot | merge_conflict | no_merge; `line` only for checks 1–3, a 1-based row of M' (F14); display data only (D1). */
export interface EditCheckEntry { readonly check?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; readonly path: string; readonly reason: EditCheckReason; readonly line?: number; }
/** R4-73: 'conflicted' (→ merge_conflict) = merge-file rc > 0, or a path both sides changed that merge-file cannot take (modify/delete, delete/modify, add/add with different
 *  content, binary, filtered, modes both changed differently); 'not_supplied' (→ no_merge) = a merge that could not be computed (an A or TIP blob unreadable, merge-file rc ≥ 128). */
export type MergedPath = { readonly kind: 'blob'; readonly ref: BlobRef } | { readonly kind: 'deleted' } | { readonly kind: 'conflicted' } | { readonly kind: 'not_supplied' };
export interface EditCheckCs {
  readonly csId: string; readonly paths: readonly string[];                          // the CS's LIVE paths
  readonly merged: ReadonlyMap<string, MergedPath>;                                    // M per checked path
  readonly candidate: ReadonlyMap<string, BlobRef | 'ABSENT' | 'unavailable'>;         // R per path; unavailable → no_snapshot
}
export interface EditCheckInput {
  readonly A: TreeView; readonly R: TreeView; readonly TIP: TreeView; readonly cs: EditCheckCs; readonly mode: 'checkpoint' | 'predict'; readonly epochBase: Oid;
  readonly skipChecks: ReadonlySet<1 | 2 | 3 | 4>;   // = M46_n (test builds)
  readonly computeLine?: boolean;                    // default true; false skips every line computation — only the test "line never changes the verdict" sets it (REG-36)
}
/** 04 §3.1: pass = every check passed and incomplete = false; failed sorted per R4-08; elapsedMs from the job start. */
export interface EditCheckResult { readonly pass: boolean; readonly failed: readonly EditCheckEntry[]; readonly incomplete: boolean; readonly elapsedMs: number; }
export function sortEntries(es: readonly EditCheckEntry[]): EditCheckEntry[];
/** Checks 1 and 4 of one checked path (pure): check 1's line = the first ERROR/MISSING span of M[p] not intersecting the REG-07 image of any TIP[p] span, else M[p]'s first span; conflict markers → the first marker row; undecodable → no line; check 4's label part on M[p]'s table, its size part (TIP[p] non-empty ⇒ M[p] not deleted, empty or < 50 % of TIP's stored bytes) only in mode 'checkpoint'. */
export function pathChecks(p: string, t: { tip: TableOrNone | null; r: TableOrNone | null; m: TableOrNone | null }, sizes: { tip: number; m: number | null }, decodes: { tip: boolean; m: boolean }, tipErrorImage: Ranges | 'WHOLE_FILE', mode: 'checkpoint' | 'predict', skip: ReadonlySet<1 | 2 | 3 | 4>): EditCheckEntry[];
/** Checks 2 and 3 from interfaceCheck(A, R, M', cs): non-removal incompatible callers and orphan calls → 2; unresolved imports and removal callers → 3; notes never fail. */
export function interfaceEntries(r: InterfaceResult, skip: ReadonlySet<1 | 2 | 3 | 4>): EditCheckEntry[];

// src/regions/editCheckService.ts (04 §3.20 "Budget and scheduling"; REG-31's exception) — Task 10
/** Checked paths = cs.paths with R[p] ≠ A[p]; M' = TIP with M[p] on them (a deletion = absent); deadline BUDGETS.editCheckMs × 1 from the job start → incomplete: true (no REGION_BUDGET); NO_TABLE{budget} or a missing object on a grammar path → incomplete; writes no object, index entry or ref. */
export function editCheck(s: RegionService, inp: EditCheckInput): Promise<EditCheckResult>;

// src/regions/foldOverlap.ts (04 §3.9; REG-23, INV-REG-FOLD, INV-REG-NOVALID-OVERLAP) — Task 1 declares OverlapClaim (type only, for the RegionJob union), Task 8 adds the rest
export interface OverlapClaim { readonly granularity: Granularity; readonly anchorBlob: Oid | null; readonly ranges: Ranges; readonly tombstones: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly anchorBlob: Oid; readonly ranges: Ranges }>; }
/** Steps 1–5: file granularity → overlap; C1's ranges and tombstones mapped from their anchor blobs to C2's with FoldMapper/1 'fold' mode; WHOLE_FILE → overlap; closed-interval intersection → overlap; an absent-path C2 has no ranges; no parse, regionSpec, label, diff, clock or time cap. */
export function foldOverlap(c1: OverlapClaim, c2: OverlapClaim, lines: (blob: Oid) => readonly Buffer[]): boolean;

// src/regions/foldBatch.ts (R4-26) — Task 8
/** One 'fold_overlap' worker job per board commit (bg priority, no deadline); a missing blob → { missing }. */
export function foldOverlapBatch(s: RegionService, pairs: ReadonlyArray<{ c1: OverlapClaim; c2: OverlapClaim }>): Promise<boolean[] | { missing: Oid }>;
```

### Board, validators, data

```ts
// src/board/ops.ts (changed; glossary §7, PRO-05, PRO-17, REG-08, SEC-35; R4-25) — Task 5 declares ClaimLabel (type only), Task 8 the rest
export interface ClaimLabel { kind: string; qualname: string; simple: string; type: string; disc: string; }
export type ReleaseReason = 'explicit' | 'answer' | 'save' | 'seat_end' | 'stop' | 'reservation_transfer';   // 'superseded' is fold-derived only
export const CLAIM_RELEASE_REASONS: ReadonlySet<ReleaseReason>;
export interface ClaimOp {
  id: string; type: 'claim';
  claim_id: string; path: string; anchor: string; base_blob: string | null;
  ranges: Array<[number, number]>; tombstones: Array<{ anchor: string; ranges: Array<[number, number]> }>;
  granularity: 'symbol' | 'block' | 'file'; label: ClaimLabel; regionSpec: string; cs_id: string;
  holder: { peer: string; seat: string; agent: string }; reserved_for_req?: string;
}
export interface ReleaseOp { id: string; type: 'release'; claim_ids: string[]; reason: ReleaseReason; }
// OpaqueOp.type loses 'claim' | 'release'; Op gains ClaimOp | ReleaseOp; OpDraft follows; PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES gains 'claim' and 'release'.
/** 04 §3.5 absent-path form: base_blob null ⇔ ranges [] ∧ tombstones non-empty. */
export function isAbsentPathForm(op: Pick<ClaimOp, 'base_blob' | 'ranges' | 'tombstones'>): boolean;

// src/board/fold.ts (changed; 02 §3.3, §3.4, §3.6; PRO-17, PRO-19, PRO-23, PRO-24, PRO-27, PRO-30; REG-23; R4-06, R4-26, R4-28, R4-29) — Task 5 declares ClaimGrant (type only), Task 8 the rest
export interface ClaimGrant { req_id: string; grantee: { peer: string; epoch: string; seat: string }; cs_id: string; since_height: number; }
/** FoldState.claims[claim_id] — 02 §3.3 verbatim. */
export interface ClaimRec {
  path: string; anchor: string; base_blob: string | null; ranges: Array<[number, number]>; tombstones: Array<{ anchor: string; ranges: Array<[number, number]> }>;
  granularity: 'symbol' | 'block' | 'file'; label: ClaimLabel; regionSpec: string; cs_id: string;
  holder: { peer: string; epoch: string; seat: string; agent: string }; op_id: string;
  state: 'HELD' | 'STALE' | 'RELEASED'; release_reason?: ReleaseReason | 'superseded'; terminal_height?: number;
  reserved_for_req?: string; grant?: ClaimGrant;
}
// FoldState.claims: Record<string, ClaimRec> (was Record<string, Json>); record_count counts claim records (PRO-06); parseFold validates them.
/** R4-26: what one board commit's claim ops need from outside the fold (a superset); pure. c1 = a folded claim_id or an earlier op id of the same commit; c2 = an op id. */
export interface ClaimFoldNeeds { readonly trees: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly commit: string; readonly path: string }>; readonly pairs: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly c1: string; readonly c2: string }>; }
export function claimFoldNeeds(prev: FoldState, c: BoardDraft): ClaimFoldNeeds;
export interface ClaimFoldInputs { treeEntry(commit: string, path: string): string | 'ABSENT'; overlaps(c1: string, c2: string): boolean; }
export interface ClaimInputsResolver { resolve(prev: FoldState, c: BoardDraft, needs: ClaimFoldNeeds): Promise<ClaimFoldInputs>; }
export class FoldObjectMissingError extends Error { readonly oid: string; }
/** 02 §3.3 7-step algorithm; inputs absent ⇒ every claim effect skipped as in Plan 2 (claim/release verdict 'unfolded'; join/leave/seat_end/save leave claims untouched). Pure, total, never throws. */
export function foldCommit(prev: FoldState, c: BoardDraft, self: PeerId, inputs?: ClaimFoldInputs): FoldStepResult;
export type OwnRejectionReason = 'generic' | 'type' | 'prev_rev' | 'validator' | 'held_subtree' | 'local_only_stub' | 'claim_overlap' | 'claim_invalid';
/** The three claim fields are optional and set only on claim rejections, so every non-claim rejection keeps exactly Plan 2's and Plan 3's shape (R4-28): claimId on every
 *  claim_overlap and claim_invalid rejection (and on a generic rejection of a claim op), overlaps only on claim_overlap, claimReason exactly when reason === 'claim_invalid';
 *  claimReason is CLAIM_REJECTED's reason param (RegionCodeParams). PRO-17's checks run in this order and the first
 *  failure names it: holder a live seat of the writer's active epoch ('validator'; a reserved_for_req op without its answer → 'reservation'), anchor == live_code ('anchor'),
 *  base_blob and the absent-path form against treeEntry(liveCode, path) ('base_blob'), every tombstone anchor's tree holds path (SEC-35 tree part, 'validator'), the claim_id
 *  is new or the same holder's ('validator'), the record cap for a new id ('validator'), then foldOverlap (reason 'claim_overlap'). An own claim line that fails parseOpLine
 *  but carries an own op id and a claim_id passing validId('claim') is claim_invalid with 'ranges' when its ranges or a tombstone's ranges is the malformed field, else
 *  'validator'; any other malformed own line keeps Plan 2's 'type'. */
export interface OwnRejection {
  opId: string; type: OpType | 'unknown'; reason: OwnRejectionReason; holdId: string | null; foldedRev: number | null;
  claimId?: string; overlaps?: string; claimReason?: 'anchor' | 'base_blob' | 'ranges' | 'validator' | 'reservation';
}
/** PRO-06 classification (R4-30, R2-L18): true iff s.claims[claimId] is undefined (a never-seen or compacted id); Task 14's outbox withhold uses it. */
export function claimCreatesRecord(s: FoldState, claimId: string): boolean;
/** PRO-19 fold helpers Plan 6 calls from the answer/request_status/presume_absent effects (R4-06; with endGrant, releaseClaimsByAnswer and releaseStaleClaims, X-25 — the one
 *  set: Plan 6 writes no mutating copy); pure, each returns a new state. Verify pass V-10 (Plan 6's O-9): the returned state differs from s only in `claims` (every
 *  other member is the same reference as in s; unchanged input → s itself), so Plan 6's in-place fold steps adopt it as `s.claims = helper(s, …).claims`. */
export function applyGrant(s: FoldState, claimIds: readonly string[], g: ClaimGrant): FoldState;
export type GrantEndCause =
  | { kind: 'landed'; peer: string; csId: string }               // (a) Cowork-CS: <cs_id> state=closed by the grantee's peer (fold step 4)
  | { kind: 'returned'; reqId: string }                           // (b) request_status{returned} (Plan 6)
  | { kind: 'seat_end'; peer: string; epoch: string; seat: string } | { kind: 'epoch_end'; peer: string; epoch: string }   // (c)
  | { kind: 'released'; claimId: string };                        // (d)
export function endGrants(s: FoldState, cause: GrantEndCause): FoldState;
/** X-25 (for Plan 6; Plan 6 reviewer): ends the one grant whose req_id is reqId (a returned request, a landed grantee CS) — endGrants ends grants in bulk by cause.
 *  height is the fold height of the ending op; no claim's state changes; unknown reqId → s unchanged. */
export function endGrant(s: FoldState, reqId: string, cause: GrantEndCause, height: number): FoldState;
/** X-25: answer{release}'s fold effect (02 §3.6): each listed claim that is HELD or STALE and held by holderSeat becomes RELEASED with release_reason 'answer' and
 *  terminal_height = height, its grant ended (end (d)); other listed ids are skipped. */
export function releaseClaimsByAnswer(s: FoldState, claimIds: readonly string[], holderSeat: { peer: string; epoch: string; seat: string }, height: number): FoldState;
/** X-25: the STALE claims of (peer, epoch) become RELEASED with no release_reason and terminal_height = height (as a rejoin of an absent epoch does, Task 8 Notes). */
export function releaseStaleClaims(s: FoldState, peer: string, epoch: string, height: number): FoldState;
/** 02 §3.6: valid iff reserved_for_req = r, an answer{release} on request r by the same writer was folded earlier in the same commit, the writer is r's to_holder peer, the holder is r's from seat, and the ordinary overlap test passes. */
export function reservationValid(s: FoldState, op: ClaimOp, writer: WriterId, earlierInCommit: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly reqId: string; readonly kind: 'release'; readonly writerPeer: string }>): boolean;
/** PRO-36 (Plan 6's presume_absent): the (peer, epoch)'s HELD claims become STALE; grants to it end. */
export function staleClaims(s: FoldState, peer: string, epoch: string): FoldState;
// class Folder (changed): constructor(repo: GitRepo, self: PeerId, restored?: FoldVerification | null, claimInputs?: ClaimInputsResolver);
//   foldTo resolves claimFoldNeeds per commit through claimInputs and always passes the inputs when claimInputs is set; a FoldObjectMissingError is thrown and the call
//     returns nothing: the caller keeps its previously persisted fold (no commit at or after the one that needed the object is folded) and the next foldTo after a fetch
//     starts from the same place (R4-26); src/engine/core.ts's prepareIteration catches it and returns { ok: false, why: 'fold_object_missing', oid } (R4-76, X-31, Task 8);
//   takeClaimRejections(): OwnRejection[]   — own claim-op rejections since the last call, oldest first (R4-28);
//   claimOrder(): ReadonlyMap<string, { height: number; line: number }>   — R4-29: the op that last made the record HELD (a re-anchor keeps it); line = the 0-based index into
//     BoardDraft.opsLines (as 8a's OpVerdict.line); a claim present in the state foldTo starts from (a checkpoint or the persisted state) gets that state's height and
//     line = its rank among those claims in code-point order of claim_id.

// src/board/outbox.ts (changed; PRO-06, PRO-09, PRO-31; R4-30…R4-32) — Task 14
// class Outbox gains:
//   addClaimSlot(claimId: string, o?: { initiates?: boolean }): void;   // idempotent; one slot per claim_id. initiates (default true): the slot makes pendingAllowed true
//                                                                     // where claim ops are allowed — only seatEffectStep's slots for a new or changed draft on a unit present in
//                                                                     // TIP (R4-35); emitStep's build-time slots (re-anchor (a), drift (b), a local-only draft whose unit is now in
//                                                                     // the live code) pass initiates: false
//   claimSlots(): readonly string[];
//   fillClaim(claimId: string, content: Omit<ClaimOp, 'id'>): void;   // EMIT's content for the intent being built; an allocated id is kept (PRO-09)
//   clearClaimFills(): void;                                      // at the start of every intent build
//   dropClaim(claimId: string): void;                             // unfills the slot for the intent being built and keeps it (a failed drift re-anchor is retried at the next push, R4-32)
//   removeClaimSlot(claimId: string): void;                       // forgets the slot: the draft was discarded, its op landed, or the claim was rejected
//   allocateClaimSlot(claimId: string): string;                   // verify pass V-10 (Plan 6's O-1): the slot's op id now — allocated from the SeqAllocator as take() would at the
//                                                                     // first placement (PRO-09) when the slot has none, else the kept id; idempotent; an allocated unfilled slot is a
//                                                                     // state landed() already renumbers (R4-32), and Plan 6 Task 1's addHead/onRenumber report it like a filled one
//                                                                     // (X-26); not persisted (toJson omits slots, R4-31): after a restart the slot allocates afresh (a display-only cid
//                                                                     // change, R6-13's cost). Plan 6's K1 sibling cid = conflictCid(allocateClaimSlot(sibling claim id), creation op id).
// Verify pass V-10 (Plan 6's O-3): Plan 6 Task 1's Outbox.addGroup (Plan 6's addition, after this task, X-26) takes claim-slot members by claim id; take() places a
//   group whole only in a build where each of its slots is filled (EMIT fills a reservation claim like any slot); an unfilled slot holds the whole group to the
//   next build; R6-30's rule (a push state that forbids claim ops builds the group without the slot) is Plan 6's. Plan 4 reviews that edit.
//   take(...) places only filled slots; landed(...) removes a landed slot and renumbers an allocated slot not in the landed intent like any op; toJson() omits every slot (R4-31);
//   pendingAllowed(...) counts only initiating slots. emitStep removes, at each build, every non-initiating slot without an allocated id that computeClaims did not fill.
// The record-creating withhold predicate (Plan 3) also returns true for a filled claim slot whose claim_id has no folded record (claimCreatesRecord, R4-30); Plan 3's
// mirrorSync (src/engine/publish.ts) starts its BOARD_FULL episode at the first withheld record-creating draft, isHoldCreation(d) or a new claim id (Task 14).
```

```ts
// src/holds/ports.ts (changed; additive; R4-37…R4-39, R4-50) — Plan 3 Task 10 declares findings? and entries? with its own element types (X-07, X-08);
// Task 5 narrows those two element types (assignable to Plan 3's) and adds the rest below; Tasks 9, 10 and 12 bind
import type { EditCheckEntry, EditCheckReason } from '../regions/editCheck';   // type-only, erased at build: the one src/regions import L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS allows here
export type { EditCheckEntry, EditCheckReason };                                // re-exported so src/holds consumers name them without a src/regions import
// Plan 3 Task 10's declaration (X-08): entries?: ReadonlyArray<{ check?: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path: string; line?: number; reason: string }> on fail and incomplete;
// failed stays the check numbers (Plan 5 reads entries, never a re-typed failed). Task 5 narrows the element to EditCheckEntry (reason: the closed EditCheckReason):
export type EditCheckVerdict =
  | { verdict: 'pass' }
  | { verdict: 'fail'; failed: ReadonlyArray<1 | 2 | 3 | 4>; entries?: readonly EditCheckEntry[] }
  | { verdict: 'incomplete'; entries?: readonly EditCheckEntry[] };
export interface EditCheckOpts { csId?: string; mode?: 'checkpoint' | 'predict'; }
export interface EditCheckPort { check(repo: GitRepo, a: Oid, r: Oid, tip: Oid, csPaths: readonly string[], opts?: EditCheckOpts): Promise<EditCheckVerdict>; }
/** F14 §4 numbering of T0 findings (FEAT-F14-07). Plan 3 Task 10 declares findings?: ReadonlyArray<{ check: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path: string; line?: number }> (X-07; its T0 drop
 *  writes last_check's stale slot from findings[0]); Task 5 narrows the element to T0Finding (+ reason, symbol?), which stays assignable. Plan 3 imports nothing from src/regions for it. */
export interface T0Finding { check: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4; path: string; line?: number; reason: EditCheckReason; symbol?: { qualname: string; simple: string }; }
export type T0Verdict = { pass: true } | { pass: false; paths: string[]; item: 1 | 2 | 3; findings?: readonly T0Finding[] } | { incomplete: true };
export interface T0Ctx { a: Oid; p: Oid; csIds: readonly string[]; }
export interface T0RegionsPort { check(repo: GitRepo, tip: Oid, out: Oid, paths: readonly string[], ctx?: T0Ctx): Promise<T0Verdict>; }
export interface GateBlockDetail {
  blocked: ReadonlyArray<{ csId: string; claimId: string; holder: { peer: string; seat: string }; path: string; reason: string }>;
  admitted: ReadonlyArray<{ csId: string; claimId: string; path: string }>;
}
export interface PublishGatePort {
  gate(repo: GitRepo, tip: Oid, A: TreeMap, R: TreeMap, paths: readonly string[], csIds: readonly string[]): Promise<{ block: null } | { block: { claimId: string; claimIds?: readonly string[]; detail?: GateBlockDetail } }>;
  claimStillBlocks(r: HoldRecord): Promise<boolean>;
}
export interface SymbolPort {
  referencesHeldOnly(repo: GitRepo, held: HoldRecord, csR: TreeMap, A: TreeMap): Promise<boolean>;
  shadowDependency?(repo: GitRepo, shadow: HoldRecord, csA: TreeMap, csR: TreeMap): Promise<boolean>;   // HLD-48 bullets 1–2; Plan 5 wires it (R4-50)
}
// src/holds/mask.ts (changed; HLD-21; R4-17) — Task 5: the held-range rows come from src/regions/mapper.ts hunks(); nothing else changes.

// last_check (glossary §29.8) is Plan 3's (X-05, X-06): src/holds/lastCheck.ts (LastCheckKind, SkipReason, LastCheck {edit_n; kind; check?; path?; line?; skip?; more},
// LastCheckStore {record, get, pass, clearCs, noteCandidate, recordFor, passedFor, sharedFor}), one memory-only instance ChangeSets.lastCheck, created and written by
// Plan 3 Task 10 (settleCandidate from this port's entries/failed, the T0 drop from findings[0]) and by Plan 5 (skips through recordFor). Plan 4 declares, creates and
```
