### Claims

```ts
// src/claims/types.ts (glossary §3 claim view; 04 §2, §3.1, §3.7, §3.11) — Task 5
export interface SeatKey { readonly peer: PeerId; readonly seat: string; }   // seat = a seat_id (32 hex) or 'human'
export type ClaimSource =
  | { readonly kind: 'folded'; readonly anchor: Oid; readonly anchorBlob: Oid | null; readonly ranges: Ranges; readonly tombstones: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly anchor: Oid; readonly anchorBlob: Oid; readonly ranges: Ranges }> }
  | { readonly kind: 'tentative'; readonly srcBlob: BlobRef; readonly srcRanges: Ranges };
export interface ViewClaim {
  readonly claimId: string; readonly path: string; readonly holder: SeatKey & { readonly epoch: string; readonly agent: string };
  readonly granularity: Granularity; readonly label: ClaimLabel; readonly regionSpec: string; readonly csId: string; readonly source: ClaimSource;
  readonly order: { readonly tier: 0 | 1; readonly a: number; readonly b: number };   // claim order: tier 0 folded (height, line), tier 1 TENTATIVE (local seq, 0) (R4-29)
  readonly grant: ClaimGrant | null; readonly reservedForReq: string | null;
}
/** HELD non-STALE folded claims + this daemon's confirmed TENTATIVE claims in local order + active go-first grants (on the records). */
export interface ClaimView { readonly tip: Oid; readonly claims: readonly ViewClaim[]; }
export interface Draft { readonly claimId: string; readonly seat: SeatKey; readonly agent: string; readonly csId: string; readonly path: string; readonly srcBlob: BlobRef; readonly srcRanges: Ranges; readonly granularity: Granularity; readonly label: ClaimLabel; readonly confirmSeq: number; }
export interface TurnContext { readonly origin: 'prompt' | 'wake'; readonly firstConfirmedInTurn: ReadonlySet<string>; }
/** One confirmed tool effect (CONFIRM input): pre = the fence/TXN pre-image; post = one stable read; secondRead = the confirming read CONFIRM step 1 needs for an empty or > 50 % shorter post-image. */
export interface SeatEffect { readonly seat: string; readonly agent: string; readonly csId: string; readonly path: string; readonly pre: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'; readonly post: DiskSnapshotId | 'unstable' | 'ABSENT'; readonly secondRead?: DiskSnapshotId; readonly turn: TurnContext; readonly viewAtOpen?: ClaimView; }
export interface ConfirmResult { readonly status: 'confirmed' | 'deferred'; readonly hunksInBase: readonly Hunk[]; readonly touches: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly holder: SeatKey; readonly reason: TouchReason }>; readonly drafts: readonly Draft[]; readonly notOwnContained: readonly Hunk[]; }
export type PredictTool = 'Edit' | 'MultiEdit' | 'Write' | 'NotebookEdit';
export interface PredictQuery { readonly seat: string; readonly agent: string; readonly tool: PredictTool; readonly input: Json; readonly path: string; readonly disk: DiskSnapshotId | 'unstable'; readonly viewBlob: BlobRef | null; readonly turn: TurnContext; }
export interface PredictResult {
  readonly status: 'located' | 'locate_failed' | 'undecided'; readonly diskLines: Ranges; readonly hunksInBase: readonly Hunk[];
  readonly touches: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly reason: TouchReason }>;
  readonly moved: { readonly fromLabel: Label; readonly toLabel: Label | 'TOMBSTONE' } | null; readonly fallbackUsed: boolean;
  readonly ownTouches: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly suspect: boolean; readonly granted: boolean }>; readonly ownContained: boolean;
}
/** reason 'claim_overlap': a CS that store.blockedBy names (an own claim op folded invalid by overlap, R4-28), reported by regionGate with no touching hunk. */
export interface GateBlocked { readonly csId: string; readonly claimId: string; readonly holder: SeatKey; readonly path: string; readonly reason: TouchReason | 'claim_overlap'; }
export interface GateAdmitted { readonly csId: string; readonly claimId: string; readonly path: string; }
export interface GateResult { readonly blocked: readonly GateBlocked[]; readonly admitted: readonly GateAdmitted[]; }
/** holdId (X-28; Task 15 fills it): the id of the K4 record this apply gave guard 'claim-breach' on path (D8 step 4's tie-break on the same rows), else null. */
export interface BreachHit { readonly claimId: string; readonly holder: SeatKey; readonly commit: Oid; readonly path: string; readonly baseMove: boolean; readonly holdId: string | null; }
export interface PlacementFailure { readonly path: string; readonly sub: 'a' | 'b'; readonly reason: 'label_mismatch' | 'tombstone' | 'whole_file' | 'fence'; readonly claimId: string | null; }

// src/claims/host.ts (R4-36) — Task 5
/** What the port bindings and steps read from the engine core (adapter: src/engine/claimHost.ts). */
export interface ClaimHostView {
  readonly repo: GitRepo; readonly regions: RegionService; readonly store: ClaimStore; readonly self: PeerId; readonly epoch: string; readonly regionSpec: string;
  fold(): { readonly tip: Oid; readonly state: FoldState; readonly order: ReadonlyMap<string, { height: number; line: number }> } | null;   // the fold at the last leased tip
  view(): ClaimView | null;   // claimView(fold(), store)
  scope(path: string): Scope; epochBase(): Oid; tipMap(): TreeMap; localSeats(): ReadonlySet<string>;
  peerDisplay(peer: PeerId): string;
  readonly attribution: HunkAttributionPort; readonly suspect: SuspectPort; readonly breach: BreachPort;
  readonly flags: { readonly perPcForeignness: boolean; readonly matchByLabel: boolean; readonly skipChecks: ReadonlySet<1 | 2 | 3 | 4> };   // M21, M22, M46_n (test builds)
  raise<C extends RegionCode>(code: C, params: RegionCodeParams[C]): void;
  readonly trace: Trace; readonly log: Log;
}
/** R4-36: outlives any single core. Every createEngineCore over the same deps calls attach with its own view, replacing the previous one (Plan 2's restartCore, a realEngine
 *  RESTART, Task 16's upgrades): the last core created wins and get() returns its view; the old core's view is no longer reachable. get() before the first attach throws. */
export class RegionHost { attach(v: ClaimHostView): void; get(): ClaimHostView; readonly attached: boolean; }

// src/claims/store.ts (04 §3.3 pins, §3.5 drafts; REG-21, REG-35; R4-24, R4-28, R4-31) — Task 5 (skeleton), Task 11 (drafts, deferrals), Task 14 (emission)
export function newClaimId(r: Random): string;   // RFC 4648 base32 lowercase of 10 random bytes (R4-24)
export class ClaimStore {
  constructor(snapshots: SnapshotStore, cache: TableCache, random: Random);
  newClaimId(): string;
  drafts(seat?: SeatKey): readonly Draft[];            // confirmed TENTATIVE, local confirmation order
  putDraft(d: Omit<Draft, 'confirmSeq'>): Draft;      // pins d.srcBlob under owner d.claimId; replaces a draft with the same claimId keeping its confirmSeq (claim order follows the first confirmation, R4-29); a new draft takes the next sequence number
  discard(claimId: string): void;                      // unpins
  emitted(claimId: string): void;                      // its op landed: the folded record takes over; unpins
  blockCs(csId: string, overlaps: string): void; blockedBy(csId: string): string | null; unblockWhere(pred: (overlaps: string) => boolean): void;   // R4-28, EMIT step 5
  deferConfirm(e: SeatEffect): void; deferred(csId: string): readonly SeatEffect[]; clearDeferred(csId: string, path: string): void;   // CONFIRM step 1
  markRejected(claimId: string, reason: string): boolean;   // true the first time per (claim_id, reason) (PRO-38)
}

// src/claims/view.ts (glossary §3; 04 §3.7 step 2; REG-04, REG-12, REG-21; F05) — Task 11
export function claimView(fold: NonNullable<ReturnType<ClaimHostView['fold']>>, store: ClaimStore): ClaimView;
/** FEAT-F05-01: the reader seat's view — holder seat ≠ reader, no STALE claim, no claim the reader holds an active grant on. */
export function claimViewOf(v: ClaimView, reader: SeatKey): readonly ViewClaim[];
/** The claim (ranges and tombstones) mapped into base by REG-07 local mode (TOMBSTONE images dropped; any WHOLE_FILE → WHOLE_FILE); regionSpec ≠ own or granularity file → WHOLE_FILE (touch step 1); matchByLabel (M22) finds the unit by label in base's table instead. */
export function mapClaimInto(s: RegionService, c: ViewClaim, base: BlobRef | 'ABSENT', path: string, ownRegionSpec: string, matchByLabel: boolean): Promise<Ranges | 'WHOLE_FILE'>;
export function isEarlier(a: ViewClaim, b: ViewClaim): boolean;   // claim order (04 §2, R4-29)
/** REG-21's "later in claim order": seat Y is later than a confirmed TENTATIVE sibling claim c iff no claim of Y in v on c's path that is earlier than c (isEarlier) intersects c,
 *  both taken from mapped (claim_id → ranges in the decision base, as mapClaimInto returned them; WHOLE_FILE intersects everything). Used by predict step 7 and by gate. */
export function seatIsLater(seat: SeatKey, c: ViewClaim, v: ClaimView, mapped: ReadonlyMap<string, Ranges | 'WHOLE_FILE'>): boolean;

// src/claims/overlapPairs.ts (R4-26, R4-27 as decided by the owner on 2026-10-08) — Task 8. Imports types of src/board/fold.ts and src/regions/foldOverlap.ts
// only, so the viewer bundle (8a Task 20) carries no worker pool, region service, cache or grammar runtime.
/** The pairs foldCommit may ask about one board commit, as OverlapClaims: c1 a folded claim_id or an earlier op id of the commit, c2 an op id (needs.pairs);
 *  anchors from prev.claims / the ops, tombstone anchor blobs from `treeEntry`. `isAncestor(anchor)` false → that tombstone's entry is 'ABSENT' (contract
 *  addition 3). Pure; no I/O. */
export function overlapPairsOf(prev: FoldState, c: BoardDraft, needs: ClaimFoldNeeds, treeEntry: (commit: string, path: string) => string | 'ABSENT',
  isAncestor: (anchor: string) => boolean): ReadonlyArray<{ c1: string; c2: string; a: OverlapClaim; b: OverlapClaim }>;

// src/claims/foldInputs.ts (R4-26) — Task 8
/** One readTreeMap per distinct commit, one readBlobs per board commit, one 'fold_overlap' job (bg, no deadline). treeEntry(anchor, path) = 'ABSENT' for a tombstone anchor
 *  that is not an ancestor of the carrying commit's live code (GitRepo.isAncestor, deterministic on every peer), so a forged anchor folds invalid instead of stalling every
 *  fold; FoldObjectMissingError {oid} is thrown only for an object the live chain reaches that is missing locally. */
export function claimFoldResolver(repo: GitRepo, regions: RegionService): ClaimInputsResolver;

// src/claims/confirm.ts (04 §3.11 CONFIRM, §3.5 Algorithm CLAIM; REG-15, REG-16, REG-18, REG-21, REG-29, REG-33, REG-35) — Task 11
export interface ClaimCtx { readonly host: ClaimHostView; readonly view: ClaimView; readonly A: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'; readonly own: SeatKey; }
/** Step 1 (stable or confirmed post-image, else 'deferred'), steps 2–3 (hunks of A[p] → post intersecting the tool lines; EOL/BOM-only gives none), step 4 (K2 touches against foreign claims of the view recorded at PreToolUse/TXN open), step 5 (drafts), notOwnContained for a wake turn.
 *  The own-claim set O of a wake turn (REG-33; also predict's ownContained) is computed from e.viewAtOpen (q's view at the Pre or TXN open; the current view when absent):
 *  this seat's HELD non-STALE and confirmed TENTATIVE claims on p, minus turn.firstConfirmedInTurn, tombstones excluded; ownContained/notOwnContained are computed only when
 *  turn.origin === 'wake' (ownContained: false, notOwnContained: [] otherwise). */
export function confirm(e: SeatEffect, ctx: ClaimCtx): Promise<ConfirmResult>;
/** Algorithm CLAIM steps 2–5: leaf units containing the post-image lines (deletion point, base unit), residue, gap blocks, nothing for <imports> or blank-only hunks, last-good table under error spans (else the in-between span as block, else file), grant drop, reuse of the seat's intersecting claim_id, a draft overlapping an earlier sibling TENTATIVE claim not recorded (reported as a touch). */
export function draftClaims(hunksInBase: readonly Hunk[], post: BlobRef, postTable: TableOrNone, ctx: ClaimCtx, csId: string, agent: string): Promise<{ drafts: Draft[]; localTouches: ConfirmResult['touches'] }>;

// src/claims/predict.ts (04 §3.11 PREDICT; REG-28, REG-33; 04 §8 Q4) — Task 11
/** Steps 0–8; step 4 of touch waits ≤ T_predict (10 ms) for an incremental re-parse of V1, else 'undecided'; ownContained false on locate_failed, undecided or fallbackUsed.
 *  R4-71: the whole call runs under ctx.host.regions.deps.budgets.deadline('gate') (BUDGETS.gateMs, 15 ms, the daemon-side gate latency); on expiry it answers
 *  {status: 'undecided', diskLines: [], hunksInBase: [], touches: [], moved: null, fallbackUsed: false, ownTouches: [], ownContained: false} (G2: CONFIRM decides) and calls
 *  ctx.host.regions.deps.onBudget(q.path, 'gate') (→ REGION_BUDGET, deduplicated by raiseRegionCode). */
export function predict(q: PredictQuery, ctx: ClaimCtx): Promise<PredictResult>;
/** Step 2: BOM strip, CRLF → LF, U+2018/2019 → ', U+201C/201D → " — only here, converted to line numbers at once (REG-01). */
export function normalizeForLocate(text: string): string;
/** Step 8's regex region (Python, JS/TS, C#); a FallbackRegion can never reach computeClaims (REG-03). */
export interface FallbackRegion { readonly kind: 'fallback'; readonly range: Range; }
export function fallbackRegion(lang: Language, rows: readonly Buffer[], line: number): FallbackRegion | null;

// src/claims/emit.ts (04 §3.5 EMIT, Re-anchor, Tombstones; REG-09…REG-11, REG-22, REG-32; PRO-31; R4-07, R4-32, R4-35) — Task 14
export interface EmitInput { readonly host: ClaimHostView; readonly liveCode: Oid; readonly liveTree: TreeMap; readonly changedPaths: ReadonlySet<string>; readonly state: FoldState; }
export interface EmitPlan { readonly fill: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly content: Omit<ClaimOp, 'id'> }>; readonly localOnly: readonly string[]; readonly drift: readonly string[]; }
/** 04 §3.1 computeClaims for every draft and every own HELD claim on a path this push changes (case a) or past D_reanchor (case b, image only): one direct fold-mode mapping to T = tree(liveCode)[p]; local-only on TOMBSTONE or an absent unit; snap to T's leaf units (last-good under error spans); [[1, lineCount]] for file; all-deleted ranges → tombstones; the absent-path form; never onto a non-LIVE path. */
export function computeClaims(inp: EmitInput): Promise<EmitPlan>;
/** REG-22 at the leased tip: each op against the folded active claims of other holder seats with the fold's own foldOverlap rule. */
export function claimPrecheck(ops: ReadonlyArray<Omit<ClaimOp, 'id'>>, state: FoldState, host: ClaimHostView): Promise<Array<{ claimId: string; csId: string; overlaps: string }>>;
/** PRO-32 (a)–(b) as a predicate (R4-07). */
export function saveReleaseDue(c: ClaimRec, landedAtOrBefore: (csId: string) => boolean, seatHasOpenCsOn: (seat: string, path: string) => boolean): boolean;

// src/claims/attribution.ts (REG-19; R4-05) — Task 5 declares HunkAttributionPort (type only), Task 12 adds csAttribution
export interface HunkAttributionPort { seatsOf(csIds: readonly string[], path: string, hunk: Hunk, self: PeerId): readonly SeatKey[]; }
export const csAttribution: HunkAttributionPort;   // seat CS → its seat; human and recovered CS → the peer's human

// src/claims/gate.ts (04 §3.7 Algorithm GATE; REG-19…REG-21, INV-REG-FENCE; M21) — Task 12
/** REG-19: foreign unless every seat in S(h) is c's holder seat; hunks attributed only to this peer's human are not foreign to claims of this daemon's seats; another seat of the same PC is foreign; perPcForeignness = M21. */
export function isForeign(c: ViewClaim, seats: readonly SeatKey[], self: PeerId, localSeats: ReadonlySet<string>, perPcForeignness: boolean): boolean;
export interface GateGroup { readonly csIds: readonly string[]; readonly paths: readonly string[]; readonly A: TreeView; readonly R: TreeView; }
/** Steps 1–4 for every path with R[p] ≠ A[p]: hunks(A[p], R[p]) and regionTable(R[p]); each claim foreign to some hunk mapped into A[p]; touch per hunk; an active grant admits only for the grantee seat's cs_id; a TENTATIVE sibling claim blocks only later seats; never returns hunks; never reads the registry. */
export function gate(host: ClaimHostView, view: ClaimView, groups: readonly GateGroup[]): Promise<GateResult>;
/** R4-37's tip assertion: thrown by regionGate(host).gate when tip !== host.fold()?.tip (also with no fold yet); Plan 3's publishStep lets it end the window — nothing is pushed
 *  and the next iteration folds the new tip and gates again (fail closed, never a pass). */
export class GateTipError extends Error { readonly tip: Oid; readonly folded: Oid | null; }
/** R4-37. Also reports every CS of the group that host.store.blockedBy(csId) names (an own claim op folded invalid by overlap, R4-28) as a detail.blocked entry
 *  {csId, claimId: <overlaps>, holder: <that claim's holder>, path: <the CS's first path>, reason: 'claim_overlap'}, so the group is PINned even when no hunk touches.
 *  Every gate call emits one 'gate' trace event. */
export function regionGate(host: RegionHost): PublishGatePort;

// src/claims/revertPredict.ts (05 REV step 6; REG-35 and REG-36 F10 clauses; R4-46) — Task 5 declares RevertPrediction (type only), Task 12 adds predictRevert
export interface RevertPrediction { readonly wouldHold: readonly GateBlocked[]; readonly wouldBreak: readonly EditCheckEntry[]; }
/** Runs gate with a host view whose attribution.seatsOf answers [{peer: self, seat: 'human'}] for every result hunk and one synthetic group {csIds: ['<self>/human/0'],
 *  paths, A: view(tip), R: view(tip, overlay)}, where overlay holds each result buffer registered in SnapshotStore as a DiskSnapshotId (oid by hashRouteC(repo, bytes, false),
 *  released when the call returns) and 'ABSENT' for a deletion; then editCheck in mode 'predict' with M' = R. Emits no trace event (only regionGate(host).gate emits 'gate'),
 *  writes no object, record, last_check or capture; a tip other than the folded one rejects with GateTipError. */
export function predictRevert(host: ClaimHostView, results: ReadonlyMap<string, Buffer | 'ABSENT'>, tip: Oid): Promise<RevertPrediction>;

// src/claims/placement.ts (04 §3.8; REG-24, ENG-29; R4-48) — Task 5 declares PlacementInput (type only), Task 15 adds placementCheck
export interface PlacementInput { readonly base: TreeView; readonly ours: TreeView; readonly theirs: TreeView; readonly out: TreeView; readonly paths: readonly string[]; readonly fence: { readonly gate: GateResult; readonly view: ClaimView; readonly publishingSeats: readonly SeatKey[] } | null; }
/** (a) identity on every symbol-table path both sides changed; (b) fence on every path out ≠ theirs with a foreign claim not admitted for it (publish only, fence non-null); any number of trees (base move: Plan 7). */
export function placementCheck(host: ClaimHostView, inp: PlacementInput): Promise<PlacementFailure[]>;

// src/claims/tieBreak.ts (03 D8 step 4; DAT-44; R4-47) — Task 15
/** How "LOCAL holder" and "TIP side" are read: conflict rows = the s1 rows covered by a hunk of hunks(s1, s2) and by a hunk of hunks(s1, s3); local claims = claims of the
 *  view at the lease value held by a seat of this peer (holder.peer === host.self) whose mapping into s1 touches the conflict rows; TIP-side claims = every other claim of
 *  that view touching them; 'breach' iff local claims ≠ ∅ and the earliest local claim isEarlier than every TIP-side claim; else 'k4'. */
export function claimTieBreak(host: ClaimHostView, view: ClaimView, path: string, s1: OidOrAbsent, s2: OidOrAbsent, s3: OidOrAbsent): Promise<'k4' | 'breach'>;

// src/claims/breach.ts (04 §3.16; REG-25; R4-51) — Task 5 declares BreachPort (type only), Task 15 adds the rest
export interface BreachCommit {
  readonly oid: Oid; readonly firstParent: Oid; readonly baseMove: boolean;
  readonly writerPeer: PeerId; readonly seats: readonly string[];                         // Cowork-Writer, Cowork-Seat
  readonly csPaths: ReadonlyMap<string, readonly string[]>; readonly closedCsIds: readonly string[];   // Cowork-CS-Paths, Cowork-CS state=closed
}
/** 04 §3.1 breach(commits (LA_C, TIP], ownHeldClaims): each touching hunk of hunks(C^1[p], C[p]) whose attribution for p holds no CS of the holder seat; the holder peer's human-only commits and a granted cs_id's closing commit skipped; mixed attribution reported; nothing rewound. */
export function breach(host: ClaimHostView, commits: readonly BreachCommit[], ownHeld: readonly ViewClaim[]): Promise<BreachHit[]>;
export interface BreachPort { onBreach(hits: readonly BreachHit[]): void; }
export function logBreach(log: Log, trace: Trace, peer: PeerId): BreachPort;   // one log-only CLAIM_BREACH_HIT {claim_id, holder, commit, path, base_move} line per hit (V-17)

// src/claims/labels.ts (04 §3.17 "Label verification"; REG-34, SEC-35; R4-52, R4-66) — Task 13
export class LabelVerifier {
  constructor(host: RegionHost, securityLog: Log);
  /** Schedules (bg worker, outside the fold and the gate decision) the re-derivation of each newly folded or re-anchored claim op this daemon did not compute; one line on mismatch. */
  schedule(claims: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly claimId: string; readonly opId: string; readonly rec: ClaimRec }>): void;
  /** The verified label, or null (rendered '?'): unverified, unfinished, block/file/absent-path, NO_TABLE, an error span on the first line, a missing blob; never schedules a parse. */
  verified(claimId: string, anchor: Oid): ClaimLabel | null;
  /** Resolves when no verification job is queued or running (tests and the SIM harness only; the engine never awaits it). */
  idle(): Promise<void>;
}
/** The RegionMapper of LIF-27/F12: labels of the leaf units touched by hunks(from, to) in to's table; a whole-file label for NO_TABLE paths; save priority. */
export function labelsOfChange(s: RegionService, from: OidOrAbsent, to: OidOrAbsent, path: string, epochBase: Oid): Promise<readonly Label[]>;
/** R4-19: the label of a file claim and of a path without a symbol table (F12's whole-file label). */
export const FILE_LABEL: ClaimLabel;   // { kind: 'file', qualname: '', simple: '', type: '', disc: '' }
/** X-23: the one display helper every renderer uses for a claim label (Plan 5's since_query labels, Plan 6's texts, Plan 7's show/since/wrap, F04-a):
 *  '?' for null; else l.qualname when it is non-empty and passes validLabel (R4-24: a label field failing validLabel renders '?'), else '?'. Raw text, never capped here:
 *  the renderer applies glossary §19's caps ({label} 80, {sym} = path::label 80) and its own sanitizer. Pure; reads nothing else. */
export function claimLabelText(l: ClaimLabel | null): string;
/** The label of the leaf unit of t whose ranges contain `line` (1-based stored-form row, REG-01; a blank row inside a unit's extent, e.g. between a class's members, is the
 *  unit's, R4-18), or null for NO_TABLE, a block table (grammar null), an <imports> row, a row outside every unit (top-level blank rows included), or a row covered by an
 *  ERROR/MISSING span (REG-34 step 4). Pure; used by the label_at handler and on a TableCache hit. */
export function leafLabelAt(t: TableOrNone, line: number): Label | null;
export type RegionNote =
  | { readonly code: 'REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH'; readonly params: RegionCodeParams['REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH'] }
  | { readonly code: 'REGION_WHOLE_FILE'; readonly params: RegionCodeParams['REGION_WHOLE_FILE'] }
  | { readonly code: 'REGION_BLOCK_ONLY'; readonly params: RegionCodeParams['REGION_BLOCK_ONLY'] }
  | { readonly code: 'REGION_CONFLICT_MARKERS'; readonly params: RegionCodeParams['REGION_CONFLICT_MARKERS'] };
/** The verdict data /cowork show <claim> (Plan 7) and deny/hold reason clauses (Plans 5, 6) render for one folded claim; never raised into status (04 §3.19, R4-54).
 *  In this order: REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH iff rec.regionSpec ≠ host.regionSpec (peer = host.peerDisplay(holder.peer), ≤ 64 characters raw, glossary §19);
 *  REGION_WHOLE_FILE iff the fold-mode mapping of the claim's ranges from its anchor blob to `against` is WHOLE_FILE (D > 5,000, REG-07);
 *  REGION_BLOCK_ONLY iff `against`'s path class is 5 or 6 (reason = PathClassVerdict.blockReason); REGION_CONFLICT_MARKERS iff class 1. [] for an unknown claim id or 'ABSENT'. Writes nothing. */
export function regionNotes(host: ClaimHostView, claimId: string, against: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'): Promise<RegionNote[]>;
/** 04 §3.17 / REG-30: the claim_id of a SHADOW key (seat_id, foreign claim_id, path): the earliest claim in claim order (isEarlier) among the foreign claims a denied hunk intersects; null when none. */
export function shadowClaimId(intersected: readonly ViewClaim[]): string | null;
/** Glossary §24 default 10, claim half: the seat's newest HELD claim by board position (claimOrder: highest height, then highest line); null when none. The request half (newest non-terminal request first) is Plan 6's. */
export function newestHeldClaim(state: FoldState, order: ReadonlyMap<string, { height: number; line: number }>, seat: { peer: PeerId; epoch: string; seat: string }): string | null;
/** cowork_release(claim_id | "all"), /cowork release <claim_id>, cowork_show(id): "all" → the seat's HELD claim ids in claim order; a claim id with a folded record → [it]; anything else → 'NOT_FOUND'. Never resolves a label or a symbol name (REG-12, REG-30). */
export function resolveClaimArg(state: FoldState, order: ReadonlyMap<string, { height: number; line: number }>, seat: { peer: PeerId; epoch: string; seat: string }, arg: string): readonly string[] | 'NOT_FOUND';

// src/claims/release.ts (X-22; 02 §3.5 release rows, PRO-30; REG-12, REG-30) — Task 14
/** The one release emitter (callers: Plan 5's seat_end, stop and cowork_release through CoordToolsPort; Plan 6's tools.release and Coordinator.humanRelease, mapping why
 *  to NOT_OWN_CLAIM / RELEASE_UNLANDED; Plan 7's /cowork release through Coordinator.humanRelease). core is the engine core's claim host view (claimHostOf(core)): src/claims
 *  cannot import src/engine (R4-02, X-110). target 'all' → resolveClaimArg(…, 'all') over the fold at the leased tip with holder's (peer, current epoch, seat); each listed id:
 *  a folded HELD claim of holder → released; an id that is only a local draft or a claim slot of holder with no folded record → refused 'unlanded'; any other id (another
 *  holder's claim, a terminal record, an unknown id) → refused 'not_own'. drafts = one release OpDraft {type: 'release', claim_ids (claim order), reason} when any id is
 *  released, else []; the caller adds it to the outbox (`Outbox.add`; PRO-45 decides where it may leave). Writes nothing, reads no label or name. Answer
 *  releases stay Plan 6's answer{release} op (releaseClaimsByAnswer). */
export function releaseClaims(core: ClaimHostView, holder: SeatKey, reason: ReleaseReason, target: readonly string[] | 'all'): { drafts: OpDraft[]; refused: Array<{ claimId: string; why: 'not_own' | 'unlanded' }> };

// src/claims/t0.ts (HLD-50 items 1–2; REG-24, REG-27; R4-38) — Task 9
/** Without ctx: {incomplete: true}, no job, git call or raise. With ctx: T0Verdict.paths = the sorted checked paths the findings are attributed to (a finding's own path when it
 *  is in paths, else the CS path of the breaking unit it follows from); item = 1 when any item-1 finding exists, else 2; findings sorted by (check, path, line ?? 0, reason);
 *  a finding's symbol is the {qualname, simple} of this daemon's own label for it (the duplicated key's label for label_count, the breaking unit for callers and orphans, the
 *  imported name for an unresolved import, the leaf unit containing the row for new_error_node, none for undecodable); line follows the edit check's rules on OUT (the first
 *  error span of OUT[p] not intersecting the REG-07 image of a TIP[p] span, else its first span; the call or import row; none for undecodable and label_count). Over 4× budget
 *  → {incomplete: true} and REGION_BUDGET {path, budget: 't0'} per checked path (raiseRegionCode dedupe); an interfaceCheck stopped by the caller grep's own 4× deadline
 *  (InterfaceResult.incompleteBudget === 'grep', R4-72) → {incomplete: true} and REGION_BUDGET {path, budget: 'grep'} per checked path instead. */
export function regionT0(host: RegionHost): T0RegionsPort;
/** F14 §4: item-1 error count or UTF-8 → 1, item-1 label count → 4, non-removal caller or orphan → 2, unresolved import or removal caller → 3. */
export function t0CheckNumber(kind: 'error_count' | 'undecodable' | 'label_count' | 'caller' | 'orphan' | 'import' | 'removal_caller'): 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;

// src/claims/symbols.ts (HLD-48, HLD-49; 05 §8 Q2; R4-50) — Task 9
export function regionSymbols(host: RegionHost): SymbolPort;

// src/claims/editCheckPort.ts (REG-36, HLD-67 step 2; R4-39…R4-41) — Task 10
/** M from the materialized candidate (TIP[p] = A[p] ⇒ M[p] = R[p]; else Plan 2's mergeFile into memory, nothing written, R4-40), the per-CS queue (one running, one waiting), entries in the verdict, the edit_check trace's checks.
 *  Verdict: incomplete → {verdict: 'incomplete', entries} (the entries found before the stop, [] when the job was abandoned at the deadline); else a non-empty failed →
 *  {verdict: 'fail', failed: <the distinct check numbers ascending>, entries: failed} (an entry without check makes failed possibly []); else {verdict: 'pass'}. An overrun writes
 *  one daemon-log record EDIT_CHECK_OVERRUN {cs_id} (04 §3.18's perf count; a log-only registry code of src/regions/codes.ts, X-30) and nothing else. The queue is keyed by opts.csId; a call without csId runs at once and is never
 *  queued or replaced. skipChecks = host.flags.skipChecks (the one M46 site); mode = opts.mode ?? 'checkpoint'. */
export function regionEditCheck(host: RegionHost): EditCheckPort;

// src/claims/ports.ts (R4-57; PRO-37) — Task 5 (SuspectPort), Task 16 (plan4Ports)

export const noSuspect: SuspectPort;
```

```ts
//   createEngineCore attaches deps.regions.host to claimHostOf(core) — every call, replacing a previous core's view (R4-36) — and constructs Folder with claimFoldResolver(repo, regions) (Task 8);
//   Prepared gains { ok: false; why: 'fold_object_missing'; oid: string }: prepareIteration catches FoldObjectMissingError from Folder.foldTo, sets foldMissing (keeping
//   sinceMono while the miss persists), leaves int.fold and core.leasedFold unchanged and returns it (no apply, no publish; the next fetch retries; Plan 2's iterate() logs its
//   generic ITERATION_SKIPPED {why} and needs no new case); a fold that completes clears foldMissing. Once Δmono − sinceMono > 60,000 the miss is surfaced like Plan 2's
//   push_unresolved carry: the status inputs carry not_converged cause 'fold_object_missing' (degraded) and the daemon log gets FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING {oid} (a log-only
//   registry code, X-30) at most once per 600,000 ms Δmono (loggedMono); no LOST_STATE escalation (R4-76, X-31, Task 8);
//   prepareIteration calls claimRejectionStep(core) once right after the fold sets leasedFold (the call site placed by Task 5 over the no-op stub, the body filled by Task 11 —
//   the reconciliation's within-plan ruling) and labelStep(core, outcome) once after each Folder.foldTo (Task 13).

// src/engine/claimHost.ts — Task 5
export function claimHostOf(core: EngineCore): ClaimHostView;

// src/engine/regionCodes.ts (R4-54) — Task 5
/** core.holdCode and the daemon log; REGION_BUDGET at most once per path per 30 min Δmono; CLAIM_REJECTED once per (claim_id, reason). */
export function raiseRegionCode<C extends RegionCode>(core: EngineCore, code: C, params: RegionCodeParams[C]): void;

// Stub behaviour before the owning task (Task 5 creates these step files): claimViewStep → null, claimViewOfStep → [], verifiedLabelStep → null, claimsHeld → 0,
// labelStep and claimRejectionStep → no-ops, settleDeferredConfirms → resolves, emitStep → {blocked: [], filled: []}, placementStep and breachStep → [],
// regionConflictHooks → the four hooks returning Plan 2's answers (hasGrammar false, need 0, tripwire true, tieBreak 'k4'), installed nowhere;
// seatEffectStep, predictStep and predictRevertStep reject with Error('<name> not bound before Task <n>').

// src/engine/claimsStep.ts — Task 11
/** CONFIRM + CLAIM; drafts to the store; an initiating claim slot per new or changed draft whose unit is in TIP (Task 14; a governed board-only push, R4-35).
 *  On a path whose host.scope(p).cls !== 'LIVE' it returns {status: 'confirmed', hunksInBase: [], touches: [], drafts: [], notOwnContained: []} and records nothing (REG-32). */
export function seatEffectStep(core: EngineCore, e: SeatEffect): Promise<ConfirmResult>;
/** On a non-LIVE path: {status: 'undecided', diskLines: [], hunksInBase: [], touches: [], moved: null, fallbackUsed: false, ownTouches: [], ownContained: false}, computing nothing. */
export function predictStep(core: EngineCore, q: PredictQuery): Promise<PredictResult>;
/** Once per iteration after the fold (R4-28): claim_overlap → store.blockCs(<the CS of the draft with that claim_id>, overlaps); claim_invalid → the draft stays TENTATIVE,
 *  its slot is removed (Outbox.removeClaimSlot, Task 14: the rejected op is never re-sent) and, when store.markRejected(claimId, claimReason) returns true,
 *  raiseRegionCode(core, 'CLAIM_REJECTED', {claim_id, reason: claimReason}); a generic rejection keeps Plan 2's renumbering; one 'claim' trace event {action: 'rejected'} per rejection. */
export function claimRejectionStep(core: EngineCore): void;
export function settleDeferredConfirms(core: EngineCore, csId: string, R: TreeMap): Promise<void>;   // CONFIRM step 1: a deferred confirm runs at the CS boundary with R[p] as post-image
export function predictRevertStep(core: EngineCore, results: ReadonlyMap<string, Buffer | 'ABSENT'>, tip: Oid): Promise<RevertPrediction>;   // Task 12

// src/engine/emitStep.ts — Task 14
/** Inside Algorithm P after C (or with TIP for a board-only push) and before Bnew: clearClaimFills, computeClaims, claimPrecheck; a failing new claim blocks its CS and restarts composition at step 2 without its group; a failing drift re-anchor stays unfilled. */
export function emitStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, b: { liveCode: Oid; liveTree: TreeMap; changedPaths: ReadonlySet<string> }): Promise<{ blocked: Array<{ csId: string; overlaps: string; claimId: string; path: string }>; filled: string[] }>;

// src/engine/mergeChecks.ts — Task 15
export function regionConflictHooks(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx): Required<Pick<ConflictCtx, 'hasGrammar' | 'need' | 'tripwire' | 'tieBreak'>>;
export function placementStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, use: 'apply' | 'publish', inp: PlacementInput): Promise<PlacementFailure[]>;
export function breachStep(core: EngineCore, it: IterationCtx, laC: Oid): Promise<BreachHit[]>;   // walks (LA_C, TIP] first-parent live commits, hands hits to deps.regions.breach

// src/engine/labelStep.ts — Task 13
export function labelStep(core: EngineCore, out: FoldOutcome): void;   // schedules verifications for newly folded claim ops
export function claimsHeld(state: FoldState | null, self: PeerId): number;    // R4-53; null → 0
export function claimViewStep(core: EngineCore): ClaimView | null;
export function claimViewOfStep(core: EngineCore, reader: SeatKey): readonly ViewClaim[];   // F05's reader-seat form (claimViewOf)
export function verifiedLabelStep(core: EngineCore, claimId: string, anchor: Oid): ClaimLabel | null;      // REG-34 cache read, never a parse (F04)
export function labelVerifierOf(core: EngineCore): LabelVerifier;   // one per EngineCore (module WeakMap), built from deps.regions.host and deps.regions.securityLog
export function claimNotesStep(core: EngineCore, claimId: string, against?: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'): Promise<RegionNote[]>;   // against defaults to A[p] of this peer's current state

// src/engine/loop.ts (changed) — class Engine gains (Task 5 declares, delegating to seatEffectStep, predictStep, claimViewStep, claimViewOfStep, verifiedLabelStep and predictRevertStep, filled by Tasks 11, 13 and 12; Task 13 adds claimNotes):
//   seatEffect(e: SeatEffect): Promise<ConfirmResult>;   predict(q: PredictQuery): Promise<PredictResult>;
//   claimView(): ClaimView | null;   claimViewOf(reader: SeatKey): readonly ViewClaim[];   verifiedLabel(claimId: string, anchor: Oid): ClaimLabel | null;
//   predictRevert(results: ReadonlyMap<string, Buffer | 'ABSENT'>, tip: Oid): Promise<RevertPrediction>;
//   claimNotes(claimId: string, against?: BlobRef | 'ABSENT'): Promise<RegionNote[]>;   // delegates to claimNotesStep (Plans 5–7: cowork_show, deny/hold clauses)
// EngineInspection gains claims: { view: ClaimView | null; drafts: readonly Draft[]; blocked: ReadonlyArray<{ csId: string; overlaps: string }>; order: ReadonlyMap<string, { height: number; line: number }> }.
// src/engine/status.ts — StatusInputs gains the optional claimsHeld?: number (Task 13; absent → 0, so every other Plan 2 and Plan 3 StatusInputs literal compiles unchanged);
//   computeStatus sets status.json.claims_held = i.claimsHeld ?? 0 (Plan 2 wrote 0); iterate() passes claimsHeld(core.leasedFold?.state ?? null, core.peerId) (claimsHeld(null, self) = 0).
//   Task 8 (X-31): NotConvergedCause (and NotConvergedWhy's cause-only member) gains 'fold_object_missing', which computeStatus treats as push_unresolved (degraded, also with
//   no path); iterate()'s status inputs set notConvergedCause to 'fold_object_missing' while core.foldMissing is older than 60 s and neither fetch_owed nor push_unresolved
//   applies — the one expression Plan 2's Task 14/17 carry already computes, extended by one branch (no Prepared.why case is added).

// src/engine/publish.ts (changed) — Task 15: Plan 3's T0 slot calls deps.t0Regions.check(repo, tip, outTree, paths, { a: <A tree oid>, p: <P tree oid>, csIds: <the group's cs ids> });
//   Task 12: the B1 slot's one PIN is hold(pinCs('claim:<claimIds[0]>', …)) followed by addGuard(id, 'claim:<id>') for every further claimIds entry, all before the window's
//   push (R4-74; Plan 3's API unchanged); a GateTipError ends the window. PublishReport gains (X-28) claimPins: ReadonlyArray<{ holdId: string; kind: 'K3' | 'K6';
//   claimIds: readonly string[]; csIds: readonly string[]; holders: readonly SeatKey[]; detail: GateBlockDetail }> — one entry per PIN the B1 slot made in the window, in
//   the order made (kind K6 when every CS of the group is a human or recovered CS, else K3; holders = the distinct holder seats of detail.blocked in claim order); [] when
//   none; breaches never enter a report (BreachPort only).

// src/engine/start.ts (changed; PRO-48 last sentence, R4-56, glossary §7 "re-announced on upgrade") — Task 16; restart.ts calls it too
/** Queues one same-epoch join draft when the folded own PeerRec's regionSpec, plugin_version, plugin_sha or config_fingerprint differs from EngineDeps (PRO-48 names all four;
 *  R4-56, widened, names the same four); returns whether it queued. Called once at start (after the first fold) and once at restart; join is in the regionSpec VERSION board-only list. */
export function reannounceJoin(core: EngineCore): boolean;

// src/daemon/run.ts (changed) — Task 5 (regions = productionRegionDeps(…); `await regions.workers.close()` after drain and on every early exit, R4-78), Task 16 (below)
//   START_REGION_RUNTIME { file: string } — the refusal for a missing runtime file (RegionRuntimeMissingError): `run` exits 1 before any state write; next command
//   "update cowork-sync" (08-lifecycle START_VERSION's wording). The constant is declared in src/regions/codes.ts (X-117); no spec code text exists: Plan 7's registry
//   (through carried.ts) and LIF-36 sequence own its row, text and handling.
//   The security Log: run.ts builds the daemon's one security Log and passes it to productionRegionDeps (X-34); each wiring line is its plan's own (X-111).
export class RegionSpecMismatchError extends Error { readonly engine: string; readonly runtime: string; }
/** R4-81: throws RegionSpecMismatchError iff the two differ. runDaemon calls it with (EngineDeps.regionSpec, deps.regions.runtime.regionSpec) before Engine.create unless its
 *  git runner is a test build (Plan 1's testBuild flag, the switch that gates wallMsForDates), and maps the error to exit 2 with one daemon-log line
 *  REGION_RUNTIME_MISMATCH {engine, runtime} (log-only, V-17) — a defect; the skipped
 *  call in test builds is the T-REG-16 / T-PRO-30 seam (R4-55), on Plan 7's L-DIST strip list (B-10). */
export function assertRuntimeRegionSpec(engineRegionSpec: string, runtimeRegionSpec: string): void;
```

```ts
// test/helpers/regionRuntime.ts — Task 1 (testSnapshot: Task 4)

/** Bundles a TypeScript entry with esbuild (platform 'node', format 'esm') into the per-process temp root and returns the .mjs path (tsx loaders are not relied on inside worker threads). */

// runWorkerLoop with their own handler table (crash: exits with code 3 on its first job in generation 0, else answers {kind: 'ok', value: []}; stall: busy-waits forever on
// map jobs, answers other kinds {kind: 'ok', value: []}; throw: its hunks handler throws), so no Task 1 test depends on a production handler.
```

| Rule | Fails on | Source |
|---|---|---|
| L-REGION-DEPS | an import of `web-tree-sitter` outside `src/regions/worker/**`; `node:worker_threads` outside `src/regions/pool.ts` and `src/entry/worker.ts`; any `fs`, `child_process`, `src/git/**` or `src/ports/fs.ts` import in `src/regions/worker/**`; an import of `src/regions/worker/inline.ts` from `src/**` | R4-09, R4-12, 11 L-PORTS region row |
| L-REGION-WRITE | in `src/regions/**` or `src/claims/**`: `hashBytes(…, true)`, `hashRouteB(…, true)`, `hashRouteC(…, true)`, `writeTreeMap`, `editTree`, `mktree`, `commitTree`, `mergeTree`, a ref update, an index write, `hash-object -w`, or a `GitRepo.run` argv whose subcommand is not `grep`, `ls-tree`, `cat-file`, `check-attr` or `hash-object` without `-w` (Plan 2's `mergeFile` — `merge-file -p`, no write — is allowed only in `src/claims/editCheckPort.ts`); `src/regions/**` importing `src/board`, `src/holds`, `src/claims` or `src/engine` | REG-02, REG-35, R4-02, R4-40 |
| L-OFFSETS | `startIndex`, `endIndex` or `startPosition.column`/`endPosition.column` outside `src/regions/worker/lineTable.ts` | REG-01, 11 L-OFFSETS |
| L-UTF8-WRITE | `TextEncoder`, `Buffer.from(<string>, …)`, or `.write(<string>` whose receiver's last identifier is neither `log` nor ends in `Log` (Plan 2's `Log.write(level, code, params)` and the security log write codes, not encoded text; R4-83), in `src/regions/**` and `src/claims/**` | 04 §3.2 item 3, 11 L-UTF8-WRITE |
| L-ONE-MAPPER | an import of `matchedRows` or `src/data/linediff.ts` in `src/regions/**` or `src/claims/**` (Task 1), and in `src/holds/mask.ts` (Task 5, in the commit that switches `mask.ts` to `hunks()`, R4-83); a `diff`/`diff-tree`/`log -p` argv in `src/regions/**` or `src/claims/**` | REG-07, R4-17 |
| L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS | an import of `src/claims/**` from `src/holds/**`; an import of `src/regions/**` from `src/holds/**` other than `src/holds/mask.ts` → `src/regions/mapper.ts` and the type-only `import type` of `src/regions/editCheck.ts` (`EditCheckEntry`, `EditCheckReason`) in `src/holds/ports.ts` | HLD-06, R4-67 |
| L-UTF8-WRITE (note) | Plan 4 adds only the 04 §3.2 item 3 clause; 11-testing's M11 clause of the same rule ("`fs` reads with an `utf8` encoding whose result feeds a write") is added by Plan 7 Task 21 (X-118) | 11 §3.10 |

---

## Tasks

Waves (tasks in one wave touch disjoint files and may run in parallel; every task waits for the waves before it). Tasks 1–4, 6 and 7 need only a merged Plan 2; **every other task (Task 5 onward) needs Plan 3 merged through its Task 18** — all of Plan 3 except Task 19, which waits on F04-a and edits only `src/since/report.ts` and its test (R4-01 as clarified by the verify pass, reconcile §7 V-15): Plan 3's Tasks 11–18 edit `src/engine/{core,loop,publish,status,start,restart}.ts`, which Plan 4's Tasks 5, 8 and 11–16 edit too, so "through its Task 10" would let them collide; a task marked **[P3 round 2]** consumes Plan 3's round-2 work (Task 10's `last_check` store, Task 17's F10 kernel, Task 18's F14 inputs), which Plan 4 never builds itself (R4-43 as amended, X-05, X-09). **`tools/lint/rules.ts`:** Plan 4 Task 1 (L-REGION-DEPS … L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS, appended) and Plan 3 Task 8 (L-HOLD-STORES, appended) both append rows; Plan 3 Task 8 merges first (Plan 3 is executing) and Plan 4 Task 1 rebases onto it before its merge — appended rows only, never an edit of a Plan 3 row. Follow-up **4-V** (after Task 18) holds the viewer rows of R4-68; it runs once 8a Task 20 (the viewer's claim inputs, which needs Task 8's `overlapPairsOf`) has merged (X-91; X-92 as decided by the owner on 2026-10-08).

- Wave A: Task 1 ‖ Task 2
- Wave B: Task 3 ‖ Task 4
- Wave C: Task 5 ‖ Task 6 ‖ Task 7
- Wave D: Task 8 ‖ Task 9 ‖ Task 10
- Wave E: Task 11
- Wave F: Task 12 ‖ Task 13
- Wave G: Task 14
- Wave H: Task 15
- Wave I: Task 16
- Wave J: Task 17
- Wave K: Task 18

Shared lines are declared once so parallel tasks never edit them: Task 1 declares the `RegionJob` union, the job dispatcher with one stub handler file per job kind (each later task fills only its own file), the budgets, the region codes (with `START_REGION_RUNTIME` and `REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES`, X-117, X-30) and all six Plan 4 lint rules (path-based, so they pass on an empty tree; each owning task adds the code they guard; L-ONE-MAPPER's `src/holds/mask.ts` clause alone lands with Task 5, R4-83); Task 5 declares every Plan 4 `TraceEvent` variant, the `MutantId` additions, `RegionDeps`, the widened Plan 3 port types, `ClaimHostView` and the `EngineCore`/`Engine`/`EngineInspection` members, which delegate to stub step functions it creates — `seatEffectStep`, `predictStep`, `claimRejectionStep`, `settleDeferredConfirms` and `predictRevertStep` in `src/engine/claimsStep.ts` (filled by Task 11; `predictRevertStep` by Task 12), `emitStep` (Task 14), `regionConflictHooks`, `placementStep` and `breachStep` (Task 15), `labelStep`, `claimsHeld`, `claimViewStep`, `claimViewOfStep` and `verifiedLabelStep` in `src/engine/labelStep.ts` (Task 13). Every task: TDD (failing test first, then code), real git against `file://` bare remotes (never mocked), real grammars through `testRegionRuntime()` (never a mocked parse), SIM tests deterministic by seed, `npm run ci` green before its commit, one commit per green step ending with the Co-Authored-By line.

### Task 1: Grammar runtime and the worker pool

**Covers:** R1-001, R1-024, R1-038 (the chapter-local numbers in `BUDGETS`), 3X-03 (budget determinism in SIM), R1-040 (module boundary), R1-061 (L-OFFSETS), R1-062 (L-UTF8-WRITE), R1-070, R1-071, R1-072, R1-152 (all six lint rules), R2-N07, R2-C01 (the budget table), R2-C02, R2-D01 (code constants), R2-H01 (worker crash, `.wasm` load failure rows), B2-11, 3X-01, 3X-04 (part), 3X-05; REG-03, REG-04 (`computeRegionSpec`), REG-31 (pool); OQ-1…OQ-5, OQ-7, OQ-27, 3Q-01, 3Q-09; R4-09…R4-13, R4-15, R4-16, R4-54 (constants); reconciliation X-30, X-37, X-107, X-117. **Depends on:** Plan 2 merged (R4-01). **Merge order:** before Plan 7 Task 4, which appends its `secret_scan` job kind to this task's worker entry (X-37). **Starts with the ABI spike (R4-10): any grammar that fails to load or parse stops the task for a controller ruling.** Wave A (parallel with Task 2).

**Files:**
- Modify: `package.json` (devDependencies `web-tree-sitter`, `tree-sitter-python`, `tree-sitter-javascript`, `tree-sitter-typescript`, `tree-sitter-c-sharp`, each an exact version with no `^`/`~`), `package-lock.json`, `tools/build/build.mjs` (bundle `src/entry/worker.ts` with `web-tree-sitter`; copy the runtime and the five grammar `.wasm` files into `<outdir>/grammars/` after the node entries are built; `checkOutdir` also accepts an existing `grammars/` directory that holds only `.wasm` files; Plan 8a's order — parseArgs → `checkOutdir` → rm/mkdir first — and its `viz` entry, client pre-bundle and plugin are kept whichever lands first, Table C C-20), `src/entry/worker.ts` (the region worker entry: as a process it keeps Plan 1's `--version` / no-op contract; as a worker thread it runs `runWorkerLoop(parentPort, workerData)`, which dispatches each job by its `kind` through one kind table — the six `RegionJob` kinds here, Plan 7 Task 4 appending `secret_scan` for its own pool instance — and rejects a job of an unregistered kind, X-37), `tools/lint/rules.ts` (six appended rules), `test/integration/build.test.ts` (the new `dist/` listing, see Notes)
- Create: `src/regions/types.ts`, `src/regions/runtime.ts`, `src/regions/budgets.ts`, `src/regions/pool.ts`, `src/regions/codes.ts`, `src/regions/queries/{python,javascript,typescript,tsx,c_sharp,index}.ts` (first versions: the capture skeleton per language; Task 4 completes them), `src/regions/worker/{protocol,main,parser,jobs,inline}.ts`, `src/regions/worker/jobs/{hunks,map,table,foldOverlap,sites,labelAt}.ts` (stubs that throw; Tasks 3, 3, 4, 8, 7, 13 fill them), `src/regions/interface.ts` (types only: `CallArgs`, `Site`, `SiteSet`; Task 7 adds the rest), `src/regions/foldOverlap.ts` (type only: `OverlapClaim`; Task 8 adds the rest), `test/helpers/regionRuntime.ts` (`testRegionRuntime`, `testWorkerPath`, `bundleForTest`), `test/helpers/workers/{parseProbe,crash,stall,order,throw}Worker.ts` (test-only worker entries with their own handler tables, bundled by `bundleForTest`)
- Test: `test/integration/grammarSpike.test.ts`, `test/unit/runtime.test.ts`, `test/unit/languageOf.test.ts`, `test/unit/budgets.test.ts`, `test/unit/pool.test.ts`, `test/unit/regionCodes.constants.test.ts`, `test/unit/lint.p4.test.ts`, `test/integration/workerPool.test.ts`, `test/integration/build.test.ts` (modified)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 1 `Fs`, `realFs`, `Clock`, `realClock`, `Random`, `seededRandom`, `PLUGIN_VERSION`, `makeTempRoot`, `VirtualClock`, `generateBigBomCrlf`, `lintSource`, `LintFinding`, the lint CLI (`npm run lint`); Plan 2 `Oid`, `FileMode`, `regionSpecId`, `PLAN2_REGION_SPEC`, `FaultFs` (its `calls` log).
- Produces: `DiskSnapshotId`, `BlobRef`, `isDisk`, `blobOid`, `Range`, `Ranges`, `Hunk`, `MapResult`, `MapMode`, `Language`, `Granularity`, `RegionUnitKind`, `Label`, `LabelKey`, `labelKey`, `ParamSlice`, `SigSlice`, `Unit`, `RegionTable`, `NoTableReason`, `NoTable`, `TableOrNone`, `PathClass`, `TreeView`, `TouchReason` (declared here, §Interfaces); `LANGUAGES`, `GRAMMAR_VERSIONS`, `GRAMMAR_FILES`, `RUNTIME_WASM`, `languageOf`, `RegionRuntime`, `RegionRuntimeMissingError`, `loadRegionRuntime`, `computeRegionSpec`, `QUERY_SOURCES`; `BUDGETS`, `BudgetKind`, `Deadline`, `RegionBudgets`, `BudgetInjector`, `clockBudgets`, `testBudgetInjector`; `WorkerPriority`, `RegionJob`, `JobOutput`, `JobResult`, `RegionWorkers`, `threadWorkers`, `JobQueue`, `inlineWorkers` (with its test-only `handlers` option); `WorkerInit`, `ToWorker`, `FromWorker`, `WorkerPortLike`, `runWorkerLoop`, `ParserHost`, `initParser`, `JobCtx`, `JobHandlers`, `runJob`; `OverlapClaim`, `CallArgs`, `Site`, `SiteSet` (types); `REGION_CODES`, `CLAIM_REJECTED`, `RegionCode`, `RegionCodeParams`, `START_REGION_RUNTIME`, `REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES` (X-117, X-30); the rules L-REGION-DEPS, L-REGION-WRITE, L-OFFSETS, L-UTF8-WRITE, L-ONE-MAPPER, L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS; `testRegionRuntime`, `testWorkerPath`, `bundleForTest`.

**Spec:** 04 §1.1 b1, §1.2 row 10, §2 "regionSpec" and chapter-local numbers, §3.1 ¶1 (module boundary, worker pool), §3.2 items 2–3, §3.3 "Runtime", §3.18 (the budget table), §3.19 (code table), §6 rows "Parse exceeds 400 ms or the worker crashes" and "Grammar `.wasm` fails to load at start", REG-02, REG-03, REG-04, REG-31; 02 §2.1 `CLAIM_REJECTED`; glossary §6 (`dist/worker.js`, `grammars/*.wasm`), §19 (code pattern), §20 ("Parse", "Gate latency", "Region numbers", "T0 checks", "Region mapper"), §27.1 (0.5 s); 11-testing §3.10 (L-OFFSETS, L-PORTS region clause, L-UTF8-WRITE), TST-03; research `runtime-dist.md` (versions, the C# ABI question); rulings R4-09…R4-13, R4-15, R4-16, R4-54.

**Tests:**

*ABI spike (R4-10; the first commit of the task)*
- `"every pinned grammar loads and parses its spike snippet"` — in-process `initParser` over `testRegionRuntime()`: `availability()` is `ok` for all five languages; each snippet parses with `rootNode.hasError === false` and the expected root type — python `module` (a 12-row file: `import os`, a decorated function, a class with one method), javascript `program` (a class with `get`/`set` and an arrow `const`), typescript `program` (an `interface`, two overload signatures plus the implementation, an `enum`), tsx `program` (a function component returning JSX), c_sharp `compilation_unit` (a namespace, a class with `Calc(int)` and `Calc(double)`, a property and a constructor); a broken variant of each (one deleted closing token) parses with `hasError === true`; any load failure, any `hasError` on a clean snippet or a missing `hasError` on a broken one stops the task for a controller ruling (another 0.27.x patch or grammar version, never a second parser) (R1-001, R1-070, REG-03, OQ-3, R4-10).
- `"the pinned versions are exact and equal package-lock.json"` — `GRAMMAR_VERSIONS['web-tree-sitter']` matches `/^0\.27\.\d+$/`; each of the six values equals `package-lock.json` `packages["node_modules/<pkg>"].version` (`typescript` and `tsx` both read `tree-sitter-typescript`, so they are equal); every one of the five packages appears in `package.json` `devDependencies` with a bare version (no `^`, `~`, range or tag) and none in `dependencies` (R4-09, R4-10, OQ-3, 3X-01, 3Q-01).

*Runtime and `regionSpec`*
- `"loadRegionRuntime reads the six .wasm files through the Fs port"` — over a Plan 2 `FaultFs` wrapping the `testRegionRuntime()` directory: `fs.calls` is exactly six `readFile <dir>/<name>` entries, `tree-sitter.wasm` first then `GRAMMAR_FILES` in `LANGUAGES` order (`tree-sitter-python.wasm`, `tree-sitter-javascript.wasm`, `tree-sitter-typescript.wasm`, `tree-sitter-tsx.wasm`, `tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm`); `runtime.dir` is the directory; `runtime.grammars` holds those five buffers; `runtime.querySources` is `QUERY_SOURCES` (R1-040, R4-12, OQ-4).
- `"a missing .wasm file throws RegionRuntimeMissingError naming it"` — the same directory without `tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm` → `RegionRuntimeMissingError` with `file === 'tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm'`; without `tree-sitter.wasm` → `file === 'tree-sitter.wasm'` (daemon start refuses; the start wiring is Task 16) (R2-H01, R4-12).
- `"computeRegionSpec is Plan 2's regionSpecId over the R4-11 parts"` — `computeRegionSpec([Buffer.from('a'), Buffer.from('b')], ['q1'])` equals `regionSpecId([sha256hex('a'), sha256hex('b'), sha256hex('q1'), 'extent-convention/1', 'label-convention/1', 'FoldMapper/1'])` (the test imports `regionSpecId` from `src/engine/deps.ts`; `src/regions` cannot); swapping the order of the inputs gives the same value; `computeRegionSpec([], []) === PLAN2_REGION_SPEC`; every hash part is a bare lowercase 64-hex digest with no file name; the result matches `/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/` (REG-04, R4-11, OQ-2, OQ-7, 3Q-09).
- `"the regionSpec golden for the pinned files"` — `(await loadRegionRuntime(realFs, dir)).regionSpec` over the `testRegionRuntime()` directory equals the constant `REGION_SPEC_GOLDEN` in the test, pinned at the spike's first green run and re-pinned by Task 4 when the query files are completed (a reviewed one-line change); flipping one byte of any of the six `.wasm` copies or appending one space to any one query source changes it; copying the six files into a differently named directory does not (REG-04, R4-11, OQ-7).
- `"a grammar that fails to load in a worker does not change regionSpec"` — with `tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm` truncated to 1,000 bytes, `runtime.regionSpec` equals `computeRegionSpec` of the six shipped (truncated) files and the five query sources, while `availability()` of the pool reports `c_sharp: 'unavailable'` (the value is computed from bytes, never from load success; R4-11) (REG-03, R4-11).
- `"a query source that does not compile makes only its language unavailable"` — `initParser(init, {...QUERY_SOURCES, python: '(nonexistent_node) @x'})` → `python: 'unavailable'`, the other four `ok` (P2: a language whose query is unusable is grammarless) (R1-024, REG-03).

*Languages (R4-16)*
- `"languageOf maps the byte-exact lowercase extension of the last component"` — `a.py`, `pkg/a.pyi` → python; `a.js`, `a.mjs`, `a.cjs`, `a.jsx` → javascript; `a.ts`, `a.mts`, `a.cts`, `types/a.d.ts` → typescript; `a.tsx`, `src/x.test.tsx` → tsx; `Svc.cs` → c_sharp; null for `Foo.PY`, `a.Py`, `a.TS`, `a.pyw`, `a.md`, `a.ipynb`, `Makefile`, `.py` (a dotfile has no extension, `path.posix.extname` semantics), `dir.py/README` (last component only) (R1-071, R4-16, OQ-27).

*Budgets (R2-C01)*
- `"BUDGETS is the 04 §3.18 table"` — deep-equal and frozen: `{gateMs: 15, parseMs: 400, mapLocalMs: 200, predictWaitMs: 10, t0Ms: 500, t0StopFactor: 4, editCheckMs: 500, editCheckStopFactor: 1, callerGrepMs: 150, partialGrepMs: 100, cacheBytes: 314_572_800, liveTreesPerWorker: 8, regionBudgetDedupeMs: 1_800_000, workerGraceMs: 1_000}` (R2-C01, R4-13, R4-15).
- `"clockBudgets deadlines run on the Clock port's monotonic time"` — `VirtualClock` at mono 1,000: `deadline('parse')` has `budgetMs` 400, `remainingMs()` 400, `expired()` false; after `advance(399)` remaining 1, not expired; after `advance(1)` expired, remaining 0; `deadline('t0', 4)` has `budgetMs` 2,000; `deadline('edit_check', 1)` 500; `deadline('gate')` 15; `deadline('caller_grep')` 150; `deadline('partial_grep')` 100; `deadline('predict_wait')` 10; `deadline('map')` 200; a wall-clock jump of ±1 h changes no `remainingMs()` (INV-CLOCK basis) (R2-C01, R4-13, R4-15).
- `"under the virtual clock no deadline expires unless injected"` — 10,000 deadlines of every kind created and checked with no `advance` → none expired; `inject.expire('parse', 2)` → the next two `parse` deadlines are expired at creation, the third is not, a `map` deadline is not; `inject.stallMs('edit_check', 600)` → the next `edit_check` deadline is expired at creation with `remainingMs()` 0 and the one after it is not; `stallMs('map', 150)` → the next `map` deadline has `remainingMs()` 50 and is not expired (R4-13, R4-15).
- `"testBudgetInjector is reached only from tests and tools"` — a scan of `src/**` finds no call of `testBudgetInjector` outside `src/regions/budgets.ts`, so Plan 7's L-DIST can strip it (R4-13).

*Pool (REG-31)*
- `"JobQueue orders fg > save > bg, FIFO within a class, fg jobs only on fg"` — push `bg1`, `save1`, `fg1`, `bg2`, `save2`, `fg2`: `next('fg')` → `fg1`, `fg2`, then `save1` (an idle fg worker takes save/bg work); `next('bg')` → `save2`, `bg1`, `bg2`, then null; `next('bg')` never returns an fg job even when only fg jobs remain (R4-13, OQ-5, REG-31).
- `"inlineWorkers runs jobs on the calling thread in submission order"` — `inlineWorkers(runtime, {handlers: {hunks: <a test handler answering [{aLo: <its call index>, aHi: 0, bLo: 0, bHi: 0}]>, map: <a spy handler>}})`: five `hunks` jobs submitted together resolve `{kind: 'ok'}` in submission order (call indices 0…4); a `map` job whose deadline is already expired at dispatch (`inject.expire('map', 1)`) resolves `{kind: 'deadline'}` and the spy handler is never called; a handler table whose `hunks` throws gives `{kind: 'crashed'}`; `availability()` is the in-process parser's; no assertion reads a production handler, so Tasks 3–13 filling them leaves this test green (R4-13, REG-31, R2-C02).
- `"threadWorkers spawns fg and bg from the built worker and reports availability"` — `threadWorkers(runtime, {threads: 2, workerPath: await testWorkerPath(), clock: realClock})` → `availability()` all five `ok` after the two `ready` messages; `close()` terminates both threads and the test process exits with no open handle (B2-11, R1-070, REG-31, OQ-5).
- `"workers load the grammars only from posted bytes"` — `loadRegionRuntime` over a temp copy, then the copy's directory is deleted, then `threadWorkers` spawns: availability all `ok` (the workers never open a file; R4-12) (R1-040, R4-12, OQ-4).
- `"a corrupt tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm makes only c_sharp unavailable and the pool keeps serving"` — a copy with `tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm` truncated to 1,000 bytes: `loadRegionRuntime` succeeds; both `threadWorkers` and `inlineWorkers` report `{python: ok, javascript: ok, typescript: ok, tsx: ok, c_sharp: unavailable}`; a following `run` resolves (Review Focus 5; the `REGION_GRAMMAR_UNAVAILABLE {language: 'c_sharp'}` status raise is wired at daemon start by Task 16, from this `availability()`); a corrupt `tree-sitter.wasm` makes all five `unavailable` and `run` still resolves (R1-024, R2-H01, REG-03).
- `"a worker that exits mid-job yields crashed and is respawned"` — `crashWorker` (exits with code 3 on its first job when `workerData.generation === 0`, otherwise answers every job `{kind: 'ok', value: []}` from its own handler table): the first `run` → `{kind: 'crashed'}`; `onRespawn` is called once with `('fg', 'crash')`; the second `run` → `{kind: 'ok', value: []}` from generation 1 (04 §6 "worker restarted") (R2-H01, R4-15, REG-31).
- `"a job past its deadline yields deadline; a worker silent 1 s later is terminated and respawned"` — `stallWorker` busy-waits forever on `map` jobs and answers every other kind `{kind: 'ok', value: []}` from its own handler table; `run(mapJob, 'fg', clockBudgets(realClock).deadline('map'))` resolves `{kind: 'deadline'}` after ≥ 200 ms and < 1,000 ms (the main thread abandons the job at its deadline); `onRespawn('fg', 'stall')` fires ≥ 1,000 ms (`BUDGETS.workerGraceMs`) after the deadline; the next fg job is answered by generation 1; a `bg` job submitted during the stall is answered by the bg worker meanwhile (R4-15, R2-C02, R2-H01).
- `"a handler that throws answers crashed and the worker is replaced"` — `throwWorker` (a test entry running the real `runWorkerLoop` with a handler table whose `hunks` throws): `run({kind: 'hunks', from: [], to: []}, 'fg', null)` → `{kind: 'crashed'}`, `onRespawn('fg', 'crash')` once, `availability()` unchanged after the respawn (a wasm trap may leave the module unusable, so no worker survives a throw); the test never depends on a production handler, so it stays green once Task 3 fills `jobs/hunks.ts` (R2-H01, R4-15).
- `"fg jobs run only on the fg thread; save and bg jobs use fg only when it is idle"` — `orderWorker` answers a `hunks` job after busy-waiting `from.length` ms with one hunk `{aLo: <0 for fg, 1 for bg>, aHi: <its job sequence>, bLo: 0, bHi: 0}`: three fg jobs submitted while bg is idle all report worker 0; with fg busy on a 300 ms job, two bg jobs report worker 1; with bg busy on a 300 ms job and fg idle, a save job reports worker 0 (R4-13, OQ-5).
- `"a worker parses the 26,436-line BOM+CRLF file off the main thread"` — `parseProbeWorker` (a test entry that calls `initParser` and parses the bytes it is sent) parses Plan 1 `generateBigBomCrlf(26_436, seededRandom('regions-26436'))` as python: the reply's `rootNode.endPosition.row` is 26,436 and `hasError` is false; no timing is asserted here — T-REG-15's event-loop row (`monitorEventLoopDelay` ≤ 20 ms during the parse) is Task 18's perf gate, run only under `test:perf:regions` (R4-64) (R2-C01, R2-C02, REG-31).
- `"the region worker entry keeps the stub contract as a process"` — `node <bundle>/worker.js --version` prints `cowork-sync worker <PLUGIN_VERSION>\n` with exit 0; without arguments it exits 0 with empty stdout and does not wait for messages (B2-11).
- `"the worker entry dispatches by job kind and rejects an unknown kind"` (X-37) — on a `threadWorkers` pool over the built entry, each of the six `RegionJob` kinds reaches its own handler (a test handler table records the kind); a job whose `kind` is not registered (`{kind: 'secret_scan', …}` before Plan 7 Task 4 registers it) resolves `{kind: 'crashed'}` without any handler running, and the pool answers the next region job; the kind table is the one place a later plan appends a kind (a source check: `src/entry/worker.ts` holds it and `src/regions/worker/jobs.ts` is reached only through it for region kinds).

*Build and `dist/` (R4-09)*
- `"build produces every build.mjs entry point and dist/grammars with exactly six .wasm files"` (`test/integration/build.test.ts`, modified) — `readdirSync(out).sort()` equals the test's explicit expected `dist/` list, to which this task appends `grammars` (X-107: one explicit list each plan appends to — Plan 1's entries, 8a's `viz.js` when it has landed, later plans' `i18n/` and `schema/` — replaced later by Plan 7 Task 19's `canonicalDistList()`; Table C C-20); `readdirSync(out/grammars).sort()` is `['tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm', 'tree-sitter-javascript.wasm', 'tree-sitter-python.wasm', 'tree-sitter-tsx.wasm', 'tree-sitter-typescript.wasm', 'tree-sitter.wasm']`; the sha256 of each equals that of the same file in `testRegionRuntime()`; in the metafile `worker.js` is the only output with an input under `node_modules/web-tree-sitter/`, and every other output imports only `node:` built-ins (which also holds 8a's VIZ-20 for `viz.js`); every import of `worker.js` is external and `module.isBuiltin(path)`; a second build over an outdir holding `grammars/` succeeds; an outdir whose `grammars/` holds `notes.txt` is refused (exit 2, stderr `/refus/`) and `notes.txt` survives; the other Plan 1 build tests pass unchanged (B2-11, R4-09, 3X-01, OQ-1, OQ-2, 3Q-01).

**Notes:**
- The spike fills `GRAMMAR_VERSIONS` from what loads: web-tree-sitter 0.27.0, tree-sitter-python 0.25.0, tree-sitter-javascript 0.25.0, tree-sitter-c-sharp 0.23.5 and the newest tree-sitter-typescript whose `.wasm` loads (R4-10); the implementer records the chosen versions and the package-internal source names of the six `.wasm` files in the ledger before any other step.
- Worker deadlines: the pool posts `remainingMs` (never an absolute time: a worker's monotonic origin differs); the worker builds its own deadline from `realClock` and checks it between files, between query captures and in the parser's progress callback; the main thread races the reply against `clock.sleep(deadline.remainingMs())` and resolves `{kind: 'deadline'}` on expiry, then terminates a worker still silent `BUDGETS.workerGraceMs` later (R4-15).
- `inlineWorkers` passes the caller's `Deadline` object into the handler; with a `VirtualClock` no synchronous handler sees time pass, so only an injected overrun expires (TST-03).
- The stub job handlers throw `Error('job <kind>: Task <n>')`; nothing in production submits a job before Task 4. No Task 1 test relies on a stub throwing: the pool tests drive crashes, order and stalls through test-only handler tables (`inlineWorkers`' `handlers` option, `runWorkerLoop`'s `handlers` parameter in the test worker entries), so they stay green as Tasks 3, 4, 7, 8 and 13 fill the handlers.
- Task 1 declares, as types only, `TouchReason` (`src/regions/types.ts`), `OverlapClaim` (`src/regions/foldOverlap.ts`) and `CallArgs`, `Site`, `SiteSet` (`src/regions/interface.ts`), exactly as §Interfaces states them, because the `RegionJob` union and Task 5's claim types need them before Tasks 6, 7 and 8 run in parallel.
- `test/integration/build.test.ts` changes its expected `dist/` listing under the header's amendment of Plan 1's "`dist/` stays the glossary §6 list" line (`grammars/` with six files appended to the test's explicit list; `worker.js` bundles `web-tree-sitter`; every other output stays `node:`-only); per X-107 the listing is one explicit list each plan appends to (so Plan 8a's seventh entry `viz`, R-V36, lands in either order, Table C C-20), replaced by Plan 7 Task 19's `canonicalDistList()`; `checkOutdir` admits `grammars/` (Plan 4), and later `i18n/` and `schema/` (Plans 5, 7). `build.mjs` copies the runtime `.wasm` from its package-internal name (`tree-sitter.wasm`, or `web-tree-sitter.wasm` in versions that renamed it; the spike records which) to `grammars/tree-sitter.wasm`, and keeps the source map of the six files inside `build.mjs` (the build-guard test copies only `build.mjs` and esbuild into its fake repository).
- L-UTF8-WRITE here is the 04 §3.2 item 3 clause only (§Interfaces lint table; controller flag 13; the M11 clause is Plan 7 Task 21's, X-118).
- **One worker entry, two pools (X-37).** `src/entry/worker.ts` is shared: Plan 7's secret-scan pool is a separate pool instance on the same entry with its own job kind; R4-15's terminate-and-respawn applies to the region pool only, and Plan 7 never terminates region workers.

**Tests:**

*Manifest and provenance (R4-21)*
- `"manifest.json and README.md list the same fixtures and every listed file exists"` — the README table's name column equals the manifest's keys; every checked-in name resolves to a file under `test/fixtures/regions/`; every generated name (`bigBomCrlf`, `bigLf`, the rc_touch and fwd posts, the S9 generator) resolves through `fixture()`; no file under the directory is unlisted, except under `golden/`, whose files belong to the tests that pin them (Task 3's `foldmapper1.json` and later goldens) (R4-21, OQ-24, 3Q-15, 2Q-07).

*The 5,960-row file and the hot zone (T-REG-02, T-REG-03, T-REG-20)*
- `"bigBomCrlf(5_960) is 5,960 BOM+CRLF rows with the hot zone at its rows"` — `rows(b).length === 5960`; `b` starts with `EF BB BF`; every row ends `\r\n`; no bare `\n`; the stripped rows are: 5300 `class Account:`, 5306 `    @property`, 5307 `    def balance(self):`, 5310 `    @balance.setter`, 5311 `    def balance(self, value):`, 5314 `        self._balance = value`, 5315–5316 empty, 5317 `@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=None)`, 5318 `def parse(text):`, 5328 `    return fields`, 5329–5330 empty, 5331 `# Sum the line items of an order, rounded to cents.`, 5332 `def compute_total(items):`, 5339 `    return round(total, 2)`, 5340–5341 empty, 5342 `def compute_tax(total, rate):`, 5344 `    return round(total, 2)`; `BIG_UNITS === 432` is the generator's own count of leaf units by construction — members, functions, consts, class residues and gap blocks, what `table.units.filter((u) => u.leaf)` counts under Task 4's unit model (a class also has one non-leaf unit, not counted) — and the README lists each template's contribution; the count under the real table is Task 4's assertion, and Task 4 may adjust the template schedule outside rows 5300–5346 to meet it (Task 4 Files); the hot zone is also the input of TC-REG-07 and T-REG-17 (this PC's human and another PC's human editing `compute_total` while a seat holds it; Tasks 12 and 17 drive them) (T-REG-02, T-REG-03, T-REG-20, TC-REG-07, OQ-24).
- `"bigLf(5_960) is the same content in LF without BOM, and both are deterministic"` — `bigLf(5960)` equals `bigBomCrlf(5960)` with the BOM removed and every `\r\n` turned into `\n`; two calls of each return byte-identical buffers; `fixture('big_wt.py')` and `fixture('big_lf.py')` return them (T-REG-03, TC-REG-03).
- `"RC_TOUCH_R2 is the 15 rc_touch R2 rows with their expected reasons"` — exactly these rows `(name, base, post, claimUnit, expect)`, the posts built from `big_lf.py` by the listed edit: `py_body_edit` (row 5335 changed) `compute_total` `base-overlap`; `py_append_after_last_line` (`    audit(total)` inserted after 5339) `compute_total` `boundary-in-extent`; `py_decorator_under_attached_comment` (`@traced` after 5331) `compute_total` `insert-inside`; `py_attached_comment_above` (`# Totals are in the order currency.` after 5330) `compute_total` `boundary-in-extent`; `py_boundary_insert_half_typed` (`def half(` at column 0 after 5339) `compute_total` `boundary-in-error`; `py_decorator_only_edit` (row 5317 → `@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=128)`) `parse` `base-overlap`; `py_append_setter_body` (`        self._audit.append(value)` after 5314) `Account.balance#setter` `boundary-in-extent`; `js_append_setter_body` (`account.js`, `    this._audit.push(value);` after row 8 inside the 6–9 setter) `Account.balance#set` `insert-inside`; `cs_new_overload_not_adjacent_to_held` (`Svc.cs`, `public decimal Calc(decimal x)` as rows 30–33) `N.Svc.Calc#(int)` `group-name`; `py_new_def_after` (4 rows after 5341) `compute_total` `none`; `py_same_tail_def_after` (the 9-row same-tail block after 5341) `compute_total` `none`; `py_overload_stubs_above` (`@overload`, `def compute_total(items: list) -> float: ...`, empty, after 5330) `compute_total` `none`; `py_sibling_edit` (row 5320 inside `parse` changed) `compute_total` `none`; `py_new_method_after_setter` (`    def reset(self):`, `        self._balance = 0`, empty, after 5315) `Account.balance#setter` `none`; `js_getter_above_setter` (`account.js`, a 3-row `get balance()` after row 5) `Account.balance#set` `none`; the half-typed row's inserted line is outside `compute_total`'s post extent and inside an ERROR span (Task 4 asserts both on the table); `claimUnit` is `qualname` plus an optional `#disc` (T-REG-02, TC-REG-02, T-REG-20).
- `"the R2 append post puts compute_total's last row at 5340"` — in `py_append_after_last_line`'s post, row 5331 is the attached comment, row 5339 `    return round(total, 2)`, row 5340 `    audit(total)`, rows 5341–5342 empty, row 5343 `def compute_tax(total, rate):` (the 04 §3.6 "post extent 5331–5340" by position; Task 4 pins the extent) (T-REG-02, OQ-24).

*Same-tail and premerge with real git (T-REG-06, T-REG-20)*
