- `"premerge: real merge-tree exits 0 and moves return round(total, 2) into compute_tax"` — `premerge/{base,ours,theirs}.py` (two peers append to `compute_total` and `compute_tax`, whose last rows are both `    return round(total, 2)`): `mergeTree(repo, base, ours, theirs)` returns `rc: 0`, and in the merged blob the rows from `def compute_tax(` to the next empty-row pair are not byte-identical to the same rows of `theirs` (the misplacement of 04 Review Focus 1); the README records the construction (T-REG-06, TC-REG-01, OQ-24).

*Sweep scenarios S1–S10 (T-REG-01, TC-REG-01, TC-REG-06, TC-REG-08)*
- `"each sweep scenario validates against SweepScenario"` — each `sweep/<name>/scenario.json` has `files` (base and the sides it needs), `claims` (`holderSeat`, `path`, `claimUnit` or `ranges`), `expect` (`byte_identical`, `held` or `conflict_hold`), `oracle` (the correct output, for `byte_identical`), `corrupting` and, for corrupting scenarios, `corruptOut` (the merge output the removed splice-and-key design pushed, which T-REG-01 forces through the real placement check and tripwires: "forced through merge outputs, must all be held", `cat_regions.md`) and a `hazard` `{path, pattern, count}`; the scenarios are S1 (rename of a held function in the propagation window, board lag and board ahead), S2a/S2b (move of `compute_total` within a file and across files), S3a/S3b/S3c (Python `@overload` stubs above a held function, a JS getter above a held setter, platform-branch duplicates), S4 (a requester deletes claimed lines), S5a/S5b (duplicated decorators on a claimed function), S6a/S6b/S6c (C# `Calc` overloads: a span with `Reset`'s edit and a `Calc(string)` hunk, a pairwise duplicate `Calc(double)` after inserting `Calc(decimal)`, `Calc(long)` added in `Svc.Part2.cs`), S7 (error trees), S8 (a merge output made undecodable), S10a/S10b (a withdrawn hunk adjacent to a kept hunk; an adjacent added or deleted symbol); S9 is `s9Generator`; `corrupting` is true exactly for S2a, S2b, S3a, S3b, S3c, S4, S5a, S5b, S6a, S6b, S8 and the S9 generator (04 §7: S2, S3×3, S4, S5×2, S6×2, S8, S9); every corrupting scenario carries `caughtBy` (R4-80) — `tripwire_b` with its `duplicatedKey` for the same-file duplicated unit keys S2a, S3a, S3b, S3c and S6b, `tripwire_c` for S8, and `b1`, `placement` or `t0` for S2b, S4, S5a, S5b, S6a and S9 as the regenerated hazard dictates (pinned at the first green run, reviewed by the controller); values the research gives only as prose are pinned at the first green run and reviewed by the controller (R4-21) (T-REG-01, TC-REG-01, TC-REG-06, TC-REG-08, R4-21, R4-80).
- `"every corrupting scenario's corruptOut shows its hazard and its oracle does not"` — for each corrupting scenario the `hazard` predicate holds on `corruptOut` and fails on the scenario's correct result (`oracle`, or `ours` merged by hand where the scenario is `held`): S3b `set balance(` occurs 2 times (1 in the oracle); S6b `public double Calc(double x)` 2 times; S2b `def compute_total(` 2 times across the output tree; S5a/S5b the decorator row 2 times above one `def`; S4 the claimed rows of `base` absent; S8 `corruptOut` fails `TextDecoder('utf-8', {fatal: true})` while `theirs` decodes; where plain git reproduces the hazard (S8 with its `ours`/`theirs`, premerge and X7 below) the integration test also checks that real `merge-tree` exits 0 with that output (T-REG-01, TC-REG-01, R4-21).
- `"s9Generator is deterministic and tracks the claimed block"` — `s9Generator('s9-0')` and a second call yield deep-equal first 50 steps; `'s9-1'` differs at some step ≤ 5; step 0's `holder` has 800 maximal non-blank row runs (blank-line blocks, > 300 rows, so path class 6); at every step `oracle` is the rows of the claimed block in `peer`, found test-side by its unique marker rows `S9-CLAIM-BEGIN`/`S9-CLAIM-END`; over 400 steps the summed absolute row-count change of `peer` is ≥ 3,000 (peer blank-line insertions and deletions above, inside and below the block, plus holder edits inside it) (T-REG-21, TC-REG-04, T-REG-20).

*fwd, errors, dupes, interface, C# (T-REG-07…T-REG-10, T-REG-20)*
- `"fwd: four 22-row insertion variants"` — `fwd/far_above`, `fwd/directly_above`, `fwd/directly_after` and `fwd/same_tail` each insert 22 rows into `big_lf.py` after rows 100, 5330, 5341 and 5341 respectively; `same_tail`'s block ends `    return round(total, 2)`, empty, empty; the expected images are Task 3's golden (T-REG-20, T-REG-01, TC-REG-01).
- `"S7 error-tree pairs differ from their clean files by one documented edit"` — `errors/S7/{py_triple_quote,py_paren,js_brace,cs_brace}` each hold `clean.*` and `broken.*` differing by exactly one changed or inserted row (an unclosed `"""`, an unclosed `(`, a missing `}` in JS and in C#), used as TIP and as local post-image (T-REG-07, TC-REG-08).
- `"rc_valid_errors and rc_dupes hold the duplicate-label shapes"` — `errors/rc_valid_errors.cs` defines `Describe()` once under `#if DEBUG` and once under `#else`; `errors/rc_valid_errors.py` has a `value` getter/setter pair and `open_console` defined in both branches of `if sys.platform == "win32":`; `dupes/rc_dupes.py` has `Account.value` twice (getter, setter) and `open_console` twice (platform branches); row numbers of each definition are listed in the README (T-REG-08, TC-REG-05).
- `"X7, x2 and x3 reproduce their caller hazards"` (integration) — `interface/X7/{base,ours,theirs}` (R4-69: `lib.py` `def foo(a, b)` becomes `def foo(a)` in ours, the CS that changed the signature; theirs adds `orders.py` row 14 `    total = foo(price, qty)`; X7's `orders.py` is its own file, not PRICE's): real `merge-tree` exits 0 and the merged `orders.py` row 14 is the 2-argument call; `x2` is the `compute_price` scenario (E1 gives `compute_price` a required `currency`, E2/E3 update its callers in `orders.py` and `reports.py`) and keeps that name; `x2` (a call to a removed function) and `x3` (`from m import n` after `n` is removed) both pass `python -m py_compile` when Python is on PATH (skipped otherwise, the skip printed) (T-REG-09, T-REG-28).
- `"C# and TS group-name fixtures"` — `csharp/Svc.cs`: `namespace N`, `public partial class Svc`, `Reset()` rows 5–8, `Calc(int x)` rows 12–15, `Calc(double x)` rows 17–20, `Describe()` ending row 28, row 29 empty; `csharp/Svc.new_overload.cs` inserts `public decimal Calc(decimal x)` as rows 30–33 (the `Calc@30` hit); `csharp/Svc.Part2.cs` is `public partial class Svc` with `Name()`, and `csharp/Svc.Part2.long.cs` adds `public long Calc(long x)`; `csharp/overloads.ts` holds `function f(x: number): number;`, `function f(x: string): string;` and the implementation, with a post adding `function f(x: boolean): boolean;` (T-REG-10, T-REG-02).

*Forms, special paths and properties (TC-REG-03, TC-REG-10, T-REG-23)*
- `"formVariant and the form fixtures"` — `formVariant(lf, {bom: true, crlf: true})` adds `EF BB BF` and turns every `\n` into `\r\n`, `{bom: false, crlf: false}` is the identity; `forms/` holds one LF and one BOM+CRLF file per language (`.py`, `.js`, `.ts`, `.tsx`, `.cs`) with Hangul, an astral emoji (U+1F600), curly quotes (U+2018/2019/201C/201D) and a U+2028 inside a string literal, and `forms/sample.utf16le.txt` (UTF-16LE with BOM, NUL bytes in its first 8,000 bytes); `special/legacy_cp949.cs` has 320 rows, is not valid UTF-8 and contains cp949 Hangul bytes (`B0 A1`), and `special/legacy_cp949.crlf.cs` is the same with CRLF (TC-REG-03, TC-REG-10).
- `"special-path fixtures"` — `special/small.md` has exactly 250 rows and `special/big.md` exactly 900 rows in blank-line-separated blocks; `special/nb.ipynb` is a two-cell notebook; `special/attributes` holds the lines `secret.env filter=secret` and `utf16.txt working-tree-encoding=UTF-16LE` (Task 4's T-REG-13 rows read them) (TC-REG-10, TC-REG-03).
- `"unitFileArb generates valid files per language"` — for each of python, javascript, typescript and c_sharp, 200 samples of `unitFileArb(lang)` under seed `'arb'` are deterministic, have ≥ 1 row, use only LF, and report `units` = the number of templates they contain (imports, functions, classes with members, consts, comments, blank runs; IIFE bodies for JS; namespaces for C#) (T-REG-23).

*Hook payloads, edit-check corpus, hold scenarios, the big files*
- `"T-REG-11 payloads carry the fields predict reads"` — `hooks/<case>/{pre.json,post.json,before.*,after.*}` for `lf_old_string_on_bomcrlf`, `curly_quotes`, `replace_all_5_functions`, `old_string_two_units`, `multiedit_two_edits`, `write_whole_file` and `notebook_edit`; each `pre.json` has `hook_event_name: 'PreToolUse'`, `tool_name`, `tool_input.file_path` (`notebook_path` for NotebookEdit) and the tool's fields (`old_string`, `new_string`, `replace_all`; `edits[]`; `content`; `cell_id`, `new_source`, `edit_mode`); `post.json` is the matching `PostToolUse`; after test-side normalization of `before` (BOM stripped, CRLF → LF, U+2018/2019 → `'`, U+201C/201D → `"`), `old_string` occurs exactly once (5 times for `replace_all_5_functions`), and in the raw `before` 0 times for `lf_old_string_on_bomcrlf` and `curly_quotes` (T-REG-11).
- `"the edit-check corpus has every T-REG-28 row and one pass and one fail case per check and language"` — `editCheckCases()` lists `{name, check, lang, expect, reason?, line?, tipEqualsA}` with A/R/TIP files: for each check 1–4 and each language group (Python, TS/JS, C#) one `pass` and one `fail` case (24 cases, TC-REG-09), grouped by REG-36's numbers (R4-70): check-2 fail cases are non-removal breaking changes (`param_removed`, `param_reordered`, `param_renamed`, `required_added`, `varargs_removed`, C# `overload_gone`) with an un-updated caller, plus the TIP-side caller not updated; check-3 fail cases are a removed or renamed symbol still called and a removed export still imported, each with an un-updated caller or importer; every fail case's `check` is the one number its `failed` reports; plus T-REG-28's named rows (passes: body-only edit, appended parameter with a default, callers updated in the same CS, new function, pre-existing ERROR in TIP, grammarless 250-row file, `.ipynb`; fails: unclosed paren `1/new_error_node`, undecodable `1/undecodable`, conflict markers `1/conflict_markers`, `foo(a,b)→foo(a)` with an un-updated caller in another file `2/incompatible_caller`, a new 2-argument call to a callee TIP made 1-argument `2/orphan_call`, a removed function still called `3/removed_name_called`, `from m import n` after removing `n` `3/unresolved_import`, a merged duplicate `def` `4/label_count`, a 60 % shrink `4/shrink`, an emptied file `4/emptied`, a 49 % shrink passing; notes-only passes: a possible caller, a stoplist name `get`, a JSON key rename), each with `TIP = A` and `TIP ≠ A` variants where the row says so, and `editcheck/burst.json` (20 edits of one function within 2 s, T-REG-29); expected `line` values not given by the spec are pinned at Task 10's first green run (T-REG-28, T-REG-29, TC-REG-09, 05 U10).
- `"exp_a and exp_bc validate against HoldScenario"` — `holdScenario('exp_a')` (a stale editor buffer reverts a teammate's landed fix inside a function the reverting seat claims: the claim must not launder the revert) and `exp_bc_b1`, `exp_bc_b2`, `exp_bc_c1` (a reverted line change, a reverted insertion, a revert mixed with a new own edit in one save) each have `files`, `steps` (`peer`, `kind: 'edit' | 'stale_save' | 'claim' | 'publish'`, `path`, `bytes`) and `claimSteps` marking where a run with claims on adds claims; step scripts not given by 05 are pinned at the first green run (05 P5, R4-21).
- `"bigBomCrlf(26_436) and oneDotThreeMb() are Plan 1's 26,436-row file"` — `bigBomCrlf(26_436)` is `generateBigBomCrlf(26_436, seededRandom('regions-26436'))`, 26,436 BOM+CRLF rows; `oneDotThreeMb()` returns the same bytes; its size is between 1,200,000 and 1,400,000 bytes (the exact size pinned at the first green run) (T-REG-15, T-REG-29, TC-REG-09).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** none (test code only).

**Notes:**
- `test/helpers/regionFixtures.ts` imports nothing from `src/regions` (it runs in Wave A beside Task 1): `RC_TOUCH_R2.expect` is the literal `FixtureTouchReason` union and `s9Generator`'s `oracle` plain row pairs; Task 6 checks their assignability to `TouchReason` and `Ranges`.
- The research corpus is not in the repository (R4-21); every fixture here is regenerated from the spec's descriptions. Plan 1's `generateBigBomCrlf` mixes blocks at about 3 rows per unit and cannot give 432 units on 5,960 rows, so `bigBomCrlf(5_960)` is a purpose-built deterministic generator: a header with `<imports>`, a schedule of 8–24-row functions and small classes, the hot zone at rows 5300–5346 and generated units after it, totalling exactly 432 leaf units by construction (members, functions, consts, class residues and gap blocks; a module docstring or any other top-level statement outside units and `<imports>` is a gap block and counts); `bigBomCrlf(26_436)` reuses Plan 1's generator unchanged, for size and timing only.
- Spec values are expected values (432 units, rows 5331–5340, git's 5339, FoldMapper/1's 5341, merge-tree's misplacement); a value the spec does not give is pinned at the first green run and reviewed by the controller (R4-21). A git version whose diff no longer places the same-tail insertion after 5339 fails the test; the controller decides.
- Every helper this task produces is declared in §Interfaces (`test/helpers/regionFixtures.ts`).

### Task 3: FoldMapper/1 and coordinates

**Covers:** R1-019 (D > 5,000 → whole-file), R1-031, R1-036, R1-042, R1-043 (pure), R1-060, R1-062, R1-064, R1-065, R1-083…R1-088; REG-01, REG-07, INV-REG-MAP, INV-CLOCK; T-REG-03 (mapper half), T-REG-15 (mapper rows: D > 5,000 → `WHOLE_FILE` in both modes, the 200 ms cap only in `local` via `BudgetInjector`), T-REG-20, T-REG-21 (pure half: the S9 mapping equals the oracle), TC-REG-04 (unit), O-REG-MAP (unit half); OQ-32, OQ-33, 2Q-15; R4-17 (the mapper), R4-18, R4-23. **Depends on:** Tasks 1, 2. Wave B (parallel with Task 4).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/regions/lines.ts` (first commit of the task, with `normalizeRanges` and `rangesIntersect`; see Notes), `src/regions/mapper.ts` (re-exports the two), `test/fixtures/regions/golden/foldmapper1.json`
- Modify: `src/regions/worker/jobs/hunks.ts`, `src/regions/worker/jobs/map.ts` (replace the Task 1 stubs)
- Test: `test/unit/lines.test.ts`, `test/unit/mapper.test.ts`, `test/unit/mapper.golden.test.ts`, `test/unit/mapper.prop.test.ts`, `test/integration/mapper.control.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `Hunk`, `Range`, `Ranges`, `MapResult`, `MapMode`, `Deadline`, `clockBudgets`, `testBudgetInjector`, `RegionJob`, `JobCtx`, `inlineWorkers`, `threadWorkers`, `testRegionRuntime`, `testWorkerPath`; Task 2 `fixture`, `bigBomCrlf`, `bigLf`, `formVariant`, `RC_TOUCH_R2`, `s9Generator`, `HOT_ZONE`; Plan 2 `rows`, `stripEnding`, `hasBom`; Plan 1 `VirtualClock`, `seededRandom`; `fast-check`.
- Produces: `lineArray`, `decodeStrict`, `isBlankRow`, `FOLD_MAPPER`, `D_CAP`, `LOCAL_MAP_CAP_MS`, `D_REANCHOR`, `linesEqual`, `hunks`, `editDistance`, `imageOf`, `mapRangesLines`, `normalizeRanges`, `rangesIntersect`; the `hunks` and `map` job handlers.

**Spec:** 04 §2 ("D", "image", "FoldMapper/1", "TOMBSTONE / WHOLE_FILE", chapter-local "one mapping ≤ 40 ms"), §3.1 (`hunks`, `mapRanges`), §3.2 items 1, 3, 5, 6, §3.4 steps 1–6 and the VERIFIED measurements, REG-01, REG-07, INV-REG-MAP, INV-REG-COORD; glossary §20 "Region mapper", `D_reanchor`; glossary §21 INV-CLOCK; §26.2 item 3; 11-testing TC-REG-04, O-REG-MAP; rulings R4-17, R4-18, R4-23.

**Tests:**

*Lines (REG-01, R4-18)*
- `"lineArray splits on 0x0A only"` — `a\r\nb<U+2028>c\nd` → rows `a\r\n`, `b<E2 80 A8>c\n`, `d` (U+2028 stays inside row 2); a lone `\r` splits nothing; the BOM stays in row 1; an empty buffer → `[]`; `x\n` → one row; on 1,000 random buffers (bytes biased to `\n`, `\r`, `E2 80 A8`, `EF BB BF`) `lineArray` deep-equals Plan 2's `rows` (the same function, never a copy) (R1-060, R1-031, REG-01).
- `"decodeStrict is fatal, keeps a BOM character and never encodes"` — `decodeStrict(Buffer.from('a\n'))` → `'a\n'`; `EF BB BF 78` → `'﻿x'` (`ignoreBOM: true` keeps it); cp949 `B0 A1` → null; a lone continuation byte `80`, the overlong `C0 80` and the surrogate `ED A0 80` → null; `src/regions/lines.ts` passes L-UTF8-WRITE (R1-062, REG-01).
- `"isBlankRow is a byte test"` — true for `\n`, `\r\n`, ` \t\r\n`, `\t\n`, an unterminated `  `, and row 1 `EF BB BF \r\n`; false for ` x\n`, `\f\n`, `C2 A0 \n` (NBSP), cp949 `B0 A1 \n`, and `EF BB BF \n` as row 2 (the BOM is stripped only on row 1) (R4-18, R1-064).

*Equality and hunks (steps 1–2)*
- `"linesEqual removes one LF, then one CR, and a BOM only on line 1"` — equal: (`a\r\n`, `a\n`), (`a`, `a\n`), (`a\r`, `a`), (`EF BB BF a\n`, `a\n`) with `lineOne` true; not equal: (`a\r\r\n`, `a\n`), (`EF BB BF a\n`, `a\n`) with `lineOne` false, (`a \n`, `a\n`), (`A\n`, `a\n`) (R1-083, R1-064).
- `"hunks has the 04 §3.1 shape"` — rows `[a,b,c]`→`[a,x,c]` → `[{aLo: 2, aHi: 2, bLo: 2, bHi: 2}]`; `[a,b]`→`[a,n,b]` → `[{aLo: 2, aHi: 1, bLo: 2, bHi: 2}]` (insertion after base line 1); `[a]`→`[n,a]` → `[{aLo: 1, aHi: 0, bLo: 1, bHi: 1}]`; `[a]`→`[a,n]` → `[{aLo: 2, aHi: 1, bLo: 2, bHi: 2}]`; `[a,b,c]`→`[a,c]` → `[{aLo: 2, aHi: 2, bLo: 2, bHi: 1}]` (pure deletion, `bHi = bLo − 1`, the shape Plan 3's `[k + 1, k]` held rows need in Task 5); `[]`→`[a,b,c]` → `[{aLo: 1, aHi: 0, bLo: 1, bHi: 3}]`; `[a,b,c]`→`[]` → `[{aLo: 1, aHi: 3, bLo: 1, bHi: 0}]`; equal arrays → `[]`; hunks are sorted and maximal (no two hunks touch on both sides) (R1-042, R1-084, REG-07).
- `"BOM+CRLF and LF forms give zero hunks"` — `hunks(lineArray(bigBomCrlf(5960)), lineArray(bigLf(5960)))` → `[]` in both argument orders; a BOM moved to row 2 is one changed row (T-REG-03, R1-064, REG-01).
- `"prefix and suffix trimming puts the same-tail insertion after line 5341"` — `hunks(big_lf.py, py_same_tail_def_after)` → `[{aLo: 5342, aHi: 5341, bLo: 5342, bHi: 5350}]`, never git's 5339 (Task 2 pins git's placement on the same pair) (R1-084, T-REG-20, R4-17).
- `"Myers is minimal, deterministic and reproduces the target"` — fast-check, 1,000 pairs of ≤ 40 rows over a 4-symbol alphabet with mixed endings: the changed-row count `Σ(aHi − aLo + 1) + Σ(bHi − bLo + 1)` equals a test-side O(N·M) LCS reference distance and `editDistance(from, to, 10_000)`; applying the hunks to `from` gives `to` up to `linesEqual`; two calls deep-equal (R1-084, R1-088, INV-REG-MAP).
- `"editDistance returns null once past its cap"` — 3,000 rows `a<i>` vs 3,000 rows `b<i>`: `editDistance(·, ·, 5_000)` → null, `editDistance(·, ·, 7_000)` → 6,000; equal arrays → 0 (R1-087).

*Images (step 3)*
- `"imageOf implements step 3 (a), (b) and (c)"` — on a 10-row base with claim `[[4, 6]]`: 2 rows inserted after base 1 → `[[6, 8]]` (a); row 5 replaced by 3 rows (`{aLo: 5, aHi: 5, bLo: 5, bHi: 7}`) → `[[4, 8]]` (b); 2 rows inserted after base 4 (`{aLo: 5, aHi: 4, bLo: 5, bHi: 6}`, `lo ≤ k < hi`) → `[[4, 8]]` (c); row 5 deleted → `[[4, 5]]`; rows 4–6 replaced by one row → `[[4, 4]]`; two claimed ranges `[[2, 3], [8, 9]]` with row 1 deleted → `[[1, 2], [7, 8]]` (R1-085, R1-043, R4-23, OQ-33).
- `"boundary insertions are never in an image"` — claim `[[4, 6]]`: 2 rows inserted after base 6 (`k == hi`) → `[[4, 6]]`; 2 rows inserted after base 3 (`k == lo − 1`) → `[[6, 8]]`; the 04 §3.4 step 3 wording "`touch` case 3" is read as `touch` step 4, which decides them (R1-085, R4-23, OQ-33).
- `"TOMBSTONE when every claimed line is deleted and nothing replaces it"` — rows 4–6 deleted → `'TOMBSTONE'`; rows 3–7 deleted → `'TOMBSTONE'`; rows 4–6 deleted with 2 rows inserted after base 7 → `'TOMBSTONE'`; rows 4–6 replaced → the replacement (R1-085).
- `"normalizeRanges and rangesIntersect use closed integer intervals"` (in `test/unit/lines.test.ts`, the first commit; `mapper.ts` re-exports the same functions) — `normalizeRanges([[5, 6], [1, 2], [3, 4]])` → `[[1, 6]]` (adjacent intervals merge); `[[1, 3], [2, 2]]` → `[[1, 3]]`; `[[3, 2]]` (zero-length) → `[]`; `[]` → `[]`; `rangesIntersect([[1, 3]], [[3, 5]])` true, `([[1, 3]], [[4, 5]])` false, `([], [[1, 1]])` false (R1-085).

*Direct mapping, caps and modes (steps 4–5)*
- `"mapping is direct, never composed"` — for 1,000 seeded triples (X, Y, Z) of 30–80 rows, `mapRangesLines(X, Z, r, 'fold')` equals `imageOf(hunks(X, Z), r)` (one diff); the crafted triple X = 10 rows with claim `[[4, 6]]`, Y = X without rows 4–6, Z = X gives `[[4, 6]]` directly while composing X→Y (`'TOMBSTONE'`) cannot reach it (R1-086, INV-REG-MAP, R1-043).
- `"D > 5,000 is WHOLE_FILE in both modes and D = 5,000 is not"` — 3,000 vs 3,000 disjoint rows (D = 6,000) → `'WHOLE_FILE'` in `fold` and in `local`; 2,500 vs 2,500 disjoint rows (D = 5,000) → a range result in both; every `local` call here takes its deadline from `clockBudgets(new VirtualClock(…))` with no injector, so the 200 ms cap never expires and only the D cap is under test (a slow runner cannot turn the D = 5,000 case into `WHOLE_FILE`) (T-REG-15's mapper row, R1-087, TC-REG-04).
- `"the 200 ms cap applies only in local mode"` — on a D = 60 pair: `inject.stallMs('map', 201)` then `mapRangesLines(…, 'local', budgets.deadline('map'))` → `'WHOLE_FILE'`; `stallMs('map', 199)` → the range result; the `fold` overload takes no deadline (a `// @ts-expect-error` passing one) and returns the range result whatever the injector holds (T-REG-15's cap row, R1-087, INV-CLOCK, TC-REG-04).
- `"fold mapping reads no clock"` — `src/regions/mapper.ts` imports nothing from `src/ports/clock.ts` and reads time only through a passed `Deadline`; a fold mapping returns the same value under a `VirtualClock` at mono 0, after `advance(10 ** 9)`, and with every budget kind injected expired (INV-CLOCK, R1-087).
- `"the constants are the frozen ones"` — `FOLD_MAPPER === 'FoldMapper/1'`, `D_CAP === 5_000`, `LOCAL_MAP_CAP_MS === 200 === BUDGETS.mapLocalMs`, `D_REANCHOR === 1_000` (R1-036, R1-088, R1-087).

*Golden and properties (T-REG-20, T-REG-21)*
- `"FoldMapper/1 golden for rc_touch, fwd and S9"` — `golden/foldmapper1.json` holds, for every `RC_TOUCH_R2` pair, the four `fwd` variants and the first 50 steps of `s9Generator('s9-0')`, the `hunks` and the images of the named claim ranges (`compute_total` `[[5331, 5339]]`, `parse` `[[5317, 5328]]`, the setter `[[5310, 5314]]`, the S9 claim); the test recomputes and deep-equals; the spec-given entries are asserted literally (same-tail hunk `{aLo: 5342, aHi: 5341, bLo: 5342, bHi: 5350}`; the image of `[[5331, 5339]]` under `py_append_after_last_line` is `[[5331, 5339]]`, a `k == hi` insertion); the other values were pinned at the first green run and reviewed by the controller; regeneration needs `COWORK_UPDATE_GOLDEN=foldmapper1` and a reviewed diff (R1-036, R1-088, T-REG-20, REG-07, R4-17).
- `"mapping is form-invariant over BOM, CRLF, LF, Hangul, astral emoji, curly quotes and U+2028"` — fast-check ≥ 1,000 runs: random row lists over an alphabet holding `한글`, U+1F600, U+2018/U+201C and U+2028; for X and Y, `hunks(lf(X), lf(Y))` deep-equals `hunks(bomCrlf(X), bomCrlf(Y))` and `hunks(lf(X), bomCrlf(Y))`; `hunks(lf(X), bomCrlf(X))` is `[]`; random ranges map to equal results in both forms (T-REG-03, REG-01, R1-064, R1-031).
- `"S9: mapped ranges equal the oracle at every step; re-anchoring past D_REANCHOR; never the 5,000 cap"` — for seeds `s9-0`, `s9-1` and `s9-2` (400 steps each): starting from `anchor = step 0's holder` and the oracle ranges, each step maps `mapRangesLines(anchor, peer, ranges, 'fold')` and asserts equality with `oracle`; when `editDistance(anchor, peer, D_CAP)` exceeds `D_REANCHOR` the test re-anchors (`anchor := peer`, `ranges := oracle`, as EMIT's case (b) does) and counts it; at least one re-anchor happens per seed; `editDistance(anchor, peer, D_CAP)` is never null; the same holds with every buffer in its BOM+CRLF working form (T-REG-21, TC-REG-04, O-REG-MAP, INV-REG-MAP).

*Control, boundaries and the job handlers*
- `"exact-equality control: real git counts 5,960 changed lines"` (integration) — `git diff --no-index --numstat` of `big_wt.py` and `big_lf.py` prints `5960\t5960\t…` (04 §3.4's exact-equality comparison), while `hunks` returns `[]` (T-REG-03, R1-064).
- `"the region module never decides disk equality"` — a scan of `src/regions/**` finds no import of `src/engine/diskEq.ts`, no `DiskEq` name and no `hash-object` argv; zero hunks are never compared with a blob id (R1-065).
- `"FoldMapper/1 is the only line mapper here"` — `src/regions/mapper.ts` imports nothing from `src/data/linediff.ts` and no `diff`/`diff-tree`/`log -p` argv; a planted `import { matchedRows }` in it fails L-ONE-MAPPER; `matchedRows` stays 03-data's canon helper (OQ-32, 2Q-15, R4-17, REG-07).
- `"the hunks and map job handlers equal the direct functions"` — through `inlineWorkers`: `{kind: 'hunks', from, to}` → `{kind: 'ok', value}` deep-equal to `hunks(from, to)` on the `RC_TOUCH_R2` pairs; `{kind: 'map', mode: 'fold'}` with a null deadline → `mapRangesLines(…, 'fold')`; `{kind: 'map', mode: 'local'}` with an injected expired `map` deadline → `{kind: 'deadline'}` (the pool abandons a job whose deadline is expired at dispatch, Task 1); the handler's own local-mode rule is pinned by calling `handle()` of `src/regions/worker/jobs/map.ts` directly with `{deadline: {expired: () => true}}` → `'WHOLE_FILE'` (and `RegionService.mapRanges` turns `{kind: 'deadline'}` into `'WHOLE_FILE'`, Task 5); through `threadWorkers` with `testWorkerPath()` the same `hunks` job on the same-tail pair returns the same value (REG-07, R4-17, R1-042, R1-043).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-ONE-MAPPER now guards real code (`src/regions/mapper.ts`); L-UTF8-WRITE guards `src/regions/lines.ts`.

**Notes:**
- Rows passed to the mapper are Plan 2 `rows()` (each with its LF); `linesEqual` strips one LF, then one CR, then, on line 1, a leading `EF BB BF`. A final row without an LF equals the same row with one.
- The 40 ms target at D = 2,000 and the gate p95 rows of T-REG-15 are timing gates run once per OS in Task 18 (R4-64); this task pins the verdict rows only.
- `src/regions/lines.ts` lands as this task's first commit (with `test/unit/lines.test.ts`) and is merged before Task 4's table step starts, because Task 4 (parallel in Wave B) consumes `lineArray`, `decodeStrict`, `isBlankRow`, `normalizeRanges` and `rangesIntersect` (the last two live in `lines.ts` for that reason; `mapper.ts` re-exports them); Task 4 edits nothing in it. `lineArray` is a re-export of Plan 2's `rows` (one helper, never copied; §Interfaces).

**Tests:**

*Path classes (REG-05, REG-06; first match wins)*
- `"pathClassOf applies rows 1–6 in order"` — over LIVE paths with `grammarOk = () => true` unless stated: a `.py` blob holding a row `<<<<<<< ours` → `{cls: 1, decodable: true}`; a `.py` blob with a NUL at byte 10 → 2; NUL at byte 8,000 (the 8,001st byte) → not binary, class 4; `-text` or `binary` attribute → 2; mode `120000` and mode `160000` → 3; `nb.ipynb` → 3; `filter=secret` → 3; `filter=lfs` → 3; `workingTreeEncoding: 'UTF-16LE'` → 3; a decodable `.py` → `{cls: 4, lang: 'python', blockReason: null}`; a 300-row `.md` → `{cls: 5, blockReason: 'no_grammar'}`; a 301-row `.md` → `{cls: 6, blockReason: 'no_grammar'}`; `special/legacy_cp949.cs` (320 rows) → `{cls: 6, lang: null, decodable: false, blockReason: 'not_utf8'}`, and its first 120 rows → `{cls: 5, blockReason: 'not_utf8'}`; a `.cs` with `grammarOk = (l) => l !== 'c_sharp'` → class 5 or 6 with `blockReason: 'grammar_unavailable'`; a binary `.ipynb` → 2; a conflict-marker binary blob → 1; `Foo.PY` → no grammar; T-REG-13's path rows give `file` for `filter=secret`, `working-tree-encoding` and the notebook, raw-split `block` for the cp949 file over 300 rows, `file` for the 250-row and `block` for the 900-row Markdown file (T-REG-13, R1-073, R1-003, R1-004, R1-015, R1-016, R1-017, REG-05, REG-06, OQ-14).
- `"working-tree-encoding is read with the other attributes"` (integration, `attrs.wte.test.ts`) — a `.gitattributes` at EPOCH_BASE with `*.utf16 working-tree-encoding=UTF-16LE`: `AttrCache.snapshot(base, ['a.utf16', 'a.txt'])` → `workingTreeEncoding` `'UTF-16LE'` and `null`; the `check-attr` argv names `working-tree-encoding` once; Plan 2's attribute tests pass with their literals widened (R4-20, OQ-14, R1-004).
- `"Markdown follows rows 5/6"` — `special/small.md` (250 rows) → class 5 `NO_TABLE{grammarless_small}`; `special/big.md` (900 rows) → class 6 block table whose units are its blank-line blocks (R1-074, R1-003).

*Tables per language (REG-05, R1-075…R1-080)*
- `"Python units, residue, consts, gap blocks and <imports>"` — on the 25-row file of the test (`import os`, `from typing import Any`, empty, `# Compute things.`, `@decorator`, `def f(a, b=1):` with a nested `def inner():` and `return inner()`, empty, `class C(Base):` with `x = 1`, a `@property` getter `v` and a `@v.setter` setter, empty, `NAME = compute()`, `total: int = 0`, `print("side effect")`, `print("more")`): `imports` `[1, 2]`; `f` `{kind: 'function', label: {kind: 'function', qualname: 'f', simple: 'f', type: '', disc: ''}, ranges: [[4, 9]]}` (attached comment and decorator included, `inner` folded in); `C` as one non-leaf unit `{kind: 'class', leaf: false, residue: false, ranges: [[11, 20]]}` and its residue leaf unit `{kind: 'class', leaf: true, residue: true, ranges: [[11, 13], [17, 17]], parentUnitId: <C's unit>}` (C's extent minus its members' extents, the blank rows 13 and 17 between members included, R4-18; extent-convention/1 step 4); `C.v` getter `{kind: 'method', label.disc: '', ranges: [[14, 16]]}` and setter `{disc: 'setter', ranges: [[18, 20]]}` with `type: 'C'`; consts `NAME` `[[22, 22]]` and `total` `[[23, 23]]` with `kind: 'const'`, `qualname` `'NAME'`/`'total'`; a gap block `{kind: 'block', granularity: 'block', label: {kind: 'block', qualname: '', simple: '', type: '', disc: ''}, ranges: [[24, 25]]}`; `errorCount` 0; `labelCounts` has one key per unit; every unit carries `sigSlice: null` until Task 7 fills the REG-26 slices (R1-075, R1-076, R1-079, R1-002, R2-B02, REG-16, R4-19, OQ-16, OQ-31, R1-034).
- `"JavaScript and TypeScript units"` — JS: a `function` declaration, a generator, `class K` residue with `get v()` and `set v(x)` as two units (`disc` `get`/`set`), class fields in the residue, `const h = () => …`, `let g = function () {…}`, `var Q = class {…}`, top-level object literal `api = { get() {…}, put: function () {…} }` → `api.get`, `api.put`, an IIFE `(function () { function inner() {} })()` whose `inner` is a top-level unit, `const x = require('x')` and `export { a } from './a'` in `<imports>`; TS: `interface I`, `type T = …`, `enum E` → `kind: 'type'`; `export function e()` starts at the `export` row; two overload signatures plus the implementation form one unit starting at the first signature with `disc: 'overload'`; TSX: a function component and an arrow component (R1-075, R1-077, R2-B02, REG-16).
- `"C# units with CLR names and overload discriminators"` — in `namespace N { class Svc { … } }` (block-scoped) and in a file-scoped `namespace N;` file: `type` residue `N.Svc`; `Calc(int)` and `Calc(double)` → `{kind: 'method', qualname: 'N.Svc.Calc', simple: 'Calc', type: 'N.Svc', disc: '(int)'}` and `disc: '(double)'`; `Calc(int x, string s)` → `disc: '(int,string)'`; a constructor → `{kind: 'ctor', simple: 'Svc', disc: '()'}`; `~Svc()` → `{kind: 'dtor', simple: 'Finalize'}`; `operator +(Svc a, Svc b)` → `{kind: 'operator', simple: 'op_Addition', disc: '(Svc,Svc)'}`; `implicit operator int(Svc s)` → `simple: 'op_Implicit'`; `this[int i]` → `{kind: 'indexer', simple: 'Item', disc: '(int)'}`; a property, an event and a field each one unit; `[Obsolete]` above a method starts its extent; `///` doc comments above attach; a nested class recurses (`N.Svc.Inner`); `using System;` and `extern alias X;` are `<imports>`; every C# method, constructor, destructor (`disc: '()'`), operator, conversion operator and indexer carries its normalized parameter type list as `disc` whether or not it is overloaded (a method that later gains an overload keeps its label), and properties, events and fields carry `disc: ''`; no label field contains whitespace (R1-075, R1-077, R1-078, R4-19, OQ-16).
- `"extent-convention/1"` — `EXTENT_CONVENTION === 'extent-convention/1'`; a Python decorator stack starts at the first decorator; a comment block directly above (no blank row) is included and one separated by a blank row is not; a comment that is the last row of the unit above stays in that unit; `lineTable.endRow({row: 9, column: 0}) === 9` and `endRow({row: 9, column: 4}) === 10` (1-based, minus one when `column == 0`); residue is the extent minus the member extents (blank rows between members included) and is multi-range (`C` residue of a class whose `x = 1` sits between two methods → two ranges); a Python function whose body has one blank row between two statements is one `[start, end]` range containing that row (R1-077, REG-16, R4-18, glossary §23 default basis).
- `"label-convention/1"` — `LABEL_CONVENTION === 'label-convention/1'`; Python `@property` getter → `disc: ''`, `@x.setter` → `'setter'`, `@x.deleter` → `'deleter'`; JS/TS `get`/`set` → `get`/`set`; same-name definitions in sibling branches of a top-level `if`/`#if` get the branch ordinal `#0`, `#1`, … appended to their own `disc` (two Python `def open_console()` → `'#0'` and `'#1'`); `labelKey(l) === l.kind + '\0' + l.qualname + '\0' + l.disc`; `labelCounts` counts each key (R1-078, R4-19).
- `"rc_dupes and rc_valid_errors have distinct label keys"` — `dupes/rc_dupes.py`: `Account.value` getter `disc ''` and setter `disc 'setter'`, `open_console` `'#0'` and `'#1'`; four distinct keys, each count 1; `errors/rc_valid_errors.cs` `Describe()` under `#if DEBUG`/`#else` → `'()#0'`/`'()#1'` (the branch ordinal is appended to the unit's own `disc`), count 1 each (T-REG-08, R1-078, R1-080).
- `"every label field of the corpus is SEC-35-safe"` — over the tables of every Task 2 grammar fixture, each non-empty label field matches `^[\p{L}\p{M}\p{N}_.:#<>,$@\[\]()-]{1,200}$`u (test-side regex; `validLabel` itself is Task 8's) and block/file labels have four empty fields (T-REG-24's label fields, R4-19, OQ-16).
- `"error spans, error counts and label counts"` — each `errors/S7/*/broken.*` table has `errorCount ≥ 1` and an `errorSpans` entry covering the broken row; each `clean.*` has `errorCount` 0; in `py_boundary_insert_half_typed`'s post, row 5340 lies in an error span and `compute_total`'s extent is `[[5331, 5339]]`; in `py_append_after_last_line`'s post `compute_total` is `[[5331, 5340]]` (04 §3.6 "post extent 5331–5340") (R1-080, T-REG-07, T-REG-02).
- `"432 identical units in both forms"` — `table(big_wt.py)` and `table(big_lf.py)` each have `units.filter((u) => u.leaf).length === BIG_UNITS` (432; if the real table counts otherwise, the task adjusts `bigBomCrlf(5_960)`'s template schedule outside the hot zone, never the constant — Files) and are deep-equal (units, ranges, labels, `imports`, `errorSpans`, `labelCounts`, `lineCount` 5,960); the BOM+CRLF bytes as a `DiskSnapshotId` give the same table (T-REG-03, TC-REG-03, R1-035).
- `"tree-sitter rows equal 0x0A-split rows in every form"` — fast-check ≥ 1,000: Python and C# files with Hangul identifiers in comments, U+1F600 and U+2028 inside string literals, curly quotes: for each unit, `ranges` are equal between the LF, CRLF and BOM+CRLF forms, and the row of a marker comment `# MARK-<n>` equals its index in `lineArray` (TC-REG-03, R1-061, REG-01).
- `"incremental and cold parses give the same table"` — `table(post, {prevRef: base})` after `table(base)` equals a cold `table(post)` for every `RC_TOUCH_R2` pair; at most `BUDGETS.liveTreesPerWorker` (8) live trees remain after nine distinct parses (`parser.liveKeys().length === 8`, the oldest evicted) (R1-081, R4-14).

*Partition and labels (INV-REG-PARTITION, T-REG-23)*
- `"leaf units, <imports> and gap blocks partition the non-blank rows"` — fast-check ≥ 1,000 runs per language over `unitFileArb(lang)` for python, javascript, typescript and c_sharp: the ranges of leaf units, `imports` and gap blocks are pairwise disjoint, and every non-blank row outside `imports` lies in exactly one of them; no blank row lies in a gap block (a blank row inside a unit's extent, e.g. a function's internal blank line or one between a class's members, belongs to that unit, R4-18), and every function or member unit is one contiguous range (T-REG-23, INV-REG-PARTITION, R1-034, R4-18, OQ-15).

*Last-good index (REG-18 index, R2-B03)*
- `"lastGood returns the newest cached table whose covering top-level unit has no error span"` — `put` of `clean.py` (ref1), then `broken.py` (ref2, unclosed paren inside `f`): `lastGood('m.py', [[5, 5]])` → ref1's table; after a newer clean ref3 → ref3; a range inside a unit with no error in ref2 → ref2; no table without errors → null; a conflict-marker blob has no table at all (R2-B03, REG-18, R1-082, R2-B03 item 4).

*Cache (R4-14) and snapshots (REG-35)*
- `"TableCache is an LRU by blobRef with pins counted in its size"` — `new TableCache(10_000)`: `put` then `get` returns the table and `lines` the line array; an entry's size is its line-array bytes plus `UNIT_COST_BYTES` (256) per unit; puts over 10,000 bytes evict least-recently-used unpinned entries first; a `DiskSnapshotId` pinned with `pin(ref, 'd1')` survives while the cache is over its cap and still counts in `bytes`; `unpin('d1')` makes it evictable; `cacheBytes` default 314,572,800 (R4-14, R1-081, OQ-6).
- `"SnapshotStore keeps working bytes only in memory under owner tokens"` — `register('a.py', oid, bytes, 'o1')` returns `{kind: 'disk', path: 'a.py', oid}`; the same `(path, oid)` returns the same handle; `retain(id, 'o2')`, `release('o1')` keeps it, `release('o2')` drops it and `bytes(id)` then throws `SnapshotGoneError`; `JSON.stringify(id)` has exactly the keys `kind`, `path`, `oid`; `size` counts live handles (REG-35, R1-035, OQ-17, INV-REG-READ).
- `"testSnapshot computes git's blob id in memory"` — `testSnapshot(store, 'a.py', bytes).oid` equals `git hash-object --stdin` of the same bytes and no object is written (`looseObjectCount` unchanged) (R1-035, OQ-17, REG-35).

*The service (REG-05, REG-35, R2-H01)*
- `"table() builds class 4 and class 6 tables in a worker, cached by blobRef"` — `RegionService` over `inlineWorkers` and a spy counting jobs: a `.py` blob → one `table` job (`cls: 4`), a second call → zero jobs; `special/small.md` → `NO_TABLE{grammarless_small}`, conflict markers → `NO_TABLE{conflict_markers}`, a binary blob → `NO_TABLE{binary}`, `nb.ipynb` → `NO_TABLE{special}`, each with no worker job; `special/big.md` and the cp949 C# file over 300 rows → one `table` job with `cls: 6` each, giving a block table over the raw `0x0A` split with no decoding (`grammar: null`, `decodable` false for the cp949 file, every unit `granularity: 'block'`) (T-REG-13, R1-041, REG-05, REG-06, R1-004, R1-016).
- `"a parse overrun, a worker crash or a missing object gives NO_TABLE{budget}"` — `inject.expire('parse', 1)` → `NO_TABLE{budget}` and `onBudget('a.py', 'parse')` once; a stub `RegionWorkers` answering `crashed` → `NO_TABLE{budget}` and `onBudget` once; a 40-hex oid absent from the object store → `bytes()` returns `'MISSING'`, `table()` returns `NO_TABLE{budget}` and the daemon log gets one line naming the oid and the path; none of these results is cached (R2-H01, T-REG-27, REG-35, R1-081).
- `"a DiskSnapshotId table equals the stored blob's table and never reaches cat-file"` — for `py_append_after_last_line`'s post as a stored blob and as `testSnapshot(...)`: deep-equal tables; under `readGuard(repo, snapshots)` the snapshot path makes no `cat-file` call at all (T-REG-27, R1-014, R1-035, INV-REG-READ).
- `"T-REG-27 (e): a snapshot-tick oid passed instead of a DiskSnapshotId degrades, never passes"` — the oid of bytes never written (computed in memory) passed as a stored `Oid` → `NO_TABLE{budget}`, one log line with that oid and the path (the whole-file touch that follows is Task 6's `touch` step 4/1) (T-REG-27, REG-35).
- `"readGuard enforces INV-REG-READ"` — the wrapped `GitRepo` throws on a `cat-file` naming a registered `DiskSnapshotId` oid in its argv or on its `--batch` stdin, and on a `grep` whose argv names neither a tree oid nor `--cached` (e.g. `grep -e x` with no tree); it lets through `cat-file --batch` with stored oids on stdin, `ls-tree <tree>` and `check-attr -z --stdin` under `attr.tree` (the service's own reads); the whole `regionService` suite runs under it (T-REG-27, INV-REG-READ, R1-040, R1-014).
- `"the service writes nothing"` — after every test of the suite `looseObjectCount(gitDir)` and every ref are unchanged; the spy runner shows only `cat-file`, `ls-tree` and `check-attr` argvs (REG-35, R1-040).
- `"view() overlays in-memory entries on a stored tree"` — `view(tree, new Map([['a.py', snapshotId], ['gone.py', 'ABSENT']]))`: `entry('a.py')` → the snapshot, `entry('gone.py')` → `'ABSENT'`, `entry('b.py')` → the stored blob with its mode, `entry('none.py')` → `'ABSENT'` (R1-040, REG-35).
- `"the tree passes L-OFFSETS and L-REGION-WRITE"` — `npm run lint` exits 0 with `startIndex`/`endIndex`/`.column` only in `lineTable.ts` (R1-061, R1-040).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-OFFSETS and L-REGION-WRITE now guard real code (`src/regions/worker/**`, `src/regions/service.ts`).

**Notes:**
- `pathClassOf` is called only for LIVE paths (REG-32): T-REG-13's scope rows (symlink, LFS path, quarantined `.claude/settings.json`, `.gitignore` SAVE_ONLY, scanner LOCAL_ONLY → no table, no prediction, no claim op) are scope decisions made by the callers and are pinned there (Tasks 11 and 14) together with T-REG-13's runtime assert; this task pins the path-class rows, including the defensive row-3 entries `120000`, `160000` and `filter=lfs`.
- The table job carries the language's query results only as rows (through `lineTable.ts`); `Unit.sigSlice` is `null` until Task 7 fills it.
- A last-good lookup reads only cached tables; a cache miss never triggers a parse of an older blob.
- `UNIT_COST_BYTES`, the fixed query capture names, the `lineTable.ts` helpers (`rowsOf`, `endRow`, `inputEdit`), `EXTENT_CONVENTION`/`LABEL_CONVENTION`, `testSnapshot`, the required `PathAttrs.workingTreeEncoding` (with the two Plan 2 test literals widened, Table A row A-5) and the unit model of `RegionTable.units` are stated in §Interfaces.

### Task 5: Plan 3 seams and the shared Plan 4 declarations

**Tests:**

*Re-baseline (R4-01; the first test, before any other change)*
- `"every Plan 2 and Plan 3 name this contract cites exists with its cited signature"` — a `tsc`-checked file imports every Plan 2 and Plan 3 name of the §Interfaces preamble list and assigns each to the type the Plan 2 contract (with its ledger rulings) and Plan 3's `contract.md` state; at run time: `plan3Ports()` deep-equals `{editCheck: grammarlessEditCheck, t0Regions: passT0Regions, gate: openGate, symbols: noSymbols}`; `await openGate.claimStillBlocks(anyRecord)` is true; `HOLD_CODES` contains `BOARD_FULL`; `heldRanges`, `assertSPub` and `assertP` are exported by `src/holds/mask.ts`; Plan 3 Task 10's amended contract is present — `T0Verdict`'s fail form has `findings?`, `EditCheckVerdict`'s `fail`/`incomplete` have `entries?`, `CoreOptions.ports` accepts `(deps: EngineDeps, peerIndex: number) => PortsOverride`, and `ChangeSets.lastCheck` exists (X-05…X-08, X-15); the ledger records whether Plan 3 Task 17's `src/holds/revertCs.ts` exists (R4-46's caller, Plan 7's verb, consumes it, X-09); a failing assertion stops the task for a controller ruling (R4-01).
- `"Tasks 1–4 stay green on merged Plan 3"` — after the rebase onto merged Plan 3 the Task 1–4 suites pass; `grep 'coworkOff:' test/` lists only `PathAttrs` literals that carry `workingTreeEncoding` (any Plan 3 literal was widened in this task) (R4-01).

*Import order and claim-free holds (R4-02, R4-67)*
- `"each source directory imports only the directories before it"` — a scan of every `src/**/*.ts` import against X-110's one order (`src/ports` → `src/text` → `src/i18n` → `src/git` → `src/sec` → `src/data` → `src/regions` → `src/board` → `src/since` → `src/holds` → `src/claims` → `src/engine` → `src/claude` → `src/coord` → `src/lifecycle` → `src/cli`; directories not yet created are vacuous): `src/regions` imports only `src/{ports,git,sec,data,regions}` (REG-02's stricter boundary); `src/board` adds `src/regions`; `src/holds` adds `src/board` and, in `mask.ts` only, `src/regions/mapper.ts` (plus the type-only `import type` of `src/regions/editCheck.ts` in `ports.ts`); `src/claims` adds `src/holds`; `src/engine` adds `src/claims`; `src/daemon` and `src/entry` may import any; `src/data/conflict.ts` imports nothing from `src/regions` (R4-02, R1-058).
- `"holds stay claim-free"` — no `src/holds` module imports `src/claims`; `src/holds/mask.ts` is the only `src/holds` module importing `src/regions` at run time, and only `mapper.ts` (`src/holds/ports.ts`'s `import type { EditCheckEntry, EditCheckReason }` from `src/regions/editCheck.ts` is the one type-only exception, erased in the bundle); Plan 3's structural test ("the revert detector, MASK, REBASE, PARK, RESTORE and R1 modules import nothing that reads claim state") passes with `src/claims/**` and `src/regions/{touch,contained,groupName,partial}.ts` added to its forbidden set; L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS passes on the tree and fails on a planted `import type { ClaimStore } from '../claims/store.js'` in `src/holds/registry.ts` (R4-67, R4-17, B3-08).

*Held ranges on FoldMapper/1 (R4-17, B3-08)*
- `"heldRanges, assertSPub and assertP keep Plan 3's values on Plan 3's fixtures"` — Plan 3's `test/integration/heldRanges.test.ts`, `mask.assert.test.ts`, `mask.p3.test.ts` and `mask.bomcrlf.test.ts` pass unchanged with the rows now from `hunks()`; a test whose expected value would change stops the task for a controller ruling (R4-17, R4-58) (B3-08, R4-17).
- `"held rows follow FoldMapper/1 on the same-tail fixture"` — an ON_DISK `revert` PATCH with `h0 = big_lf.py`, `h1 = py_same_tail_def_after` and `S_disk[p] = h1`: `heldRanges` → `{'hot.py': [[5342, 5350]]}` (after 5341); a held pure deletion (`h0 = T9`, `h1 = T9-{3}`, Plan 3's notation) stays `[[3, 2]]`; `assertSPub` on the same record passes (B3-08, R4-17).
- `"mask.ts takes its rows from hunks() and nothing else"` — `src/holds/mask.ts` imports `hunks` from `../regions/mapper.js` and no `matchedRows`; the L-ONE-MAPPER clause this commit adds for `src/holds/mask.ts` passes on the tree, and `lintSource` of `import { matchedRows } from '../data/linediff.js'` in `src/holds/mask.ts` → L-ONE-MAPPER (the row moved here from Task 1, R4-83) (R4-17, B3-08).

*Widened Plan 3 ports (types only; A-01…A-03, A-12)*
- `"EditCheckPort widens additively and narrows Plan 3's entries element"` — type-level: `grammarlessEditCheck` still satisfies `EditCheckPort`; an `EditCheckEntry` is assignable to Plan 3 Task 10's declared element `{check?; path; line?; reason: string}` and a value of the narrowed `EditCheckVerdict` is assignable to Plan 3's (X-08); `check(repo, a, r, tip, paths)` and `check(repo, a, r, tip, paths, {csId: 'p/1', mode: 'predict'})` compile; `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [1, 4], entries: [{check: 1, path: 'a.py', reason: 'new_error_node', line: 3}, {check: 4, path: 'a.py', reason: 'label_count'}]}` and `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [1]}` are `EditCheckVerdict`s; `{verdict: 'incomplete', entries: [{path: 'a.py', reason: 'no_snapshot'}]}` is one; an exhaustive `switch` over `EditCheckReason` has exactly the 14 values of R4-42; Plan 3's `settleCandidate` and `captureStep` compile and their suites pass (2X-01, A-01, R4-39).
- `"T0RegionsPort widens additively and narrows Plan 3's findings element"` — `passT0Regions` satisfies it; a `T0Finding` is assignable to Plan 3 Task 10's declared element `{check; path; line?}` (X-07), and Plan 3's T0 drop reading `findings[0]` compiles unchanged; `check(repo, tip, out, paths, {a, p, csIds: ['p/1']})` compiles; `{pass: false, paths: ['b.py'], item: 2, findings: [{check: 2, path: 'b.py', line: 14, reason: 'incompatible_caller', symbol: {qualname: 'foo', simple: 'foo'}}]}` is a `T0Verdict`; Plan 3's T0 slot compiles unchanged (2X-03, A-02, R4-38).
- `"PublishGatePort widens additively"` — `openGate` satisfies it; a result `{block: {claimId: 'abcdefghij234567', claimIds: ['abcdefghij234567', 'bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb'], detail: {blocked: [{csId: 'p/1', claimId: 'abcdefghij234567', holder: {peer: P1, seat: 'human'}, path: 'a.py', reason: 'base-overlap'}], admitted: []}}}` compiles; Plan 3's B1 slot reads only `block.claimId` and its suites pass; `claimStillBlocks(r)` keeps its signature (2X-04, 2X-05, A-12, R4-37).
- `"SymbolPort gains an optional shadowDependency"` — `noSymbols` satisfies it; a binding with `shadowDependency(repo, shadow, csA, csR)` compiles; no Plan 3 module calls `shadowDependency` (source scan; Plan 5 wires it) (2X-06, R4-50).
- `"the forward type declarations change no behaviour"` — a `ConflictCtx` without the four hooks still type-checks and Plan 2's `conflict` suites pass unchanged; `PlacementInput`, `RevertPrediction`, `ClaimLabel`, `ClaimGrant`, `HunkAttributionPort`, `BreachPort`, `EditCheckReason` and `EditCheckEntry` are importable types, and their modules export no runtime value yet (R1-058, A-01, A-12).
- `"the edit_check trace may carry checks"` — `{kind: 'edit_check', peer, cs, verdict: 'fail', checks: [1, 3]}` and the Plan 3 form without `checks` are both `TraceEvent`s; Plan 3's emit and trace tests are unchanged (Task 10's emit always sets `checks`) (2X-07, A-03, B2-14, R4-63).
- `"the Plan 4 trace variants carry no duration, wall time, label, offset or edit-check text"` — one event each of `gate`, `claim` (every `action` value), `placement` and `breach` is recorded by `TraceRecorder` and returned by `ofKind`; across the four variants no key is named `durationMs`, `wallMs`, `label`, `line`, `offset`, `startIndex` or `reason` other than `gate.blocked[].reason` (a `TouchReason` or `'claim_overlap'`, the `GateBlocked.reason` union) (B2-14, R4-63).

*Mutants (R4-62)*
- `"MutantId gains M21, M22 and M46_1…M46_4, reaching src/claims as flags"` — `MutantId` accepts the six new ids beside Plan 2's and Plan 3's; `claimHostOf(core).flags` is `{perPcForeignness: false, matchByLabel: false, skipChecks: ∅}` without mutants, `perPcForeignness: true` with `testMutants = {M21}`, `matchByLabel: true` with `{M22}`, `skipChecks = {2, 4}` with `{M46_2, M46_4}` (B2-13, R4-62).

*Host, deps and the consumer map (R4-36, R1-058)*
- `"RegionHost re-attaches per core and refuses use before attach"` — `new RegionHost().get()` throws `/not attached/`; `createEngineCore(deps)` leaves `deps.regions.host.attached === true` with `get()` returning `claimHostOf(core)`; a second `createEngineCore` over the same deps re-attaches: `get()` returns the new core's view (`get().store === core2.claims`) and the first core's view is no longer reachable through the host; two cores built from two `RegionDeps` each attach their own host (R4-36).
- `"the host follows the core restartCore built"` — `makeCores({n: 1})`, the host captured as `h = p.deps.regions.host` before any restart: after Plan 2's `restartCore(p)` (a second `createEngineCore(p.deps)`), `h.get().store === p.core.claims` of the new core and, after its first `prepareIteration`, `h.get().fold()` equals the new core's `leasedFold` (never the old core's); nothing throws; every Plan 2 suite that calls `restartCore` (`restart`, `recover`, `loststate`, `start`, `incident.flow`, `publish.carries`) and Plan 2's `realEngine` RESTART path pass with `regions` bound (Tasks 9, 10, 12 and 16 bind their ports to the same host and so follow it too) (R4-36).
- `"claimHostOf exposes the core's region state"` — `host.repo === core.repo`, `host.regions === core.regions`, `host.store === core.claims`, `host.self === core.peerId`, `host.regionSpec === deps.regionSpec`; `fold()` is null before the first `prepareIteration` and afterwards `{tip: it.tip, state: it.fold, order}` (`core.leasedFold` set after every fold); `view()` is null until Task 11 binds `claimView`; `raise(code, params)` reaches `raiseRegionCode` (R4-36).
- `"the 04 §3.1 consumer map is declared on the engine"` — type-level and runtime: `Engine` has `seatEffect`, `predict`, `claimView`, `claimViewOf`, `verifiedLabel` and `predictRevert`; before Tasks 11–13 `claimView()` → null, `claimViewOf(k)` → `[]`, `verifiedLabel(id, a)` → null, and `seatEffect`, `predict` and `predictRevert` reject with `/not bound before Task 1[12]/`; `inspect().claims` deep-equals `{view: null, drafts: [], blocked: [], order: new Map()}`; `claimsHeld(state, self)` → 0 (R1-058, R4-36).
- `"productionRegionDeps loads dist/grammars and spawns the two workers"` (integration) — after a build into a temp outdir: `productionRegionDeps({fs: realFs, clock: realClock, distDir: out, securityLog})` has `runtime.dir === out/grammars`, `workers.availability()` all `ok` and `RegionDeps.securityLog === securityLog` (the Log passed in, X-34); `src/daemon/run.ts` builds exactly one security Log and passes that one (a source check: one construction of a `security.jsonl` writer in `run.ts`, none under `src/engine/` or `src/claims/`), whose one write appends one JSON line to `<logDir>/security.jsonl`; a `distDir` without `grammars/` rejects with `RegionRuntimeMissingError`; `workers.close()` ends both threads (B3-05, R4-36).
- `"runDaemon closes the worker pool after drain and on an early exit"` (integration) — `runDaemon` over a built `dist/` with a fixture clone, stopped by SIGINT: `close()` is called exactly once, after the drain, `runDaemon` resolves and no worker thread remains (the `dist/daemon.js` process exits within Plan 2's CLI-test timeout); a start that fails after `productionRegionDeps` built the pool (`daemon.lock` already held by a planted second daemon, or `Engine.create` made to throw by a fault-injected `Fs`) also closes the pool exactly once and leaves no thread; a refusal before it (Plan 2's `START_TOOL_VERSION` below the git floor) spawns no thread at all; Plan 2's daemon CLI tests pass unchanged (R4-78, B3-05).
- `"testRegionDeps binds inline workers, the test runtime and virtual budgets"` — `testRegionDeps({clock: vclock, injector})` has `availability()` all `ok`, deadlines from `clockBudgets(vclock, injector)` and `attribution`, `breach`, `suspect` undefined (their defaults are bound by Tasks 12 and 15) (B3-05).
- `"every EngineDeps construction site carries regions and still spreads plan3Ports()"` — a source scan of the five sites finds `regions:` and `plan3Ports()` (Plan 4's `plan4Ports()` line after it is Task 16's, X-111); every Plan 2 and Plan 3 suite passes with `regions` bound (B3-05).
- `"Plan 3's per-peer CoreOptions.ports function sees each peer's deps.regions"` — `makeCores({n: 2, ports: (deps, i) => ({gate: <a recording PublishGatePort over deps.regions.host>})})` (Plan 3 Task 10's form, X-15) calls the function once per peer with that peer's deps, whose `regions` is already set (`deps.regions.host` is that peer's host), and index (0, 1), and each peer's `deps.gate` is the binding built for its own host; the static object form `{gate: openGate}` still applies to every peer as in Plan 3; the same for `makeEngines` (R4-77, Table A row A-17).
- `"Plan 4 creates no last_check store"` — `src/holds/lastCheck.ts` is Plan 3's file (its `LastCheckStore` has `record`, `get`, `pass`, `clearCs`, `noteCandidate`, `recordFor`, `passedFor`, `sharedFor`, X-05); `EngineCore` has no `lastCheck` member; a source scan finds no call of `ChangeSets.lastCheck`'s writers (`record`, `recordFor`, `pass`, `passedFor`, `sharedFor`, `noteCandidate`, `clearCs`) under `src/regions/**`, `src/claims/**` or a Plan 4 `src/engine/*` file (X-05, X-06; R4-43 as amended).

*`RegionService.hunks` and `mapRanges`*
- `"hunks and mapRanges run FoldMapper/1 in a worker over blobRefs"` — `hunks('ABSENT', blob)` → one pure insertion of all rows; `hunks(a, b)` equals `hunks(lineArray(a), lineArray(b))`; `mapRanges(a, b, [[5331, 5339]], 'fold')` equals `mapRangesLines` on the same rows; a `DiskSnapshotId` target maps from its in-memory bytes (F05's local mode); a missing blob → `'WHOLE_FILE'`; a local mapping under `inject.stallMs('map', 201)` → `'WHOLE_FILE'` and, with the optional `path` argument, `onBudget(path, 'map')` once (R4-17, R1-058).

*Codes (R4-54)*
- `"raiseRegionCode writes holdCode and the log, REGION_BUDGET at most once per path per 30 min Δmono"` — `VirtualClock`: `raiseRegionCode(core, 'REGION_BUDGET', {path: 'a.py', budget: 'parse'})` sets `core.holdCode = {code: 'REGION_BUDGET', params: {path: 'a.py', budget: 'parse'}}` and writes one `warn` log line with that code and those params; the same call at +1,799,999 ms writes nothing; `{path: 'a.py', budget: 'map'}` inside the window writes nothing (per path, not per kind); `{path: 'b.py', budget: 'parse'}` writes; at +1,800,000 ms the `a.py` call writes again; a wall-clock jump of −1 h does not reopen the window (R4-54).
- `"CLAIM_REJECTED once per (claim_id, reason); other codes every time"` — `(abcdefghij234567, 'anchor')` twice → one write; `(abcdefghij234567, 'ranges')` → a write; the first pair again after `advance(10 days)` → nothing; `REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH` raised three times → three writes; `REGION_BLOCK_ONLY` logs at `info`, every other region code at `warn` (R4-54).
- `"region codes collide with no other plan's code and carry SEC-38-clean params"` — `REGION_CODES ∪ {CLAIM_REJECTED, START_REGION_RUNTIME} ∪ REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES` is disjoint from `HOLD_CODES` and from Plan 2's code registry; the code-collision scan (X-29: one rule for all plans) collects every code name declared in any `src/**/codes.ts` module and fails on an exact duplicate name declared in two modules — never on a prefix, so a planted `CLAIM_BREACH` in `src/coord/codes.ts` passes and a planted second `CLAIM_OVERLAP` there fails; and no module other than `src/regions/codes.ts` declares any of Plan 4's names (definitions, not uses: `host.raise('REGION_BUDGET', …)` in `src/claims/t0.ts`, `raiseRegionCode(core, 'CLAIM_REJECTED', …)` in `src/engine/claimsStep.ts` or the `RegionNote` literal types in `src/claims/labels.ts` are uses and pass, R4-83); every param value of every raise in this suite is an id, a path, a number or a closed-enum string (R4-54).

*Claim ids and the store skeleton*
- `"newClaimId is RFC 4648 base32 lowercase of 10 Random bytes"` — with a `Random` returning 10 zero bytes → `'aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa'`; ten `0xff` → `'7777777777777777'`; the bytes of `helloworld` → `'nbswy3dpo5xxe3de'`; bytes `00 01 … 09` → `'aaaqeayeaudaocaj'`; 1,000 ids from `seededRandom('c')` all match `^[a-z2-7]{16}$`, are distinct and repeat exactly under the same seed; `newClaimId` reads exactly 10 bytes per call and no clock (R4-24 basis, R4-36).
- `"the ClaimStore skeleton blocks CSs and remembers rejections"` — `blockCs('p/1', 'abcdefghij234567')` → `blockedBy('p/1') === 'abcdefghij234567'`; `unblockWhere((o) => o === 'abcdefghij234567')` → null; `markRejected('abcdefghij234567', 'anchor')` → true, then false, then `markRejected(…, 'ranges')` → true; `drafts()` → `[]` (Task 11 fills drafts and deferrals, Task 14 emission) (R4-36).

*Verdicts create nothing (R2-M07)*
- `"region verdict calls leave the registry, the outbox and the object store unchanged"` — `assertNoWrites(core, fn)` snapshots the registry head, `Outbox.toJson()`, the state ref and `looseObjectCount`, runs `fn` and asserts all four unchanged; it passes for `Engine.claimView()`, `claimViewOf`, `verifiedLabel`, `emitStep`, `placementStep`, `breachStep`, `labelStep`, `core.regions.table`, `hunks` and `mapRanges`, and fails on a planted `core.holds.hold(...)` (the helper's own check); Tasks 10, 12 and 15 reuse it on the real functions (R2-M07).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS now guards real code (`src/holds/mask.ts` imports the mapper).

**Notes:**
- Task 5 declares every shared Plan 4 line so the parallel tasks after it never edit one file together: the trace variants, the `MutantId` union, `RegionDeps`, the widened port types, the claim types, `ClaimHostView` and the `Engine`/`EngineCore`/`EngineInspection` members with their stub step functions. No stub step is called from the Plan 2/3 pipeline until its owning task wires it.
- `RegionServiceDeps.regionSpec` is `runtime.regionSpec` (the tables' producer); `ClaimHostView.regionSpec` is `EngineDeps.regionSpec`, which stays `PLAN2_REGION_SPEC` until Task 16 makes them equal (R4-55); before Task 16 a fixture whose test expects the host's regionSpec to be `X = testRegionRuntime().regionSpec` sets `PeerFixture.deps.regionSpec = X` (Plan 2's seam, Task 13's notation).
- **Host attachment (R4-36).** `createEngineCore` calls `deps.regions.host.attach(claimHostOf(core))` on every call; the host is created with the deps (before any core), so a later core over the same deps — Plan 2's `restartCore`, `realEngine`'s RESTART, Task 16's upgrade — replaces the view and every port bound to the host follows it.
- **Worker pool lifecycle (R4-78).** `src/daemon/run.ts` builds `regions = await productionRegionDeps(…)` before `Engine.create` and awaits `regions.workers.close()` after drain and on every early exit (a refusal, a thrown start), so the process exits as in Plan 2; Task 16 adds the refusal code and the regionSpec assertion around it. Between Tasks 5 and 16 a `RegionRuntimeMissingError` from `loadRegionRuntime` (no thread spawned yet) propagates as Plan 2's generic start failure; this task adds no refusal code for it (`START_REGION_RUNTIME` is Task 16's).
- Type-only declarations created here for later tasks' files, exactly as §Interfaces states them: `EditCheckReason`, `EditCheckEntry` (`src/regions/editCheck.ts`, Task 10 adds the functions); `ClaimLabel` (`src/board/ops.ts`) and `ClaimGrant` (`src/board/fold.ts`), Task 8 adds the rest; `HunkAttributionPort` (`src/claims/attribution.ts`, Task 12 adds `csAttribution`); `BreachPort` (`src/claims/breach.ts`, Task 15 adds `breach` and `logBreach`); `PlacementInput` (`src/claims/placement.ts`, Task 15 adds `placementCheck`); `RevertPrediction` (`src/claims/revertPredict.ts`, Task 12 adds `predictRevert`); the `ConflictCtx` members `hasGrammar?`, `need?`, `tripwire?` and `tieBreak?` (`src/data/conflict.ts`, a Plan 2 file edited type-only here because the stub `regionConflictHooks` returns `Required<Pick<ConflictCtx, …>>`; Task 9 makes `conflict()` call them and adds `TAKE_TIP.breach?`). These are the stub step signatures' only forward types; every other name they use is declared in §Interfaces.
- `testRegionDeps` lives in `test/helpers/regionDeps.ts` (never under `src/`), the optional `edit_check.checks`, `RegionService.mapRanges`' optional `path`, `assertNoWrites` and the stub steps' behaviour before their owning tasks are stated in §Interfaces.

### Task 6: The touch predicate, containment and the group-name rule

**Covers:** R1-006, R1-007, R1-011, R1-044, R1-045, R1-100…R1-108, R1-110…R1-113, R2-B01, R2-H01 (partial-type grep row); REG-04 (touch step 1), REG-13, REG-14, REG-17, REG-33 (the containment form); T-REG-02, T-REG-10 (unit and git-grep parts), T-REG-25 (unit table), TC-REG-02, the glossary §23 default; 2Q-12, 3Q-18, R2-J05 (04 §8 Q8); R4-18 (the blank residue row), R4-44 (the partial-type grep), R4-45. **Depends on:** Tasks 3, 4. Wave C (parallel with Tasks 5, 7).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/regions/touch.ts` (re-exports `TouchReason` from `types.ts`), `src/regions/contained.ts`, `src/regions/groupName.ts`, `src/regions/partial.ts`
- Test: `test/unit/touch.test.ts`, `test/unit/touch.rcTouch.test.ts`, `test/unit/contained.test.ts`, `test/unit/groupName.test.ts`, `test/integration/partialGrep.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `TouchReason`, `Hunk`, `Ranges`, `Granularity`, `Language`, `Unit`, `TableOrNone`, `RegionTable`, `BUDGETS`, `clockBudgets`, `testBudgetInjector`, `inlineWorkers`, `testRegionRuntime`; Task 2 `RC_TOUCH_R2`, `fixture`, `bigLf`, `HOT_ZONE`; Task 3 `hunks`, `imageOf`, `mapRangesLines`, `rangesIntersect`, `normalizeRanges`, `lineArray`; Task 4 `RegionService`, `RegionServiceDeps`, `TableCache`, `SnapshotStore`, `testSnapshot`, `readGuard`; Task 2 `FixtureTouchReason`; Plan 2 `GitRepo` (`run` with `indexFile` and `abort`; no `config`, R4-44), `GitCallOptions`, `makeRepoFixture`, `Oid`; Plan 1 `VirtualClock`.
- Produces: `TouchReason` (re-export), `TouchClaim`, `TouchInput`, `touch`, `OwnClaimImage`, `contained`, `PartialTypeInfo`, `groupNameHit` (with its optional `hunk` argument), `partialTypeFiles`, `declaresPartial` (all in §Interfaces).

**Spec:** 04 §2 (image, label key), §3.1 (`touch`, `contained`), §3.6 (steps 1–6, the paragraph after them, the containment form, VERIFIED rc_touch R2), §3.12 (group-name addition, the partial-type grep and its fallback), §3.3 `extent-convention/1` step 2, §6 row "Partial-type grep fails/times out", §8 Q8, REG-04, REG-13, REG-14, REG-17, REG-33, INV-REG-NOLABEL; glossary §1 S5, §23 (attached-comment default), §26.1 W6; 11-testing TC-REG-02; rulings R4-44, R4-45.

**Tests:**

*The rc_touch R2 table (T-REG-02, TC-REG-02)*
- `"the 15 rc_touch R2 rows give their verdicts and reasons"` — for each `RC_TOUCH_R2` row: base and post tables from a `RegionService` over `inlineWorkers` (post as `testSnapshot`), the claim = the base table's unit selected by `claimUnit` with `granularity: 'symbol'` and the own regionSpec, `inBase` = its ranges (`mapRangesLines(base, base, ranges, 'local', deadline)`), `inPost` = `imageOf(hunks(base, post), inBase)`, `groupNameHit` from `groupNameHit` (C# rows) or false; `touch` over every hunk of `hunks(base, post)` gives: `py_body_edit` touch `base-overlap`; `py_append_after_last_line` touch `boundary-in-extent` (only via step 4; post extent 5331–5340); `py_decorator_under_attached_comment` touch `insert-inside`; `py_attached_comment_above` touch `boundary-in-extent`; `py_boundary_insert_half_typed` touch `boundary-in-error`; `py_decorator_only_edit` touch `base-overlap`; `py_append_setter_body` touch `boundary-in-extent`; `js_append_setter_body` touch `insert-inside`; `cs_new_overload_not_adjacent_to_held` touch `group-name` (range test disjoint, hit `Calc@30`); and no touch (`none`) for `py_new_def_after`, `py_same_tail_def_after`, `py_overload_stubs_above`, `py_sibling_edit`, `py_new_method_after_setter` and `js_getter_above_setter` (T-REG-02, TC-REG-02, R1-108, R1-100…R1-106, REG-13).
- `"a comment-only insertion directly above a held unit touches it (glossary §23)"` — Python `# note` directly above `compute_total`'s comment, C# `/// <summary>` directly above `Calc(int)`'s `[Obsolete]`-free signature, and JS `// note` directly above the setter: touch `boundary-in-extent`; the same comment separated by an empty row: no touch (TC-REG-02, R1-104, glossary §23 default).

*Steps and their order (REG-13, REG-04)*
- `"step 1: a foreign regionSpec, a WHOLE_FILE mapping or a file claim touches every hunk"` — a hunk 200 rows away from the claim: `c.regionSpec = 'f'.repeat(64)` → touch `degraded-whole-file`; `inBase: 'WHOLE_FILE'` → `degraded-whole-file`; `granularity: 'file'` → `degraded-whole-file`; with the own regionSpec, a mapped range and `symbol` → `none` (REG-04, R1-101, T-REG-02).
- `"steps 2 and 3 on a 10-row base with claim [[4, 6]]"` — a hunk modifying row 6 → `base-overlap`; a deletion of rows 3–4 → `base-overlap`; a pure insertion after row 4 → `insert-inside`; after row 5 → `insert-inside`; after row 6 (`k == hi`) and after row 3 (`k == lo − 1`) → step 4; after row 7 → `none`; a deletion of rows 1–2 → `none` (R1-102, R1-103, R1-106).
- `"step 4 decides boundary insertions by extent, error span or missing table"` — with `post` a table where the inserted rows lie in the extent of the leaf unit containing the claim's image → `boundary-in-extent`; outside it but covered by an `errorSpans` entry → `boundary-in-error`; `post` = `NO_TABLE{budget}` (and every other `NO_TABLE` reason) → `boundary-no-table`; outside the extent with no error and a table → `none`; for a `block` claim on `special/big.md`, a row inserted directly after the claimed block with no empty row → `boundary-in-extent` and a row inserted after an empty row following it → `none` (R1-104, T-REG-02).
- `"a modify hunk on a blank residue row touches the residue claim"` — Task 4's 25-row Python file; the claim is `C`'s residue (`granularity: 'symbol'`, `inBase` `[[11, 13], [17, 17]]`, R4-18); a foreign post replacing blank row 13 (between `x = 1` and the `@property` getter) by `    z = 3` gives the hunk `{aLo: 13, aHi: 13, bLo: 13, bHi: 13}` → touch `base-overlap`; the same with blank row 17 → `base-overlap`; a post replacing blank row 21 (outside `C`'s extent) by `y = 2` → `none` (R4-18, REG-15, 04 §3.3 extent-convention/1 step 4).
- `"step 5: a group-name hit touches with no range overlap; steps run in order"` — `groupNameHit: true` with a hunk 200 rows away → `group-name`; a hunk that both modifies a claimed row and has `groupNameHit: true` → `base-overlap` (the first matching step wins); `groupNameHit: false` and nothing else → `none` (R1-105, R1-106, REG-17).
- `"touch takes no registry, own-claim or label input"` — type-level: `TouchClaim` has exactly `granularity` and `regionSpec`, `TouchInput` exactly `inBase`, `inPost`, `post`, `groupNameHit`; `touch.ts` and `contained.ts` import nothing from `src/board`, `src/holds` or `src/claims` (L-REGION-WRITE) and read no `Label` field (source scan) (R1-107, REG-13, REG-14).
- `"labels can add a touch, never remove one"` — fast-check 500 runs over the 15 rows: replacing every label string of the base and post tables with random `validLabel`-safe strings never turns a `touch: true` of steps 1–4 into false, and changes verdicts only where `groupNameHit` changes (C# rows) (REG-14, R1-107, INV-REG-NOLABEL basis).
- `"touch is the one exported touch decision"` — a scan of `src/**` finds no other export named `touch`, `touches`, `isTouch*` or `touchesClaim`, and `TouchReason` has exactly the eight values of §Interfaces; type-level: Task 2's `FixtureTouchReason` and `TouchReason` are mutually assignable and `s9Generator`'s `oracle` is assignable to `Ranges` (Task 2 imports nothing from `src/regions`) (R1-006, R1-044, REG-13).

*Containment (REG-33, T-REG-25 unit table)*
- `"contained: body edits, deletions and inside insertions of an own claim"` — own claim set O = `compute_total` HELD (`inBase [[5331, 5339]]`): `py_body_edit`'s hunk → contained (condition 2); a deletion of rows 5334–5335 → contained; an insertion after row 5333 → contained (3); `py_append_after_last_line` (boundary, inserted row inside the post extent) → contained (4) (T-REG-25, R1-110, R1-112, R1-045).
- `"not contained: spanning hunks, error- or table-decided boundaries, group-name-only hits, WHOLE_FILE own claims"` — a hunk modifying rows 5339–5342 (own function and the unclaimed `compute_tax`) → not contained; `py_boundary_insert_half_typed` (decided only by an ERROR span) → not contained; the same boundary insertion with `post = NO_TABLE{budget}` → not contained; `cs_new_overload_not_adjacent_to_held` with O = `Calc(int)` → not contained (`contained` has no group-name input); O whose only member has `inBase: 'WHOLE_FILE'` → not contained for every hunk (contributes nothing to U) (T-REG-25, R1-113, REG-33).
- `"an own file claim contains every hunk on its path"` — O = one claim with `granularity: 'file'` → every hunk of the 15 rows' posts is contained (condition 1) (T-REG-25, R1-112).
- `"contained never consults the registry, labels or group names, and only narrows"` — type-level: `OwnClaimImage` has exactly `granularity`, `inBase`, `inPost`; the caller removes from O the claims first confirmed during the current wake turn and passes nothing for non-LIVE paths (quarantined paths are never contained) — those two T-REG-25 rows are pinned at the caller, Task 11 (`confirm.notOwnContained`, `predict.ownContained`); `contained` returns false whenever `touch` would return `boundary-in-error` or `boundary-no-table` for the same hunk (R1-011, R1-110, R1-111, R1-113, REG-33).

*Group names (REG-17, T-REG-10 unit part)*
- `"C# overload additions hit a claimed member by (type qualname, simple)"` — claimed `N.Svc.Calc(int)`: added unit `N.Svc.Calc(decimal)` (S6(b)) → true; added `N.Svc.Calc(long)` with `label.type 'N.Svc'` from `Svc.Part2.long.cs` → true; added `N.Svc.Reset2()` → false; added `N.Other.Calc(int)` → false; added constructor `N.Svc.Svc(int)` while the ctor `N.Svc.Svc()` is claimed → true; added operator `op_Addition` while another `op_Addition` overload is claimed → true; an added indexer `Item(string)` while `Item(int)` is claimed → true (R2-B01, REG-17, T-REG-10).
- `"TypeScript overload sets hit; Python and JavaScript never"` — claimed TS `f` (`disc: 'overload'`) with a post adding `function f(x: boolean): boolean;` → true; Python `@overload` stubs of `compute_total` (`py_overload_stubs_above`) → false; JS `get balance()` above the claimed setter → false (`lang` python/javascript always false) (R2-B01, REG-17, T-REG-10).
- `"a failed partial grep makes every hunk of every partial file of the type a hit"` — `partial = {simpleName: 'Svc', files: ['Svc.cs', 'Svc.Part2.cs'], failed: true}`: a hunk in `Svc.Part2.cs` adding `Name2()` → true; a hunk in `Other.cs` whose bytes `declaresPartial('Svc')` → true; a hunk in `Other.cs` without a partial `Svc` declaration → false; with `failed: false` the `Name2()` hunk → false (R2-B01, REG-17, R2-H01).

*The partial-type grep (git; R4-44, R4-45)*
- `"partialTypeFiles greps the private index with the spec's pattern"` (integration) — a private index (absolute `GIT_INDEX_FILE` under the fixture's cowork dir, built by `read-tree` of a commit holding `Svc.cs`, `Svc.Part2.cs`, `Other.cs` (`class Svc2`), `a[b] c.cs` and `한글.cs` (both `partial class Svc`)): `partialTypeFiles(s, 'Svc')` → `{simpleName: 'Svc', files: ['Svc.Part2.cs', 'Svc.cs', 'a[b] c.cs', '한글.cs'] (sorted by code unit), failed: false}` — a glob character, a space and a non-ASCII name come back verbatim from the `-z` output (`*` and `?` are not used: Git for Windows refuses them in index paths); the spy runner shows one argv `grep -z -n -E <pattern> --cached` with `GIT_INDEX_FILE` = the private index, carrying exactly one `-c core.quotepath=off` — the runner's forced pair — and called with no `config` option (`core.quotepath` is a Plan 2 forced key, R4-44); `partial class SvcExtra` and `partial class Svc2` do not match, `partial class Svc{`, `partial  struct Svc<T>` and `partial record Svc` at end of row match (R4-44, R4-45, REG-17, T-REG-10).
- `"an index entry naming a missing object makes the grep fail, never 'no conflict'"` — `update-index --cacheinfo 100644,<40-hex oid not in the store>,Ghost.cs` on the private index → `partialTypeFiles` returns `{simpleName: 'Svc', files: [], failed: true}` (git's non-zero exit); `groupNameHit` then answers true for a hunk in `Svc.Part2.cs` through `hunk.fileDeclaresPartial` (its post bytes `declaresPartial('Svc')`) and false for `Other.cs` (the 04 §3.12 W6 case; 04 §8 Q8 closed by R4-45) (R4-45, 2Q-12, 3Q-18, R2-H01).
- `"a grep past its 100 ms budget is a failure"` — `inject.expire('partial_grep', 1)` → `{failed: true, files: []}` with no git call; with a `VirtualClock` and a spy runner that withholds the grep's result until its `AbortSignal` fires: the call passes `GitCallOptions.abort`, stays pending at +99 ms, and after the test advances the clock to +100 ms (`BUDGETS.partialGrepMs`) the signal fires and the runner's `aborted: true` result becomes `failed: true` (R2-H01, R4-45, REG-17).
- `"the partial grep reads only the index and writes nothing"` — under `readGuard`, the argv carries `--cached`; the private index file's bytes, every ref and `looseObjectCount` are unchanged after each call (R4-45, REG-17).

**Crashpoints:** none. **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE's subcommand allow-list now guards real code (`grep` in `src/regions/partial.ts`).

**Notes:**
- `touch` and `contained` are pure: callers compute `inBase` (REG-07 local mode into `A[p]`, tombstones included), `inPost` (the image of `inBase` under `hunks(base, post)`) and the post table; the C# and TS group-name inputs come from this daemon's own tables (REG-14: labels add, never clear).
- Who looks up claims on sibling partial files for a hunk (the claim is on `Svc.cs`, the hunk on `Svc.Part2.cs`) is the caller's orchestration (Task 12's `gate`, Task 11's `predict`/`confirm`); this task supplies `partialTypeFiles`, `declaresPartial` and `groupNameHit`.
- Paths containing `:` are refused by Plan 2's `validPathSyntax` and are never LIVE, so R4-44's `:x.py` case can only arise for the pathspec lists of Tasks 7 and 10, which pin it; the partial grep takes no pathspec and returns names verbatim from `-z` output.

**Contract additions** (stated once in §Interfaces, "Touch, containment, group names …"; listed here so the task reads alone):
1. `groupNameHit`'s optional fifth parameter `hunk?: { path: string; fileDeclaresPartial: boolean }` (the 04 §3.12 failure fallback).
2. `declaresPartial(bytes, simpleName)` in `src/regions/partial.ts`.
3. The portable grep pattern of `partialTypeFiles` (the spec's trailing `\b` spelled `([^[:alnum:]_]|$)`, `<Name>` checked against `^[\p{L}_][\p{L}\p{N}_]*$` before it enters the argv); the pattern test above pins it.
