2. `callerHits(s, out, name, paths, epochBase, deadline)` in the same file — the raw union of parts (i) and (ii) before the definition/comment/string filter, so the R2-E12 property compares hit sets exactly.
3. The Python `module` `SigSlice` is carried by the `const` unit whose qualname is `__all__`, every UPPER_CASE module name is a `const` unit of its own (R4-19; the unit model in §Interfaces' `Unit` comment), `RegionTable` gains no field; C# `overloadKey` = `<type qualname> + '\0' + <simple>`.

**Spec:** 04-regions §3.1 (`regionTripwire`, `interfaceCheck` with `symbolDelta`/`referencedNames`), §3.10 (signature slices, classes, callers, callees, import names, arity, note-only cases, budget incl. the rev 2.4 split), §3.15 (forms (a)–(c), the relative-only rule), §3.18 rows "Region part of T0" and "Caller grep + filtering", §3.20 "Caller search without a stored merged tree" (the split, used here for any `out` with an overlay), REG-02, REG-24 (tripwire), REG-26, REG-27, REG-31, REG-35, §5 INV-REG-TRIPWIRE, INV-REG-READ; glossary §10 K7, §18 (forced `LC_ALL=C`), §20 "Region numbers" (stoplist, 50 hits, 150 ms on 611 files) and "T0 checks"; 05-holds HLD-48, HLD-49, HLD-50 items 1–2, §8 Q2; 03-data D9 step 5 (ii) (the same forms); tests T-REG-08, T-REG-09, TC-REG-01; rulings R4-44, R4-50.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/tripwire.test.ts` — tables from `RegionService.table` over `inlineWorkers(testRegionRuntime())`; theirs = TIP's blob, ours = the local side, out = the merge or concat output.
- `"form (a): errorCount(out) above errorCount(theirs) is a violation, equal or lower is not, and a new path counts theirs as 0"` — Python `p.py` whose theirs has one unclosed paren in `def a()` (n = `table(theirs).errorCount` ≥ 1): out = theirs with a body edit in `def b()` → `[]`; out = theirs plus an unclosed bracket in `def b()` → exactly `[{form: 'a', theirs: n, out: m}]` with `m = table(out).errorCount > n`; out with the `def a()` paren fixed → `[]`; `theirs = null` (new path) and a valid out → `[]`, an out with one unclosed bracket → `[{form: 'a', theirs: 0, out: table(out).errorCount}]` (REG-24, 04 §3.15 (a), R2-B04, R1-055 signature `regionTripwire(theirs, ours, out, decodes)`).
- `"form (b): a label key counted in out above max(theirs, ours) is a violation"` — theirs, ours and out each hold `def compute_total` once → `[]`; out holds two copies (a corrupt splice) → `[{form: 'b', key: labelKey({kind: 'function', qualname: 'compute_total', simple: 'compute_total', type: '', disc: ''}), theirs: 1, ours: 1, out: 2}]`; concat shape (ours = LOCAL): theirs 0, ours 1, out 1 `def helper` → `[]`; theirs 1, ours 1, out 2 → one form-(b) violation for `helper` (REG-24, 04 §3.15 (b), 03 D9 step 5 (ii) "no duplicated qualname").
- `"form (c): theirs decodes as UTF-8 and out does not"` — out = theirs with byte `0xFF` written into a comment (out's table is the class-5/6 table of a non-decodable path) → `[{form: 'c'}]` and no form-(a)/(b) entry (out has no symbol table); theirs cp949 (does not decode) and out cp949 → `[]` (04 §3.15 (c)).
- `"T-REG-08 unit: the absolute forms are never applied"` — `rc_valid_errors` C# file (`#if/#else` around a method header; theirs carries ERROR/MISSING nodes, the research run named `MISSING ';'`, `ERROR`, `MISSING #endif`) with an edit to another method → `[]`; the Python getter/setter + platform-branch file (`rc_dupes`: `Account.value` ×2, `open_console` ×2) with an unrelated edit → `[]`; the same two files with a second copy of an existing method (out count = max + 1) → one form-(b) violation, and with an unclosed brace in another method → one form-(a) violation (T-REG-08, REG-24 "absolute … forms MUST NOT be used", INV-REG-TRIPWIRE).
- `"TC-REG-01 tripwire counts: the same-file duplicate splices are flagged by form (b) and the undecodable one by form (c)"` — for each corrupting scenario whose manifest `caughtBy` is `tripwire_b` (S2a, S3a, S3b, S3c, S6b; R4-80): theirs = its TIP blob, ours = its local blob, out = its regenerated `corruptOut` → no form-(a) violation and at least one form-(b) violation whose `key` is the scenario's `duplicatedKey`, with `out = max(theirs, ours) + 1` for it (a splice may duplicate more than one unit, e.g. S3c's platform branches); for S8 (`caughtBy: 'tripwire_c'`) → `[{form: 'c'}]` and no form-(a)/(b) entry (its out has no symbol table); S2b, S4, S5a, S5b, S6a and S9 are not asserted here — their mechanism (`b1`, `placement` or `t0`) is asserted by Task 15's region-gap suite (04 §3.15's sentence describes the lost research corpus; T-REG-01 accepts any mechanism; TC-REG-01 O-REG-TRIPWIRE input).
- `"INV-REG-TRIPWIRE property (≥ 1,000 cases)"` — fast-check over seeded valid Python and TypeScript files of 1–20 units from a 12-name vocabulary: (i) `regionTripwire(t, o, t, …)` is `[]` for every t, o; (ii) out = t with one unit of t duplicated, where count(o) ≤ count(t) for that key → exactly one form-(b) violation for that key with `out = count(t) + 1`; (iii) out = valid t with one `(` inserted at a statement start → a form-(a) violation (INV-REG-TRIPWIRE, REG-24).
- `"regionTripwire reads tables and decode flags only"` — source scan: `tripwire.ts` imports only `src/regions/types.ts`; the result for the same tables is identical when every `Label.qualname` of `ours` is prefixed with `x` except for the key-equality cases (REG-02 verdicts only, REG-14).

`test/unit/interface.test.ts` — slices and classes over real parses (inline workers); the PRICE notation of the Notes.
- `"sigSlice: Python function, class and module slices"` — `@cache\ndef f(a, /, b, c=1, *args, d, e=2, **kw) -> int:` → `{lang: 'python', unit: 'function', params: [a posonly, b pos, c pos hasDefault, args varargs, d kwonly, e kwonly hasDefault, kw varkw] (destructured null, type null), decorators: ['cache'], returns: 'int'}`; `class Cart(Base, Mixin):` with methods `add`, `total` and `TAX = 0.2` → `{unit: 'class', bases: ['Base', 'Mixin'], methods: ['add', 'total'], assignments: ['TAX']}`; a module with `__all__ = ['f', 'g']`, `MAX_ITEMS = 5`, `helper_x = 1` → the `__all__` const unit carries `{unit: 'module', all: ['f', 'g'], upperNames: ['MAX_ITEMS']}` and `MAX_ITEMS` is a `const` unit (R1-079, R1-140, contract addition 3).
- `"sigSlice: JS/TS and C# slices"` — `export function total(items, {tax, discount} = {}, ...rest)` → `params: [items plain, (destructured ['tax', 'discount'], hasDefault), rest rest], exported: true, exportName: 'total'`; `export class Cart { add() {} get size() {} }` → `members: ['add', 'size']`; C# `public static decimal Calc(int a, ref decimal b, out int c, params int[] rest)` in `class Svc` → `{isPrivate: false, modifiers: ['public', 'static'], returns: 'decimal', params: [a plain int, b ref decimal, c out int, rest params int[]], overloadKey: 'Svc\0Calc'}`; `public int Count { get; }` → `memberType: 'int'`; a `private` method → `isPrivate: true` (R1-140).
- `"jsonKeyPaths returns sorted dot paths with [] for array elements, null for invalid JSON"` — `{"a":{"b":1},"c":[{"d":2}]}` → `['a', 'a.b', 'c', 'c[].d']`; `{` → null (R1-140 JSON row).
- `"classifyUnit: the breaking kinds per language"` — Python `f(a, b)` → null: `removed`; → `f(a)`: `param_removed`; → `f(b, a)`: `param_reordered`; → `f(a, c)`: `param_renamed`; `f(a)` → `f(a, b)`: `required_added`; `f(a, *args)` → `f(a)` and `f(a, **kw)` → `f(a)`: `varargs_removed`; TS `export function total(items)` → `function total(items)`: `removed` (export removed); TS `total(items)` → `total(items, tax)`: `required_added`; C# sole overload `Calc(int a)` → `Calc(int a, int b)`: `overload_gone`; each `{cls: 'breaking'}` (REG-26, R1-141).
- `"classifyUnit: compatible and note"` — body-only, `f(a)` → `f(a, b=1)`, `f(a)` → `f(a, *, k=None)`, TS `total(items)` → `total(items, tax = 0)`, C# `Calc(int a, int b)` added beside an unchanged `Calc(int a)`, a changed `private` C# method, and `classifyUnit(null, ours)` → `{cls: 'compatible', breaking: null}`; a decorator change and `f(a, b=1)` → `f(a, b=2)` → `{cls: 'note', breaking: null}` (REG-26, R1-141).
- `"a rename is a removal plus an addition"` — `def compute_price(…)` → `def calc_price(…)` (same body): `classifyUnit(compute_price, null)` is `removed`, `classifyUnit(null, calc_price)` is compatible, and `interfaceCheck` reports one `Breaking {name: 'compute_price', kind: 'removed', removal: true}` and `symbolDelta.added` = [the `calc_price` label] (R1-141, glossary §22 aliases deferred).
- `"classification is net over the CS, base versus ours, never per edit"` — base `f(a, b)`, an intermediate `f(a)` that is never passed, ours `f(a, b)` with a body change → no `Breaking` (REG-26 "net over the whole CS").
- `"CALLER_STOPLIST and MAX_HITS_PER_NAME"` — the set is exactly `get set run main init __init__ update load save close` (10 names) and `MAX_HITS_PER_NAME === 50` (glossary §20 "Region numbers", R1-146).

`test/unit/arity.test.ts` — `arityCompatible` over slices from real parses.
- `"Python arity: required positional, surplus positional, keywords, splats"` — `def f(a, b, c=1, *, k, m=2)`: `f(1, 2, k=3)` true; `f(1, k=3)` false (b missing); `f(a=1, b=2, k=3)` true; `f(1, 2, 3, 4, k=1)` false (no `*args`); `f(1, 2, k=1, z=2)` false (no `**kwargs`); `f(*xs)` and `f(1, 2, **opts)` → `'note'`; `def g(a, *args, **kw)`: `g(1, 2, 3, z=4)` true (R1-145).
- `"JS/TS arity: required ≤ count ≤ max without rest"` — `function t(a, b = 1)`: `t()` false, `t(1)` true, `t(1, 2)` true, `t(1, 2, 3)` false; `function u(a, ...r)`: `u(1, 2, 3, 4)` true; `t(...xs)` → `'note'` (R1-145).
- `"C# arity: some overload covers the count and every named argument"` — overloads `Calc(int a)` and `Calc(int a, int b, int c = 0)`: `Calc(1)`, `Calc(1, 2)`, `Calc(1, 2, 3)`, `Calc(b: 2, a: 1)` true; `Calc(1, 2, 3, 4)` false; `Calc(a: 1, d: 2)` false; with `Calc(int a, params int[] rest)` added, `Calc(1, 2, 3, 4)` true; types are not checked (`Calc("x")` true) (R1-145).

`test/integration/interfaceCheck.test.ts` — real git (`makeRepoFixture`, trees from `commitFiles`), real grammars, `interfaceCheck(s, {base, ours, out, csPaths, csRows, epochBase}, budgets.deadline('t0', 4))` unless a test says otherwise; `csRows` are the b-side rows of `hunks(base[p], ours[p])`.
- `"T-REG-09 unit: X7's re-merged caller of the old arity is an incompatibleCallers entry at orders.py:14"` — Task 2's X7 fixture (R4-69): base A, ours P (Alice's CS turns `lib.py` `def foo(a, b)` into `def foo(a)` and updates every caller that existed), out OUT (the CAS re-merge with Bob's side, whose `orders.py` row 14 is `    total = foo(price, qty)`) → `breaking: [{path: 'lib.py', name: 'foo', kind: 'param_removed', removal: false, label: <foo's label>}]`, `incompatibleCallers: [{path: 'orders.py', line: 14, name: 'foo', removal: false}]`, `incomplete: false`, `incompleteBudget: null`; the negative control (Bob's row 14 calls `foo(price)`, one argument) → `incompatibleCallers: []` (T-REG-09, REG-27, R1-142, R1-009, 04 §3.10 VERIFIED X7).
- `"callers the CS wrote itself are never entries, and a CS that updates every caller produces none"` — PRICE: ours makes `compute_price(qty, unit, currency)` and rewrites `orders.py:5` and `reports.py:5` with three arguments → `incompatibleCallers: []`; the same CS without the `reports.py` update → `[{path: 'reports.py', line: 5, name: 'compute_price', removal: false}]` (R1-142 "a definite call not written by this CS").
- `"orphan call: a call this CS adds to a callee TIP made incompatible"` — base `def foo(a, b)` in `util.py`; out (TIP) `def foo(a)`; ours adds rows 7–8 `def run2():` / `    return foo(1, 2)` to `main.py` (which imports `foo` from `util`) → `orphanCalls: [{path: 'main.py', line: 8, name: 'foo', removal: false}]`, `incompatibleCallers: []` (T-REG-09 orphan, R1-143).
- `"unresolved import names: Python dotted, relative and JS/TS specifiers"` — `orders.py` (in the CS) adds row 2 `from pricing import CURRENCY_DEFAULT` while out's `pricing.py` lacks it → `unresolvedImports: [{path: 'orders.py', line: 2, name: 'CURRENCY_DEFAULT', removal: false}]`; `from a.b import n` resolves `a/b.py` before `a/b/__init__.py`; `from ..m import n` in `pkg/sub/x.py` resolves `pkg/m.py`; `import {n} from './m'` tries `m.ts`, `m.tsx`, `m.js`, `m.jsx`, `m.mjs`, `m.cjs`, then `m/index.*` in that order; an importer outside the CS (`x.py: from m import n`, row 1) of a name this CS removed from `m.py` → `{path: 'x.py', line: 1, name: 'n', removal: true}` (T-REG-09 "a removed name imported via `from m import n`", R1-144).
- `"possible-only hits, more than 50 hits and stoplist names produce notes only"` — `obj.compute_price(1, 2)` on an unknown receiver → note `possible`, no entry; a breaking change to a name with 51 definite-looking hits → one note `too_many_hits`, no entry; removing `def save(self)` → note `stoplist`, no entry; a JSON key rename in `config.json` → note `json_key`, no `Breaking` (T-REG-09, R1-146, REG-27).
- `"an insertion before a unit produces no breaking entry"` — ours inserts `def tax(x):` above `compute_price` (every later row shifts by 3) → `breaking: []`, `symbolDelta.added` = [the `tax` label] (T-REG-09 last clause).
- `"definite versus possible callers"` — definite: a same-file bare call; `from pricing import compute_price` + `compute_price(…)`; `import pricing` + `pricing.compute_price(…)`; `self.m()` inside the defining class; a TS named import from `./total` + `total(…)`; `this.m()` inside the class; a C# call inside the same type, inside another partial part of it, and `Svc.Calc(…)`; possible: `x.compute_price(…)` with an unknown receiver (R1-142).
- `"hits in the definition itself, in comments and in strings are dropped, except UPPER_CASE names in strings"` — `# compute_price(1)` and `"compute_price(1, 2)"` give no caller; removing `MAX_ITEMS` keeps the hit in `getattr(cfg, "MAX_ITEMS")` as a `possible` note (R1-142).
- `"symbolDelta and referencedNames (05 §8 Q2 default)"` — ours adds `CURRENCY_DEFAULT = 'EUR'` and `def fmt2(x)`, appends `rate=1` to `fmt(x)` and gives `compute_price` a required `currency`; its added rows include `from decimal import Decimal` and a call `fmt2(CURRENCY_DEFAULT)` → `symbolDelta.added` = [`const CURRENCY_DEFAULT`, `function fmt2`], `symbolDelta.signatureChanged` = [`compute_price`, `fmt`] (labels; a body-only change is not listed), `referencedNames.imports` = `['Decimal']`, `referencedNames.identifiers` = the sorted unique identifier tokens of the added rows (including `fmt2`, `CURRENCY_DEFAULT`, `Decimal`) (R1-148, R4-50, OQ-35, HLD-48, HLD-49).
- `"the caller grep is one git grep per breaking name with explicit pathspec magic"` — spy runner on a `compute_price` break over a tree with `pricing.py`, `orders.py`, `README.md`: exactly one argv `grep -z -n -w -F -e compute_price <out tree oid> -- :(literal)orders.py :(literal)pricing.py` (paths with a symbol table only, `README.md` absent), run with `attrTree = epochBase` (so `-c attr.tree=<EPOCH_BASE>`), no `config` option (the built argv carries exactly one `-c core.quotepath=off`, from Plan 2's forced list — `core.quotepath` is a forced key, R4-44), `okCodes [0, 1]`, `literalPathspecs` unset (no `GIT_LITERAL_PATHSPECS` in the child environment), `LC_ALL=C` forced (R4-44, OQ-26, REG-27, glossary §18).
- `"paths with *, ?, [ or a leading : stay literal in the split search"` — out = `view(TIP, {'a*[b].py': <DiskSnapshotId>})` over a TIP holding `a*[b].py`, `ab.py` and `:x.py`, each calling `foo`: `callerHits` returns the overlay row of `a*[b].py`, TIP's rows of `ab.py` and `:x.py`, and no TIP row of `a*[b].py`; the git argv carries `:(exclude,literal)a*[b].py` (a glob exclude would also have dropped `ab.py`) (R4-44, R2-E12).
- `"the split caller search equals a grep of a stored copy of out over 1,000 seeded trees"` — fast-check, 1,000 cases: a TIP of 5–12 Python/TS files over a 6-name vocabulary, an overlay on 1–3 paths (one deletion, one path named with `*` or `[`); `callerHits(s, view(TIP, overlay), name, …)` equals, as a set of `(path, line)`, `git grep -z -n -w -F -e <name>` on a stored copy of M' that the test builds afterwards with test-side `hash-object -w`/`mktree`; `looseObjectCount` is unchanged by `callerHits` itself (measured before the test builds its copy) (R2-E12, T-REG-28 property shape, REG-35).
- `"wholeWordRows is the byte-wise git grep -w -F predicate"` — rows `foo(x)`, `foo_bar()`, `xfoo()`, `é foo`, `foo2`, `foo` with name `foo` → `[1, 4, 6]` (bytes ≥ 0x80 are boundaries under `LC_ALL=C`) (R2-E12, contract addition 1).
- `"over budget the check stops and returns incomplete, never a pass"` — `testBudgetInjector().expire('t0', 1)` with `clockBudgets(clock, injector).deadline('t0', 4)` (2,000 ms = `BUDGETS.t0Ms × BUDGETS.t0StopFactor`) → `{incomplete: true, incompleteBudget: 'caller'}` with the X7 inputs; the same with the edit check's deadline `deadline('edit_check', 1)` (500 ms) expired → `incompleteBudget: 'caller'`; a grep whose `abort` fires (the spy delays it past the deadline) → `incomplete: true`; with no injection the X7 result is complete (`incompleteBudget: null`) (R1-147, REG-27, REG-31).
- `"the caller grep stops at its own 4× budget"` — `expire('caller_grep', 1)` with an unexpired T0 deadline (`deadline('t0', 4)`) → `{incomplete: true, incompleteBudget: 'grep'}` on the X7 inputs, the grep never started (or aborted when it had); the grep deadline is `deadline('caller_grep', 4)` (600 ms = 4 × `BUDGETS.callerGrepMs`); `interfaceCheck` raises nothing itself (R4-72, 04 §3.18 "Caller grep + filtering", REG-31).
- `"a WHOLE_FILE image of the CS's rows keeps the check complete and checks every caller"` — ours rewrites `orders.py` into 6,000 new rows (D > 5,000) keeping one `compute_price(qty, 2)` call while the CS made `currency` required → `incomplete: false` and that call is an `incompatibleCallers` entry at its `out` row (R1-147, P2; Notes).
- `"interfaceCheck writes nothing and reads no DiskSnapshotId oid through cat-file"` — the X7 check with ours and out given as `view(tree, overlay)` of `DiskSnapshotId`s, run on `readGuard(repo, snapshots)`: no guard failure, `looseObjectCount` unchanged, no ref and no index file changed (REG-02, REG-35, INV-REG-READ).
- `"every InterfaceResult field is filled"` — the X7 result has exactly the keys `breaking, incompatibleCallers, orphanCalls, unresolvedImports, notes, incomplete, incompleteBudget, symbolDelta, referencedNames`, and every `Finding.line` is a 1-based row of `out` (an overlay path's in-memory row) (R1-056, F14 line source).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE (the region greps run `grep` only, through `GitRepo.run`), L-OFFSETS (`slices.ts` and `jobs/sites.ts` read positions only through `lineTable.ts`), L-UTF8-WRITE, L-REGION-DEPS (`slices.ts` lives under `src/regions/worker/**`; `interfaceService.ts` imports no `web-tree-sitter`), L-ONE-MAPPER (rows written by the CS come from `hunks`, never `git diff`).

**Notes:**
- PRICE notation (Python, LF): `pricing.py` = `CURRENCY = 'EUR'` (1), blank (2, 3), `def compute_price(qty, unit):` (4), `    return qty * unit` (5), blank (6, 7), `def fmt(x):` (8), `    return f'{x:.2f}'` (9); `orders.py` = `from pricing import compute_price` (1), blank (2, 3), `def checkout(qty):` (4), `    return compute_price(qty, 2)` (5); `reports.py` = `import pricing` (1), blank (2, 3), `def report(q):` (4), `    return pricing.compute_price(q, 3)` (5). Tasks 9, 10 and 12 reuse it.
- `slices.ts` builds slices and `Site`s by walking named nodes and fields of the worker's tree; it adds no query source, so `regionSpec` and Task 1's golden do not change. `inComment`/`inString` come from node ancestry.
- Callees and "not written by this CS": `interfaceCheck` maps `csRows` from `ours[p]` into `out[p]` with `mapRangesLines(…, 'local', deadline)`; a caller hit whose row lies in that image is written by the CS; a call site of `ours` in `csRows` is resolved in `out` and reported at its image row (a TOMBSTONE image drops it). A `WHOLE_FILE` image (D > 5,000 or the local cap) counts no row of `out[p]` as written by the CS, so every definite caller there is checked (P2: more entries, never fewer), and the CS's new calls on p are then taken from `out[p]`'s call sites (their `out` rows); the result stays complete, so a whole-file rewrite cannot keep T0 `incomplete` forever.
- The deadline is the caller's: T0 (Task 9) passes `deadline('t0', 4)`, the edit check (Task 10) `deadline('edit_check', 1)`; the caller search also runs under its own `deadline('caller_grep', 4)` (600 ms), created when the search starts, and every grep's `abort` fires at the earlier of the two (a local helper of `interfaceService.ts`); `incompleteBudget` names the one that expired (R4-72). `REGION_BUDGET` (`t0` or `grep`) is raised by the T0 binding, never here, and never for the edit check.
- X7 (R4-69) is Task 2's `lib.py`/`foo` fixture with its own `orders.py`; the PRICE notation below is a separate set of files for the other tests.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/regions/foldBatch.ts`, `src/claims/overlapPairs.ts`, `src/claims/foldInputs.ts`
- Modify: `src/regions/foldOverlap.ts` (Task 1 created it with the type `OverlapClaim`; keep it verbatim and add `foldOverlap`), `src/board/ops.ts` (`ClaimLabel`, `ReleaseReason`, `CLAIM_RELEASE_REASONS`, `ClaimOp`, `ReleaseOp`, `isAbsentPathForm`; `OpaqueOp.type` loses `'claim' | 'release'`; field-by-field parse; `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES` gains both), `src/board/fold.ts` (`ClaimGrant`, `ClaimRec`, typed `FoldState.claims`, `parseFold` of claims, the `claim`/`release` rows, the claim effects of `join`/`leave`/`seat_end`/`save`, fold step 4 grant landings, claim compaction and the ended-seat reference check, `ClaimFoldNeeds`, `claimFoldNeeds`, `ClaimFoldInputs`, `ClaimInputsResolver`, `FoldObjectMissingError`, the optional fourth argument of `foldCommit`, `OwnRejectionReason`, `OwnRejection.claimId`/`overlaps`/`claimReason`, `applyGrant`, `GrantEndCause`, `endGrants`, `reservationValid`, `staleClaims`, and for Plan 6 `endGrant`, `releaseClaimsByAnswer`, `releaseStaleClaims` (X-25, Plan 6 reviewer), `claimCreatesRecord`, `Folder`'s `claimInputs`, `takeClaimRejections`, `claimOrder`), `src/sec/validators.ts` (`IdKind` + `'claim'`, `validLabel`), `src/regions/worker/jobs/foldOverlap.ts` (Task 1's stub → the `fold_overlap` handler), `src/engine/core.ts` (`createEngineCore` constructs `Folder` with `claimFoldResolver(repo, regions)`; `Prepared` gains `{ok: false, why: 'fold_object_missing', oid}`, `prepareIteration` catches `FoldObjectMissingError` and keeps `EngineCore.foldMissing`, contract addition 7, R4-76), `src/engine/status.ts` (`NotConvergedCause` and `NotConvergedWhy` gain `'fold_object_missing'`, degraded like `push_unresolved`, X-31) and `src/engine/loop.ts` (only the one `notConvergedCause` expression of `iterate()`'s status inputs gains the `fold_object_missing` branch; no `Prepared.why` case — the landed `iterate()` has no exhaustive switch, X-31, flag 15 closed), `test/helpers/foldBoard.ts` (claim and release lines; `generateClaimBoard`, `importClaimBoard`, contract addition 6); the Plan 2 fixtures that typing breaks (approved, X-32; Notes): `test/unit/ops.test.ts`, `test/unit/fold.test.ts`, `test/unit/outbox.test.ts`; when 8a Task 2 has merged, 8a's `test/unit/viz/verdicts.test.ts` claim/release rows (X-90, Notes); no viewer file and no viewer row (8a Task 20, then follow-up 4-V, R4-68)
- Test: `test/unit/ops.claim.test.ts`, `test/unit/fold.claim.test.ts`, `test/unit/foldOverlap.test.ts`, `test/unit/grants.test.ts`, `test/integration/foldClaims.test.ts`, `test/unit/overlapPairs.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Plan 2 `OpType`, `Op`, `OpDraft`, `OpaqueOp`, `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES`, `parseOpLine`, `serializeOps`, `OP_MAX_LINE_BYTES`, `BoardDraft`, `BoardCommitData`, `CsTrailer`, `WriterId`, `buildBoardCommit`, `readBoardCommit`, `FoldState`, `FoldStepResult`, `FoldOutcome`, `foldCommit`, `serializeFold`, `parseFold`, `initialFoldState`, `Folder` (`foldTo`, `verification`), `OwnRejection`, `CHECKPOINT_EVERY`, `COMPACT_AGE`, `validId`, `validPathSyntax`, `parseOpId`, `canonicalJson`, `GitRepo` (`readTreeMap`, `readBlobs`, `isAncestor`, `hasObject`), `Oid`, `PeerId`, `seededRandom`, `generateBoard`, `genDraft`, `foldAll`, `honestCheckpoints`, `importBoard`, `makeRepoFixture`, `commitFiles`, `makeCores`; Plan 3 `OwnRejectionReason` (`'prev_rev' | 'validator' | 'held_subtree' | 'local_only_stub'`), `Folder.takeHoldRejections` (the pattern), record-cap counting of `hold` records; Plan 8a `OpVerdict`, `FoldStepResult.opVerdicts` (when landed, V-01); Task 1 `RegionJob` (`fold_overlap`), `RegionWorkers`, `inlineWorkers`, `testRegionRuntime`; Task 3 `mapRangesLines` (`'fold'`), `lineArray`, `normalizeRanges`, `rangesIntersect`, `D_CAP`; Task 4 `RegionService` (`lines`, `deps.workers`); Task 5 `EngineCore.regions`, `ClaimHostView`, `RegionHost`.
- Produces: `ReleaseReason`, `CLAIM_RELEASE_REASONS`, `ClaimOp`, `ReleaseOp`, `isAbsentPathForm`, `ClaimRec`, `ClaimFoldNeeds`, `claimFoldNeeds`, `ClaimFoldInputs`, `ClaimInputsResolver`, `FoldObjectMissingError`, the widened `foldCommit`, `OwnRejectionReason`, `OwnRejection` (optional claim fields), `applyGrant`, `GrantEndCause`, `endGrants`, `endGrant`, `reservationValid`, `staleClaims`, `releaseClaimsByAnswer`, `releaseStaleClaims`, `Folder.takeClaimRejections`, `Folder.claimOrder`, `validId('claim')`, `validLabel`, `foldOverlap`, `foldOverlapBatch`, `overlapPairsOf`, `claimFoldResolver`, `Prepared`'s `fold_object_missing`, `EngineCore.foldMissing`, `NotConvergedCause`'s `fold_object_missing` (`ClaimLabel` and `ClaimGrant` are declared by Task 5, `OverlapClaim` by Task 1, `FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING` by Task 1 in `REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES`); contract additions 1–8.

**Contract additions** (stated once in §Interfaces; numbered because the tests cite them):
1. `OwnRejection.claimReason` and the order of PRO-17's checks that names it, including the own malformed claim line with a valid `claim_id` (`src/board/fold.ts`, the `OwnRejection` comment).
2. `claimCreatesRecord(s, claimId)` (`src/board/fold.ts`; the PRO-06 classification Task 14's withhold uses, R4-30, R2-L18).
3. `claimFoldResolver`'s `'ABSENT'` answer for a tombstone anchor that is not an ancestor of the carrying commit's live code, and `FoldObjectMissingError` only for an object the live chain reaches (`src/claims/foldInputs.ts`).
4. `Folder.foldTo` on `FoldObjectMissingError`: nothing is folded at or after the commit that needed the object; the next `foldTo` after a fetch starts from the same place (R4-26; the `Folder` comment in `src/board/fold.ts`).
5. `Folder.claimOrder()`'s `line` (0-based index into `BoardDraft.opsLines`) and the order of claims present in the starting state (R4-29).
6. `generateClaimBoard` and `importClaimBoard` (`test/helpers/foldBoard.ts`, §Test helpers).
7. `prepareIteration` catches `FoldObjectMissingError` from `Folder.foldTo` and returns `{ok: false, why: 'fold_object_missing', oid}`; `int.fold` and `core.leasedFold` stay as they were; the iteration applies and publishes nothing; the next iteration's fetch retries; `core.foldMissing` keeps the first miss's anchor until a fold completes, and a miss older than 60 s Δmono is `not_converged` cause `fold_object_missing` (`degraded`) with one `FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING {oid}` log record at most every 10 min (R4-76, X-30, X-31; the `src/engine/core.ts` and `status.ts` comments in §Interfaces "Engine, trace").
8. `overlapPairsOf` (`src/claims/overlapPairs.ts`) is the one place that turns named needs and resolved tree entries into `OverlapClaim` pairs, the tombstone ancestry rule included; `claimFoldResolver` calls it, 8a Task 20's viewer reader calls it, and the module imports types only (R4-27 as decided by the owner on 2026-10-08).

**Spec:** glossary §3 (claim, `claim_id`, claim view, HELD/STALE), §7 (`claim`, `release`, `join.regionSpec`, `save.released_claim_ids`, "Fold", compaction), §20 "Board caps"; 02-protocol §3.3 (inputs, fold state `claims`, per-commit algorithm steps 3–4 and 7, the `join`, `leave`, `seat_end`, `claim`, `release`, `save` rows), §3.4, §3.5, §3.6 "Grants and reservations", §3.9 "Claims under another regionSpec", §8 Q6, PRO-06, PRO-14…PRO-17, PRO-19, PRO-20, PRO-23, PRO-24 (bullets 1–3), PRO-27, PRO-30; 04-regions §3.5 (op shape, absent-path form, reserved claims), §3.9, §6 "Blob object missing when folding", REG-08, REG-23, §5 INV-REG-FOLD, INV-REG-NOVALID-OVERLAP; 09-security SEC-35 (`validId`, `validLabel`, the tree part); 12-visualizer §3.3.2, R-V03; tests T-PRO-01…-04, -10…-12, -30, T-REG-14, TC-PRO-02; rulings R4-06, R4-24…R4-29.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/ops.claim.test.ts`
- `"parseOpLine types a claim op with exactly the glossary §7 fields"` — a line with every §7 field, `reserved_for_req`, and extra `text: 'x'`, `blob: <oid>` parses to a `ClaimOp` whose keys are exactly `id, type, claim_id, path, anchor, base_blob, ranges, tombstones, granularity, label, regionSpec, cs_id, holder, reserved_for_req`; `holder` keeps exactly `peer, seat, agent`; `label` exactly the five fields (B2-01, R2-L02, REG-08, R1-089, R4-25).
- `"parseOpLine refuses a malformed claim field"` — each → null: `claim_id` `'ABCDEFGHIJ234567'`, `'0123456789abcdef'` (0, 1, 8, 9 are outside `[a-z2-7]`), 15 or 17 characters; `path` `'../x.py'`, `''`; `anchor` `'xyz'`; `base_blob: null` with `ranges: [[1, 2]]`; `base_blob: null` with `tombstones: []`; `ranges` `[[0, 3]]`, `[[5, 4]]`, `[[9, 12], [1, 4]]` (unsorted), `[[1, 4], [4, 6]]` (overlapping), `[[1.5, 2]]`; `granularity: 'line'`; a `label` without `disc` or with `disc: 0`; `cs_id: 'x'`; `holder.peer: 'ABC'`; `holder.seat: 'human'`; `reserved_for_req: 'r1'`; a tombstone with `anchor: 'x'` or `ranges: []`. Accepted: `[[1, 4], [5, 6]]` (adjacent, disjoint); the absent-path form (`base_blob: null`, `ranges: []`, one tombstone); a block label `{kind: 'block', qualname: '', simple: '', type: '', disc: ''}`; `label.qualname: 'a b'` (parses; `validLabel` fails only at render) (R4-25, SEC-35, B2-01).
- `"parseOpLine types a release op; superseded is never valid in an op"` — reasons `explicit`, `answer`, `save`, `seat_end`, `stop`, `reservation_transfer` parse; `superseded` → null; a `claim_ids` entry failing `validId('claim')` → null; `claim_ids: []` parses (it folds as a no-op); `CLAIM_RELEASE_REASONS` is exactly the six (R2-L05, R4-25).
- `"isAbsentPathForm is base_blob null with ranges [] and at least one tombstone"` — the four combinations of (`base_blob` null/oid) × (ranges empty/non-empty) with one tombstone, and null/empty with no tombstone → true only for null/empty/one (04 §3.5, R1-089).
- `"PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES contains claim and release and OpaqueOp no longer does"` — type-level: `OpaqueOp['type']` is exactly `'request' | 'request_status' | 'answer' | 'note' | 'presume_absent'`; `Op` includes `ClaimOp | ReleaseOp`; at run time `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES.has('claim')` and `.has('release')` (B2-01, V-01).
- `"serializeOps writes claim and release ops as canonical lines and round-trips them"` — the canonical line of a claim op has keys in UTF-16 order at every level (`anchor, base_blob, claim_id, cs_id, granularity, holder{agent, peer, seat}, id, label{disc, kind, qualname, simple, type}, path, ranges, regionSpec, tombstones, type`), and `parseOpLine` of it is deep-equal to the op (R4-25).
- `"validId('claim') is RFC 4648 lowercase base32 of 16 characters"` — accepts `abcdefghij234567` (Plan 3's test value) and `aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa`; rejects `ABCDEFGHIJ234567`, `0123456789abcdef`, `abcdefghij23456`, `abcdefghij2345678`, `abcdefghij23456=` (R4-24, OQ-13, 2Q-10, 3Q-19, 2X-10, R1-032).
- `"validLabel applies SEC-35's pattern per non-empty field"` — accepts `compute_total`, `Svc.Calc(int)`, `List<int>`, `op_Addition`, `a.b:c#d,e$f@g[0]-h`, `계산`, `Ünïcödé`, a 200-character name; rejects `a b`, `x‮` (a bidi control, category Cf), `a\tb`, a 201-character name; `''` is not checked (R4-24, SEC-35, 2X-10).

`test/unit/fold.claim.test.ts` — Plan 2's `Board` driver folding hand-built drafts; `stubInputs({trees, overlaps})` is a `ClaimFoldInputs` built from literal maps (`treeEntry` throws on an unlisted `(commit, path)`, `overlaps` on an unlisted pair); P1 = self (epoch E1, seats S1, S2), P2 (E2, seat T1), P3 (E3, seat U1); `K(…)` a claim line, `R(…)` a release line.
- `"T-PRO-01 claim row: [join(1), seat_start(2), claim(3)] from a new epoch of a peer with last_seq 4,812 folds all three valid"` — writer seq 4, the claim anchored at `c.liveCode` with `base_blob = treeEntry(liveCode, path)` and holder the seat started by line 2 → `claims[id]` is HELD with `holder.epoch` = the new epoch, `op_id` = the line-3 id; `last_seq` = 4; `landedOwnOpIds` has the three ids; no own rejection (T-PRO-01, PRO-17, PRO-20).
- `"PRO-17 validity rows each name their claimReason"` — own lines, one per row: holder seat not live (`seat_end` folded earlier) → `{reason: 'claim_invalid', claimReason: 'validator'}`; `holder.peer` = P2 on a P1 writer, no reservation → `'validator'`; `anchor` ≠ `c.liveCode` → `'anchor'`; `base_blob` ≠ `treeEntry(liveCode, path)` → `'base_blob'`; the path absent in the anchor tree with a non-null `base_blob`, or the absent-path form while the path exists → `'base_blob'`; a tombstone whose anchor tree lacks the path → `'validator'`; a `claim_id` whose record belongs to P2/T1 → `'validator'`; a new id at 5,000 records → `'validator'` while a re-anchor of an existing id at 5,000 records is valid; every row leaves `claims` unchanged and carries `claimId` (PRO-17, R2-L03, PRO-24 bullet 3, R4-28, contract addition 1).
- `"an own malformed claim line with a valid claim_id is claim_invalid, other malformed lines keep Plan 2's type"` — own line with `ranges: [[5, 4]]` → `{reason: 'claim_invalid', claimId, claimReason: 'ranges'}`; with `holder.seat: 'human'` → `'validator'`; with `claim_id: 'x'` or no `claim_id` → Plan 2's `{reason: 'type'}` with no `claimId`, `overlaps` or `claimReason` key (the object deep-equals Plan 2's shape, R4-28); a teammate's malformed line → no rejection (contract addition 1).
- `"an own claim op rejected by the generic checks stays generic and is never K3"` — own claim at seq ≤ `last_seq` → `{reason: 'generic'}` in `ownRejections`, absent from `takeClaimRejections()` (PRO-24 bullet 1, R4-28).
- `"claim effects: new id, re-anchor, re-claim"` — new id → HELD with every op field, `holder.epoch = w.epoch`, `op_id = op.id`, `state: 'HELD'`; re-anchor (same id, same `(peer, seat)`, HELD, record with `reserved_for_req: r` and a `grant`) → `anchor, base_blob, ranges, tombstones, cs_id, label` replaced, `reserved_for_req` deleted, `op_id`, `granularity`, `regionSpec`, `grant` and `holder.epoch` kept; re-claim of a RELEASED (`release_reason: 'explicit'`, `terminal_height: 40`) or STALE record of the same holder → HELD with every field from the op, `holder.epoch` the writer's, and `release_reason`, `terminal_height`, `grant`, `reserved_for_req` absent — byte-identical to folding the same op on a state where compaction already dropped the record (R2-L04, T-PRO-02, T-PRO-03 "a re-claim clears `reserved_for_req`").
- `"the absent-path form folds HELD and its tombstones keep blocking"` — P1/S1's op for `old.py`, a path absent from the carrying commit's live code, with `base_blob: null`, `ranges: []` and one tombstone `{anchor: L3, ranges: [[10, 20]]}` where `treeEntry(L3, 'old.py')` is a blob → HELD with `base_blob: null`; a later P2 claim on `old.py` at an anchor where the file exists again, whose ranges intersect the tombstone's image (stub pair answering overlap) → invalid `claim_overlap`; the same op with `treeEntry(L3, 'old.py')` = `'ABSENT'` → `claimReason: 'validator'` (04 §3.5 absent-path form, REG-09, REG-10, PRO-17, R1-089).
- `"release releases each listed HELD or STALE claim of the writer's (peer, epoch) and skips the others"` — `R([own HELD, own STALE, P2's HELD, unknown], 'explicit')` at height 50 → the first two RELEASED with `release_reason: 'explicit'`, `terminal_height: 50`, their grants deleted (end (d)); P2's untouched; `R([P2's HELD], 'explicit')` by P1 → no-op and P1's own rejection `'type'`; a release by an older epoch of the holder peer → skipped (R2-L05, PRO-30).
- `"T-PRO-04: join, leave, seat_end and save claim effects"` — P2's `join` of a new epoch at height 60 → every HELD and STALE claim of the old epoch RELEASED `superseded` with `terminal_height: 60`, grants whose grantee is an old-epoch seat end; a rejoin of P2's `absent` epoch → its STALE claims RELEASED with no `release_reason`, its HELD ones unchanged; P2's `leave` → its claims RELEASED `stop`, grants to its seats end; `seat_end(T1)` → T1's claims RELEASED `seat_end`, grants to T1 end; P1's `save` with `released_claim_ids: [own S1 HELD, P2's HELD, unknown]` → only the first RELEASED `save` (T-PRO-04, PRO-20, PRO-23, R2-L06, B2-03).
- `"fold step 4: a closed Cowork-CS trailer by the grantee's peer ends the grant"` — claim X (P1/S1) with `grant {req_id, grantee: {peer: P2, epoch: E2, seat: T1}, cs_id: 'T1…/3', since_height: 70}`: a later P2 commit with trailer `{csId: 'T1…/3', state: 'closed'}` → `grant` deleted, X still HELD; the same trailer written by P3, `state: 'checkpoint'`, or another `cs_id` → grant kept (PRO-19 end (a), B2-03).
- `"PRO-30 lifecycle table: every fold row of 02 §3.5"` — HELD → RELEASED by `release` (each of the six reasons), `seat_end`, `leave`, a superseding `join`; HELD → STALE by `staleClaims`; STALE → RELEASED by a same-epoch rejoin, `leave`, a superseding `join`; HELD → HELD + grant (`applyGrant`) → HELD (any grant end); a reservation HELD → RELEASED by `release{reservation_transfer}`; a reservation HELD → HELD (ordinary) by its holder's re-claim; RELEASED/STALE (not compacted) → HELD by a same-holder re-claim; no other transition is reachable from a 200-op random sequence (R2-L07, PRO-30 "no TTL, renewal, dormancy, automatic grant").
- `"STALE claims never block; reservations block every seat but their holder; grants do not change blocking"` — with `stubInputs` answering overlap for the pair: a P3 claim over P2's STALE claim → HELD; over a reservation HELD by P2/T1 (`reserved_for_req: r`) → invalid for P3, while T1's own op re-anchors it and clears `reserved_for_req`; over a P1 claim granted to P2/T1 → invalid for P3 (and for P2) (T-PRO-02, R1-099, PRO-17).
- `"the later of two overlapping claims is the invalid one, within a commit and across commits"` — P1's `K(a)` at line 0 and P2's `K(b)` overlapping it at line 1 of one commit → a HELD, b invalid; the same two in consecutive commits → the second invalid; P2's own rejection `{reason: 'claim_overlap', claimId: b, overlaps: a}`; a re-anchor of b's holder over a's lines is validated the same way and folds invalid (PRO-17, PRO-24 bullet 2, R4-28, B2-04, R1-137).
- `"claim validity ignores regionSpec (T-PRO-30 fold half)"` — `K(a, regionSpec 'rs-1')` and an overlapping `K(b, regionSpec 'rs-2')` of another seat → b invalid; disjoint → both HELD; `foldCommit` has no regionSpec input (type-level: its parameters are `(prev, c, self, inputs?)`) (R2-L15, T-PRO-30, REG-04 "the fold never uses regionSpec").
- `"a label failing validLabel still folds and the fold stores the op's label verbatim"` — `label.qualname: 'a b'` → HELD with that label; folding with every label field replaced by random `validLabel` strings gives the same validity set (labels are never a fold input; 02 §8 Q6 superseded by REG-34: re-derivation is Task 13's display step) (SEC-35, R2-L16, REG-14).
- `"compaction drops RELEASED claims older than two checkpoints and never a HELD or STALE one"` — RELEASED at `terminal_height` 500 is dropped at height 1,024; at 513 kept at 1,024 and dropped at 1,280; HELD and STALE claims created at height 10 are kept at 1,024 and 1,280; `record_count` falls by the dropped count; an ended seat referenced by a STALE claim is kept at 1,024 and dropped at the first checkpoint after the claim is RELEASED and compacted (R2-L19, PRO-27, B2-03).
- `"claim records count toward the 5,000-record cap"` — a state with 4,999 records + a new claim → `record_count` 5,000; a further new claim → invalid (`'validator'`); a re-anchor and a re-claim of a non-compacted RELEASED id at 5,000 → valid; `claimCreatesRecord` is true for a never-seen and a compacted id, false for HELD, STALE and non-compacted RELEASED ids (PRO-06, R2-L18, contract addition 2).
- `"without inputs every claim effect is skipped exactly as in Plan 2"` — `foldCommit(prev, c, self)` over a commit with a valid `claim`, `release`, a superseding `join`, `seat_end`, `leave` and a `save` with `released_claim_ids`: `claims` deep-equals `prev.claims`; the claim and release lines are no-ops with no own rejection (8a verdict `unfolded`); every non-claim field equals the same commit folded with inputs (R4-26, R4-27 fallback, 3Q-02).
- `"V-01: claim and release verdicts are effective or rejected when inputs are passed"` — **[8a]** (runs once Plan 8a's `opVerdicts` has landed): a valid claim → `effective`; an overlap-invalid claim → `rejected`/`type`; a malformed claim line → `rejected`/`malformed`; the same commit without inputs → `unfolded` for both (V-01, R-V03). `opVerdicts` stays exact (X-90: effective iff `applyOp` is true and the type ∈ `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES`); because this commit adds `claim` and `release` to `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES`, it also updates their rows in 8a's `test/unit/viz/verdicts.test.ts` when 8a Task 2 has merged — rows that fold with inputs or assert `PLAN2_FOLDED_TYPES` membership flip to `effective`/`rejected`, rows folding without inputs stay `unfolded` (R4-26) — and Plan 2 code constructs no `FoldStepResult` literal (X-90's ledger note).
- `"claimFoldNeeds lists a superset of every tree entry and overlap pair foldCommit asks"` — a commit `[K(A, p, P1/S1, new), K(B, p, P2/T1), R([X]), K(C, q, P1/S2, one tombstone at anchor L3)]` on a prev with HELD X (p, P3/U1), STALE Y (p) and HELD Z (p, P1/S1): `trees` = `(liveCode, p)` ×2, `(liveCode, q)`, `(L3, q)`; `pairs` include `(X, A)`, `(X, B)`, `(Z, B)`, `(A.id, B.id)` and exclude Y (STALE) and `(Z, A)` (same holder seat); property (500 random commits from `generateClaimBoard`): a spy `ClaimFoldInputs` that throws on any key not in `claimFoldNeeds(prev, c)` never throws while `foldCommit` folds the commit (R4-26, OQ-11, 2Q-05, A-11, 2X-09).
- `"Folder.takeClaimRejections drains own claim rejections oldest first; FoldOutcome.ownRejections keeps them too"` — `foldTo` over three commits with one `claim_overlap` and one `claim_invalid` own rejection → `takeClaimRejections()` returns both in fold order, then `[]`; non-claim rejections are not drained by it (R4-28, A-04, B2-04).
- `"claimOrder: the op that last made the record HELD; a re-anchor keeps it"` — a new claim folded at height 12, line 3 → `{height: 12, line: 3}`; its re-anchor at (15, 0) → still `{12, 3}`; a release then a re-claim at (20, 1) → `{20, 1}`; a Folder starting from a checkpoint at height 256 holding claims `bbbb…`, `aaaa…` → `{256, 0}` for `aaaa…`, `{256, 1}` for `bbbb…` (R4-29, contract addition 5).
- `"parseFold reads typed claims and refuses a malformed claim entry"` — `parseFold(serializeFold(s))` deep-equals s with HELD, STALE, RELEASED, granted and reserved claims; an entry with `state: 'TENTATIVE'`, an extra key, a non-integer `terminal_height` or a `holder` without `epoch` → null (R2-L01, B2-02).

`test/unit/grants.test.ts` — the pure PRO-19 helpers Plan 6 calls (R4-06).
- `"applyGrant sets the fold shape on every listed claim"` — `applyGrant(s, [X, Y], {req_id: R, grantee: {peer: P2, epoch: E2, seat: T1}, cs_id: 'T1…/3', since_height: 70})` → both records carry exactly that `grant`; `s` is not mutated (R1-033, R2-L10, 2X-16).
- `"endGrants ends a grant at each of (a)–(d)"` — `{kind: 'landed', peer: P2, csId: 'T1…/3'}` ends it, a landing by P3 does not; `{kind: 'returned', reqId: R}` ends grants with that `req_id` only; `{kind: 'seat_end', peer: P2, epoch: E2, seat: T1}` and `{kind: 'epoch_end', peer: P2, epoch: E2}` end grants to that grantee; `{kind: 'released', claimId: X}` ends X's grant only; no cause touches a claim's `state` (T-PRO-03 "the grant ends by each of (a)–(d)", PRO-19, R2-L10).
- `"reservationValid follows 02 §3.6"` — with request `q` folded under key `"R|T1"` (`from` P3/U1 live, `to_holder` P2/T1) in a hand-built `requests` map: valid for a P2-written op with `reserved_for_req: R`, holder P3/U1 and `earlierInCommit = [{reqId: R, kind: 'release', writerPeer: P2}]`; false when the answer is missing, written by P3, the op's writer is not q's `to_holder` peer, the holder is not q's `from` seat, or the `from` seat has ended (R2-L11, PRO-19, T-PRO-03 reservation rows).
- `"before Plan 6 every reservation-shaped claim op folds invalid"` — a P2 claim with `reserved_for_req` and an earlier `answer` line in the same commit (an opaque no-op) → own rejection `claimReason: 'reservation'`; T-PRO-03's "a reservation line before the answer line is invalid" holds for the same reason (R4-06, OQ-21, 3Q-06, 2Q-04).
- `"staleClaims turns the (peer, epoch)'s HELD claims STALE and ends grants to it"` — P2/E2's two HELD claims → STALE, keeping `terminal_height` absent; a P1 claim granted to T1 loses its grant; an overlapping P3 claim afterwards folds HELD (STALE never blocks) (PRO-36 shape, R2-L07).
- `"endGrant ends exactly one grant by req_id"` (X-25) — claims X and Y granted under `req_id` R1 and Z under R2: `endGrant(s, R1, {kind: 'returned', reqId: R1}, 80)` deletes the grants of X and Y only, Z keeps its grant; every claim keeps its `state`; an unknown req id → a state deep-equal to `s`; `s` is not mutated (PRO-19 ends (a)/(b), X-25).
- `"releaseClaimsByAnswer releases the listed claims of the answering holder seat"` (X-25) — `releaseClaimsByAnswer(s, [X, Y, W], {peer: P1, epoch: E1, seat: S1}, 90)` where X is HELD and Y STALE by P1/S1 and W is P2's → X and Y RELEASED with `release_reason: 'answer'`, `terminal_height: 90`, their grants deleted; W untouched; the result equals folding the same claims with a `release{answer}` op by P1 at height 90 (02 §3.6 answer{release}, X-25).
- `"releaseStaleClaims releases a (peer, epoch)'s STALE claims"` (X-25) — after `staleClaims(s, P2, E2)`, `releaseStaleClaims(s', P2, E2, 95)` → both claims RELEASED with no `release_reason` and `terminal_height: 95` (as a rejoin of an absent epoch, Notes); P2's HELD claims of another epoch and every other peer's claims untouched; a later compaction treats them like any RELEASED claim (X-25, PRO-27).
- `"the Plan 6 helpers are pure"` — each of `applyGrant`, `endGrants`, `endGrant`, `staleClaims`, `releaseClaimsByAnswer` and `releaseStaleClaims` returns a new state and leaves its input deep-equal to a pre-call copy; the same call twice gives deep-equal results; every member of the result other than `claims` is reference-equal to the input's, and a call that changes nothing returns the input itself (X-25, R4-06; verify pass V-10, Plan 6's O-9).

`test/unit/foldOverlap.test.ts` — pure, line arrays from inline blobs.
- `"step 1: either side file-granular overlaps"` — C1 `file` with ranges `[[1, 3]]` vs C2 `[[50, 60]]` → true; C2 `file` → true (R1-135).
- `"step 4: closed-interval intersection at one anchor"` — same anchor blob X: C1 `[[10, 20]]` vs C2 `[[20, 30]]` → true; vs `[[21, 30]]` → false (adjacent claims never overlap, 04 §3.13) (R1-135, REG-23).
- `"step 2: C1 is mapped into C2's anchor blob with FoldMapper/1 fold mode"` — C1 `[[10, 20]]` at X; Y = X with 5 rows inserted above row 1 → C1's image `[[15, 25]]`; C2 at Y `[[26, 30]]` → false, `[[25, 30]]` → true; C1's tombstone `{anchor blob Z, [[40, 50]]}` whose image in Y intersects C2's ranges → true; C2's own tombstones are never tested as C2 ranges (R1-135, R1-136).
- `"step 3: a WHOLE_FILE mapping overlaps; fold mode has no time cap"` — Y a 6,000-row rewrite of X (D > `D_CAP`) → true whatever the ranges; with `testBudgetInjector().stallMs('map', 300)` the verdict and the call are unchanged (fold mode takes no deadline: type-level, `mapRangesLines(…, 'fold')` has no deadline parameter) (REG-23, INV-CLOCK).
- `"an absent-path C2 has no ranges: only step 1 can make it overlap"` — C2 `{anchorBlob: null, ranges: [], tombstones: [{…}]}` vs any symbol C1 → false and no mapping runs (a spy `lines` callback is never called); vs a `file` C1 → true (R1-136, 04 §3.9).
- `"foldOverlap reads no regionSpec, label, clock or parse"` — `OverlapClaim` has no such field (type-level); a source scan shows `foldOverlap.ts` imports only `mapper.ts` and `types.ts`; `foldBatch.ts` imports no `src/regions/worker/{parser,tables}.ts` (R1-138, REG-23, INV-REG-FOLD).

`test/integration/foldClaims.test.ts` — real git (`makeRepoFixture`), live chains from `commitFiles` or `importClaimBoard`, `Folder` with `claimFoldResolver(repo, regions)` over `inlineWorkers`.
- `"T-PRO-02 with real blobs"` — overlapping, disjoint, re-anchor, tombstone, whole-file (C2's anchor blob a 6,000-row rewrite of C1's) and different-anchor claims; over a STALE claim (valid), over a reservation (invalid unless same seat), over a granted claim (invalid for third parties); a same-holder re-claim of a RELEASED or STALE `claim_id` → HELD with the same state before and after compaction drops the old record; the same `claim_id` from another holder → invalid; the later overlapping claim is always the invalid one (T-PRO-02 verbatim, PRO-17, REG-23).
- `"T-REG-14: four peers fold 1,000 seeded claim ops identically whatever their speed or tables"` — `generateClaimBoard('treg14', 1,100)` with 1,000 claim/release ops (concurrent overlapping claims with different anchors, re-anchors, tombstones, whole-file and reservation-shaped claims) folded by 4 Folders: peer 1's resolver awaits 10× its measured time before answering, peer 2's runtime has a corrupt `tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm` (its tables differ); validity sets, own-rejection sets per writer and `serializeFold` at every height divisible by 256 are byte-identical; for every pair of claim ops of different holder seats where the later one folded invalid by overlap, test code's `foldOverlap(earlier, later)` is true (T-REG-14, R1-139, INV-REG-FOLD, O-REG-FOLD input).
- `"INV-REG-NOVALID-OVERLAP after every commit"` — on the same board, after every fold step, for every pair of active (HELD, non-STALE) claims of different holder seats on one path, `foldOverlap(earlier, later)` (claim order) is false (INV-REG-NOVALID-OVERLAP, T-REG-14).
- `"T-PRO-10 claim half: four peers, 2,000 commits, seven checkpoints"` — `generateClaimBoard(seed, 2,000)` (first seed under `npm test`, 200 seeds per OS under `npm run test:sim`, R3-55's rule); peers with `VirtualClock` wall offsets of −600 s, 0, +300 s, +600 s and one resolver slowed 10×: byte-identical canonical states at every height; the fold from genesis equals the fold from each of the 7 checkpoints; no HELD or STALE claim is ever compacted (T-PRO-10, INV-PRO-FOLD, PRO-27).
- `"T-PRO-11: fuzzed claim lines never make the fold throw and foldCommit stays ≤ 50 ms per 1,000 ops"` — 300 commits of 1,000 claim/release lines with random bytes, truncated JSON, nesting depth 70, `ranges` of `[[1, 1e308]]` and 16 KiB+ lines: `foldCommit` never throws; with inputs prefetched by the resolver, `foldCommit` alone (real clock, median of 5) takes ≤ 50 ms per commit (T-PRO-11 "excluding mapper time", R4-26).
- `"T-PRO-12: forged claim ops naming other peers are no-ops"` — 500 claim lines written by P2 whose `holder.peer` is P1 or P3, whose holder seat is another peer's, or whose epoch is retired → all no-ops; the reservation shape is also a no-op (no `answer{release}` folds before Plan 6, R4-06) (T-PRO-12, INV-PRO-WRITER).
- `"TC-PRO-02 claim part: an op folded twice is a no-op and an invalid claim op is told only to its writer"` — a P1 claim op re-sent in a later commit with the same id → generic no-op; P2's overlap-invalid op appears in P2's Folder rejections only, identically on 4 Folders (TC-PRO-02, PRO-24).
- `"a missing anchor blob stops the fold; a forged tombstone anchor folds invalid"` — the blob of a valid claim's anchor is deleted from the object store (`.git/objects` file removed by test code): `foldTo` throws `FoldObjectMissingError` with that oid, the persisted fold is unchanged, and after the object is restored the next `foldTo` gives the uninterrupted result; a claim op whose tombstone anchor is a random oid not reachable from the live chain → `claimReason: 'validator'` and the fold continues (04 §6 "the fold does not guess", R4-26, contract additions 3–4).
- `"a Folder with a resolver never folds a claim op without inputs"` — a spy around `foldCommit` sees a fourth argument on every commit `Folder.foldTo` folds when `claimInputs` is set, and none when it is not; across 20 `generateClaimBoard` seeds every valid claim op is HELD after its commit and every claim or release line is either effective or rejected (no `unfolded` verdict once 8a's `opVerdicts` exists); the test-build assertion fails a run whose resolver is replaced by one returning `undefined` inputs (R4-26 "a test-build assertion fails any `unfolded` claim verdict when inputs were passed").
- `"the resolver makes one readBlobs and one fold_overlap job per board commit with claim ops"` — spy runner and a counting `RegionWorkers`: a 10-commit board with claim ops in 4 commits → 4 `cat-file --batch` calls and 4 `fold_overlap` jobs at priority `bg` with no deadline; commits without claim ops cost none (R4-26, REG-31).

`test/unit/overlapPairs.test.ts` (R4-27 as decided by the owner on 2026-10-08)
- `"claimFoldResolver pairs through overlapPairsOf, and the in-process answers equal the worker's"` — over 20 `generateClaimBoard` seeds, the pairs `claimFoldResolver` sends to `foldOverlapBatch` deep-equal `overlapPairsOf` over the same needs and entries, and `foldOverlap` run in process on those pairs gives the worker's answers; a forged tombstone anchor (present, not an ancestor) reads `'ABSENT'` in both (contract addition 3).
- `"overlapPairsOf is pure and imports types only"` — no `Promise`, I/O or clock; an import scan of `src/claims/overlapPairs.ts` finds only type imports of `src/board/fold.ts` and `src/regions/foldOverlap.ts`, and none of `src/regions/{pool,service,runtime,cache,snapshots}.ts`, `src/regions/worker/**`, `web-tree-sitter` or `src/engine/**` (8a Task 20 bundles it into `dist/viz.js`).
- `"a missing fold object ends the iteration and the next fetch retries"` — `makeCores({n: 2})`: peer 0 already holds (has fetched and applied) the live commit the claim will anchor at; the test then deletes, in peer 0's clone, the loose object of that commit's blob of the claimed path (git never resends an object reachable from a ref the client advertises, so the next fetch does not bring it back); peer 1 lands a board-only claim op anchored there; peer 0's next `prepareIteration` fetches the board commit successfully and `Folder.foldTo` throws `FoldObjectMissingError` while resolving the claim's inputs → `{ok: false, why: 'fold_object_missing', oid: <that blob>}`, `core.foldMissing.oid` = that blob, `core.leasedFold` and the persisted fold unchanged, no apply and no `governedPush` call in that iteration, and `iterate()` resolves (no throw; Plan 2's generic `ITERATION_SKIPPED {why: 'fold_object_missing'}` line); while the miss is younger than 60 s Δmono `status.json` has no `fold_object_missing` cause and the log no `FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING`; after 60 s (virtual clock) `status.json` is `degraded` with `not_converged` cause `fold_object_missing` and one `FOLD_OBJECT_MISSING {oid}` record is logged (the code is in `REGION_LOG_ONLY_CODES`), a second one only once 600,000 ms Δmono have passed since the first, and no LOST_STATE or incident is entered; after the object is restored, the next iteration folds the claim HELD, `core.foldMissing` is null, the cause leaves `status.json` and the run continues (R4-76, X-30, X-31, contract addition 7, 04 §6 "the fold does not guess").
- `"non-claim rejections keep Plan 2's and Plan 3's shape"` — Plan 2's `fold.test.ts`/`folder.test.ts` rejection literals and Plan 3's hold-rejection tests deep-equal unchanged: a non-claim `OwnRejection` has no `claimId`, `overlaps` or `claimReason` key (R4-28).
- `"the core's Folder folds claim ops"` — `makeCores({n: 2})`: peer 1's outbox gets a `seat_start` draft and a typed claim draft anchored at its current TIP (`base_blob = TIP[p]`), one board-only publish lands both in one commit, and the claim is HELD in peer 0's `core` fold after its next iteration; `createEngineCore` passes `claimFoldResolver(repo, core.regions)` (R4-26, PRO-17).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE (`foldInputs.ts` reads trees and blobs only), L-ONE-MAPPER (`foldOverlap.ts` maps only through `mapper.ts`), L-REGION-DEPS (`src/board/fold.ts` imports `src/regions/foldOverlap.ts`'s types and nothing under `src/regions/worker/**`; R4-02 order `src/regions` → `src/board` holds).

**Notes:**
- The fold stays pure, total and synchronous: every git read and every mapping happens in `claimFoldResolver` before `foldCommit` runs (one `readTreeMap` per distinct commit, one `readBlobs`, one `fold_overlap` job); `foldCommit` reads only `inputs.treeEntry` and `inputs.overlaps`, and an unlisted key is a defect caught by the spy property above.
- `reservationValid` needs the `answer{release}` lines folded earlier in the same commit; `foldCommit` passes it the list its own `answer` branch collects, which is always empty until Plan 6 gives `answer` an effect (R4-06; Cross-plan C-10; Plan 6's `CommitCtx` builds it then, X-25).
- Rejoin of an `absent` epoch releases its STALE claims with no `release_reason` (02 §3.3 names none); `releaseStaleClaims` (X-25) is the same effect as a pure helper for Plan 6.
- **Shared fold loop (X-90).** 8a Task 2 (`opVerdicts`), Plan 3 Task 3 (hold effects) and this task edit the same `foldCommit` loop and `FoldStepResult`: this task rebases onto both, keeps `opVerdicts` exact and flips the `claim`/`release` rows of 8a's verdict test in this commit (V-01 test above). Plan 6 Task 2 rebases onto this task.
- **Viewer rows (X-91, R4-68; X-92 as decided by the owner on 2026-10-08).** Not in this task: 8a Task 20 binds `overlapPairsOf` in the viewer, then follow-up 4-V adds the claim rows; this task only keeps `src/claims/overlapPairs.ts` pure and import-light (the second test above).
- **Ruling (R4-58; approved by the 2026-10-08 reconciliation, X-32, with the Plan 2 reviewer; recorded in the Plan 2 ledger by F-P2-5).** Plan 3's own-hold-line test uses a `hold` line, so it is unaffected; the HLD-06 structural test gains `src/claims` (Task 5). Original request: Typing `claim` and `release` (R4-24, R4-25) makes Plan 2 fixtures malformed: `test/unit/ops.test.ts` (`everyType()`'s claim uses `claim_id: '0123456789abcdef'` and `label: {…, type: null, disc: 0}`; `opaqueCanon()` loses `claim` and `release`), `test/unit/fold.test.ts` (the case "claim, release, request, … fold as no-ops": its own claim line with `label: {kind: 'fn'}` now gives a `claim_invalid` rejection, so the claim and release lines become typed lines folded without inputs, verdict `unfolded`, and the other opaque types stay), `test/unit/outbox.test.ts` (`claim(n)` = `{type: 'claim', claim_id: 'cl-n', path}` becomes a typed `ClaimOp` draft). These are fixture rewrites of the kind Plan 3 Task 3 made for `hold`, not changed verdicts; the task asks the controller to confirm them before editing. If Plan 3 Task 3's test "an own hold line that fails parsing is rejected validator …" uses an own claim line with a valid `claim_id`, its expected `'type'` becomes `claim_invalid` (contract addition 1) and is part of the same request. Plan 2's `generateBoard` claim line (`{id, type, path, claim_id}`) stays malformed, so its boards' checkpoint bytes do not change.

- `"T-REG-08 T0 half: unrelated edits pass, an extra def or a new ERROR node fails item 1"` — the `rc_valid_errors` C# file and the `rc_dupes` Python file with an edit to another unit → `{pass: true}`; OUT with a second copy of `Account.value`'s getter → `{pass: false, item: 1, findings: [{check: 4, reason: 'label_count', path, symbol: {qualname: 'Account.value', simple: 'value'}}]}` (no `line`); OUT with an unclosed brace in another method → `{check: 1, reason: 'new_error_node', line: <first new span row>}` (T-REG-08, REG-24, HLD-50 item 1, INV-REG-TRIPWIRE).
- `"T-REG-09 port half: X7's old-arity caller is K7 item 2 at orders.py:14"` — X7's A, P, TIP, OUT (R4-69) → `{pass: false, paths: ['lib.py'], item: 2, findings: [{check: 2, path: 'orders.py', line: 14, reason: 'incompatible_caller', symbol: {qualname: 'foo', simple: 'foo'}}]}` (`paths` = the CS path of the breaking unit, `orders.py` not being a checked path); the negative control (Bob's row 14 a 1-argument call) → `{pass: true}` (T-REG-09, HLD-50 item 2, R1-149, R2-M05).
- `"T-REG-09 port half: orphan call, unresolved import, removal caller, updated callers, notes"` — PRICE-based: TIP made `foo(a)` while P adds `foo(1, 2)` at `main.py:8` → `{check: 2, reason: 'orphan_call', path: 'main.py', line: 8}`; P adds `from pricing import CURRENCY_DEFAULT` at `orders.py:2` and OUT's `pricing.py` lacks it → `{check: 3, reason: 'unresolved_import', line: 2}`; P renames `compute_price` → `calc_price` without updating `orders.py:5` → `{check: 3, reason: 'removed_name_called', path: 'orders.py', line: 5}`; P updating every caller → pass; possible-only hits, a stoplist name and a JSON key rename → pass (T-REG-09, R2-O01, R4-38).
- `"every finding carries path:line and the F14 §4 check number"` — the findings of the three tests above, checked against `t0CheckNumber` of their kind, are exactly the `check` values asserted; `undecodable` and `label_count` findings carry no `line` (FEAT-F14-07, R2-O01, A-02, 2X-03, contract addition 1).
- `"T0 runs only on paths with a symbol table and the absolute forms never apply"` — a CS also changing `notes.md` and `data.bin`: no finding for them, and `rc_valid_errors`'s pre-existing ERROR nodes give no finding (REG-24 relative forms).
- `"over 4× budget T0 is incomplete and raises REGION_BUDGET once per path"` — `testBudgetInjector().expire('t0', 1)` → `{incomplete: true}`; `host.raise` saw `REGION_BUDGET {path: 'pricing.py', budget: 't0'}` once per checked path, and a second overrun within 30 min Δmono raises nothing new (`raiseRegionCode` dedupe); `expire('caller_grep', 1)` with the T0 deadline unexpired → `{incomplete: true}` and `REGION_BUDGET {path: 'pricing.py', budget: 'grep'}` once per checked path (`incompleteBudget === 'grep'`, R4-72), never `t0`; a `NO_TABLE{budget}` on a grammar path (`expire('parse', 1)`) → `{incomplete: true}` (REG-27, REG-31, 04 §6 "Caller/import check over budget").
- `"regionT0 writes no object and reads no snapshot oid"` — under `readGuard`, `looseObjectCount` unchanged across the X7 check (REG-35, INV-REG-READ).

`test/integration/conflict.grammar.test.ts` — Plan 2's `conflict()` with a test-local hook binding of the shape Task 15's `regionConflictHooks` takes: `hasGrammar(p)` = `languageOf(p) !== null` and that language `'ok'` in `availability()`; `need` from `RegionService.table(s2)`; `tripwire` = `regionTripwire(table(s3), table(s2), table(out), decodes)` with `out` registered in `SnapshotStore` under `hashRouteC(repo, out, false)` and a `deadline('parse')` per parse; `ctx.ref` answers null unless a test says otherwise (Plan 2's `canon` then keeps the block's bytes).
- `"I-DAT-05 grammar half: an EOF-append race concats with the definition separator"` — `p.py` base `def a():\n    pass\n`; TIP appends `def tip_helper():\n    return 1\n`; LOCAL appends `def local_helper():\n    return 2\n` (no blank rows) → `CONCAT` whose blob is exactly `def a():\n    pass\ndef tip_helper():\n    return 1\n\n\ndef local_helper():\n    return 2\n` (`need` = 2, T ends with 0 blank rows, L starts with 0); with both blocks already led by two blank rows the separator is 0 rows; the output parses with `errorCount` 0 and one unit per name (I-DAT-05, DAT-42, R4-47).
- `"I-DAT-05 grammar half: an import-block race concats with no separator"` — base `import os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef a():\n    pass\n`; TIP inserts `import json\n` after `import sys`, LOCAL `import re\n` → `CONCAT` = `import os\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport re\n\n\ndef a():\n    pass\n` (`need` = 0) (I-DAT-05).
- `"I-DAT-05 grammar half: two different helper definitions fall through to TAKE_TIP with a record"` — TIP appends `def helper(x):\n    return x\n`, LOCAL `def helper(y):\n    return -y\n` (no shared row, so D9 step 2 qualifies) → leg (ii) reports `helper` at 2 > max(1, 1) → `{kind: 'TAKE_TIP', entry: s3, stages: {s1, s2, s3}}`; no fused blob is written (I-DAT-05 "no fusion", REG-24 exception, R2-B04).
- `"D9 (b): a grammar path qualifies without an insertionMerge glob, a grammarless one only with it"` — with `hasGrammar` bound, the `.py` race above concats with `merge.insertionMerge` empty; `notes.md` concats only when it matches the glob; a `.cs` race on a runtime whose `tree-sitter-c_sharp.wasm` is corrupt does not qualify (TAKE_TIP) unless globbed; with `hasGrammar` absent the `.py` race is TAKE_TIP as in Plan 2 (DAT-42, B2-07, R4-47).
- `"D9 step 3 need per 03: 2, 1 or 0"` — L starting with a top-level Python `def` or `class` → 2; a method appended at the end of a class body (indented `def`) → 1; a TS `export function b() {}`, a JS `function b() {}` and a C# method appended to a class → 1; L starting with `x = 1` → 0; the `firstNonBlankRow` passed equals L's first non-blank row's row in `s2` (contract addition 2) (B2-07, DAT-42).
- `"the D9 step-3 blank rows take the base row's ending, LF when none (conflict.ts:234)"` — vector 1: base `import os\nimport sys\n` (LF), both sides rewrite row 2 to `import sys\r\n` (a clean merge, so the output row before the region ends CRLF) and both append a different top-level def with no blank rows → the two separator rows are `0A` bytes (Plan 2's code took `0D 0A` from the output row); vector 2: a CRLF-dominant base `def a():\r\n    pass\r\n` where both sides insert a different top-level def before row 1 → separator rows `0A` (Plan 2 fell back to CRLF from the base form); with `ctx.ref` = the base, canon then rewrites them as usual (the Plan 2 ledger minor, B2-07).
- `"D9 step 5 leg (ii) fails when a parse misses the 400 ms budget"` — `testBudgetInjector().expire('parse', 3)` (the s3, s2 and out parses) → the hook answers false → `TAKE_TIP` with a record, never `CONCAT` (03 D9 step 5 "the parse completes within the parse budget or the tripwire fails", REG-31).
- `"I-DAT-17 with grammars: D8 step 0a accepts the merged output only when leg (ii) passes"` — the I-DAT-17 self-concat (`A = def a(): pass`, LOCAL `+ local_helper`, TIP = `A + tip_helper + local_helper`) → `CONCAT(s3)`, no record; the variant where LOCAL also edited `def a()` after the capture → `CONCAT` of the merge-file output with the region replaced by T (the edit kept); a variant whose post-capture edit adds a second `def tip_helper` → leg (ii) fails → `TAKE_TIP` with a record (I-DAT-17, DAT-42, 03 D8 step 0a, glossary §25.2 item 8).
- `"P-DAT-02 with leg (ii): every output is a fully checked concat or TAKE_TIP with s2 = ours"` — fast-check, 1,000 random Python triples (EOF appends, import-block inserts and def insertions from an 8-name vocabulary, BOM and CRLF variants): each verdict is `CONCAT` whose blob passes legs (i)–(iii) re-checked by the test with `regionTripwire` and `rows` directly (not through the hook), or `TAKE_TIP` whose `stages.s2` is ours, or `DEFER` when `localOnly`; no output contains a marker row absent from all inputs (P-DAT-02, INV-DAT-NOMARKER).
- `"F-DAT-03: disabling each tripwire leg is caught by P-DAT-02"` — with leg (ii) disabled (the hook replaced by `async () => true`) the `helper(x)`/`helper(y)` case concats and P-DAT-02's test-side check fails on it; Plan 2's switches for legs (i) and (iii) still fail it as before (F-DAT-03).
- `"D8 step 4: tieBreak is called once per TAKE_TIP verdict and only then"` — a scripted hook answering `'breach'` → the verdict carries `breach: true`; `'k4'` → no `breach` key; it is called with `(path, s1, s2, s3)` for a content `TAKE_TIP`, a binary unit and a modify/delete unit, and never for `CONCAT` or `DEFER`; with the member absent every Plan 2 `conflict` test passes unchanged (R4-47, contract addition 3, OQ-22, 2Q-08, 2X-18).
- `"absent hooks keep Plan 2's behaviour"` — Plan 2's `test/integration/conflict.test.ts` cases (no hooks bound) produce byte-identical verdicts (R4-47 "an absent member keeps Plan 2's behaviour exactly").
- PF-DAT-02's real-time gate (a concat on the 26,436-row file ≤ 0.5 s including both parses) is not in this file: it is a row of Task 18's `test/perf/regions.perf.test.ts`, run once per OS under `test:perf:regions`, with this file's test-local hook binding (R4-64).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE (`t0.ts` and `symbols.ts` call no writing git command; the `hashRouteC(…, false)` of a concat output in the test binding writes nothing), L-ONE-MAPPER (`t0.ts` and `symbols.ts` take hunks and images only from `mapper.ts`), L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS (`src/data/conflict.ts` imports nothing from `src/regions` or `src/claims`; the hooks arrive through `ConflictCtx`).

**Notes:**
- `regionT0` is the T0 port Plan 3's slot calls on every OUT and after every CAS re-merge (HLD-50); without `ctx` it can only be `incomplete`, so until Task 15 edits Plan 3's T0 slot to pass `{a: A, p: P, csIds}`, a test that injects `regionT0` must pass `ctx` itself. Items 1–2 have no shrink clause (D2 changes nothing here; item 3 stays Plan 3's `t0Shrink`).
- T0's checked paths are the call's `paths` with a symbol table in `TIP[p]` or `OUT[p]`; `interfaceCheck` runs once per call over all of them with `deadline('t0', 4)` (2,000 ms) and `csRows` from `hunks(A[p], P[p])`.
- The D9 hooks are bound test-locally here; Task 15's `regionConflictHooks(core, it)` is the production binding and re-runs this file with it.
