**Tests:**

`test/unit/editCheck.test.ts` — pure `editCheck(s, inp)` over `TreeView`s whose overlays are `testSnapshot` handles on a committed TIP tree; `cs.merged` supplied by the test. Files (Task 7's PRICE plus): `lib.py` = `def helper(x):` (1), `    return x + 1` (2), blank (3, 4), `def compute(a):` (5), `    return helper(a) * 2` (6); `util.py` = `def foo(a, b):` (1), `    return a + b` (2); `main.py` = `from util import foo` (1) … `def run():` (4), `    return foo(1, 2)` (5); `c.py` a 1,000-byte Python file; `e.py` = `def a():` / `    return 1` / blank / blank / `def z():` / `    return 26`; `notes.md` 250 rows; `nb.ipynb`; `config.json`. A = TIP unless a test says `TIP ≠ A`.
- `"T-REG-28 pass rows"` — each `{pass: true, failed: [], incomplete: false}`: a body-only edit of `compute_price`; `compute_price(qty, unit, currency='EUR')`; a required `currency` with `orders.py:5` and `reports.py:5` updated in the same CS; a new `def tax(x)`; TIP `pricing.py` already holding an unclosed paren in `fmt` and the CS editing `compute_price`'s body only; a 250-row `notes.md` edit; an `.ipynb` edit; notes only (`obj.compute_price(1)`, removing a stoplist `def save`, a `config.json` key rename) (T-REG-28, REG-36, R2-E10, R2-K02).
- `"T-REG-28 check 1 rows with line"` — R `pricing.py` row 5 `    return (qty * unit` → `[{check: 1, path: 'pricing.py', reason: 'new_error_node', line: L}]` with `L = table(M).errorSpans[0][0]`; with TIP already holding an error span in `compute_price` and the CS adding a new one in `fmt` → `line` lies in `fmt` (≥ 8), not at the old span, because the old span intersects the REG-07 image of TIP's span; R inserting `def tax(` at row 6 (a half-typed boundary insertion) → check 1; R with byte `0xFF` in a comment → `{check: 1, path: 'pricing.py', reason: 'undecodable'}` with no `line`; M `pricing.py` holding rows 6–10 `<<<<<<< a`, …, `>>>>>>> b` → `{check: 1, reason: 'conflict_markers', line: 6}` (T-REG-28 rev 2.5, R2-E06, R2-E11, R2-O01, F-01).
- `"T-REG-28 check 2 rows: an un-updated caller outside the CS and an orphan call"` — R `compute_price(qty)` (a positional parameter removed), `orders.py` and `reports.py` not in the CS → `[{check: 2, path: 'orders.py', reason: 'incompatible_caller', line: 5}, {check: 2, path: 'reports.py', reason: 'incompatible_caller', line: 5}]` (the TIP rows of files outside the CS); `TIP ≠ A`: TIP `util.py` `def foo(a):`, A `def foo(a, b):`, R adds `main.py` rows 7–8 `def run2():` / `    return foo(3, 4)` → `[{check: 2, path: 'main.py', reason: 'orphan_call', line: 8}]` (M[main.py] = R[main.py]; the row of M) (T-REG-28, R2-E07, R2-E11).
- `"T-REG-28 check 3 rows: a removed name still called and an import of a removed name"` — R `lib.py` deletes rows 1–4 (`helper`) → `[{check: 3, path: 'lib.py', reason: 'removed_name_called', line: 2}]`; R `pricing.py` deletes `compute_price`, rows 4–5 only (55 of PRICE's 107 bytes remain, above half, so no check-4 `shrink` entry) → `[{check: 3, path: 'orders.py', reason: 'unresolved_import', line: 1}, {check: 3, path: 'orders.py', reason: 'removed_name_called', line: 5}, {check: 3, path: 'reports.py', reason: 'removed_name_called', line: 5}]` (T-REG-28, R2-E08, R2-E11).
- `"T-REG-28 check 4 rows: a duplicated def through the merge and the size part"` — `TIP ≠ A`: TIP `e.py` inserts `def go():` / `    return 1` / blank / blank above `def a()`, R appends blank / blank / `def go():` / `    return 2` after `def z()`, M their clean merge → `[{check: 4, path: 'e.py', reason: 'label_count'}]`; `c.py` TIP 1,000 bytes and M 400 bytes → `shrink`; M 0 bytes → `emptied`; M deleted → `deleted`; M 499 bytes → `shrink`; M 500 bytes → pass; a grammarless `notes.md` shrunk by 60 % → `shrink`; check-4 entries carry no `line` (T-REG-28, R2-E09, TC-REG-09 (4)).
- `"non-check reasons carry no check number and no line"` — `cs.candidate` `'unavailable'` for `pricing.py` → `[{path: 'pricing.py', reason: 'no_snapshot'}]`; `cs.merged` `{kind: 'conflicted'}` → `merge_conflict`; `{kind: 'not_supplied'}` → `no_merge`; each with `pass: false`, `incomplete: false` (R2-E04, R2-E05, R2-O02, F-02, FEAT-F12-06).
- `"NO_TABLE{budget} or a missing object on a grammar path makes the result incomplete"` — `testBudgetInjector().expire('parse', 1)` on `pricing.py` → `{pass: false, incomplete: true}`; a TIP blob oid removed from the object store → `{incomplete: true}`; neither raises `REGION_BUDGET` (R2-E10, REG-31 exception, REG-36).
- `"the sorted failed list of a multi-entry failure is exact"` — one CS with `d.py` (M = R with marker rows starting at row 4), `pricing.py` deleting `compute_price`'s rows 4–5 only (55 of 107 bytes remain: no `shrink` entry for it), `c.py` at 400 of 1,000 bytes, `e.py` duplicated through the merge and `f.py` conflicted → `failed` deep-equals `[{check: 1, path: 'd.py', reason: 'conflict_markers', line: 4}, {check: 3, path: 'orders.py', reason: 'unresolved_import', line: 1}, {check: 3, path: 'orders.py', reason: 'removed_name_called', line: 5}, {check: 3, path: 'reports.py', reason: 'removed_name_called', line: 5}, {check: 4, path: 'c.py', reason: 'shrink'}, {check: 4, path: 'e.py', reason: 'label_count'}, {path: 'f.py', reason: 'merge_conflict'}]` — the key `(check ?? 9, path, line ?? 0, reason)`; `sortEntries` of any shuffle of it returns it (R4-08, R4-42, R2-K05 "in full").
- `"entries carry only check, path, reason and line, and reason is the closed enum"` — every entry of every test in this file has keys ⊆ `{check, path, reason, line}` and a `reason` in the 14-value `EditCheckReason`; no label, symbol, name or code text appears (D1, SEC-38, R4-42, 2Q-09).
- `"mode predict drops check 4's size part and keeps its label part"` — the multi-entry CS in mode `'predict'` → the same list without the `c.py` `shrink` entry; `c.py` deleted or emptied → no entry; the `e.py` `label_count` entry stays (D2, D-02, R2-K06, R4-08).
- `"TIP[p] = A[p] means M[p] = R[p], asserted"` — a `cs.merged` naming a blob other than R[p] on a path with `TIP[p] = A[p]` → `{incomplete: true}` and one daemon-log record naming the path, never a pass (R4-41, 2Q-23, R2-E04).
- `"checked paths are the CS's LIVE paths with R[p] ≠ A[p]"` — `cs.paths` with `pricing.py` (changed), `orders.py` (R = A) and `.gitignore` excluded by the caller → only `pricing.py` gets tables and entries (a counting `RegionWorkers`) (R2-E03).
- `"line never changes the verdict"` — every pass and fail row of this file run with `computeLine: false` gives the same `pass` and `incomplete`, and the same `failed` with `line` removed (REG-36 F14 clause, contract addition 1).
- `"editCheck writes no object, index entry or ref and reads no DiskSnapshotId oid through cat-file"` — the multi-entry case under `readGuard(repo, snapshots)`: no guard failure, `looseObjectCount`, the private index bytes and `git for-each-ref` output unchanged (REG-36, REG-35, R2-E12).
- `"an in-memory M' gives the same verdict as the same content stored"` — the multi-entry case with every overlay a `DiskSnapshotId` vs the same blobs committed by test code → identical results (F-03 edit-check half, R2-E15, 04 §8 Q9).
- `"the edit check stops at 1× its budget"` — `clockBudgets(clock, injector)` with `injector.expire('edit_check', 1)` → `{incomplete: true, failed: []}`; the deadline is `BUDGETS.editCheckMs × BUDGETS.editCheckStopFactor` = 500 ms (never T0's 4×) (R2-E13, REG-36).

`test/unit/editCheck.prop.test.ts` — fast-check, ≥ 1,000 cases per property per PR (TC-REG-10; the 100,000-case nightly is Plan 7's, R4-64).
- `"TC-REG-10 (a) form invariance within a path class"` — generated (A, R, TIP) Python and TS triples in LF and in BOM+CRLF give the same verdict; cp949 vs cp949 with CRLF, and UTF-16LE vs UTF-16LE with BOM, give the same verdict; a separate case pins cp949's grammarless verdict (checks 1–3 pass, a 60 % shrink fails 4) and UTF-16LE's binary verdict (NO_TABLE{binary}: checks 1–3 pass, the size part applies) (TC-REG-10 (a) as R4-22 reads it, 2Q-11).
- `"TC-REG-10 (b) determinism with a cold and a warm cache"` — the same triple through a fresh `TableCache` and through one already holding every table, and through two `RegionService` instances → deep-equal results (TC-REG-10 (b), R2-E13 shared caches).
- `"TC-REG-10 (c) soundness against an independent parse"` — valid generated Python modules whose calls all go through `from <module> import <name>` over an 8-name vocabulary; random edits from the check classes (remove a def, change an arity, add or drop an import, add a call, break a bracket, duplicate a def): for every passing verdict, a test-side check using `web-tree-sitter` directly finds no ERROR/MISSING count above TIP's and every imported vocabulary name defined in M' with a compatible arity (TC-REG-10 (c)).
- `"TC-REG-10 (d) R == A passes"` — any generated A with R = A and any TIP → pass (TC-REG-10 (d)).
- `"TC-REG-10 (e) monotonic in callers"` — a breaking change failing check 2 with k listed callers; adding the updates of all k callers to the CS → check 2 passes; dropping one update → fails again with exactly that caller (TC-REG-10 (e), R2-E07).
- `"TC-REG-10 (f) merge awareness"` — R calls `n` through `from m import n`; TIP removes `n` from `m.py` → check 3 fails with `unresolved_import` at the import's row; the same R with TIP = A passes (TC-REG-10 (f), R2-E08).

`test/integration/editCheckPort.test.ts` — `makeCores`, `regionEditCheck` injected per peer through Plan 3's `CoreOptions.ports` function form (`ports: (deps) => ({editCheck: regionEditCheck(deps.regions.host)})`, R4-77, X-15), candidates from `checkpoint{source: 'edit', candSeq, paths}` signals in materializing iterations (Plan 3's flow), except where a test calls the port directly.
- `"M comes from the materialized candidate and nothing is written"` — TIP = A: no `merge-file` argv in `gitCallsOf`; TIP ≠ A on two paths: exactly two `merge-file -p --object-id` calls (current R[p], base A[p], other TIP[p]); in both, `looseObjectCount`, the private index bytes and every ref are unchanged across the check (R4-40, R2-E04, 2Q-02, REG-35).
- `"merge outcomes the check cannot take"` — merge-file rc 1 → `merge_conflict`; R deletes a path TIP changed (delete/modify), R changes a path TIP deleted (modify/delete), both add a path with different content (add/add), both change a binary path, and both change a path's mode differently → `merge_conflict` too, as `merge-tree` reports them (R4-73), each `{path, reason: 'merge_conflict'}` with no `check`, and Plan 3's `last_check` slot for the CS of kind `conflict` (written by its `settleCandidate`, X-06); both sides adding identical bytes and a mode change on one side only → no entry (`M[p] = R[p]`); a merge that cannot be computed (the TIP blob's object removed after the capture, so `merge-file` ends rc 128) → `no_merge`; an R blob removed from the store → `no_snapshot` (R4-40, R4-73, R2-E04).
- `"the port verdict carries entries and failed is their distinct check numbers"` — the multi-entry CS of the unit file → `{verdict: 'fail', failed: [1, 3, 4], entries: <the 7 sorted entries>}`; a pass → `{verdict: 'pass'}`; Plan 3's `settleCandidate` keeps the earlier ready pair on that fail (05 U10 "a fail after a pass leaves the earlier ready pair") (R4-39, R2-E01, OQ-10, A-01, 2Q-01, contract addition 2).
- `"T-REG-29 scheduling half: one check runs and one waits per CS"` — the queue is exercised by calling the port directly (under Plan 3's flow `captureStep` awaits each check, so at most one call per CS is ever in flight there, R4-39): a `RegionWorkers` wrapper holds the first job of the first check on a latch; 20 concurrent `regionEditCheck(host).check(repo, a, r_i, tip, paths, {csId: 'p/1'})` calls (i = 1…20): at most one check is running and one waiting at any time; the 18 replaced waiting calls resolve `{verdict: 'incomplete', entries: []}`; the running call (i = 1) is never cancelled and the last waiting call (i = 20) runs after it; a call without `csId` runs at once; a second CS's calls run independently (R2-E13, R4-39, contract addition 4).
- `"the latest passing candidate becomes the ready pair under Plan 3's flow"` — 20 checkpoint signals of one CS (candSeq 1…20 over 2 s of virtual time) with the first check held on the latch: after the latch opens, candSeq 20's content (passing) becomes the CS's ready pair (R2-E13, HLD-67 step 2).
- `"T-REG-29 timing half: an injected 0.6 s worker stall is incomplete with no REGION_BUDGET, no record and no message"` — `inlineWorkers` on the peer's `VirtualClock`, `testBudgetInjector().stallMs('edit_check', 600)` (the next `edit_check` deadline reports 600 ms elapsed, so it is expired at creation against its 500 ms budget; no clock moves and no other timer fires) → `{verdict: 'incomplete', entries: []}`; the real-time form (`threadWorkers`, the real clock, a worker stalled 0.6 s) is Task 18's perf row, not part of this file; `host.raise` never called, the registry head, outbox and `status.json` `message_code` unchanged; one daemon-log record `EDIT_CHECK_OVERRUN {cs_id}` (X-30) (REG-31 exception, R2-E13, 05 §6 edit-check rows).
- `"the edit_check trace carries the failed check numbers only"` — the multi-entry fail → `{kind: 'edit_check', cs, verdict: 'fail', checks: [1, 3, 4]}`; a pass and an abandoned check → `checks: []`; no trace event, log record, `status.json` field, board op or registry blob of the run contains an entry's `path` with its `line`, or any `reason` (B2-14, A-03, R4-63, D1).
- `"Plan 3's last_check slot is fed from this verdict at HLD-67 step 4 (X-05, X-06, X-08)"` — reading Plan 3's `ChangeSets.lastCheck.get(csId)` (the store and its writes are Plan 3 Task 10's; this test pins only the integration with this binding): the multi-entry fail at candSeq 7 → `{edit_n: 7, kind: 'fail', check: 1, path: 'd.py', line: 4, more: 6}` (glossary §29.8's shape: `check`/`path`/`line` from `entries[0]`, `more = entries.length − 1`); a check failing only with `merge_conflict` (merge-file rc 1, or a delete/modify both-sides path, R4-73) → `kind: 'conflict'` with `path` and no `check`; `no_snapshot`, `no_merge` or incomplete → `kind: 'incomplete'`; a passing capture at candSeq 8 clears it; the CS's close clears it; a daemon restart starts empty; the state ref, `cs/*.json`, `meta.json` and the board never contain it; no Plan 4 module calls a `LastCheckStore` writer (R4-43 as amended, 2Q-03, C-1, R2-K05, 05 U10 `last_check` clause).
- `"D1: the failed list is display data written nowhere"` — over a run with failing checks (capturing `Trace`, `Log`, `status.json` writer, outbox and registry): Plan 4 writes no entry to any record, board op, log line, `status.json` field, carrier, mirror or notification; the only places the entries exist are the port's return value and Plan 3's in-memory `last_check` slot derived from it (R4-08, D-01, R2-K05, OQ-28, 2Q-21; X-01: who may read the slot is not tested here — the daemon does not test who asked).
- `"05 U10 with the real binding"` — one fixture per check, fail then pass: (1) an Edit leaving an unclosed bracket, then closed; (2) a parameter made required while `orders.py` (another file of the merged tree) is not updated, then updated in the same CS; (3) a call to a function the CS removed, and `from pricing import CURRENCY_DEFAULT` that the merged tree lacks, then fixed; (4) a duplicated definition and a > 50 % shrink of a touched file; a grammarless file passes 1–3 and fails 4 on a shrink; each pass replaces the ready pair, a fail after a pass leaves the earlier pair journaled and it publishes; a fail writes no registry commit, board op or `event` (05 U10, HLD-67 steps 2–4).
- `"a human CS and a recovered CS never call regionEditCheck"` — a counting wrapper stays at 0 over their captures (R2-E02, HLD-67).
- `"Plan 3's editcheck tests pass with regionEditCheck injected"` — Plan 3's `test/integration/editcheck.test.ts` cases re-run with this binding (its grammarless size cases keep their verdicts: a 1,000-byte TIP merged to 499 bytes fails `[4]`, 500 passes); a case that changes its expected value stops the task for a controller ruling (R4-58, B3-01, R3-03).
- `"INV-REG-EDITCHECK unit form: a failing check changes when a capture is taken, never what is pushed"` — the same scripted turn run with `regionEditCheck` and with a port that always answers fail: every blob pushed in either run passed B1 and T0 on its real P/OUT; with the failing port nothing publishes before the CS's close and the closing capture publishes through T0; neither run leaves a record, event or message from the edit check (INV-REG-EDITCHECK, R2-E14).
- `"the predict mode through the port"` — `check(…, {mode: 'predict'})` on a 60 % shrink of `c.py` → `{verdict: 'pass'}`; the same call in mode `checkpoint` → `fail [4]`; a `predict` call writes no `last_check` (D2, F-03 edit-check half).

`test/integration/editCheck.corpus.test.ts` — the edit-check unit corpus of the exit criterion (TC-REG-09, T-REG-28 on Task 2's fixtures, the M46 kills).
- `"TC-REG-09: one pass and one fail fixture per check in Python, TS/JS and C#"` — grouped by REG-36's check numbers (R4-70; spec edit 13): (1) a half-typed block adding an ERROR or MISSING node absent from TIP → fail; an ERROR node already in TIP and unchanged → pass; (2) a non-removal breaking change — a removed, reordered or renamed parameter, a new required parameter, removed varargs, a C# sole overload gone — whose callers in the merged tree are not updated → fail; the same change with every caller updated in the same CS (same file and another file of the CS) → pass; a caller added on the TIP side and not updated → fail; (3) a removed or renamed symbol still called, a removed export still imported, a call to a function the CS removed, or a local import name the merged tree does not define → fail; the same removal with every caller and importer updated in the CS → pass; (4) a label count above max(TIP, local) → fail; a > 50 % shrink → fail; a 49 % shrink → pass; a grammarless file passes 1–3 and fails 4 on a > 50 % shrink — in Python (`pricing.py`, `orders.py`), TS (`total.ts`, `cart.ts`) and C# (`Svc.cs`, `Svc.Part2.cs`); every fail fixture's `failed` deep-equals `[n]` for its own check n (its `EditCheckCase.check`) (TC-REG-09, R2-K02, R4-70).
- `"each M46_n lets its check-n fail fixture pass and no other"` — `setMutants(p, ['M46_n'])` (`host.flags.skipChecks = {n}`) for n = 1…4: every check-n fail fixture of each language passes, and every other fail fixture still fails with exactly its own `[m]` (m ≠ n) — possible because each fixture reports one check number only (R4-70) (TC-TST-02 form; B2-13, R4-62).
- `"T-REG-28 on the rc_touch, X7, x2/x3 and S7 fixtures with TIP = A and TIP ≠ A"` — rc_touch body edits and appends → pass; X7's old-arity caller (`foo(a,b)→foo(a)`, R4-69) → `{check: 2, path: 'orders.py', reason: 'incompatible_caller', line: 14}`; x2's E1 alone (`compute_price` gains a required `currency`, `CURRENCY_DEFAULT` added, callers not yet updated) → check 2 at each caller row; x3's import of a name the merged tree lacks → check 3; S7's unclosed triple quote, unclosed paren, and JS and C# unclosed braces → check 1 with `line` = the first new error span's row; each again with a TIP that moved elsewhere in the file (M by merge-file) (T-REG-28, R1-057, R1-010).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE (`src/claims/editCheckPort.ts` is the one allowed `mergeFile` site; `hashRouteC(…, false)` only), L-ONE-MAPPER (check 1's TIP-span images come from `mapper.ts`), L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS (unchanged: `src/holds/ports.ts`'s type-only import of `src/regions/editCheck.ts` is still the one allowed; Plan 3's `src/holds/lastCheck.ts` imports nothing from `src/regions`, X-05).

**Notes:**
- `regionEditCheck(host).check(repo, a, r, tip, csPaths, opts)` resolves `a`, `r`, `tip` to trees (`<oid>^{tree}`), keeps the LIVE paths (`host.scope(p)`) with `R[p] ≠ A[p]`, computes `M[p]` per R4-40 and R4-73 (`TIP[p] = A[p]` or `R[p] = TIP[p]` ⇒ `R[p]`; a both-sides path `merge-file` cannot take ⇒ `{kind: 'conflicted'}`; else `mergeFile(repo, R[p], A[p], TIP[p])` into memory, registered in `SnapshotStore` with `hashRouteC(repo, bytes, false)` under an owner token released when the call returns), builds `M' = view(TIP, overlay)` and calls `editCheck` with `skipChecks = host.flags.skipChecks` (the one M46 site) and `mode = opts.mode ?? 'checkpoint'`.
- Check 2 and 3 entries come from one `interfaceCheck(s, {base: A, ours: R, out: M', csPaths, csRows, epochBase}, deadline('edit_check', 1))`; the split caller search of Task 7 runs because `M'` has an overlay.
- **Flag for the controller:** `src/holds/ports.ts` imports `EditCheckEntry`/`EditCheckReason` from `src/regions/editCheck.ts` (header §Interfaces), so Task 1's L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS row must allow an `import type` of `src/regions/editCheck.ts` from `src/holds/ports.ts` (type-only, erased at build); Task 5 created that module's two types for this reason.

### Task 11: Claim view, CONFIRM, PREDICT and Algorithm CLAIM

**Covers:** R1-005, R1-013, R1-020, R1-021, R1-039, R1-046, R1-047, R1-063, R1-090…R1-093, R1-025 (the whole unit at any length, positive form), R2-K07, R2-M06, R4-71 (`predict` under the gate budget), R4-18 (blank-row-only hunks claim nothing), R1-098, R1-110…R1-114 (call sites), R1-160 (claim view, `mapRanges` local), R2-A01…R2-A14, R2-B02 (claims nothing for `<imports>`/blank-only), R2-B03 (items 1–3), R2-J01, R2-O05 (prerequisites); REG-09 (local-only drafts), REG-12, REG-15, REG-16, REG-18, REG-21, REG-28, REG-29, REG-32, REG-33, REG-35 (drafts); T-REG-07 (CLAIM half), T-REG-11 (unit form over recorded hook payloads), T-REG-13 (no prediction or claim on non-LIVE paths), T-REG-18 (the local overlap check), T-REG-19 (`ownTouches.granted`; a requester's new Edit still a foreign touch at `predict`), T-REG-25 (`ownContained`, `notOwnContained`), T-REG-27 (a)–(c); OQ-19 (the test helper), OQ-20, 2Q-17, 3Q-05, A-07, A-10, 2X-11, 2X-13, 2X-14, 2X-15, 2X-20; R4-04, R4-31, R4-62 (the M22 site). Depends on: Tasks 6, 8. Wave E.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/claims/view.ts`, `src/claims/confirm.ts`, `src/claims/predict.ts`, `test/helpers/seats.ts`
- Modify: `src/claims/store.ts` (Task 5's skeleton: drafts in local order, pinned post-images, deferred confirms, blocked CSs, rejection marks), `src/engine/claimsStep.ts` (Task 5's stubs `seatEffectStep`, `predictStep`, `claimRejectionStep`, `settleDeferredConfirms` filled), `src/engine/csStep.ts` (a CS boundary capture calls `settleDeferredConfirms(core, csId, R)` before the capture is journaled), no `src/engine/core.ts` line: Task 5 placed the one `claimRejectionStep(core)` call in `prepareIteration` right after the fold sets `leasedFold`, and this task fills the step body (contract addition 4; the reconciliation's within-plan ruling)
- Test: `test/unit/claimView.test.ts`, `test/unit/predict.test.ts`, `test/unit/confirm.test.ts`, `test/unit/draftClaims.test.ts`, `test/integration/seatEffect.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 `hunks`, `mapRangesLines`, `imageOf`, `normalizeRanges`, `rangesIntersect`, `lineArray`, `isBlankRow`, `D_CAP`; Task 4 `RegionService` (`table`, `lines`, `bytes`, `hunks`/`mapRanges` from Task 5, `view`), `TableCache` (`lastGood`, `pin`, `unpin`), `SnapshotStore`, `pathClassOf`, `RegionTable`, `Unit`, `NoTable`; Task 5 `ClaimStore` (skeleton), `newClaimId`, `ClaimView`, `ViewClaim`, `ClaimSource`, `Draft`, `SeatKey`, `SeatEffect`, `ConfirmResult`, `PredictQuery`, `PredictResult`, `TurnContext`, `RegionHost`, `ClaimHostView` (`fold`, `view`, `store`, `scope`, `localSeats`, `suspect`, `flags.matchByLabel`, `trace`, `raise`), `claimHostOf`, `testRegionDeps`, `noSuspect`, `raiseRegionCode`, the `Engine.seatEffect`/`predict`/`claimView`/`claimViewOf` members and the `claim` trace variant; Task 6 `touch`, `TouchClaim`, `TouchInput`, `TouchReason`, `contained`, `OwnClaimImage`, `groupNameHit`, `partialTypeFiles`; Task 8 `ClaimRec`, `ClaimGrant`, `FoldState.claims`, `Folder.claimOrder`, `Folder.takeClaimRejections`, `OwnRejection` (`claimId`, `overlaps`, `claimReason`), `foldOverlap`, `OverlapClaim`; Task 1 `BUDGETS` (`predictWaitMs` 10, `mapLocalMs` 200, `gateMs` 15), `testBudgetInjector`, `languageOf`; Task 2 `fixture` (rc_touch, S7, the recorded hook payloads), `bigBomCrlf(5_960)`; Plan 2 `Outbox.add`, `OpDraft`, `ScopeClass`, `makeCores`, `writeWork`, `prep`, `captureReady`, `setMutants`, `stateA`; Plan 3 `CsSignal` (`effect`, `checkpoint`, `stop`, `seat_end`), `Engine.signal`, `ChangeSets`, `captureStep`, the recovered CS (HLD-36), `pinCs` (the K2 shape is Plan 5's; used only to show no record is made here).
- Produces: `claimView`, `claimViewOf`, `mapClaimInto`, `isEarlier`, `ClaimCtx`, `confirm`, `draftClaims`, `predict`, `normalizeForLocate`, `FallbackRegion`, `fallbackRegion`, the `ClaimStore` draft API (`drafts`, `putDraft`, `discard`, `emitted`, `blockCs`, `blockedBy`, `unblockWhere`, `deferConfirm`, `deferred`, `clearDeferred`, `markRejected`), `seatEffectStep`, `predictStep`, `claimRejectionStep`, `settleDeferredConfirms`, the bodies of `Engine.seatEffect` and `Engine.predict`, `ScriptedSeat`, `scriptedSeat`; contract additions 1–5.

**Contract additions** (stated once in §Interfaces; numbered because the tests cite them):
1. `seatIsLater(seat, c, v, mapped)` in `src/claims/view.ts` — REG-21's "later in claim order", used by `predict` step 7 here and by Task 12's `gate`.
2. The empty results of `predictStep` and `seatEffectStep` on a non-LIVE path (REG-32; `src/engine/claimsStep.ts` comments).
3. `ClaimStore.putDraft` keeps the `confirmSeq` of a replaced draft with the same `claimId`; a new draft takes the next sequence number (R4-29; `src/claims/store.ts`).
4. `claimRejectionStep(core)` once per iteration after the fold: `claim_overlap` → `store.blockCs`; `claim_invalid` → the draft stays TENTATIVE, its claim slot is removed with `Outbox.removeClaimSlot` so the rejected op is never re-sent (Task 14 adds that call together with the slots themselves; before Task 14 no slot exists) and `CLAIM_REJECTED` is raised once per `(claim_id, reason)` through `store.markRejected`; one `claim` trace event `{action: 'rejected'}` per rejection (`src/engine/claimsStep.ts` comment; the `prepareIteration` call site in `src/engine/core.ts`).
5. The own-claim set O of a wake turn, computed from `e.viewAtOpen` and only when `turn.origin === 'wake'` (REG-33; `src/claims/confirm.ts` comment).

**Spec:** glossary §3 (claim view, TENTATIVE/confirmed, ready pair, wake work set S5, `T_predict`), §10 K1/K2, §20 "Region numbers", "Shrink guard", §23 (attached-comment default), §25 F2, F7, §26 W1–W3, W6; 04-regions §2, §3.1 (`predict`, `confirm`, the claim view and `mapRanges`), §3.2 item 4, §3.5 Algorithm CLAIM and "Readers", §3.6 "Containment form", §3.7 "Grants" (no admission at `predict`), §3.11 (PREDICT steps 0–8, CONFIRM steps 1–5), §3.13, §3.14, §6 rows "`old_string` not found", "Prediction cannot decide …", "Code moved …", "Wake-turn edit …", the two rev 2.3 read rows, REG-09, REG-12, REG-15, REG-16, REG-18, REG-21, REG-28, REG-29, REG-32, REG-33, REG-35, §8 Q4; 02-protocol §3.5 (TENTATIVE rows), PRO-37 (SUSPECT, read through `SuspectPort`); 06-claude §3.7.6, §3.8.2a, §3.12.2 (the interfaces Plan 5 binds); F05 §2.1 step 3 (FEAT-F05-01, prerequisites only); tests T-REG-07, T-REG-11, T-REG-13, T-REG-18, T-REG-19, T-REG-25, T-REG-27 (a)–(c); rulings R4-04, R4-29, R4-31, R4-62.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/claimView.test.ts` — a fold state from Task 8's `Board` driver, a `ClaimStore` with drafts, `inlineWorkers`; P1 = self with seats S1, S2; P2 with seat T1.
- `"claimView is the HELD non-STALE folded claims, then this daemon's confirmed TENTATIVE drafts in local order, with grants on the records"` — folded A (P2/T1, HELD, order (10, 0)), B (STALE), C (RELEASED), D (P1/S1, HELD with a grant, order (12, 3)); drafts E (S1, `confirmSeq` 1), F (S2, 2) → `view.claims` = [A, D, E, F] with `order` `{tier: 0, a: 10, b: 0}`, `{0, 12, 3}`, `{1, 1, 0}`, `{1, 2, 0}`; B and C absent; D's `grant` set; E and F have `source.kind: 'tentative'` with their pinned `srcBlob` (R1-039, REG-21, R4-29, A-07).
- `"isEarlier orders tier 0 before tier 1, tier 0 by (height, line), tier 1 by confirmSeq"` — (10, 0) < (10, 1) < (11, 0) < any tentative; seq 1 < seq 2; a re-confirmed draft keeps its seq (contract addition 3) (R4-29).
- `"claimViewOf excludes the reader's own claims, STALE claims and claims the reader holds an active grant on, and keeps sibling drafts with their srcBlob"` — reader P1/S2 → [A, D, E] minus a claim granted to S2; E's source is its `DiskSnapshotId` (FEAT-F05-01, R1-160, R2-O05, 2X-20).
- `"mapClaimInto maps ranges and tombstones by REG-07 local mode, a sibling draft from its srcBlob"` — A `[[10, 20]]` at anchor blob X, base = X with 5 rows inserted above → `[[15, 25]]`; a tombstone `[[40, 45]]` whose image is TOMBSTONE in base is dropped; a sibling draft maps from its `srcBlob`; any WHOLE_FILE image (D > 5,000) → `'WHOLE_FILE'`; `regionSpec` ≠ own or `granularity: 'file'` → `'WHOLE_FILE'` (R1-005, R1-098, REG-12, REG-04).
- `"RegionService.mapRanges local mode onto a DiskSnapshotId view blob"` — from an anchor oid to a `testSnapshot` with 3 rows inserted above row 1: `[[10, 20]]` → `[[13, 23]]`; every claimed row deleted → `'TOMBSTONE'`; a 6,000-row rewrite → `'WHOLE_FILE'`; with `testBudgetInjector().expire('map', 1)` → `'WHOLE_FILE'` (the 200 ms local cap) (F05 prerequisite, R1-160, R2-O05).
- `"M22 site: with matchByLabel a renamed held body is matched by label instead of by lines"` — A claims `compute_total`; base renamed it `compute_sum` (same body): `mapClaimInto(…, false)` → the renamed body's rows; with `matchByLabel` (M22) → `[]` (no unit with the claim's label in base), so a mutant gate would miss it (R4-62, REG-12, INV-REG-NOLABEL).
- `"no claim reader finds a claim by label"` — 200 seeded runs with every label field of every view claim randomized: `claimView`, `claimViewOf` and `mapClaimInto(…, false)` return the same claim ids and ranges (REG-12, R1-098, INV-REG-NOLABEL).
- `"seatIsLater: a TENTATIVE sibling claim is later only than seats without an earlier intersecting claim"` — S1's draft c (seq 2) on `compute_total`: S2 with no claim on p → later; S2 holding an earlier HELD claim intersecting c → not later; S2 holding an earlier claim on another unit of p → later (contract addition 1, REG-21).

`test/unit/predict.test.ts` — `predict(q, ctx)` over `testSnapshot` disk handles; Task 2's recorded hook payloads.
- `"T-REG-11 unit form: predicted hunks equal CONFIRM hunks on the recorded payloads"` — for Edit with an LF `old_string` on the BOM+CRLF `big_wt.py`, curly quotes, `replace_all` across 5 functions, an `old_string` spanning two units, MultiEdit and Write: `predict(...).hunksInBase` deep-equals `confirm(...)`'s `hunksInBase` on the recorded PostToolUse post-image; with a foreign HELD claim on one of the touched units the prediction has its touch; NotebookEdit → every foreign claim on p touches (T-REG-11, REG-28, R2-A03…R2-A05, 2X-13).
- `"normalizeForLocate changes no row count"` — BOM stripped, CRLF → LF, U+2018/2019 → `'`, U+201C/201D → `"`; property (1,000 strings over BOM, CRLF, LF, Hangul, astral emoji, curly quotes, U+2028): the number of `0x0A` bytes is unchanged (REG-01, R1-063, R2-A03).
- `"locate: replace_all lists every match, an ambiguous match every candidate, no match is locate_failed and never a whole-file claim"` — `replace_all` over 5 occurrences → 5 hunks; an ambiguous `old_string` occurring twice → both candidates' hunks; an absent `old_string` → `{status: 'locate_failed', hunksInBase: [], touches: [], ownContained: false}` (R2-A04, REG-28, 04 §6).
- `"Write: the view becomes the normalized content; tool lines are the b-side lines and deletion points"` — a Write replacing rows 5331–5340 of `big_wt.py` → `hunksInBase` covers exactly those rows (R2-A04, R2-A05).
- `"unstable disk bytes answer undecided at once (T-REG-27 (a))"` — `disk: 'unstable'` → `{status: 'undecided', hunksInBase: [], touches: []}` and no worker job (R2-A02, REG-35, T-REG-27 (a)).
- `"path classes 1–3 and .ipynb predict the whole file"` — a binary file, a `filter=crypt` file and an `.ipynb`: every foreign claim on p is in `touches` (reason `degraded-whole-file` through `touch` step 1's WHOLE_FILE input) (R2-A02).
- `"the moved-code check: a different leaf-unit label or a TOMBSTONE image sets moved"` — view blob with the matched lines inside `compute_total`, current disk with `compute_tax` inserted above so the lines now sit in `compute_tax` → `moved: {fromLabel: compute_total's, toLabel: compute_tax's}`; the matched lines deleted on disk → `toLabel: 'TOMBSTONE'`; no view blob → `moved: null` (R2-A06, R1-020, glossary §25 F7).
- `"touches: foreign HELD claims and earlier sibling TENTATIVE claims; a grant never admits at predict"` — a P2 claim on `compute_total` → `touches: [{claimId, reason: 'base-overlap'}]`; S2's TENTATIVE claim (earlier than any S1 claim on p) touched by S1 → listed; a TENTATIVE claim of S2 when S1 holds an earlier intersecting claim → not listed; a P2 claim granted to S1's CS → still `touches` (T-REG-19 predict half, R2-A07, REG-21).
- `"ownTouches lists this seat's own claims with suspect and granted"` — S1's own HELD claim with an active grant to P2/T1 → `ownTouches: [{claimId, suspect: false, granted: true}]`; with a `SuspectPort` answering true for it → `suspect: true`; `noSuspect` → false (T-REG-19 `ownTouches.granted`, R2-A07, 2X-15).
- `"predict past its 15 ms gate budget is undecided and raises REGION_BUDGET {budget: 'gate'}"` — the same query as the T-REG-11 Edit on `big_wt.py` with a foreign HELD claim on the touched unit: with `testBudgetInjector().expire('gate', 1)` (the call's `deadline('gate')`, `BUDGETS.gateMs` 15 ms) → `{status: 'undecided', diskLines: [], hunksInBase: [], touches: [], moved: null, fallbackUsed: false, ownTouches: [], ownContained: false}` (G2, CONFIRM decides — never a "no conflict" answer) and `RegionServiceDeps.onBudget(q.path, 'gate')` called once, which `raiseRegionCode` turns into `REGION_BUDGET {path, budget: 'gate'}` deduplicated per path per 30 min Δmono; without the injection the same query is `located` with its touch (R4-71, REG-31, 04 §3.11, §3.18 rows 1–2).
- `"step 4 waits at most T_predict for the post-image table, else undecided (04 §8 Q4 default)"` — an append after `compute_total`'s last line (a boundary insertion) with `testBudgetInjector().expire('predict_wait', 1)` → `status: 'undecided'` and no touch for that hunk, while a body edit of another claimed unit in the same call is still a `base-overlap` touch; without injection → `boundary-in-extent` touch (R2-A07, R2-J01, REG-28).
- `"the fallback region per language when the disk table is not cached; it never reaches computeClaims"` — Python `def`/`async def`/`class`, JS/TS `export default async function`, `const x =`, C# member-indentation lines: `fallbackRegion` returns `{kind: 'fallback', range}` from the nearest preceding definition line to the line before the next; `predict` sets `fallbackUsed: true`; type-level: `FallbackRegion` is not assignable to `Draft` or to `computeClaims`' inputs (R2-A08, REG-03).
- `"ownContained for a wake turn (T-REG-25)"` — `turn: {origin: 'wake', firstConfirmedInTurn: ∅}`, O = S1's HELD `compute_total`: a body edit → `ownContained: true`; a hunk spanning `compute_total` and its unclaimed neighbour → false; a claim first confirmed in this wake turn → false; `locate_failed`, `undecided` and `fallbackUsed` → false; an own `file` claim → true for a whole-file prediction; `origin: 'prompt'` → false (T-REG-25, REG-33, R2-A09, R1-021, R1-110, R1-111 (O minus first-confirmed claims), R1-112 (conditions 1–4), R1-113, 2X-14).
- `"PredictResult carries exactly 04 §3.1's fields"` — type-level and runtime key set `status, diskLines, hunksInBase, touches, moved, fallbackUsed, ownTouches, ownContained` (R1-046, R2-A10).

`test/unit/confirm.test.ts`
- `"CONFIRM step 1: an empty or > 50 %-shorter post-image without a confirming second read is deferred (T-REG-27 (b))"` — pre 1,000 bytes, post 0 bytes or 400 bytes, no `secondRead` → `{status: 'deferred', hunksInBase: [], touches: [], drafts: [], notOwnContained: []}` and `store.deferred(csId)` holds the effect; `post: 'unstable'` → deferred too (R2-A11, REG-29, REG-35).
- `"a Write that really empties the file confirms on two agreeing reads (T-REG-27 (c))"` — post 0 bytes with a `secondRead` of the same bytes → `status: 'confirmed'`, `hunksInBase` deleting every row (R2-A11).
- `"EOL/BOM-only differences give no hunks"` — pre `big_lf.py`, post `big_wt.py` → `hunksInBase: []`, `drafts: []` (REG-15, R2-A12).
- `"touches use the view recorded at the Pre or TXN open: a claim folded between Pre and Post is not a CONFIRM touch"` — `viewAtOpen` without P2's claim, the current view with it → `touches: []` (the hunk is B1's, K3) (R2-A13, glossary §10 K2).
- `"CONFIRM touches carry the holder"` — P2's claim in `viewAtOpen` → `touches: [{claimId, holder: {peer: P2, seat: T1}, reason: 'base-overlap'}]` (R1-047, R2-A13).
- `"notOwnContained is computed only for a wake turn (T-REG-25)"` — `origin: 'wake'` with O = `compute_total`: a hunk inside → not listed; a hunk in the unclaimed neighbour → listed; a boundary insertion decided only by an ERROR span or `NO_TABLE`, and a group-name-only hit → listed; `origin: 'prompt'` → `[]` (T-REG-25, R2-A14, REG-33, R1-114).

`test/unit/draftClaims.test.ts` — Algorithm CLAIM steps 2–5 on `big_wt.py` and small fixtures.
- `"a body edit drafts the leaf unit containing it"` — an edit inside `compute_total` of `big_wt.py` → one draft, `granularity: 'symbol'`, `srcRanges: [[5331, 5340]]`, `srcBlob` the post-image handle, `label` compute_total's (R1-090, REG-15, 04 §3.6 VERIFIED extent).
- `"unit choice per REG-15"` — a nested function's edit → its named ancestor; a class-residue edit → the residue ranges only; an edit spanning two methods → two drafts, never the class; a top-level statement outside units → its gap block (`granularity: 'block'`); an `<imports>` edit and a blank-row-only hunk → no draft (also a blank-row-only hunk inside a class residue, whose blank rows belong to the residue's extent: "blank-line-only hunks claim nothing" is this algorithm's rule, not the unit shape's, R4-18); a Write rewriting the file with two changed functions → exactly those two (no percentage threshold) (REG-15, REG-16, R2-B02).
- `"an edit of the cp949 C# file over 300 rows drafts its raw-split block"` — `special/legacy_cp949.cs` (320 rows, not UTF-8, path class 6) with one row changed inside its third blank-line-separated block → exactly one draft, `granularity: 'block'`, `label` the block label of R4-19 (four empty fields), `srcRanges` = that block's raw `0x0A`-split rows; a hunk spanning two blocks → two block drafts; no draft is `symbol` or `file` (Review Focus 2, REG-05, REG-06, R4-19).
- `"a pure deletion drafts the post-image unit at the deletion point, else the base unit"` — deleting two rows inside `compute_total` → `compute_total`'s post-image extent; deleting a whole unit between two others → the base unit's label with ranges at the deletion point (R1-090).
- `"error trees never yield a claim range or label (T-REG-07 CLAIM half)"` — S7 post-images (unclosed triple quote, unclosed paren, JS and C# unclosed brace): with a last-good table cached for the path the draft comes from it mapped forward; touched lines in no last-good unit → the in-between span as `block`; no last-good table → `granularity: 'file'`; no draft range or label equals one read from the erroring tree (T-REG-07, REG-18, R2-B03 items 1–3).
- `"a unit inside a foreign claim granted to this seat's CS is not drafted"` — P2's claim on `compute_total` granted to S1's `cs_id` → S1's edit there gives no draft (T-REG-19 "the requester emits no claim on the granted lines", R1-091).
- `"draft reuse: an intersecting own claim keeps its claim_id, else a new one from the seeded Random"` — S1's HELD claim on `compute_total` and a second edit there → the same `claim_id`; a first edit of `compute_tax` → `newClaimId()` matching `^[a-z2-7]{16}$` and equal across two runs with the same seed (R1-092, R4-24).
- `"local overlap check: a draft overlapping an earlier sibling's TENTATIVE claim is not recorded and is a touch (T-REG-18 local half)"` — S2 drafted `compute_total` first; S1's edit there → `drafts: []`, `localTouches: [{claimId: S2's, holder: S2, reason}]`; S1's edit of another unit → recorded (R1-093, REG-21, T-REG-18).
- `"a draft on a unit absent from TIP is a local-only draft that blocks this daemon's other seats"` — S1 adds a new `def tax()`: the draft is in `view.claims` (tier 1) and S2's prediction inside it touches; nothing is queued for the board by this task (REG-09; emission is Task 14).
- `"drafts pin their srcBlob and write no object"` — `TableCache.pin` holds the `DiskSnapshotId` above the 300 MB cap; `discard` and `emitted` unpin; `looseObjectCount` unchanged by `draftClaims` and `putDraft` (REG-35, R1-092).

`test/integration/seatEffect.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 2})`, `scriptedSeat` on peer 0.
- `"scriptedSeat queues seat_start before its first draft and seat_end with release{seat_end} at its end"` — `start()` then `edit('src/p.py', …)`: peer 0's outbox holds a `seat_start` draft before any claim slot exists; `end()` queues `seat_end` and `release{claim_ids: <its folded claims>, reason: 'seat_end'}` and discards its drafts (PRO-20, R4-04, OQ-19, 2Q-17, 3Q-05, 2X-11).
- `"Engine.seatEffect runs CONFIRM and Algorithm CLAIM and keeps the draft in daemon memory"` — a body edit of `compute_total` → `{status: 'confirmed', drafts: [<one draft, srcRanges [[5331, 5340]]>]}`; `engine.claimView()` lists it as tier 1; one `claim` trace event `{action: 'draft', claim, cs, path, anchor: null, ranges: [[5331, 5340]]}` (no label); no object, ref or registry commit is written (A-10, R1-092, REG-35).
- `"a deferred confirm ends at the CS boundary with R[p] as the post-image (T-REG-27 (b))"` — `edit` writes a 0-byte post-image once (no second read) → `deferred`; the next stable read re-runs it for the same `cs_id` with the untorn verdict; in a variant the CS closes first → `settleDeferredConfirms` runs with the capture's `R[p]` and yields the same drafts and touches as an untorn run (R2-A11, REG-29).
- `"T-REG-13: no prediction and no claim on non-LIVE paths"` — `.gitignore` (SAVE_ONLY), `.claude/settings.json` (QUARANTINED), a secret-scanner `local_only` path, a symlink and an LFS path: `predict` and `seatEffect` return the empty results of contract addition 2, no draft, no table job (T-REG-13, REG-32, R1-013, R2-A01).
- `"claimRejectionStep blocks the CS on an overlap rejection and raises CLAIM_REJECTED once on any other"` — first S1's `scriptedSeat.edit` creates the draft (its `claim_id` k and `cs_id`), then a hand-built own board commit carries a claim op with that same `claim_id` k that overlaps P2's earlier claim → after the fold `store.blockedBy(<the draft's csId>)` = P2's claim id; a second draft k2 of S1, then an own claim op with `claim_id` k2 and a wrong `anchor` → `status.json` `message_code: 'CLAIM_REJECTED'`, `message_params {claim_id: k2, reason: 'anchor'}` once, a second identical rejection raises nothing, the draft k2 stays TENTATIVE (PRO-24 bullets 2–3, R4-28, contract addition 4).
- `"a restart drops every draft (R4-31)"` — after drafts exist, RESTART (a new `Engine` from disk): `engine.claimView()` has no tier-1 claim and the store is empty; the next confirmed edit drafts again (R4-31, OQ-20).
- `"a human CS and a recovered CS take no claim"` — a human edit inside `compute_total` and the recovered CS after a restart run no `confirm` and make no draft (R4-04; 07 COO-09 humans take no claims).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** L-ONE-MAPPER (every hunk and image from `mapper.ts`), L-UTF8-WRITE (`normalizeForLocate` decodes only; row numbers come from `0x0A` positions of the normalized string, never a re-encoded buffer), L-OFFSETS, L-REGION-WRITE (`src/claims/*` here call no writing git command).

**Notes:**
- Before Plan 5 the only callers of `Engine.seatEffect` and `Engine.predict` are tests and the SIM's scripted seats (R4-04); production builds fold and gate against no claims until Plan 5 binds the hooks (Cross-plan C-3, C-5).
- `confirm` computes drafts but `seatEffectStep` alone writes them to the store (and Task 14 adds their outbox slots); `predict` records nothing (predicted TENTATIVE claims are Plan 5's, C-5).
- Step 4 of `touch` in `predict` asks for an incremental re-parse with `prevRef` = the disk blob's cached tree and a `predict_wait` deadline of `BUDGETS.predictWaitMs` (10 ms); the whole `predict` call runs under one `deadline('gate')` (15 ms, R4-71) created at its start, and every worker job it submits carries the earlier of the two deadlines.

### Task 12: The B1 publish gate and the F10 predictions

**Covers:** R1-050, R1-058 (HLD consumer), R1-120…R1-127, R1-163 (the `WOULD_HOLD` half), B3-03, R2-M01, R2-M04, R2-O03, F-03 (the gate half and `predictRevert`), D-02; REG-19, REG-20, REG-21 (gate), INV-REG-FENCE (gate half), INV-REG-NOLABEL; T-REG-06 (gate half: a claimed premerge neighbour holds the requester's CS), T-REG-12 (the human-editor case, K6), T-REG-17 (the own-PC human and other-PC human cases), T-REG-18 (B1 blocks the later seat), T-REG-19 (grant admission; a second requester CS blocked), T-REG-24, TC-REG-07, 05 I2 and I16 (K3 PIN contents), TC-F10-08, -09, -14, -15 (verdict halves); OQ-8, OQ-18, 2Q-06, A-12, 2X-02, 2X-04, 2X-05, 2X-12; R4-05, R4-37, R4-46, R4-62 (the M21 site), R4-74 (the multi-guard PIN), R4-77 (per-peer port bindings); reconciliation X-09 (the F10 caller), X-15, X-27, X-28 (`PublishReport.claimPins`). Depends on: Tasks 6, 10, 11. Wave F (parallel with Task 13: no shared file).

- Modify: `src/claims/attribution.ts` (Task 5 created it with the type `HunkAttributionPort`; keep it verbatim and add `csAttribution`), `src/claims/revertPredict.ts` (Task 5 created it with the type `RevertPrediction`; keep it verbatim and add `predictRevert`), `src/engine/publish.ts` (Plan 3's B1 slot: the group's one PIN is `hold(pinCs('claim:<claimIds[0]>', …))`, then `addGuard(id, 'claim:<id>')` for every further `claimIds` entry, all before the window's push — Plan 3's API unchanged, R4-74; the slot appends one `PublishReport.claimPins` entry per PIN it made and `PublishReport` gains `claimPins` (`[]` when none), X-28; a `GateTipError` ends the window with nothing pushed), `src/engine/claimsStep.ts` (`predictRevertStep`)

- Produces: `csAttribution`, `isForeign`, `GateGroup`, `gate`, `regionGate`, `GateTipError`, `predictRevert`, `predictRevertStep` (the body of `Engine.predictRevert`), `PublishReport.claimPins` (X-28; Table B B-16) (`HunkAttributionPort` and `RevertPrediction` are declared by Task 5); contract additions 1–4.

**Contract additions** (stated once in §Interfaces; numbered because the tests cite them):
1. `GateTipError` in `src/claims/gate.ts`: `regionGate(host).gate` throws it for a tip other than the folded tip (also with no fold yet); Plan 3's `publishStep` lets it end the window (fail closed, R4-37).

**Tests:**

`test/unit/foreign.test.ts`
- `"REG-19: a claim is foreign unless every attributed seat is its holder seat"` — holder P1/S1 (P1 = self, `localSeats` {S1, S2}): `S(h)` = [P1/S1] → not foreign; [P1/S2] → foreign (another seat on the same PC); [P1/S1, P1/S2] → foreign; [P1/human] → not foreign (the same-daemon human exception); [P2/human] → foreign; holder P2/T1 with [P1/human] → foreign (the exception covers only claims of this daemon's seats); two agents of S1 are one seat (R1-120, REG-19).
- `"M21 site: perPcForeignness makes another seat of the same PC not foreign"` — with `flags.perPcForeignness` the [P1/S2]-on-P1/S1 row answers not foreign and every other row is unchanged (R4-62, 11 §3.9 M21).
- `"csAttribution: seat CSs give their seat, human and recovered CSs the peer's human, multi-CS groups every member on p"` — `seatsOf(['<S1>/3'], 'a.py', h, P1)` → [P1/S1]; `['<P1>/human/2']` → [P1/human]; a recovered CS → [P1/human]; `['<S1>/3', '<S2>/1']` where only S2's CS has `a.py` in its paths → [P1/S2]; where both have it → [P1/S1, P1/S2] (R4-05, OQ-18, 2Q-06, 2X-12).

`test/unit/gate.test.ts` — `gate(host, view, groups)` with `host = claimHostOf(core)` of `makeCores({n: 1})`, hand-built `ClaimView`s and `GateGroup`s over committed blobs.
- `"Algorithm GATE: hunks(A[p], R[p]) on changed paths only, foreign claims mapped into A[p], touch per hunk, blocked per attributed CS"` — `compute_total` claimed by P2/T1 at anchor blob X; A = X with 3 rows inserted above; R edits `compute_total`'s body → `blocked: [{csId, claimId, holder: P2/T1, path, reason: 'base-overlap'}]`, `admitted: []`; a path with `R[p] = A[p]` is never parsed (a counting `RegionWorkers`); the registry head is unchanged and no `HoldRegistry` method is called (R1-121, REG-20, REG-13).
- `"the result never carries hunks to remove"` — type-level: `GateResult` has only `blocked` and `admitted`, each entry ids, a path and a reason (REG-02, R1-121 step 4).
- `"a confirmed TENTATIVE sibling claim blocks only later seats"` — S2's draft c on `compute_total`: S1's CS editing it (S1 with no earlier intersecting claim) → blocked; S1 holding an earlier HELD claim intersecting c → not blocked by c (`seatIsLater`) (REG-21, R1-126).
- `"an active grant admits exactly the grantee's cs_id; a second CS of the grantee and other seats stay blocked; the holder's own hunks are never blocked"` — S1's claim with `grant {grantee: P2/T1, cs_id: 'T1…/4'}`: T1's CS `T1…/4` touching it → `admitted: [{csId, claimId, path}]`; T1's CS `T1…/5` → blocked; P3's CS → blocked; S1's own CS → neither (the holder's hunks are not foreign) (T-REG-19, R1-124, PRO-19).
- `"STALE claims are not in the view and block nothing; a foreign regionSpec claim is whole-file"` — a STALE P2 claim on `compute_total` → nothing blocked; a P2 claim with `regionSpec 'rs-old'` on `compute_total` → a hunk in `compute_tax` on the same path is blocked with reason `degraded-whole-file` (R1-120, REG-04).
- `"the B1 gate never blocks on line-ending or BOM form alone"` — A = `big_lf.py`, R = `big_wt.py` (BOM+CRLF, the same content) → no hunk, nothing blocked; R = `big_wt.py` with a body edit in `compute_total` → exactly the blocked set of the all-LF run (Review Focus 2).
- `"T-REG-24 / TC-REG-05: randomized labels never shrink the blocked set, and M22 does"` — 200 seeded runs over the rc_touch and C# overload fixtures with every `ViewClaim.label` field randomized: the blocked set is a superset of the unrandomized run's (equal here); with `flags.matchByLabel` (M22) the randomized runs block fewer CSs, so the assertion fails on M22 (T-REG-24, TC-REG-05, INV-REG-NOLABEL, R4-62).

`test/unit/gate.test.ts` — `regionGate(host)` (the `PublishGatePort` binding).
- `"regionGate.gate returns null or the earliest blocking claim with every blocking claim and the detail"` — nothing touched → `{block: null}`; a group blocked by claims (12, 3) and (10, 0) → `{block: {claimId: <(10, 0)>, claimIds: [<(10, 0)>, <(12, 3)>], detail: {blocked, admitted}}}` in claim order, `detail` being 04 §3.1's `gate(A, R, attribution, claimView@leasedTip)` result (R4-37, R1-050, OQ-8, 2X-04, A-12).
- `"regionGate.gate throws GateTipError for a tip other than the folded tip"` — `gate(repo, <older tip>, …)` rejects with `GateTipError {tip, folded}`; with no fold yet it rejects too (contract addition 1, R4-37).
- `"a CS whose own claim op folded invalid by overlap is blocked at its group's next gate call"` — `store.blockCs('<S1>/3', P2's claim)` and no touching hunk → `{block: {claimId: P2's claim, detail: {blocked: [{csId: '<S1>/3', reason: 'claim_overlap', …}]}}}` (R4-28, PRO-24 bullet 2, contract addition 3).
- `"claimStillBlocks re-runs GATE on hunks(H0, H1) of the record at the host's tip"` — a PIN `claim:<P2's id>` over `a.py` whose H1 edits P2's claimed unit → true; after P2's claim is RELEASED in the fold, or STALE, or granted to the record's `cs_id` → false; a PIN carrying two `claim:` guards answers true while either blocks; with no fold → true (fail closed) (R4-37, B3-03, X-05 as 2X-05, Table 3 row `claim`).
- `"every regionGate.gate call emits one gate trace event with ids, paths and reasons only"` — `{kind: 'gate', peer, tip, csIds, blocked: [{cs, claim, holder, path, reason}], admitted: [...]}`; no label, row number or byte offset in it (R4-63, INV-REG-COORD).

`test/integration/gateB1.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 3})` (peer 0 publishes, peer 1 holds claims through `landClaim`, peer 2 is the third PC), each peer's own `regionGate` injected through Plan 3's `CoreOptions.ports` function form (`ports: (deps) => ({gate: regionGate(deps.regions.host)})`, R4-77, X-15), `scriptedSeat` for seat CSs.
- `"T-REG-12: hook-free rewrites of a held function never reach the remote live ref and the CS is held whole (K3)"` — peer 1's T1 holds `compute_total` in `p.py`; peer 0's seat S0 rewrites it hook-free three ways (in place, through a temp file and rename, and with CRLF endings — the daemon-side forms of `python -c`, `sed -i` and `Set-Content`): after every window `remoteCode`'s `p.py` is unchanged; one PIN with guard `claim:<T1's id>`, origin S0's, `H0 = A`, `H1 = R` over S0's CS's LIVE paths; the disk keeps S0's bytes; S0 has no claim op on the board; the composed P of every window equals A on `p.py` (B1 gates R before P, so P changes no line inside the claim) (T-REG-12, REG-20, R1-122, R1-123, R2-M01, INV-REG-FENCE gate half).
- `"T-REG-12 human case: another PC's human editor save into the held function is held as K6"` — a human edit of `compute_total` on peer 0 → one PIN `claim:<T1's id>` with `origin` human and `cs_ids` `['<P0>/human/<n>']`; nothing published for its group (T-REG-12, R2-M04).
- `"TC-REG-07 / T-REG-17 with M21: own-PC human publishes, other-PC human and a same-PC sibling are held, and M21 lets the sibling through"` — peer 0's S1 holds `compute_total` (`landClaim(f, 0, …)`): peer 0's human edits it → published in the next window; peer 2's human edits it → K6 PIN on peer 2; peer 0's S2 writes into it hook-free → K3 PIN; with `setMutants(peer0, ['M21'])` the S2 write publishes, so this test fails on M21 (TC-REG-07, T-REG-17, REG-19, R4-62).
- `"T-REG-17: a Bash write attributed to two seats where only one holds the claim is held"` — S1 (holder) and S2 both have effects on `p.py` in one group; S2's write lands inside S1's claim → the group is one PIN `claim:<S1's id>` (R1-120, REG-19).
- `"T-REG-18 B1 half: with the hook disabled, B1 blocks the later sibling and no overlapping claim op is pushed"` — S1 drafts `compute_total` (TENTATIVE, seq 1); S2 writes into it hook-free → S2's CS is held at B1 (REG-21); no board op of the run carries a claim on `compute_total` for S2 (T-REG-18, R1-126).
- `"T-REG-06 gate half: a claimed premerge function holds the requester's CS before P is composed"` — premerge fixture: peer 1's T1 holds `compute_total`; peer 2 has landed `compute_tax` (the same-tail neighbour); peer 0's CS changes `compute_total`'s `return total` to `return round(total, 2)` → B1 holds it as one PIN `claim:<T1's id>`; no live commit contains `return round(total, 2)` in any function, and `compute_tax` on the remote stays byte-identical to peer 2's version (T-REG-06 gate half, R1-125; the merge and identity halves are Task 15's).
- `"T-REG-19 grant admission with a granted fold"` — peer 1's host fold wrapped with `applyGrant(state, [S1's claim], {req_id, grantee: P1/T1, cs_id: '<T1>/4', since_height})` (no `answer` folds before Plan 6, R4-06): T1's CS `<T1>/4` inside the claim publishes; T1's next CS `<T1>/5` on the same lines is held; peer 0's S1 editing its own granted claim publishes (`gate` never blocks the holder's own hunks; the T-GRANT_PAUSE deny is Plan 5's) (T-REG-19, R1-124).
- `"05 I16: each K3 PIN contains only the LIVE path"` — S0's CS edits `src/a.py` (inside T1's claim) and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (QUARANTINED) → the PIN's `paths` = `['src/a.py']`; `git cat-file -p` over every object reachable from `refs/cowork/*` never shows the workflow bytes; the workflow file stays on disk, masked (05 I16, HLD-45, INV-SECRET).
- `"05 I2 with real claims: Bob's claim first holds Alice's whole change-set, and one commit carries all three files after the release"` — x2 fixture: peer 1's T1 claims `checkout` in `orders.py`; peer 0's seat CS edits `pricing.py` (E1), `orders.py` (E2, inside `checkout`) and `reports.py` (E3) → one PIN `claim:<T1's id>` over the three LIVE paths; the remote live tree shows no change to any of them while held and peer 2's tree holds A's three files; `landRelease(f, 1, [id], 'explicit')` → `recordStep`'s `claimStillBlocks` answers false, the guard releases, and one live commit carries all three (05 I2, B3-03, R1-058, HLD-45).
- `"a group blocked by claims of two holder seats is one PIN with one claim guard per claim, earliest first"` — peer 1's T1 and peer 2's U1 hold two units of `p.py`; S0's CS touches both → one record with guards `[claim:<earlier>, claim:<later>]`, made by Plan 3's `hold(pinCs('claim:<earlier>', …))` then `addGuard(id, 'claim:<later>')` (two registry commits, R4-74), both before the window's push, which pushes nothing of the group; HLD-07 attaches a repeat block to the same record (R4-37, R4-74, HLD-45 "one PIN").
- `"PublishReport.claimPins names each PIN the B1 slot made (X-28)"` — in the run above, the window's `PublishReport.claimPins` is exactly `[{holdId: <the record's id>, kind: 'K3', claimIds: [<earlier>, <later>], csIds: [<S0's CS>], holders: [<T1>, <U1>], detail: <the regionGate detail>}]`; the T-REG-12 human case's report carries one entry with `kind: 'K6'`; a window whose gate blocks nothing carries `claimPins: []`; no report field carries a breach (BreachPort only) (X-28, B-16).
- `"B1 is recomputed from scratch after a CAS loss against the new tip's claim view"` — `wrapGit` makes peer 0's first push lose its lease while peer 1 lands a claim on the edited function in that window → the retry's gate (new leased tip, new view) holds the CS; nothing of it is on the remote (R1-122, ENG-40).
- `"an own claim op folded invalid by overlap is K3-held at its group's next B1 call"` — `landClaim(f, 0, …)` for S1 with ranges overlapping T1's earlier claim → the fold rejects it (`claim_overlap`), `claimRejectionStep` blocks S1's CS, and the next window PINs S1's group with `claim:<T1's id>` (R4-28, PRO-24 bullet 2, R2-M01).
- `"gate on a stale tip ends the window without a push"` — peer 0 with a ready seat CS; before its publish the test sets `core.leasedFold` back to the fold of the previous board tip (a test seam on the core; the lease itself moved), so Plan 3's B1 slot calls `gate(repo, <the leased tip>, …)` against a host whose `fold().tip` is older → `GateTipError {tip, folded}`, the window ends with `pushCalls` unchanged and no record made; the next window's `prepareIteration` sets `leasedFold` again and it gates and publishes normally (contract addition 1, R4-37; the unit row above pins `GateTipError` on a direct call, and the missing-object path is Task 8's, R4-76).

`test/integration/revertPredict.test.ts` — `makeCores({n: 2})`; the result buffers stand in for Plan 3 Task 17's `revertInverse()` (Cross-plan C-2, X-09; Plan 7's verb is the production caller); BUSY is set up by the test (`A[p] = TIP[p]` on every result path).
- `"TC-F10-08 verdict half: a result hunk inside B's foreign HELD claim is WOULD_HOLD naming B"` — peer 0's published CS `s1/4` changed `h()` in `a.ts`; peer 1's T1 claims `h()`; the revert result restores `h()`'s old body → `wouldHold: [{csId: '<P0>/human/0', claimId: <T1's>, holder: {peer: P1, seat: T1}, path: 'a.ts', reason: 'base-overlap'}]`, `wouldBreak: []` (TC-F10-08, R2-O03, R1-127).
- `"the revert's own PC's seat claims are not foreign"` — the same with `h()` claimed by peer 0's S1 → `wouldHold: []` (REG-19 human exception, F-03).
- `"TC-F10-09 verdict half: a removed name still used is WOULD_BREAK"` — `s1/4` added `export function f()` to `a.ts`; peer 1 later published `b.ts` = `import { f } from './a';` (1), blank (2), `export const y = f();` (3); the revert result removes `f` → `wouldBreak: [{check: 3, path: 'b.ts', reason: 'unresolved_import', line: 1}, {check: 3, path: 'b.ts', reason: 'removed_name_called', line: 3}]` (TC-F10-09, R2-O03).
- `"TC-F10-15 verdict half: a 75 % shrink and a deleted CS-created file are not WOULD_BREAK"` — `s1/4` added 300 rows to a 100-row file and created `n.ts`, untouched since; the result shrinks the file to its 100 rows and deletes `n.ts` → `wouldBreak: []` (mode `predict` drops `shrink`, `emptied`, `deleted`; D2) (TC-F10-15, D-02, R4-46).
- `"TC-F10-14 verdict half: a preview writes nothing and keeps nothing"` — across `predictRevert`: `looseObjectCount` unchanged, registry head, outbox, journal and state ref unchanged, no `last_check` slot, no capture, no `gate` or `edit_check` trace event; `SnapshotStore.size` after the call equals its value before (the buffers released), and the buffers' oids equal `hashRouteC(repo, bytes, false)` with no object for them (TC-F10-14, REG-35 F10 clause, R4-46).
- `"a gate prediction over in-memory buffers equals the gate over the same content stored"` — the TC-F10-08 result vs `gate` over the same result committed by test code → equal `blocked` (R1-127).
- `"predictRevert feeds M' = TIP with the result buffers and runs no merge"` — the spy runner sees no `merge-file` and no `merge-tree` during the call; `editCheck` runs in mode `'predict'` with `A = TIP`, `R = M' = view(TIP, overlay)` and `cs.merged` = the buffers' handles (`{kind: 'deleted'}` for ABSENT) (F-03, R4-46, 2X-02).
- `"Engine.predictRevert delegates to predictRevertStep at the leased tip"` — `engine.predictRevert(results, tip)` equals `predictRevert(claimHostOf(core), results, tip)`; a `tip` other than the leased one rejects with `GateTipError` (B-8, contract additions 1 and 4).

**Crashpoints:** none new. **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE (`gate.ts`, `revertPredict.ts`: `hashRouteC(…, false)` only, no writing git command), L-ONE-MAPPER (GATE's hunks and mappings come from `mapper.ts`), L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS (the B1 edit stays in `src/engine/publish.ts`; no `src/holds` module imports `src/claims`).

**Notes:**
- `gate` maps each foreign claim with `mapClaimInto(s, c, A[p], p, ownRegionSpec, host.flags.matchByLabel)` (the M22 site is Task 11's), calls `touch` per hunk with the post-image table of `R[p]`, the group-name hit of Task 6 and the partial-type info, and attributes each hunk with `host.attribution.seatsOf(group.csIds, p, h, self)`; `isForeign` reads `host.flags.perPcForeignness` (the one M21 site).
- Before Plan 5 binds CCI-27's table, `csAttribution` over-attributes a hunk of a multi-CS group to every member that touched p (R4-05, the safe direction); Plan 6 reads one request per holder seat from `detail` and `PublishReport.claimPins` (Cross-plan B-7, B-16, C-11; X-27: `detail` keeps this camelCase form and Plan 6 derives a grant's `req_id` from `ViewClaim.grant`).
- `claimPins.kind` is `'K6'` when every CS of the PINned group is a human or recovered CS, else `'K3'` (glossary §10: K3 blocks a seat CS, K6 a human CS); `holders` are the distinct holder seats of `detail.blocked` in claim order (X-28).
- B1 runs on the rebased ready trees R (HLD-45), so INV-FENCE holds on P (04 §3.7 "Relation to P"); the placement fence sub-check on OUT is Task 15's.

**Notes:**
- **Test notation (Tasks 13–15).** `M15` is the 15-row LF Python file `src/m.py`: `def a():` / `    return 1` / `` / `` / `def b(x):` / `    y = x + 1` / `    return y` / `` / `` / `def c():` / `    v = 3` / `    v = v + 1` / `    v = v * 2` / `    v = v - 1` / `    return v`. Its table (extent-convention/1): units `a` rows 1–2, `b` rows 5–7, `c` rows 10–15, each labelled `{kind: 'function', qualname: <name>, simple: <name>, type: '', disc: ''}`; rows 3, 4, 8, 9 are blank and belong to no unit (R4-18). `M15{6:    y = x + 2}` replaces row 6; `M15+{7:    z = 1}` inserts that row after row 7 (`b` becomes rows 5–8); `M15-{5..9}` deletes rows 5–9; `M15^4` inserts the four rows `def z():` / `    return 0` / `` / `` above row 1 (`b` becomes rows 9–11, `c` rows 14–19); `M15↓b` moves `b` with its two trailing blank rows below `c` (`a` 1–2, `c` 5–10, `b` 13–15; FoldMapper/1 keeps `c` and maps `b`'s old rows to TOMBSTONE, D = 10 < 16). `p0`, `p1`, `p2` are the fixture's peers; `s1`, `s2` are fixed 32-hex seat ids of p0, `t1` of p1. `X` is `runtime.regionSpec` of `testRegionRuntime()`; `X'` is `regionSpecId(['test-upgrade/1'])`, set through `PeerFixture.deps.regionSpec` (Plan 2 test seam, R4-55). Before Task 16 makes it the default, the host's regionSpec is `EngineDeps.regionSpec` = `PLAN2_REGION_SPEC` (Task 5 Notes), so in Tasks 11–15 every fixture whose test names `X` as the host's or an emitted claim's regionSpec sets `PeerFixture.deps.regionSpec = (await testRegionRuntime()).regionSpec` through the same seam (a hand-built host for a unit test takes `regionSpec: X`); from Task 16 on the seam is a no-op. "A claim op landed by p1" means `p1.core.outbox.add` of a `seat_start` draft and a typed `claim` draft whose `anchor` is the current live code tip and `base_blob` its blob of the path, published by p1's next iteration as ordinary board-only ops (Task 14's EMIT is not needed for that); a foreign holder that never runs an engine is Plan 3's team writer with `teamOps`.
- **Learning of new claim ops.** `labelStep(core, out)` walks `out.state.claims` and calls `labelVerifierOf(core).schedule` for every record whose `(claim_id, anchor)` key the verifier has not seen, except records whose holder is `(core.peerId, the current epoch)` (verified by construction, REG-34 step 1); a re-anchor changes `anchor` and so is a new key (step 5). The verifier keeps a FIFO and runs one job at a time at priority `bg`; it emits no trace event and writes no op, so SIM determinism (TST-03) holds whatever the job timing.
- **Verification of one key (REG-34 steps 2–4).** Not verified, with no job, when `granularity ≠ 'symbol'`, the op is in the absent-path form or `ranges` is empty. Otherwise: `bytes = regions.bytes(base_blob)` (`'MISSING'` → not verified); `pathClassOf(path, bytes, facts at EPOCH_BASE, grammarOk)` ≠ 4 → not verified; a `TableCache` hit for `base_blob` → `leafLabelAt(table, ranges[0][0])`; else one `label_at` job (`bg`, deadline `parse` 400 ms; `deadline`/`crashed` → not verified, **no** `REGION_BUDGET`: 04 §3.18's "Label verification" row says "label rendered `?`"). Verified iff the returned label equals the op's `label` in all five fields; a non-null different label is a mismatch and writes, once per `(claim_id, anchor)`, one record to `RegionDeps.securityLog` (`<gitdir>/cowork/log/security.jsonl`, level `security`): code `SEC_RECORD_INVALID`, params exactly `{field: 'label', validator: 'verifyLabel', op_id, claim_id, holder_peer, anchor, path}` (R4-52). A null label (error span, blank row, unit gone) is "not verified" without a log line.
- **What reaches `status.json` (R4-54).** `REGION_BUDGET` (through `RegionServiceDeps.onBudget` → `raiseRegionCode`, deduplicated per path per 30 min Δmono there), `CLAIM_OVERLAP`, `CLAIM_REJECTED` and `REGION_GRAMMAR_UNAVAILABLE` go to `core.holdCode` and the daemon log, and from there through Plan 3's R3-61 ladder (`lastEvent`) into `message_code`/`message_params`. `REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH`, `REGION_WHOLE_FILE`, `REGION_BLOCK_ONLY` and `REGION_CONFLICT_MARKERS` are only `regionNotes` verdict data (status skew is PRO's `VERSION_SKEW`/`VERSION_PAUSED`). The edit check and label verification raise no code (R2-D02).
- **T-REG-26's other clauses.** Its "`gate` results identical" clause needs Task 12's `gate`, which lands in parallel: it is pinned by Task 12's T-REG-24 test and by Task 17's O-REG-NOLABEL and label-noise run; its "gate p95 within glossary §20 while verification parses run" clause is Task 18's T-REG-15 `predict` run (the gate latency is `predict`'s, R4-71; verification jobs queued on the `bg` worker during the measurement). Rendering of `?` in T1, `/cowork show` and the Stop block is Plans 5–7's; this task pins the value they render.
- **F04 rows (R4-66, X-76).** F04-a is the Plan 2 controller's follow-up F-P2-1 (`src/since/report.ts`, `src/data/zone.ts`), whose label slot is `sinceInit`'s optional `o.verifiedLabel: (claimId, anchor) => SinceLabel | null` from its creation (passed through by `walkSince`; no function is named `sinceReport`; `ClaimLabel` is assignable to the structural `SinceLabel`, and this task may retype the slot to `ClaimLabel` in place — C-15, X-23, verify pass V-6). At the task's start the implementer checks for `src/since/report.ts`. Present: the three `[F04-a]` tests below are created as written against it. Absent: they are not created here, `src/since/**` is not touched, and the ledger opens a Plan 4 follow-up task "F04 claim rows" (owner: the Plan 4 controller) that creates them as written when F04-a merges. The `C-15` input supersedes req-3 A-08's `verifiedLabels` map shape; likewise the header's `ClaimView {tip, claims}` supersedes A-07's `{at, claims, grants}` (grants live on the records).

**Tests:**

`test/unit/labels.test.ts` — `inlineWorkers`, `testRegionRuntime()`, hand-built fold states from `test/helpers/foldBoard.ts`.

*`leafLabelAt` and the `label_at` job*
- `"leafLabelAt returns the leaf unit's label for every row of M15 and null elsewhere"` — rows 1–2 → `a`'s label, 5–7 → `b`'s, 10–15 → `c`'s; rows 3, 4, 8, 9 (blank), 0 and 16 → null; `NO_TABLE{binary}` → null; a class-6 block table of a 320-row `docs/long.txt` → null at every row (R4-19, REG-34 step 3, R2-B07).
- `"label_at reads 1-based 0x0A rows: identical labels on bigBomCrlf(5_960) and its LF form"` — 50 rows drawn from `seededRandom('label-rows')` in 1…5,960 plus row 1 (the BOM row) and the first row of `compute_total`: the job's label on the BOM+CRLF bytes equals the LF bytes' label at every row, and the `compute_total` row returns `compute_total`'s label; no job input or output carries a byte or character offset (R1-162, REG-01, INV-REG-COORD).
- `"an ERROR span on the asked row gives null"` — `M15{6:    y = (x + 1}` (unclosed paren, S7 shape): row 6 → null, rows 1 and 15 still `a` and `c` (REG-34 step 4, REG-18).
- `"F14 line rows name the unit the label_at job finds there"` — Task 2's X7 fixture (R4-69, `foo(a,b)→foo(a)`): the check-2 entry of Task 10's corpus case "X7's old-arity caller" carries `path` `orders.py` and `line` 14 (its TIP row); `leafLabelAt(table(TIP[orders.py]), 14)` is the label of the unit containing that call (X7's calling function in `orders.py`), and row 14 of the CRLF form of `orders.py` gives the same label (R1-162, FEAT-F14-07).

*LabelVerifier*
- `"an honest symbol claim verifies after its job"` — a folded claim record on `src/m.py` anchored on M15, ranges `[[5,7]]`, label `b`'s, holder p1: `verified(id, anchor)` is null before `idle()` resolves and `{kind: 'function', qualname: 'b', simple: 'b', type: '', disc: ''}` after; one `label_at` job ran, or zero when `TableCache` already held M15's table (then `leafLabelAt` answered) (REG-34 steps 2–3, R2-B07, R1-012).
- `"a label differing in any one of the five fields is a mismatch"` — five records each changing one field (`kind: 'method'`, `qualname: 'b2'`, `simple: 'bb'`, `type: 'T'`, `disc: '1'`), each passing `validLabel`: all five `verified` → null after `idle()`; five security records, one per `(claim_id, anchor)` (REG-34 step 3, SEC-35).
- `"the mismatch record carries ids and paths only"` — forged `qualname: 'Evil.run'`, `simple: 'run'`: the one security record has code `SEC_RECORD_INVALID` and params with exactly the keys `field, validator, op_id, claim_id, holder_peer, anchor, path` (`field` `'label'`, `validator` `'verifyLabel'`, `path` `'src/m.py'`); its serialized line contains neither the text `Evil.run` nor a JSON string `"run"`; the daemon log gains no line (R4-52, SEC-35, SEC-38, 2Q-13, 3Q-17).
- `"one record per (claim_id, anchor): rescheduling writes none, a re-anchor of the forged claim writes one more"` — scheduling the same forged record twice → 1 record; the same claim re-anchored on `M15^4` with ranges `[[9,11]]` and the same forged label → 2 records in total (REG-34 step 5, PRO-38).
- `"block, file, absent-path, NO_TABLE, first-row error span, missing blob and pending checks are not verified and log nothing"` — a block claim on the 900-row `docs/long.md`; a `file` claim on `img.bin`; an absent-path op (`base_blob: null`, `ranges: []`, one tombstone); a `src/x.py` anchor blob containing a `<<<<<<< ours` row (class 1); `M15` with `testBudgetInjector().expire('parse', 1)` (NO_TABLE{budget}) and no cached table; a record whose first range starts on the unclosed-paren row; a `base_blob` absent from the object store; and an honest record queried before `idle()`: every `verified` → null, zero security records, `raiseRegionCode` never called with `REGION_BUDGET` (REG-34 step 4, 04 §3.18 "Label verification" row, R2-B07).
- `"verified() never schedules a parse"` — a spy on `RegionWorkers.run`: 1,000 `verified` calls on unknown `(claim_id, anchor)` pairs → zero jobs and every answer null (R4-66, 2X-19, A-07, FEAT-F04-06).
- `"claimLabelText renders a verified label and ? otherwise (X-23)"` — `claimLabelText(null)` → `'?'`; `b`'s verified label → `'b'`; `{kind: 'method', qualname: 'Engine.iterate', simple: 'iterate', type: 'Engine', disc: ''}` → `'Engine.iterate'`; a label whose `qualname` is `''` (a block or file label) or fails `validLabel` (`'a b'`) → `'?'`; a 200-character valid `qualname` is returned whole (the renderer caps it, glossary §19); the function is pure (no worker job, no log line) (X-23, R4-24, REG-34).
- `"own current-epoch claims are verified by construction; own earlier-epoch claims are re-derived"` — holder `(self, current epoch)` → `verified` = the op's label with zero jobs; the same record with holder epoch `e0` (an earlier epoch of self) → scheduled, verified after its job (REG-34 step 1).
- `"the cache is keyed by (claim_id, anchor)"` — claim `k` verified at anchor L1 (M15); its re-anchor op at L2 (`M15^4`, ranges `[[9,11]]`) → `verified(k, L2)` null until its job finishes, then `b`'s label; `verified(k, L1)` keeps `b`'s label; a drift re-anchor by image (L3 = M15 plus 1,001 rows appended, ranges `[[5,7]]` unsnapped) verifies too (REG-34 step 5, 04 §3.17 step 3 last sentence).
- `"verification is display-only"` — 20 commits from `foldBoard` with claim and release ops: `serializeFold` after each `foldCommit`, the `touch` verdict of each of the hunks `M15{6:…}`, `M15{12:…}`, `M15+{7:    z = 1}` against each claim (Task 6 `touch` with p0's own table), `claimView(...)` and `claimsHeld(...)` are identical with the verifier absent, with every `label_at` job stalled 10,000 ms (`stallMs('parse', 10_000)`), and after `idle()` (REG-34 last sentence, 04 §3.17 "Verification is display-only", R2-B07).

*Region notes (T-REG-16 verdict data)*
- `"regionNotes: a claim under another regionSpec gives REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH naming the holder"` — host regionSpec X, folded claim on `b` with regionSpec `regionSpecId(['FoldMapper/1'])`, holder p1 whose folded display is `Bob` → `[{code: 'REGION_SPEC_MISMATCH', params: {path: 'src/m.py', claim_id, peer: 'Bob'}}]`; a 100-character display → `peer` of 64 characters; the same claim with regionSpec X → `[]`; and `touch` on the hunk `M15{12:    v = v + 2}` (in `c`, rows away from `b`) against the mismatching claim → `{touch: true, reason: 'degraded-whole-file'}` (T-REG-16 show half, REG-04, R1-018).

*F12 `labelsOfChange`*

### Task 14: Claim emission at publish

**Covers:** R1-022 (§1.2 row 8: never published, CS blocked), R1-049, R1-052, R1-094…R1-097, R2-L13 (`saveReleaseDue`, the claim-side predicate), B2-05, B3-07, R2-K04, R2-L08, R2-L09 (blocking and `CLAIM_REJECTED`), R2-L12 (the dependency), R2-L17, R2-L18 (the withhold); PRO-06, PRO-09, PRO-24, PRO-31, PRO-45, REG-09, REG-10, REG-11, REG-22; T-REG-04, T-REG-05, T-REG-19 (precheck), T-REG-21, T-REG-27 (d), T-PRO-05, T-PRO-07, T-PRO-23, T-PRO-33, TC-REG-06; OQ-12, 2Q-18, A-05; R4-07 (`saveReleaseDue`), R4-28, R4-30…R4-32, R4-35; reconciliation X-22 (`releaseClaims`), X-26 (claim slots are the only claim path; Plan 6 Task 1 rebases onto this task). **Depends on:** Tasks 8, 11, 12, 13 (`resolveClaimArg`). Wave G.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/claims/emit.ts`, `src/claims/release.ts` (`releaseClaims`, X-22)
- Modify: `src/engine/emitStep.ts` (Task 5 created it as a stub; fill `emitStep`, keeping its signature), `src/board/outbox.ts` (claim slots, the widened withhold, `pendingAllowed` over initiating slots), `src/engine/publish.ts` (Algorithm P: `emitStep` after C — or with TIP for a board-only push — and before Bnew; the recomposition at step 2 after a precheck block; the caps rule for re-anchors riding code; Plan 3's `mirrorSync` there starts its `BOARD_FULL` episode at the first withheld record-creating draft — `isHoldCreation(d)` or a filled claim slot with `claimCreatesRecord(fold, claim_id)` — not only at a withheld hold creation), `src/engine/claimsStep.ts` (`seatEffectStep` adds an initiating claim slot for each new or changed draft whose unit is present in TIP; `claimRejectionStep` also removes the slot of a `claim_invalid` op)
- Test: `test/unit/emit.test.ts`, `test/unit/outbox.claims.test.ts`, `test/unit/release.test.ts`, `test/integration/emitPublish.test.ts`, `test/integration/claimRace.test.ts`, `test/integration/claimStates.test.ts`
