**Contract additions** (stated once in §Interfaces, `src/board/outbox.ts` and `src/engine/claimsStep.ts`; listed here so the task reads alone):
- `Outbox.addClaimSlot(claimId, o?: { initiates?: boolean })` — only `seatEffectStep`'s slots for a new or changed draft on a unit present in TIP initiate (R4-35); `emitStep`'s build-time slots pass `initiates: false`, and `pendingAllowed` counts initiating slots only.
- `Outbox.dropClaim` (unfill for the intent being built, slot kept, R4-32) versus `Outbox.removeClaimSlot` (slot forgotten: draft discarded, op landed, claim rejected); an allocated op id survives rebuilds (PRO-09); `emitStep` removes every non-initiating unallocated slot `computeClaims` did not fill.
- `claimRejectionStep` (Task 11's step) also calls `removeClaimSlot` for a `claim_invalid` rejection, so the rejected op is never re-sent (PRO-24 bullet 3).
- `releaseClaims(core, holder, reason, target)` in `src/claims/release.ts` (§Interfaces "Claims"; X-22): the one release emitter for Plans 5, 6 and 7; `core` is `claimHostOf(core)`.

**Spec:** 04 §3.5 (op shape, absent-path form, Algorithm CLAIM step 4, Algorithm EMIT steps 1–5, Re-anchor (a)/(b), Tombstones, Reserved claims), §3.9 (the overlap rule `claimPrecheck` reuses), REG-08…REG-11, REG-22, REG-32 (no re-anchor onto a non-LIVE path), REG-35 (no object for a draft); 02-protocol §3.1 (caps, record-creating ops), §3.5 (claim lifecycle; save release), §3.8 PRO-45 table, §3.11 step 2, PRO-06, PRO-08, PRO-09, PRO-17, PRO-20, PRO-24, PRO-31, PRO-32 (a)–(b), PRO-38, PRO-45; 01-engine §3.12 steps 1–4 ("Ops riding with code", EMIT before Bnew), ENG-17, ENG-42; glossary §8 (FREEZE and VERSION board-only lists), §20 (`D_reanchor` 1,000, Board caps), §27.2 (sliding team guard); tests T-REG-04, -05, -19, -21, -27 (d), T-PRO-01 (the `[join, seat_start, claim]` order), -05, -07, -23, -33, TC-REG-06, TC-HLD-05 shape; rulings R4-07, R4-28, R4-30…R4-32, R4-35.

**Notes:**
- **Where EMIT runs (R4-32).** `emitStep(core, it, b)` runs at every build of a publish intent — the first build and every CAS-loss rebuild — after C (a code push: `liveCode = C`, `liveTree = tree(C)`, `changedPaths = paths(diff(TIP, C))`) or, for a board-only push, with `liveCode = TIP`, `liveTree = tipMap`, `changedPaths = ∅`; always before Bnew. Order: `clearClaimFills()`; add build-time slots (non-initiating) for every own HELD claim on a changed path (re-anchor (a)), every own HELD claim with `D(anchor blob, TIP[p]) > 1,000` (re-anchor (b), only when the push happens anyway — a board-only or code push already due) and every draft not yet slotted; `computeClaims` (fold-mode mapping from `srcBlob` or the anchor blob, never through `A[p]`, never the local time cap); `claimPrecheck` against the fold at the leased tip; per failure: a drift re-anchor → `dropClaim` (claim stays valid at its anchor), any other → `store.blockCs(csId, overlaps)`, `raiseRegionCode('CLAIM_OVERLAP', {cs_id, claim_id, overlaps, path})`, trace `claim{action: 'precheck_block'}`, and Algorithm P restarts at step 2 without that CS's group (its next B1 call returns the block from `blockedBy`, Task 12, and Plan 3's B1 slot makes the K3 PIN `claim:<overlaps>`); every passing op → `fillClaim`. `take()` then places filled slots in seq order with the other ops, subject to `allowedInAll` (claim only in ok/degraded, PRO-45) and the withhold (a filled slot whose `claim_id` has no folded record is record-creating, R4-30).
- **Caps (01 §3.12 "Ops riding with code").** When a re-anchor (a) of a group's path cannot fit in the board commit's 1,000 ops / 64 KiB after the ops placed before it in seq order, that group is dropped from C whole and C is recomposed; it publishes, with its re-anchor, in a later window.
- **Seat ids and PRO-20.** A scripted seat's `seat_start` draft is added before its first claim slot, so its seq is lower and `take()` puts both into the same board commit (T-PRO-01's `[join(1), seat_start(2), claim(3)]` order for a new epoch).
- **No claim-initiated push but the draft's (R4-35).** Only an initiating slot makes `publishPending` true; re-anchors (a) ride their code commit, drift re-anchors (b) wait for any other push, a save release is Plan 7's and also never initiates (it is a `release` op that rides the next window).
- **Restart (R4-31).** `Outbox.toJson()` omits slots and `ClaimStore` is memory-only, so a restart has no draft and no slot; an allocated seq of a slot that never landed is a gap (PRO-08/09).
- **Releases (X-22).** Plan 4 emits no release on its own (a save release is Plan 7's, R4-07); `releaseClaims` only builds the draft and the refusals for its three callers, who add the draft to the outbox and map `why` to their codes (Plan 6 maps to `NOT_OWN_CLAIM` and `RELEASE_UNLANDED`). Claim slots stay the only path of a claim op (X-26): Plan 6's `reserveKey(d)` returns `null` for a claim op and its `addHead`/`onRenumber` renumber filled claim slots; Plan 6 Task 1 rebases onto this task.

**Tests:**

`test/unit/outbox.claims.test.ts`
- `"a claim slot is placed only when filled and keeps its op id across rebuilds"` — `addClaimSlot('k1')` unfilled → `take` places nothing; `fillClaim('k1', {anchor: L1, ranges: [[5,7]], …})` with the allocator at 10 → op id `<peer>/<epoch>/10`; `clearClaimFills()` then `fillClaim('k1', {anchor: L2, ranges: [[9,11]], …})` → `take` places id 10 with the new content; an intent with writer seq 12 lands without `k1` → `landed` renumbers it to 13 (PRO-09, R4-32, OQ-12, A-05).
- `"allocateClaimSlot gives an unfilled slot its op id now and keeps it"` (verify pass V-10, Plan 6's O-1) — `addClaimSlot('k2')` unfilled, allocator at 20 → `allocateClaimSlot('k2')` returns `<peer>/<epoch>/20` and a second call returns the same id; `take` still places nothing for `k2`; a later `fillClaim('k2', …)` is placed with id 20; a landing of writer seq 21 without `k2` renumbers it like any allocated slot; `Outbox.fromJson(o.toJson())` has no slot and no id for `k2`.
- `"dropClaim unfills for the current intent only; removeClaimSlot forgets the slot"` — after `dropClaim('k1')` `claimSlots()` still lists `k1` and `take` places nothing for it; a refill at the next build places it with its old id; after `removeClaimSlot('k1')` it is gone (R4-32).
- `"toJson omits every claim slot"` — an outbox with 3 ordinary ops and 2 slots (one with an allocated id) → `Outbox.fromJson(o.toJson())` has the 3 ops and `claimSlots()` = `[]` (R4-31, 2Q-18).
- `"pendingAllowed counts only initiating slots and only where claims may leave"` — one initiating slot → `pendingAllowed(['ok'])` true, `(['frozen'])` false, `(['version_board_only'])` false; one non-initiating slot alone → false in every state (R4-35, R2-L17, PRO-45).
- `"T-PRO-23 claim and release rows"` — with a filled claim slot and a `release{explicit}` draft queued, `take` for each `PushState` (Plan 2's `allowedInAll`): `ok` → [claim, release]; `frozen` → [release]; `version_board_only` → [release]; `version_proto`, `incident`, `config_net`, `offline`, `nonretryable` → [] (T-PRO-23, PRO-45, R2-L08).
- `"T-PRO-33: at 5,000 folded records a new claim id is withheld while re-anchors, re-claims and releases leave"` — state `record_count` 5,000: a filled slot for `'aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa'` (no record) is withheld; a re-anchor of a HELD record, a same-holder re-claim of a non-compacted RELEASED record and a `release` are placed; a re-claim of a compacted id (no record) is withheld (the `BOARD_FULL` episode needs an `EngineCore` and is asserted in `claimStates.test.ts`) (T-PRO-33, PRO-06, B3-07, R2-L18, R4-30).

`test/unit/emit.test.ts` — `inlineWorkers`, hand-built `EmitInput`s; notation of Task 13's Notes.
- `"EMIT on a board-only push snaps the draft's image to TIP's leaf unit, anchor = TIP"` — draft on `b`: `srcBlob` = `testSnapshot(M15{6:    y = x + 2})`, `srcRanges` `[[5,7]]`; `liveCode` = TIP (M15), `changedPaths` ∅ → one fill `{path: 'src/m.py', anchor: TIP, base_blob: <M15 blob>, ranges: [[5,7]], tombstones: [], granularity: 'symbol', label: b's, regionSpec: X, cs_id, holder: {peer: p0, seat: s1, agent}}` and no other field (R1-049, R1-094 steps 1–4, REG-08, PRO-31).
- `"EMIT at the code push re-anchors onto C with the same claim_id"` — HELD `k` on `b` anchored L1 (M15); TIP = `M15^4`; C[p] = `M15^4` with row 10 changed → fill for `k`: `anchor` C, `base_blob` C[p], `ranges` `[[9,11]]` (R1-096, REG-11 (a)).
- `"the image is one direct fold-mode mapping, never composed through A[p] and never time-capped"` — 200 triples from `seededRandom('emit-direct')` (srcBlob, an intermediate A, T): `computeClaims`' ranges equal the snap of `mapRangesLines(src, T, srcRanges, 'fold')` and differ from the composed mapping in at least one triple; with `expire('map', 1000)` the fills are unchanged (R1-094 step 2, REG-22 last clause).
- `"a new unit stays local-only until a live code commit contains it"` — a draft on `d` (`M15` + `` / `` / `def d():` / `    return 4`): board-only push (TIP lacks `d`) → `localOnly` = [d's claim], no fill; a code push whose C contains `d` → fill with `ranges` `[[18,19]]` (REG-09, R1-094 step 3).
- `"snapping uses the last-good table under error spans, else file"` — T = `M15{6:    y = (x + 1}` with M15's table cached as the last good table of `src/m.py` → `[[5,7]]`; with no cached table → `granularity: 'file'`, `ranges: [[1,15]]` (R1-094 step 4, REG-18).
- `"a file claim covers [[1, lineCount(T)]]"` — a `file` claim on `docs/short.md` (250 rows) after one appended row → `[[1,251]]` (R1-094 step 4).
- `"a range all of whose lines the seat deleted becomes a tombstone at its last anchor"` — HELD `k` on `b` at L1; C[p] = `M15-{5..9}` → fill `ranges: []`, `tombstones: [{anchor: L1, ranges: [[5,7]]}]`, `base_blob` = C[p] (non-null: the path exists) (REG-10, R1-097).
- `"a move within a file fills the old-location tombstone and the new-location claim together"` — HELD `k` on `b` at L1 plus the seat's draft `k'` on the moved `b` of `M15↓b`: one `EmitPlan.fill` holds `k` (`ranges: []`, tombstone `[[5,7]]`@L1) and `k'` (`ranges: [[13,15]]`) (REG-09, R1-097, T-REG-05 unit half).
- `"a whole-file move uses the absent-path form"` — the seat renames `src/m.py` to `src/n.py`: `k` → `{path: 'src/m.py', base_blob: null, ranges: [], tombstones: [{anchor: L1, ranges: [[5,7]]}]}` (`isAbsentPathForm` true) and the draft on `src/n.py` → `[[5,7]]`, in one fill (R1-097, PRO-17).
- `"a drift re-anchor maps the image only, past D_reanchor"` — HELD `k` at L1; TIP[p] = M15 with 1,001 rows appended → `k` in `drift`, fill `ranges` `[[5,7]]` unsnapped at the new anchor; 1,000 rows appended → not in `drift` (R1-096, REG-11 (b)).
- `"no re-anchor onto a non-LIVE path"` — `host.scope('src/m.py')` QUARANTINED (a new EPOCH_BASE) → no fill, no `localOnly`, the record keeps its anchor (REG-32, R4-07).
- `"claimPrecheck is the fold's validity test at the leased tip"` — a new claim on `b` against: p1's HELD `[[5,7]]`@L1 → `[{claimId, csId, overlaps: <p1's id>}]`; p1's HELD claim on `c` → `[]`; a STALE p1 claim on `b` → `[]`; a claim of the same seat → `[]`; a p1 `file` claim on `src/m.py` → overlap (R1-052, R1-095, REG-22, PRO-17).
- `"saveReleaseDue implements PRO-32 (a)–(b)"` — `cs_id` landed at or before Lk and no open CS of the seat on the path → true; not landed → false; landed but an open CS on the path → false (R4-07, PRO-32).

`test/unit/release.test.ts` — `releaseClaims` (X-22) over `claimHostOf(core)` of `makeCores({n: 1})` with a hand-built fold at the leased tip; holder p0/s1.
- `"releaseClaims releases the holder's HELD claims and refuses the rest"` — target `[k1, k2]`, both HELD by p0/s1 → `drafts` = one `{type: 'release', claim_ids: [k1, k2] (claim order), reason: 'explicit'}`, `refused` = `[]`; target `[k1, kp1]` where `kp1` is p1's HELD claim → the release names `k1` only and `refused` = `[{claimId: kp1, why: 'not_own'}]`; a claim id that is only s1's TENTATIVE draft or an allocated, unlanded claim slot → `refused: [{claimId, why: 'unlanded'}]` and no draft when nothing else is released; an unknown id or a RELEASED record → `'not_own'`; a label or a symbol name (`'compute_total'`) is never resolved (REG-12, REG-30) (X-22).
- `"'all' releases every HELD claim of the holder seat, and nothing is written"` — `releaseClaims(host, s1, 'seat_end', 'all')` → one draft with s1's HELD ids in claim order (`resolveClaimArg(…, 'all')`), STALE and other seats' claims excluded; s1 with no HELD claim → `{drafts: [], refused: []}`; across the calls the outbox, the claim store, the registry head and `looseObjectCount` are unchanged (`assertNoWrites`) (X-22, PRO-30).
- `"a released draft folds as the release row of Task 8"` — the returned draft, added to p0's outbox with `Outbox.add` by the test (the callers' step), lands and folds the claims RELEASED with that reason and `terminal_height` = the landing height (T-PRO-04 release row, R2-L05).

`test/integration/emitPublish.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2, files: {'src/m.py': M15, 'src/other.py': …}})`, scripted seats (`test/helpers/seats.ts`).
- `"T-REG-27 (d): a confirmed edit's draft is emitted by a board-only push before any capture, and no object is written for it"` — p0's seat s1 `edit('src/m.py', M15{6:…})`; at the next `rounds(1)` one push lands whose `ops.jsonl` is `[seat_start(s1), claim(k)]` in that order with live code unchanged; `k`'s ranges `[[5,7]]` equal the oracle; `cat-file -e <post-image oid>` fails in p0's clone and the remote; `inspect().ready` is `[]`; `looseObjectCount` grew only by the board commit's own objects (one ops blob, its trees, one commit) (T-REG-27 (d), REG-35, PRO-31, R2-K04, R2-L12, PRO-20).
- `"the board-only claim push is an ordinary governed attempt"` — with 12 landed board commits observed by p0 in the last 60 s Δmono, p0 makes no attempt until the sliding team guard admits one, then lands the claim (R2-K04, glossary §27.2).
- `"a CAS-loss rebuild keeps the op id and recomputes anchor and ranges"` — p0's CS on `b` with `k` filled; at p0's `publish.beforePush` p1 lands `M15^4`; p0's push loses the CAS; the rebuilt intent carries `k` with the same op id, `anchor` = the new C over p1's commit and `ranges` `[[9,11]]` (PRO-09, PRO-31, OQ-12, A-05, R4-32).
- `"re-anchor (a) rides the own code commit; re-anchor (b) waits for another push"` — once `k` is HELD, p0's CS on `b` publishes: that board commit carries `k`'s re-anchor and no extra push happens; p1 then appends 1,001 rows below `c` over three pushes: p0 makes zero attempts in the next 40 rounds; p0's next CS on `src/other.py` carries `k`'s drift re-anchor (R1-096, REG-11, R2-L17, R4-35).
- `"T-REG-21: S9 churn keeps mapped ranges on the oracle and re-anchors once D crosses 1,000"` — `s9Generator('t-reg-21')`, holder p1 (`core.autocrlf=true`) with a block claim on the generator's claimed block, p0 applying 3,000 lines of churn; after every step p1's fold-mode mapping of the claim into its `A[p]` and into its working-form bytes equals the generator's oracle; the claim's anchor changes on the first holder push after D > 1,000; no mapping of the run reaches `D_CAP` (T-REG-21, REG-11, INV-REG-MAP).
- `"T-REG-05 / TC-REG-06: moves within a file and across files"` — p0's s1 holds `k` on `b`; s1 applies `M15↓b` unpublished: p1's CS editing row 6 of its `A` (M15) is held at B1 (one PIN, guard `claim:k`); p0 publishes: one board commit carries `k`'s tombstone `[[5,7]]`@L1 and the new claim `[[13,15]]`; p1 with its apply deferred (`FaultFs.holdLock('src/m.py')`) is still held by the tombstone; after its apply, `p1.engine.predict` of an Edit at row 14 reports a touch of the new claim; the same with `b` moved to `src/n.py`; no blob of any live commit on the remote has two `def b(` rows; the same two runs on Task 2's S2 fixture leave no pushed blob with two `compute_total` definitions (T-REG-05, TC-REG-06, REG-09, REG-10).
- `"T-REG-04 / TC-REG-06: a rename in the propagation window, board ahead and board lag"` — s1 renames `b` to `b2` (row 5 `def b2(x):`; the draft reuses `k`, its image intersects): (i) board ahead — p1 folds `k`'s re-anchor at L2 while its apply of L2 is deferred → p1's CS editing row 6 is held; (ii) board lag — a test-only wrapper calls `dropClaim('k')` in p0's rename push so the board keeps `k` at L1 while live code is L2; p1 applies L2, edits row 6 → held; in both orders no claim is looked up by name and no pushed blob carries p1's row-6 change; both orders repeated on Task 2's S1 fixture (the requester's edit inside the renamed body) give the same holds (T-REG-04, TC-REG-06, REG-12).

`test/integration/claimRace.test.ts`
- `"T-REG-19: a claim op that would overlap a claim folded after the local capture is never pushed and its CS is held K3"` — p0's s1 and p1's t1 both edit `b`; p1's claim lands first; p0's next build: `claimPrecheck` fails → `k0` is in no pushed `ops.jsonl`; `blockedBy(cs)` = p1's id; B1 → one PIN with guard `claim:<p1's id>` over the CS's LIVE paths; `status.json` `CLAIM_OVERLAP {cs_id, claim_id: k0, overlaps: <p1's id>, path: 'src/m.py'}`; trace `claim{action: 'precheck_block'}` (T-REG-19, REG-22, R1-095, R2-L09, PRO-24, R4-28).
- `"the race across a CAS loss: the rebuilt intent drops the op and blocks the CS"` — p0's intent is built before p1's claim lands; p1 lands at p0's `publish.beforePush`; p0's rebuild fails the precheck → as above, and the code of the rest of p0's window still publishes (T-REG-19, OQ-12).
- `"a failing drift re-anchor is dropped and the claim stays valid"` — p0's s1 holds `k` on `b` and p1's t1 holds `k1` on `c`, both anchored at L1 (M15); Plan 3's team writer (non-conforming, no B1) pushes one hunk replacing rows 7–10 (`b`'s last row, the two blank rows, `def c():`) by four new rows and appends 1,001 rows after `c`; at p0's next push (its CS on `src/other.py`) `k`'s drift image takes in that hunk's b-side, which `k1`'s image also covers → the precheck fails → `dropClaim('k')`, no CS blocked, no `CLAIM_OVERLAP`; `k` stays HELD at L1 in both peers' folds and is retried at p0's following push (R1-095, REG-22, R4-32).
- `"T-PRO-05: a validator-rejected own claim is not K3, raises CLAIM_REJECTED once and is never re-sent"` — p0's `fillClaim` wrapped (test-only) to set `anchor` to the parent of the live code → the op folds invalid; `takeClaimRejections` → `claim_invalid`; no PIN; `status.json` `CLAIM_REJECTED {claim_id: k, reason: 'anchor'}`; `k` stays a TENTATIVE draft; over 20 rounds no op with `claim_id: k` is pushed; a second forced rejection of `(k, 'anchor')` raises no second code (T-PRO-05, PRO-24, PRO-38, R2-L09, R4-28).
- `"a generic-check rejection of an own claim op is renumbered and never K3"` — `claimRejectionStep` given `{type: 'claim', reason: 'generic', claimId: 'k'}` → no `blockCs`, no code; the slot is kept and `renumberAtOrBelow` re-numbers it (PRO-24 bullet 1, R4-28).
- `"an own claim op folded invalid by overlap holds its CS at the next B1"` — `claimPrecheck` wrapped (test-only) to return `[]`, so p0 pushes `k0` over p1's earlier claim; it folds invalid with `claim_overlap` → `blockCs(cs, <p1's id>)` → the group's next B1 returns the block and Plan 3's slot makes the PIN `claim:<p1's id>` (PRO-24 bullet 2, R4-28, 02 §6 "folded invalid anyway").
- `"an idle 30-minute run with a HELD claim and peer churn above D_reanchor makes no claim-initiated push"` — TC-HLD-05 shape: p0 holds `k`; p1 pushes 1,200 rows of churn in 3 pushes then idles; over 1,800,000 ms virtual p0 makes 0 push attempts and the quiescence check sees 0 attempts in 40 extra rounds (R4-35, R2-L17, PRO-38, INV-CONVERGE).
- `"a restart drops unemitted drafts and unlanded claim slots"` — p0 with a local-only draft on `d` and an allocated, unlanded slot for `k`: after `restart(core)` `claims.drafts()` = `[]`, `outbox.claimSlots()` = `[]`, the journaled outbox JSON has no slot; s1's next edit of `b` drafts again (R4-31, 2Q-18).

`test/integration/claimStates.test.ts`
- `"T-PRO-07: a CAS-loss rebuild, the regionSpec VERSION pause and renumbering give no false K3"` — p0's allocator at 10: intent 1 = [claim `k` (10), `request_status` r (11)], writer 12; p1 lands at p0's `publish.beforePush` → CAS lost; p0's `deps.regionSpec` set to `X'` before its next iteration → regionSpec VERSION pause; intent 2 = [r (11)], writer 13, lands; `deps.regionSpec` back to X → pause ends; `k` lands with seq 14 (> 13), folds HELD, no PIN, no `CLAIM_OVERLAP`, no `CLAIM_REJECTED` (T-PRO-07, PRO-09, PRO-45, R2-L08).
- `"claims stay TENTATIVE under FREEZE and publish after it"` — EPOCH freeze on p0 (a `git stash` in its clone) with a draft on `b` and a HELD claim `k2` released explicitly: the `release` lands, the claim op does not; after `git stash pop` (E1) the claim lands in the next window (PRO-31, PRO-45, REG-11).
- `"T-PRO-33 episode: a withheld new claim alone starts BOARD_FULL once"` — `makeEngines({n: 2})` with p0's folded `record_count` at 5,000 (a `foldBoard` state of 5,000 records imported before start) and no hold creation pending: s1's edit fills a new claim slot, which the take withholds → `core.holdCode` is `BOARD_FULL` and one `event{kind: health, reason_code: BOARD_FULL}` is drafted for the episode (Plan 3's `mirrorSync`, now triggered by a withheld claim creation); a second withheld new claim in the next window drafts no second event; after a release lands and the folded `record_count` is below the cap the episode ends and the claim lands (T-PRO-33, PRO-06, R4-30, Plan 3's episode).
- `"caps: a group whose re-anchor does not fit is dropped from C whole"` — p0's outbox holds 1,000 earlier-seq opaque ops; p0's ready CSs on `src/m.py` (HELD `k` needs re-anchor (a)) and `src/other.py` (no claim): the window's C carries only `src/other.py`; the next window carries the `src/m.py` group with `k`'s re-anchor in the same board commit (B2-05, 01 §3.12 "Ops riding with code").

**Crashpoints:** none new; claim slots ride the push intent's `op_ids` through Plan 2's existing `publish.*` anchors; no new yield point (the tests use `publish.beforePush`). **Lint:** L-REGION-WRITE (`src/claims/emit.ts` writes nothing), L-ONE-MAPPER (EMIT maps only through `src/regions/mapper.ts`); no new rule.

### Task 15: Merge-time region checks in apply and publish

**Covers:** R1-051, R1-054, R1-130…R1-134, R2-B05, B2-06, B2-08, B2-09, R2-M02, R2-M03; DAT-41, DAT-44, ENG-29, REG-24, REG-25, INV-REG-NOWRITE (unit form), INV-REG-FENCE (placement half); T-REG-01, T-REG-06 (identity and fence), T-REG-22 (incl. a hand-built base-move commit), TC-REG-01, O-OFFDISK's `claim-breach` exception, the D8 step-4 test; OQ-23, 3Q-14, A-09, 2X-17, 2X-18 (binding); R4-38 (the T0 call site), R4-47 (binding), R4-48, R4-49, R4-51, R4-69 (X7), R4-79 (findings on the verdict), R4-80 (the region-gap suite per mechanism); reconciliation X-15, X-28 (`BreachHit.holdId`; breaches travel only through `BreachPort`). **Depends on:** Tasks 9, 12, 14. Wave H.

**Files:**

**Notes:**
- **Call sites (R4-48; OQ-23 settled as a direct call, not a port).** `applyStep`: for every path where `mergeTree` returned rc 0 and both `S_disk[p] ≠ A[p]` and `TIP[p] ≠ A[p]`, `placementStep(core, it, 'apply', {base: A, ours: S_disk, theirs: TIP, out: M_disk, paths, fence: null})`. `publishStep`: after OUT is built and before T0, `placementStep(core, it, 'publish', {base: A, ours: P, theirs: TIP, out: OUT, paths: both-sides paths ∪ {p : OUT[p] ≠ TIP[p] and the view has a foreign claim on p}, fence: {gate: this window's B1 results, view, publishingSeats}})`. A failed path enters Plan 2's `conflict()` as a `'content'` unit with the merge's stages; the verdict is `TAKE_TIP` (TIP on disk / in OUT, the local blob a CONFLICTED record, K4), never a group PIN; one `placement` trace event per failed path. The check reads the table-cache entries T0's tripwires parsed.
- **The fence's foreign claims.** A claim is foreign to the published delta unless every publishing seat of the path's groups is its holder seat (REG-19 through Task 12's `isForeign` with the CSs' attribution); an `admitted` entry of the window's B1 result for `(claim, path)` exempts it.
- **Breach walk.** `breachStep(core, it, laC)` reads the first-parent commits of `(LA_C, TIP]` (`firstParents`, `readCommit`, the `Cowork-*` trailers), builds `BreachCommit`s only for commits changing a path on which a seat of this peer holds a HELD claim, calls `breach`, sets each hit's `holdId` to the id of the K4 record this apply gave guard `claim-breach` on that path (`applyStep`'s `holdOutcome` of the D8 step-4 `TAKE_TIP`), else null (X-28), and hands the hits to `deps.regions.breach.onBreach` once per apply (none when empty) — the only route a breach travels; no `ApplyReport` field carries breaches (X-28). `logBreach` writes one daemon-log line per hit with ids only and emits one `breach` trace event per hit; Plan 6 binds `BreachPort` and posts `event{kind: breach}` (C-11).

**Tests:**

`test/unit/placement.test.ts` — `inlineWorkers`; notation of Task 13's Notes; trees built with `commitTree`.
- `"identity passes when each hunk's post-image unit is the unit at its image in out"` — base M15, ours `M15+{7:    z = 1}`, theirs `M15^4`, out = their merge (`b` rows 9–12) → `[]` (REG-24 (a), R1-131, R1-051).
- `"identity fails the premerge same-tail path"` — Task 2's `premerge` fixture (ours appends to `compute_total`, theirs to `compute_tax`; `merge-tree` rc 0 moves `return round(total, 2)` into `compute_tax`) → `[{path, sub: 'a', reason: 'label_mismatch', claimId: null}]` (T-REG-06, R1-131).
- `"identity fails on a TOMBSTONE or WHOLE_FILE image"` — out = theirs (ours' inserted row missing; placementCheck takes any four trees) → `reason: 'tombstone'`; theirs rewriting 5,001 rows of `bigBomCrlf(5_960)` while ours edits row 10 → `reason: 'whole_file'` (R1-131).

- `"fence fails a path whose out changes a line inside a foreign claim not admitted"` — p1's HELD claim on `b`; out differs from theirs at row 6 → `[{sub: 'b', reason: 'fence', claimId: <p1's id>}]`; the same with an `admitted` entry `{csId, claimId: <p1's id>, path}` in the gate result → `[]` (REG-24 (b), R1-132).

`test/integration/placement.test.ts` — `makeEngines({n: 2})`.
- `"T-REG-06 unclaimed, apply side: an identity failure takes TIP and records the local blob"` — `premerge`: p0's unpublished append to `compute_total`; p1's append to `compute_tax` lands; p0's apply: `mergeTree` rc 0, identity fails → `conflict()` `TAKE_TIP`: p0's disk holds TIP's blob, one CONFLICTED PATCH with guards `[conflict]` holds p0's blob as `s2`, trace `placement{use: 'apply', sub: 'a'}`; `compute_tax` is byte-identical to p1's version in every live commit on the remote (T-REG-06, DAT-41, ENG-29, R1-133, B2-08, R2-M02, R4-48).
- `"T-REG-06 unclaimed, publish side: OUT takes TIP on the failed path and the rest of the CS publishes"` — p0's ready CS covers the `premerge` path and `src/other.py`; p1 lands first; p0's publish: OUT[premerge path] = TIP, one CONFLICTED record, C carries `src/other.py`; no PIN record (R1-133, B2-08, R4-48, OQ-23).
- `"T-REG-06 fence variant: a block-claimed grammarless neighbour fails the fence sub-check"` — the `premerge` content as `calc.txt` padded to 320 rows (class 6); p1 holds a block claim on `compute_tax`'s block; p0's append in `compute_total`'s block passes B1; the merge moves the row into `compute_tax`'s block → fence fails → `TAKE_TIP`, K4 record; no live commit changes `compute_tax`'s block (T-REG-06, R1-132, INV-REG-FENCE).
- `"with B1 bypassed, the fence alone keeps foreign-claimed lines out of C"` — p0 with `CoreOptions.ports.gate = openGate` (fault injection) edits row 6 inside p1's HELD claim on `b`: p0's publish fails (b) for `src/m.py` → `TAKE_TIP`, CONFLICTED record, trace `placement{use: 'publish', sub: 'b'}`; C has no row-6 change (INV-REG-FENCE placement half, REG-24, R4-48).
- `"a both-sides edit whose image lands in a moved function's new neighbour fails identity"` — the `premerge` layout with `compute_tax` (unclaimed) first elsewhere in the file: p1 moves it to directly below `compute_total` and publishes; p0's unpublished append at the end of `compute_total` merges with rc 0 after the moved neighbour's identical `return round(…, 2)` row (the case is pinned at its first green run, R4-21) → identity fails (`label_mismatch`), `TAKE_TIP`, one CONFLICTED record (R1-131, B2-08, REG-24).
- `"placement reuses T0's parse of OUT"` — a spy on `RegionWorkers.run`: per publish, one `table` job per distinct OUT blob across T0 and placement (R1-133, R4-48).
- `"INV-REG-NOWRITE unit form"` — for each landed publish of the T-REG-01 cases: every blob of tree(C) equals `TIP[p]`, `P[p]` or `OUT[p]`; recomposing the window with B1 replaced by a port that returns only the recorded `blocked` list and placement replaced by the recorded failures gives the same C tree oid (INV-REG-NOWRITE, R2-M02).
- `"the T0 slot passes ctx and gets item-2 findings"` — X7 (R4-69) through the engine: p1 lands `orders.py` with row 14 `    total = foo(price, qty)`; p0's CS changes `lib.py`'s `foo(a, b)` to `foo(a)` and publishes; `deps.t0Regions` is `regionT0(deps.regions.host)` wrapped by a test-side recorder (`ports: (deps) => ({t0Regions: recording(regionT0(deps.regions.host))})`, Plan 3's function form, R4-77, X-15): the recorded call carries `ctx = {a, p, csIds}` and its verdict is `{pass: false, paths: ['lib.py'], item: 2, findings: [{check: 2, path: 'orders.py', line: 14, reason: 'incompatible_caller', symbol: {qualname: 'foo', simple: 'foo'}}]}`; the publish makes one K7 PIN with guard `t0` over the CS's paths and pushes nothing of it (findings travel on the verdict only, no record field carries them, R4-79); with the Plan 3 call shape (no `ctx`) the same port answers `{incomplete: true}` (R4-38, R4-79, TC-REG-01).
- `"apply and publish bind regionConflictHooks"` — an EOF-append race on M15 (p0 and p1 each append a different top-level `def` after row 15) → `CONCAT` with exactly 2 blank rows between the defs, no record; two different `def helper` bodies → D9 tripwire label count → not qualified → `TAKE_TIP` plus a K4 record; the same races with the hooks unbound give Plan 2's verdicts (R4-47, 2X-18, DAT-42).

**Notes:**
- **Upgrades in tests.** A peer "upgrades to `X'`" by being re-created with `Engine.create` on the same clone with `PeerFixture.deps.regionSpec = X'` and then `restart` (test builds only); a downgrade is the reverse; the new core re-attaches the same `RegionHost`, so the bound ports follow it (R4-36). The runtime and the worker pool stay the real ones, so tables are the same on both sides and only the regionSpec comparison differs — which is all REG-04 and PRO-48 consult.

**Notes:**
- **The profile (R4-59).** 3 peers per `CLAIMS_PEER_PROFILE`; two scripted seats on p0 (`s1`, `s2`) and on p1 (`t1`, `t2`), p2 human only; 60 steps per seed; weights (per 100 draws): `seat_body_edit` 14, `seat_append_last` 6, `seat_decorator_above` 4, `seat_comment_above` 4, `seat_new_unit` 6, `rename_unit` 5, `move_unit` 4, `move_unit_cross` 3, `seat_delete_unit` 3, `hookfree_write` 8, `race_same_peer` 6, `race_two_peers` 8, `human_own_pc` 5, `human_other_pc` 5, `same_tail_append` 5, `half_typed_boundary` 5, `peer_churn` 6, `seat_end` 3 (a seat that ended starts again at its next draw). Generated edits keep Plan 3's rules: ≥ 2 unchanged rows between edits meant to merge, one fresh `CWM_` marker per edit (TST-08), mtimes from the virtual clock. Every peer's region `GitRepo` is wrapped in `readGuard` (INV-REG-READ); workers are `inlineWorkers` and budgets never expire unless a test injects them (TST-03). Grants never occur (no `answer` before Plan 6); base moves never occur (Plan 7) — those INV-FENCE exceptions are vacuous here and unit-tested in Tasks 12 and 15.
- **INV-FENCE oracle (R4-60).** For every `publish` event with a landed `push` (and, before the retry, for every `compose` event that follows a CAS loss): `L` = the board tip the push leased; active claims = `testClaimState(L)` HELD records (STALE excluded); for every claim `c` on a path p that `hunks(TIP[p], C[p])` changes: `c`'s ranges and tombstones mapped into `TIP[p]` with FoldMapper/1 (`fold`); a changed TIP row inside a mapped range, or an inserted row with `lo ≤ k < hi`, or an inserted row the model places inside the claimed unit's extent in `C[p]`, must be authored (model) by `c`'s holder seat or by `{holder peer, 'human'}`; anything else fails, naming the commit, path, claim, row and author.
- **INV-REG-NOWRITE (R4-61).** For every landed publish: test-side `git merge-tree --write-tree --merge-base=<aTree> <pTree> <tip>` (MERGE_FLAGS through `ctx.git`), then each path of that window's `placement` events and of the `publish` event's `conflicted` list replaced by `TIP[p]` and each `replaced` concat blob put in place — the result must equal tree(C); every blob of tree(C) must equal `TIP[p]`, `P[p]` or that recomposed `OUT[p]`. No engine function is called.
- **What M46_n shows in SIM.** R4-62 pairs `M46_n` with TC-REG-09's check-n unit fixture (Task 10). Here its SIM form only asserts the effect the mutant makes visible — a check-n failing scripted edit captured and published mid-turn; O-EDITCHECK, which would fire on it, is Plan 5's (R3-49, R3-50).

- `"TC-REG-02, -06 and -08 SIM forms"` — seeds weighted `seat_comment_above` 15, `rename_unit` 15, `move_unit` 10, `move_unit_cross` 10, `half_typed_boundary` 15: INV-FENCE passes; a comment inserted directly above a unit held by another seat is held (glossary §23 default); no live commit holds two copies of a generated unit (model check); no claim op is anchored on a tree whose error spans cover its first row (TC-REG-02, TC-REG-06, TC-REG-08).
- `"T-REG-29 SIM half (INV-REG-EDITCHECK): forced-fail edit checks change only when, never what"` — seed `claims-e-0` with scripted multi-edit turns whose mid-turn edit checkpoints are `checkpoint` steps (`scriptedSimSeat.checkpoint`, R4-82): run A with `forceEditCheckFail`, run B with the same script minus every `checkpoint` step (turn-end publication only, the rev 2.3 timing): the sequence of pushed blobs per path is byte-identical; in run C (real checks) every pushed blob has no `placement` failure and a passing T0 in the trace (INV-REG-EDITCHECK, T-REG-29 SIM half).
- `"T-REG-29 SIM: ten passing edits publish mid-turn; a broken-caller turn publishes nothing until a K7 PIN at its close"` — `claimsSetup('claims-t29-0', {peers: 4, script})` with the default budget B = 12: (i) s1 makes ten passing edits of `src/big.py`, each followed by a `checkpoint` step, and no `turn_end` until the script's last step: at least one landed publish carries s1's CS state before that `turn_end`, and the receiver-apply latency (s1's landing → each other peer's apply, virtual time) is written to the run report, not gated here (the publish p95 ≤ 40 s soak gate is Plan 7's, Table C C-18); (ii) in a later turn s1 gives a function of `src/big.py` a new required parameter (`required_added`) and keeps editing it, while a definite same-file caller in a row the turn never edits stays un-updated, each edit followed by a `checkpoint` step: every `edit_check` event of the turn has `checks: [2]`, no publish carries s1's CS before its `turn_end`, and the closing capture's publish makes exactly one K7 PIN with guard `t0` over s1's CS paths, nothing of it pushed (T-REG-29, INV-REG-EDITCHECK, HLD-50 item 2, R4-82).
- `"glossary invariants Plan 4 supports hold on claims seeds"` — on claims-f-0…claims-f-4: no peer pushes an `event` op of a conflict kind (INV-ONE-EVENT); no claim op names `.env` or `.github/workflows/ci.yml` although seats edit both (INV-SECRET, REG-32); the same seed with every virtual wall clock shifted +3,600,000 ms gives identical claim ids and validity (INV-CLOCK); every K3 CS shows its hold or `CLAIM_OVERLAP` in `status.json` within 2 iterations (INV-NOSILENT); Plan 3's INV-NOLOSS and INV-MASK and Plan 2's INV-PUSHED-IS-MERGE pass (R2-G01, R2-G02, R1-150).
- `"detection halves of the cross-chapter tests"` — one scripted claims run: `race_two_peers` → a K3 PIN and the path's live code unchanged (TC-COO-03); a team writer's `teamPush` inside s1's claim → one breach hit (K5), `human_other_pc` → a K6 PIN, a seat removing a called function → a `t0` PIN with a check-3 finding (K7) (TC-COO-05); t1's hooked edit into s1's claim → `ConfirmResult.touches` names s1's claim (TC-CCI-07); `Engine.predictRevert` of a result touching t1's claim → `wouldHold` names t1 and nothing is written (TC-F10-08, -09) (R2-N06).
- `"Plan 2's six and Plan 3's ten exit oracles stay green on claims seeds; edits, faults and holds stay green under plan4Ports"` — claims-x-0…claims-x-3, edits-x-0, faults-x-0 and holds-x-0 with every exit oracle (R2-G01, R4-59).

`test/sim/claims.mutants.test.ts` — TC-TST-02 form: the expected oracle last in the list, the mutant off → every oracle passes.
- `"M21 fails INV-FENCE"` — scripted: s1 claims `b` in `src/big.py`; s2 (same peer) hook-free writes inside `b`: with M21 (`isForeign` per PC) the CS publishes and INV-FENCE fails at that publish naming s2's row in s1's claim; with M21 off the CS is held (TC-TST-02, TC-REG-07, R4-62, R2-N04, B2-13, R1-151).
- `"M22 fails INV-FENCE and O-REG-NOLABEL"` — scripted with `RealEngineOptions.claims.boardLag = (id) => id === <t1's claim on b>` on peer 1 (the T-REG-04 (ii) wrapper of Task 14: `dropClaim` at every intent build while it answers true): t1 claims `b` and renames it `b2` in a push whose re-anchor is thus withheld (board lag), so the board keeps label `b` at the old anchor while live code has `b2`; s1 applies and edits inside `b2`: with M22 (the claim mapped into s1's base by the label `b`, which no unit there carries) no touch is found and s1's CS publishes → O-REG-NOLABEL fails at that gate event and INV-FENCE at the publish; with M22 off FoldMapper/1 maps the claim onto `b2` and the CS is held (TC-TST-02, TC-REG-05, R4-62, R2-N04, B2-13, R1-151).
- `"each M46_n lets a check-n failing edit be captured mid-turn"` — four scripted runs, n = 1…4: s1 makes an edit failing check n only (unclosed paren; a non-removal signature change — a new required parameter or a removed parameter — with an un-updated definite caller, R4-70; a call to a function the CS removed; a duplicated `def`), then a `checkpoint` step and no `turn_end`: with `M46_n` off the trace shows `edit_check{checks: [n]}`, no capture and no landed publish of that state; with `M46_n` on the state is captured and published before any `turn_end` (TC-TST-02, TC-REG-09 SIM form, R4-62, R2-N04, B2-13).

`test/sim/holds.p5.test.ts`
- `"05 P5 / INV-HLD-CLAIMFREE: exp_a and exp_bc give identical S_pub and record sequences with claims off, on and racing"` — Plan 3's `hold/exp_a` and `hold/exp_bc` scripts, B1 bound to `openGate` in all runs (the one sanctioned claim input, HLD-06), run three ways: `claims: 'off'`; `'on'` (scripted seats claim every unit the scripts edit); racing (a seeded team writer's `teamOps` claim and release ops interleaved with the scripts' pushes): per peer, `inspect().sPub` after every `mask` event and the registry record sequence compared on (kind, paths, guards, h0, h1, state, residence) are identical across the three (05 P5, INV-HLD-CLAIMFREE, B3-06, R4-67).
- `"no src/holds module imports src/claims"` — Task 5's structural test re-run: Plan 3's scan of `src/holds/{revert,mask,rebase,evaluate,provenance}.ts` and `src/engine/{holdOps,detect}.ts` with `src/claims` in the forbidden set stays green, and only `src/holds/mask.ts` imports `src/regions/mapper.ts` (B3-06, R4-67, L-CLAIMFREE-HOLDS).

**Crashpoints:** none new; the `claims` profile arms Plan 2's existing names through `SimOptions.crash` exactly as the `holds` profile. **Lint:** none (test and tool code).

### Task 18: The exit run, performance gates and CI budgets

**Covers:** roadmap row 4 exit (INV-FENCE and O-REG-* on 1,000 `claims` seeds per OS; the edit-check unit corpus); 3X-07 (the exit run), R2-J02, R2-K03 (R1-154, R2-O06, V-01, V-02 moved with the viewer rows to follow-up 4-V, X-91); T-REG-15 (gate p95 ≤ 15 ms from cache over ≥ 200 `predict` calls on the 26,436-line BOM+CRLF fixture, R4-71; event-loop delay ≤ 20 ms during a worker parse; mapper ≤ 40 ms at D = 2,000 on 5,960 lines), T-REG-29 (p95 ≤ 0.5 s over ≥ 200 checkpoints with `M[p] = R[p]`, real time per OS; the real 0.6 s worker stall), TC-REG-09's budget row, PF-DAT-02; R4-58 (the `ci-config` exception), R4-59, R4-64, R4-71; reconciliation X-04 (this plan's spec-edit batch), X-112, X-113 (the CI rule: `claims` 15 per leg). **Depends on:** Task 17. The viewer rows of R4-68 are not here: they land in follow-up 4-V, after 8a Task 20 (X-91, X-92). Wave K.

**Files:**
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (per leg, by the X-113 rule: `claims` 15 beside `faults` 15 and `holds` 15 and every other merged profile at its count, `edits` the remainder — 55 while no Plan 5–7 profile has merged; Plan 2's per-Node offsets; one `test:perf:regions` step per OS on the Node 20 leg), `.github/workflows/sim-exit.yml` (+ `claims` shards per OS; each plan adds its own shards, X-113), `package.json` (scripts `sim:exit:claims` and `test:perf:regions`), `test/unit/ci-config.test.ts` (extended: the `claims: 15` entry appended to the per-profile count table of the X-113 rule, which Plan 3 Task 16 first asserts (F-P3-8) — R4-58's named exception; every other `ci-config` assertion kept), `test/integration/sim.seeds.cli.test.ts` (the `claims` profile)
- Modify **after owner approval (X-04):** the spec files of this plan's spec-edit batch (header "Spec edits this plan lists"), applied against rev 2.5 after Plan 3's batch, the commit naming the batch it follows; items rev 2.5 already carries are dropped (X-03)
- Create: `test/perf/regions.perf.test.ts`
- Ledger: the exit artifact (below)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `threadWorkers`, `clockBudgets`, `BUDGETS`; Task 2 `bigBomCrlf(26_436)`, `bigBomCrlf(5_960)`, `oneDotThreeMb()` (Task 2's README states whether the 1.3 MB fixture and the 26,436-line file are the same bytes; each gate below names the fixture its spec row names); Task 3 `mapRangesLines`, `editDistance`; Task 10 `editCheck`, `regionEditCheck`; Task 11 `predict`, `PredictQuery`, `ClaimCtx`; Task 12 `gate`; Task 13 `LabelVerifier.schedule`; Task 15 `regionConflictHooks`; Plan 2 `conflict`; Task 17 `claimsSetup`, `exitOracles(ns, {holds, claims})` (X-112), `run-seeds --profile claims`; Plan 1 `realClock` (`machineMonoMs`); Plan 2 `run-seeds`/`run-shard` CLIs, `ci-config.test.ts`; Plan 3 R3-51, R3-55, R3-58's shard rule, `npm run test:sim` and Task 16's X-113 count table in `ci-config.test.ts`.
- Produces: the perf gates; the PR legs and `sim-exit.yml` claims shards; `npm run sim:exit:claims` (= `tsx tools/sim/run-seeds.ts --profile claims --seeds 1000 --parallel 8`, a rehearsal), `npm run test:perf:regions`; this plan's applied spec-edit batch (after owner approval, X-04).

**Contract additions** (stated once in §Interfaces "Test helpers" and §File Structure): `package.json` script `test:perf:regions` (`node --test` over `test/perf/**` with real ports; never part of `npm test`, whose virtual clock cannot time anything).

**Spec:** 04 §3.18 (every row with a number), §7 T-REG-15, T-REG-29; glossary §20 (Gate latency, Parse, Region mapper, Region numbers, CI row "200 seeds/OS per PR"), §27.1 (≤ 0.5 s on a 1.3 MB file; the latency model), §26.2 item 4 (≥ 200 calls); 11-testing §3.3 run steps 4–5, §3.11 (PR legs, 20-minute leg budget), §7 TC-REG-09 (budget row), TST-13, TST-17 (Plan 7's soak); 04 §8 Q5, Q7 (Plan 7 measures, R4-64); roadmap row 4 exit; rulings R3-51, R3-55, R3-58, R4-59, R4-64; reconciliation X-03, X-04, X-112, X-113.

**Notes:**
- **Perf gates (R4-13, R4-64).** `test/perf/regions.perf.test.ts` runs only under `npm run test:perf:regions`, with `threadWorkers(runtime, {threads: 2, workerPath: 'dist/worker.js', clock: realClock})` and `clockBudgets(realClock)`, after `npm run build`, once per OS on the Node 20 leg of `ci.yml`; a gate miss fails the leg. Percentiles are nearest-rank over the stated number of calls after 10 warm-up calls; the report JSON (`artifacts/perf-regions-<os>.json`) records every percentile, the cache bytes and the share of edit checks ending `incomplete` (inputs to Plan 7's soak and 04 §8 Q5/Q7, R4-64).
- **Exit run (R3-51, R3-55, R3-58 applied to `claims`).** This task first lands the `ci.yml` legs and `sim-exit.yml` with a provisional `s = 20` claims seeds per shard, records each OS's p50/p95 per-seed time from the first green PR run in the ledger, then sets `s = min(100, ⌊0.8 × 120 min × 4 ÷ p95⌋)` per OS in a following commit of this task; shards run `run-seeds --profile claims --seeds <s> --from <s·k> --prefix exit- --parallel 4` for k = 0…⌈1,000 / s⌉ − 1, `timeout-minutes: 120`, each uploading its report; every profile asserts `exitOracles(ns, {holds: {markers}, claims: {model}})` where it has a model and `exitOracles(ns, {holds: {markers}})` otherwise (X-112).
- **Reproducing a failing seed (TST-03).** Every shard report lists each failing seed with its full rerun command — `tsx tools/sim/run-seeds.ts --profile claims --prefix exit- --from <n> --seeds 1` (plus `--mutant <id>` when a mutant was on) — which reproduces the failure byte-identically (op-log, trace, remote ref oids) on the same OS; the ledger records that command, the OS and the failing oracle for any exit failure, and a fix is re-verified by rerunning it before the shard is re-dispatched.
- **Viewer rows (R4-68, X-91, X-92).** Not in this task: they land in follow-up 4-V once 8a Task 20 has merged; R4-27 is decided by the owner's choice of 2026-10-08 (the viewer's fold takes claim inputs). The exit criterion is the claims run plus the edit-check corpus; the ledger records 4-V's state.
- **Spec-edit batch (X-03, X-04).** After the exit evidence and the owner's approval of the batch, this task applies the header's spec-edit list to `docs/spec` against rev 2.5, after Plan 3's batch (one order: Plan 2 → 8a → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6 → 7 → 8b), re-checking each item against rev 2.5 (an item rev 2.5 already carries is dropped; item 1 is dropped already; item 11 is verified only: the owner's choice of 2026-10-08 applied it on the proposals branch), in one commit that names the batch it follows.

**Tests:**

`test/perf/regions.perf.test.ts` (real time, per OS)
- `"T-REG-15: predict p95 ≤ 15 ms from cache over ≥ 200 calls on bigBomCrlf(26_436)"` — the gate budget is the PreToolUse region decision's (R4-71): a warm `TableCache` and line arrays holding the fixture's table; one foreign HELD claim on a function mid-file; 200 `predict(q, ctx)` calls (after 10 warm-up calls), each an Edit `PredictQuery` whose `old_string` lies inside that function; because `predict` stops at its 15 ms `deadline('gate')` and answers `undecided` past it (R4-71), the observable form of "p95 ≤ 15 ms" is: at least 95 % of the 200 calls (≥ 190) answer `located` with that claim in `touches` (`predictUndecidedShare` ≤ 0.05), every other call answers `undecided` (never a `located` answer without the touch), and the nearest-rank p95 latency of the `located` answers is reported as `gateP95Ms`; with 30 label verifications queued on the `bg` worker during a second run of the 200 calls, the `located` share is still ≥ 95 % and every `located` verdict equals the first run's (T-REG-15, T-REG-26 gate-p95 clause, 04 §3.18 rows 1–2, glossary §26.2 item 4).
- `"B1 gate timing is reported, not gated"` — 200 `gate(host, view, [group])` calls on a ready group with one hunk inside the same function: their nearest-rank p95 is written to the report as `b1GateP95Ms`; no threshold applies (the spec gives B1 no time budget, R4-71).
- `"T-REG-15: event-loop delay ≤ 20 ms during a worker parse"` — `monitorEventLoopDelay({resolution: 1})` around a cold `table` job of `bigBomCrlf(26_436)` on the `fg` worker: max delay ≤ 20 ms (T-REG-15, 04 §3.18 "Event-loop block").
- `"T-REG-15: mapper ≤ 40 ms at D = 2,000 on 5,960 lines"` — `bigBomCrlf(5_960)` and a copy with 1,000 rows replaced (D = 2,000 by `editDistance`): `mapRangesLines(…, 'fold')` p95 over 200 runs ≤ 40 ms; D > 5,000 → WHOLE_FILE in both modes, a 200 ms local deadline → WHOLE_FILE only in `local` mode (T-REG-15, 04 §3.18 "One mapping").
- `"T-REG-29: editCheck p95 ≤ 0.5 s over ≥ 200 checkpoints with M[p] = R[p] on oneDotThreeMb()"` — 200 successive single-hunk edits of the 1.3 MB fixture with `TIP = A` through `regionEditCheck(host).check` on `threadWorkers`: nearest-rank p95 ≤ 500 ms; the `incomplete` share is reported (T-REG-29, R2-K03, 04 §3.18 "Edit check").
- `"TC-REG-09 budget row: ≤ 0.5 s p95 over ≥ 200 checks on bigBomCrlf(26_436) in the worker"` — the same measurement on the 26,436-row BOM+CRLF fixture (one run serves both gates when Task 2's README records them as the same bytes) (TC-REG-09 budget row).
- `"T-REG-29: a real 0.6 s worker stall is incomplete with no REGION_BUDGET"` — `regionEditCheck(host).check` on `threadWorkers` with `clockBudgets(realClock)` while a test worker entry stalls the check's job 600 ms (real time): `{verdict: 'incomplete', entries: []}`, `host.raise` never called, one daemon-log record `EDIT_CHECK_OVERRUN {cs_id}` (X-30), the registry head and `status.json` `message_code` unchanged (the real-time form of Task 10's injected-stall test) (T-REG-29, REG-31 exception, R2-E13).
- `"PF-DAT-02: a concat on the 26,436-row file takes ≤ 0.5 s including both parses"` — two EOF appends to `bigBomCrlf(26_436)` (a Python file), `conflict()` with Task 15's `regionConflictHooks` on `threadWorkers` and the real clock: the median of 5 calls, the tripwire's parses included, ≤ 500 ms, each verdict `CONCAT` (PF-DAT-02, moved here from Task 9).
- `"the perf report carries Plan 7's soak inputs"` — `artifacts/perf-regions-<os>.json` has `gateP95Ms` (`predict`'s located answers), `predictUndecidedShare`, `b1GateP95Ms`, `eventLoopMaxMs`, `mapperP95Ms`, `editCheckP95Ms`, `editCheckIncompleteShare`, `concatMedianMs`, `parseMsMax`, `cacheBytes` and the fixture names; the soak on the real hot file (04 §8 Q5, Q7) and the 100,000-case nightly properties stay Plan 7's (R2-J02, R4-64, R2-K03).

`test/unit/ci-config.test.ts` (extended)
- `"ci.yml legs follow the X-113 rule with claims 15"` — the per-profile count table gains `claims: 15` (R4-58's named exception; the rule and every other `ci-config` row unchanged): each of the six legs runs `sim:seeds` with `--profile claims --seeds 15`, `--profile faults --seeds 15`, `--profile holds --seeds 15`, every other merged non-`edits` profile at its table count (`seats` 15, `coord` 15, `lifecycle` 10 once merged) and `--profile edits --seeds <100 − their sum>` (55 when only `faults`, `holds` and `claims` are merged), whatever the merge order; `--from 0` on Node 20 legs and `--from 100` on Node 24 legs, `--parallel 4`, job `timeout-minutes: 45`, and the final step reports the leg's SIM minutes against 20 as a warning, never a failure: 100 seeds per leg, 200 per OS per PR; the Node 20 legs also run `npm run test:perf:regions` (R4-59, X-113, TST-13, glossary §20 CI row).
- `"sim-exit.yml adds claims shards covering seeds 0…999 exactly once per OS"` — `workflow_dispatch` and the schedule kept; per OS `npm run test:sim` once, Plan 2's 10 `edits` shards, Plan 3's `holds` shards and `claims` shards `--from s·k` for k = 0…⌈1,000 / s⌉ − 1 with `s ≤ 100` (the provisional 20 or the measured value named in the ledger), `timeout-minutes: 120`, reports uploaded; `package.json` has `sim:exit:claims` (R4-59, roadmap row 4 exit, R3-55).
- `"the exit corpus: test:sim runs the edit-check unit corpus"` — `npm run test:sim` includes Task 10's T-REG-28, TC-REG-09 and TC-REG-10 files and Plan 3's 05 U10, and its run kills `M46_1`…`M46_4` (each fixture of TC-REG-09 fails with its mutant on) (roadmap row 4 exit "edit-check unit corpus", R4-62).

`test/integration/sim.seeds.cli.test.ts` (extended)
- `"run-seeds and run-shard accept --profile claims with the R3-51 report fields"` — `--profile claims --seeds 2 --prefix exit-` exits 0; `--profile claims2` exits 2; a seed made to fail (`--mutant M21` on a scripted M21 seed) is reported with its rerun command `tsx tools/sim/run-seeds.ts --profile claims --prefix exit- --from <n> --seeds 1 --mutant M21`, and running that command fails the same oracle at the same op with the same trace (R4-59, TST-03).
